137. eḻuttup poṟippukaḷuṭaṉ oṟṟik kompuk kāṇṭāmiruka (mākkal steatite) muttirikaḷ aintum, eṅkum kiṭikkāta iṉṉoru oṟṟik kompu kāṇṭāmiruka muttiriyiṉ uṭpuṟattil nīḷcatura veṟṟiṭam uḷḷatu. veṟṟiṭam cetukkappaṭṭatu ēṉ  eṉṟu puriyavilli.
138(i). akaḻāyvil caṅku, cippi, cempu, peyṉ ākiyavaṟṟil  ceyta poruḷkaḷ (Artefacts) kiṭittaṉa. kompuk kippiṭiyuṭaṉ ceppuk katti, cempup pāttiram pōṉṟavi ērāḷamākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. (orupāttirattiṟkuḷ ki vaḷiyal eṭṭu; pōrukkup payaṉpaṭuttum kōṭari camayac caṭaṅkiṟku payaṉpaṭṭirukkak kūṭiyatu, iviyum kiṭittaṉa)  cempi urukkum ulikaḷ illi; āṉāl cempi urukkum ciṟu caṭṭikaḷ crucibles kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. iṅku mukkiya uṟpatti caṅku vaḷiyal, peyaṉ āparaṇaṅkaḷ, cātāraṇa āparaṇakkal tayārippukaḷ, kaḻuttu māli maṇikaḷ pōṉṟavi ākum. peyaṉ āparaṇakkaṟkaḷ cuṟṟuc cuvariṉ uṭpakutiyilum, maṇikaḷ (beads) veḷiyilum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. 
(ii). kattikaḷukkup pakkattil perumaḷavu mīṉ elumpukaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. iṅku karuvāṭum tayārikkap paṭṭirukkalām.  mākaṉ (j‰nghija Oman) nāṭṭukku ēṟṟumati ceyyap payaṉpaṭṭa periya kaṟuppu niṟac cāṭikaḷum kiṭittaṉa.

iyal - 4 
cintu nākarikat toḻil nuṭpamum kaṭṭiṭak kaliyum
pirō ṭekṉālaji (Pyro Technology) eṉpatu tamiḻil nerupput toḻil nuṭpatti (atāvatu neruppi uruvākkutal, kaṭṭuppāṭṭil vittiruttal, kūṭṭutal / kuṟittal / mitappaṭuttal / toṭarcciyākap payaṉpaṭuttal mutaliyavaṟṟik) kuṟippatu. veppam eṉpatu kaṭattappaṭum cakti; atu uṇavic camikkap payaṉpaṭuvatōṭu, ulōkaṅkaḷi urukkutal, vaṭivamittal, kalappu ulōkam ceytal pōṉṟa palavaṟṟukkup payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. āti maṉitaṉ toḻil nuṭpattil veppappayaṉpāṭu aṭippaṭi ātāramāka viḷaṅkiyatu. 
2. āti maṉitarkaḷiṉ toḻilnuṭpat tiṟaṉik kuṟittu matippiṭātīrkaḷ eṉṟu colvati  tolliyal tuṟi mīṇṭum mīṇṭum meyppittuḷḷatu. neruppu eṉpatu oru karuvi (Fire is a tool). cintu māntar neruppik koṇṭu kaḷimaṇ pāṇṭaṅkaḷic cūḷiyiṭṭu cerāmik uruvākkiṉar. maṭpāṇṭam cūṭēṟṟiya piṟaku cerāmik ākiṟatu. immāṟṟattil maṭpāṇṭattiṉ uruvam māṟāmal uṟutippāṭu maṭṭum valuvākiṟatu. cintu makkaḷ ceppu ulōkat tāti 1083 pāki celciyacil urukkik karuvikaḷ, āyutaṅkaḷ āparaṇaṅ kaḷic ceyṉar. takaram allatu tuttanāka (zinc) tātukkaḷi urukki, urukiya cempuṭaṉ kalantu veṇkalam eṉṟa kalappu ulōkatti uruvākkiṉar. veṇkalattip paravalākap payaṉpaṭuttiya cintu nākarikatti veṇkala ūḻi nākarikam eṉṟu aḻikkiṉṟaṉar.
3. meḻuku uruvam ceytu, ataṉ mēl urukiya ulōkat tiravatti ūṟṟip piṉṉar cūṭēṟṟiyavuṭaṉ meḻuku uruki veḷi vantuviṭum; ulōka uruvam kiṭikkum -- iti urukum  meḻukut toḻilnuṭpam (Lost wax technology) eṉpar. itu cintuveḷiyil vaḻaṅkiyatu. (ulōkac cilikaḷi appaṭittāṉ iṉṟum ceykiṉṟaṉar.)  mākkallil muttiri ceytu cūṭukāṭṭi valuvāṉa (iṉṟum aḻiyāmal irukkum) muttirikaḷiyum ceytaṉar. terakōṭṭā ciṟu pommikaḷi cintuveḷik kaliñarkaḷ kali /vaḻipāṭṭup poruḷāka uruvākkiṉārkaḷ.
4. cintu uṟpattip poruḷkaḷil mutaliṭam vakitta āparaṇa maṇikaḷ (beads), peyaṉ (Faience) eṉappaṭum āparaṇakkaṟkaḷ pōṉṟavi kaṭum veppattil uruvākkappaṭṭavi. peyaṉ tayārippil 1200 pāki celciya veppam kaṭṭup pāṭāṉa muṟiyil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. kaḻuttu mālikkut tēviyāṉa maṇikaḷil tuḷiyiṭuvataṟkuriya tuḷik karuvikaḷiyum (nuṉiyil poruttum tuḷippāṉkaḷuṭaṉ) ceytaṉar. intat tuḷik karuviyāṉatu ulōkattilum, tuḷippāṉ (drill - bit) karuṅkallilum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. avi viṭūriyam (Agate) pōṉṟa valumikka āparaṇakkaṟkaḷilum tuḷi pōṭum tiṟaṉ peṟṟavi. kaḷimaṇṇil ciṟiya aḷavu poṭi ceyta karuṅkallik (Flint) kalantu `kalpāṇṭaṅkaḷi (Stoneware) uruvākkiṉar.  cintuveḷik kiviṉip poruḷkaḷil ciṟantavi intak kalpāṇṭaṅkaḷ ākum.
5. harappā neruppucār toḻilnuṭpam  mikavum uyartaramāṉatu; avarkaḷ ulōkaṅkaḷi mikavum nērttiyākak kiyāḷum tiṟaṉ peṟṟiruntaṉar. aṟivum aṉupavamum oruṅkē aminta toḻilāḷar poṟiyiyalāḷar cintuveḷiyil vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷuṭiya vētiyiyal (Chemistry) aṟivu  viyappaḷikkiṟatu. ulōkaṅkaḷik kalakkumpōtu ētō oru kiriyā ūkkiyip (catalyst) payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. apporuḷ eṉṉaveṉṟu teriyavilli. (An Encylopedia of the History of Technology: Ian Mcneil: (1990) Taylor & Francis.)
6. nakaramipput tiṭṭamiṭal, nakara nirmāṇam, cukātāramāṉa kuḷiyal vacati nilattaṭiyil cellum (puti cākkaṭi) kaḻivunīr vāykkāl, valip piṉṉal teru pōṉṟa paṇṭiya cintunākarika aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ viyappūṭṭuvaṉavākavum iṉṟum piṉpaṟṟak kūṭiya mātirikaḷākavum uḷḷaṉa. āḻkaṭal payaṇattiṟkuriya kappalkaṭṭum toḻilnuṭpam, kaṭaṟ kāṟṟu - kaṭal nīrōṭṭam paṟṟiya toḻil nuṭpam ivaṟṟil tērnta mālumikaḷum cintu nākarikattil iruntaṉar.
7(i). cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum cintu nākarikattiṉ taṉicciṟappuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷāka amikiṉṟaṉa. kaḷimaṇ kaṟkaḷ vēṟu; cūḷiyiṭṭuc cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷ vēṟu. kaḷimaṇ ceṅkal nīriṉ arippit tākkuppiṭikkātu. (avi kaḷimaṇ, vikkōl, pul ākiyavaṟṟip picintu accukaḷil vārttu veyilil kāya vittavi). cuṭṭaceṅkaṟkaḷ vicēṭamākak kaṭṭiya cūḷi aṭuppil cūṭēṟṟic ceypavi. pala maṇinēram 500 pāki celciya veppattil cūṭēṟṟi valuvāṉa ceṅkaṟkaḷi tayārikkalām. veṇkala ūḻiyiṉ ciṉṉam cuṭṭa ceṅkal. kimu 3200 - 1900 ākiya 1500 āṇṭukaḷic cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟ payaṉpāṭṭuk kālam eṉalām.
(ii). kuḷiyal aṟi, kaḻivunīr vāykkāl, kiṇaṟṟuc cuvar, veḷḷap perukkut taṭuppu ivaṟṟukku ceṅkaṟkaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟu aṅku ceytu payaṉpaṭuttiya ceṅkaṟkaḷ pala kōṭi ākum. avaṟṟuḷ perumpakuti tiruṭṭuppōyiṉa allatu piṟkālak kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. muḻuvatum ceṅkaṟkaḷāl maṭṭumē kaṭṭiya cintu nakaram etuvum illi. uṭpakkam kaḷimaṇ kaṟkaḷ, kaḷimaṇ; veḷippakkam ceṅkal eṉṟa muṟiyil muṉṉaṇi nakaraṅkaḷāṉa harappā, mokeñco-tarō, kaṉverivālā, rākkikārki pōṉṟa nakarkaḷil ciṟanta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. (tolavirā, caṉku-taro eṉpaṉa vitivilakku: tolavirā kallāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. rōjti kallum kaḷimaṇṇumākak kaṭṭappaṭṭatu.)
(iii). kaḷañciyam, nakaraccuṟṟuccuvar, citadel eṉum uyarmaṭṭap pakuti, ivaṟṟi uṟutiyāṉa muṟiyil ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭiṉar. ceṅkaṟkaḷic ceyya perum parappaḷavu kāṭukaḷ aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. veḷḷapperukku āpatti eṉṟum etirnōkkiya mokeñco-tarōvi `ceṅkal nakaram eṉṟē kuṟippiṭalām! nakaramayamākkalukkum ceṅkaṟkaḷukkum neruṅkiya toṭarpu uṇṭu.  mokeñco-tarō mēṭṭiṉ mītuḷḷa perum nīccal kēṇi (Great Bath) nīr kaciyātapaṭi cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭi, ataṉ mītu (iyaṟkiyil kiṭikkum) Bitumen eṉappaṭum tārp pūccu ceytum uruvākkappaṭṭatu.
8. cintup perunakaraṅkaḷ toḻilnuṭpa mēmpāṭu kāraṇamākavē vaḷarcci aṭintaṉa; poruḷ uṟpattiyum vaṇikamum perukiṉa. perunakara makkaḷukkāṉa uṇavi vēḷāṇ pakutikaḷ viḷittaṉa. annakaraṅkaḷil vāḻnta makkaḷil perumpālōr ceytoḻil vallavarkaḷāka (Artisans / Craftsmen) tiṭṭamiṭum atikārikaḷ, nirvākikaḷ, vaṇikar ākiyōrāka iruntaṉar. mikacciṟanta nakarppuṟanākarikam aṅku tiṭṭamiṭṭu uruvākkappaṭṭatu. ulakiṉ mutalāvatu nakarppuṟanākarikam (Urban Civilization) cintuveḷiyiltāṉ uruvāṉatu eṉak karutappaṭukiṟatu.


cintuveḷi niṟuttalaḷavi, nīṭṭalaḷavi, eṭi aḷavukaḷ
(The Indus weights & measures)

9. cintu nākarika makkaḷ eṭi aḷavīṭu, nīḷa / uyara / akala aḷavīṭu ākiyavip paṟṟiya aṟivil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉar. kālak kaṇippilum  tiṟaṉ peṟṟiruntaṉar. mokeñco-tarō, harappā nakaraṅkaḷiṉ amiviṭaṅkaḷip pūmiyiṉ turuva oḻuṅkiṉ aṭippaṭiyil terivu ceytirukkalām eṉpar cila kaṇita nipuṇarkaḷ. cintu nilapparappu muḻuvatiṟkum orē mātiriyāṉa (uniform) niṟuttal, nīṭṭal aḷavīṭukaḷi uruvākkiya vakiyil ulakilēyē muṉṉōṭikaḷāka iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ aḷavīṭukaḷ mikavum tulliyamāṉavi (extremely precise). mikac ciṟiya nīṭṭal pirivu 1.704 milli mīṭṭar. (lōtal -il kiṭitta yāṉit tantattil ceyta) `maṭṭap palakiyil (ruler) anta aḷavīṭu kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. veṇkala ūḻikkālattiṉ mikacciṟiya (ciṟanta) aḷavīṭu itu. vēṟu ennākarikattilum ittakiya ciṟiya nīṭṭalaḷavu illi. cintup poṟiyiyalāḷar tacamapiṉṉa muṟiyil nīṭṭal, niṟuttal aḷavīṭukaḷic ceytaṉar. niṟuttal aḷavīṭu avarkaḷuṭiya aṟukōṇa eṭikkal (Hexahedron) mūlam aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu.
10. ceṅkal uṟpattiyilum avarkaḷ tacamapiṉṉa muṟiyi 4:2:1 eṉṟa orēmātiriyāṉa vikitattil payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. cintup parappaḷavu muḻuvatum inta vikitam piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭatu. mokeñco-tarō maṭṭap palakiyil tacama piṉṉa muṟiyil ovvoru pakutiyum (unit) 33.5 milli mīṭṭar (1.32 aṅkulam) aḷavu ākum. inta aḷavil tavaṟu nērumāyiṉ atu oru aṅkulattiṉ 0.0005 pākatti miñcātu eṉpar.
11. cintu niṟuttalaḷavit tiṭṭam 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1, 2, 5, 10, 20, 50, 100, 200, 500 eṉṟa aṭippaṭiyilum, taṉiyoru aḷavīṭṭup pākam 28 kirām eṭiyuḷḷatākavum amintatu. itu āṅkilēyarkaḷuṭiya avuṉcu (Ounce) kirēkkarkaḷuṭiya aṉciyā (Uncia) ākiyavaṟṟiṟku nikarāṉatu. ciṉṉañciṟiya poruḷkaḷiṉ (maṇikaḷ, āparaṇaṅkaḷ) eṭi mēṟkūṟiya vikitattilum taṉiyoru aḷavīṭṭup pākam (each unit) 0.871 kirām eṭiyuḷḷatākavum amintatu.   The Science of Measurement: Arthur Klein: (1974): Dover, New York.
12. cumēriyāviṉ nippūr (Nippur) akaḻāyvil ki.mu. 2650 cārnta kiyūpiṭ (Cubit) nīṭṭalaḷavu ulōkak kōl (rod) kiṭittatu. atu 518.6 milli mīṭṭar (20.42) nīḷam. mēṟkulakiṉ kiyūpiṭ ilattiṉil kiyūpiṭṭam (Cubitum) ākum. muḻaṅki, vaṭa intiyāviṉ hata, teṉṉintiya muḻam eṉpaṉa muḻaṅki (aṭimutal naṭuviral nuṉivariyāṉa) aḷavik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.   kiyūpiṭ 45.72 ce.mī. koṇṭatu; hata muḻa aḷavukku nikarāṉatu. 
13. ivaṭṭā ṣikiyō eṉṟa kaṇita nipuṇar cintu niṟuttal, nīṭṭal aḷavīṭu paṟṟip kuṟippiṭuvatu varumāṟu: lōtalil kiṭitta āṉit tanta nīṭṭal aḷavu kōlil kāṇum mikak kuṟinta aḷavīṭṭup pākam 1.79 milli mīṭṭar. atu patam (fathom = 6 mo) aḷavīṭṭiṉ 1/940 pākamākum.
14. mokeñco-tarōvil kiṭitta oru kaṭal cippit tuṇṭil poṟitta nīṭṭal aḷaviṉ nīḷam 6.72. mimī. atu fathom -iṉ 1/1250l oṉṟu paṅku ākum. harappāvil kiṭitta veṇkala aḷavu kōliṉ nīḷam 9.33 milli mīṭṭar. (fathom-ïš 1,180 il oru pākam) akum.
“The minimum sub-division of the Ivory linear measure of Lothal was 1.79mm (that is equal to 1/1940 of a fathom)
In the fragment of a shell made in Mohenjo-Daro it was 6.72 mm (that is equal to 1/1250 of a fathom).
In the Bronze  scale of from Harappa it was 9.33 mm. (that is equal to 1/1180 of a fathom)”
- Weights and measures in the Indus Valley: Encyclopedia of the history of science, Technology and medicine in non-western cultures:  Heline Selin: Springer edition 2008.
15. cintu makkaḷ uruvākkiya niṟuttal nīṭṭal aḷavīṭukaḷtām pāracīka vaḷikuṭā nāṭukaḷukkum, mattiya āciya nāṭukaḷukkum paraviṉa; aṅkē inta aḷavīṭukaḷ ciṟcila māṟṟaṅkaḷōṭu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭalāyiṉa.
cintuveḷik kiṇaṟukaḷum paṭikkaṭṭuk kiṇaṟukaḷum 
(Wells and stepwells):

16(i). mokeñco-tarō peru nakaril māttiram ēṟattāḻa 700 kiṇaṟukaḷ iruntaṉa. cintu nākarikattil kiṇaṟukaḷ, paṭikkaṭṭuk kiṇaṟukaḷ, nīrttēkkaṅkaḷ mukkiya iṭam vakikkiṉṟaṉa. mikap paḻiya paṭikkaṭṭuk kiṇaṟukaḷ aṅku uḷḷaṉa. kimu 2500 aḷavil kiviṭappaṭṭa oru kiṇaṟṟil mūṉṟu vitamāṉa amippukaḷ oṉṟākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉa: kuḷikkum kuḷam, nīr maṭṭam vari paṭikkaṭṭukkaḷ mūlam iṟaṅkic ceṉṟu nīr eṭukkum vacati; camaya mukkiyattuva muḷḷa paṭivaṅkaḷ.
(ii). camaṇa, putta matattiṉar taṅkaḷ kōyilkaḷ mutaliya vaṟṟil paṭikkaṭṭuk kiṇaṟukaḷi iṇittaṉar. tolavirāvil pāṟikaḷi veṭṭi amitta nīrt tēkkaṅkaḷil paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ nīr maṭṭam varic ceṉṟaṉa. piṟkālattilum tāṅk (Dhank) - 550 - 625 AD, piṉāmal (Bhinamal) 850 - 950 AD mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil paṭikkaṭṭuk kiṇaṟukaḷ / kuḷaṅkaḷ amikkappaṭṭaṉa.
(iii). intiyāviṉ āḻamāṉa paṭikkaṭṭuk kiṇaṟukaḷil oṉṟu (irājatāṉ mānila) apanēri kirāmattil (Abhaneri) cant payōri (Chand Baori) eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ uḷḷatu; kipi 800-900 aḷavil amintatu; 100 aṭi āḻam, nīr maṭṭam varip paṭikkaṭṭukaḷiṉ eṇṇikki 3500. nūṟu varuṭaṅkaḷukku mēlāṉa kaliyaḻakumikka intak kiṇaṟṟi iṉṟu vari makkaḷ payaṉpaṭutti varukiṉṟaṉar.
cintu nākarika kaṭṭiṭak kali (The architecture of the Indus civilization):
17. varalāṟṟuc ciṟappu vāynta nākarikaṅkaḷi avi viṭṭuc ceṉṟa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ tām iṉam kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. ekiptiṉ piramiṭu, mecapotāmiyāviṉ cikurāṭ, cintunākarikap perunakaraṅkaḷ, cīṉāviṉ peruñcuvar pōṉṟavi eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷ. kaṭṭiṭak kaliyiṉ mukkiya nayaṅkaḷ piṉvaruvaṉa: kaṭṭiyavi nilittu niṟka vēṇṭum; ettēvikkāka kaṭṭiṉarō ati niṟivēṟṟuvatuṭaṉ, aḻakuṇarcciyiṉ vaṭivamāka amiyavēṇṭum. cintuk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiyum inta aṭippaṭiyil pārkka vēṇṭum.
18. cintu nakaraṅkaḷic cuṟṟi akaṉṟa, uyarnta matil cuvarkaḷik kaṭṭiyiruntaṉar. cuvarkaḷil vāyilkaḷ, katavukaḷ iruntaṉa. vaṇikak kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṟkākavum, āṟṟu veḷḷattit taṭuppataṟkākavum cuṟṟuccuvar mikavum valuvāka amikkappaṭṭatu. nakariṉ uṭpuṟattē uḷḷa cuvarkaḷ uyaram kuṟintavi. potuk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ, makkaḷ vāḻviṭaṅkaḷ, cantikaḷ, (citadel eṉappaṭum)  uyarnta mēṭṭup pakuti, toḻil niliyaṅkaḷ, toḻilviṉip paṇiyāḷar vāḻviṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavi nakaril kuṟippiṭṭa pakutikaḷil tiṭṭamiṭṭu amikkappaṭṭaṉa. uyarmēṭum, cuṟṟuccuvarum ellā nakaraṅkaḷilumē iruntaṉa.
19. nakarat terukkaḷi kiḻakku - mēṟkākavum kuṟukkut terukkaḷ vaṭakku-teṟkākavum ūṭum pāvumāka, valip piṉṉal (grid) muṟiyil amittaṉar. teruviṉ aṭiyil vīṭukaḷiṉ kaḻivu nīrik koṇṭu ceṉṟa kaḻivunīrvāykkālkaḷ (cākkaṭi) amikkappaṭṭaṉa.
20. āṟṟōra nakaraṅkaḷi veḷḷap perukkiliruntu kāppāṟṟuvataṟkāka maṇṇik koṭṭi,  kuṭiyiruppup pakuti takunta uyarattil amikkappaṭṭatu. pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka itu naṭantatāl cintu āṟṟōraṅkaḷil iyaṟkiyākavē mēṭṭup pakutikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa eṉkiṟār jē.em. bfndha®. cuṟṟuc cuvarkaḷum ivvāṟē uyarttappaṭṭu vantaṉa. terukkaḷum kaḻivu nīr vāykkālkaḷum avvāṟē. cintu makkaḷil nīriyiyal cār poṟiyiyal - Hydrulic Engineering mikac ciṟantatu. mokeñco-tarō nakaramāṉatu oṉṟiṉ mēl oṉṟāka ēḻu taṭavi uyarttik kaṭṭiya aṭukkukaḷik koṇṭatu. kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ māttiramalla, terukkaḷ, cākkaṭikaḷum kūṭa mikunta ciramattuṭaṉ uyarttappaṭṭu vantaṉa.
21. mokeñco-tarōviṉ ēḻu aṭukkukaḷukkuk kīḻ eṉṉa irukkiṟatu eṉṟu teriyavilli; ataṉ kīḻ akaḻāyvu ceyya iyalavilli; ammaṭṭattil ūṟum nīr taṭaṅkalāka iruntatu. cintunākarika mēlāyvukkāka hārp (HARP)” eṉṟa harappā tolliyal ārāyccit tiṭṭam -  Harappa Archeological Research Project 1986il uruvākkappaṭṭatu. attiṭṭattil hārvarṭ palkalikkaḻakap pērāciriyar ār. ec. meṭō, vikāṉciṉ-maṭicaṉ palkalik kaḻaka jē.em. keṉōyar, niyū yārk palkalik kaḻaka rīṭṭā riṭ ākiyōr uḻikkiṉṟaṉar. anta amippum pākitāṉ tolliyal tuṟiyum iṇintu harappāvil aṇmiyil naṭattiya nilatti ūṭuruvum mupparimāṇa āyvil ituvari  aṟiyappaṭāta maṇmēṭukaḷ ainti aṭiyāḷam kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar;- avi piramāṇṭamāṉa cuṟṟuc cuvarkaḷuṭaṉ iruntaṉa. harappā mikap periyatoru vāḻviṭamākavum, uccakaṭṭa kālattil aṅku 50,000 makkaḷ vāḻntirukkalām eṉṟum karutappaṭukiṟatu.
22. veḷḷap perukkit taṭukka maṇmēṭukaḷi uruvākkiya pōtellām kiṇaṟukaḷiṉ cuṟṟuc cuvarkaḷiyum uyartta vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. eṉavētāṉ harappā, mokeñco-tarō nakarak kiṇaṟukaḷ mika uyarnta vaṭṭac cuvarkaḷuṭaṉ irukkiṉṟaṉa. perunakara vīṭukaḷil taṉik kiṇaṟukaḷ, mēl māṭi kīḻmāṭi (ellāvaṟṟiṟkum mēl, moṭṭi māṭi) ivi iruntaṉa. pala vīṭukaḷil camiyal aṟikaḷ iraṇṭu: oṉṟu vīṭṭukku veḷiyē, iraṇṭāvatu vīṭṭukkuḷḷēyē maḻikālac camiyal aṟi. harappā varalāṟip putiya ceytikaḷiyum cērttu eḻuta vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.
23. araṇmaṉikaḷ, kōṭṭik kottaḷaṅkaḷ, kōyilkaḷ ivi cintuveḷi iṭipāṭukaḷil illi. āṉāl potu makkaḷ kuḷikkum kuḷaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. tolavirāvil pātukāppu amippukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. aṅku irunta aintu kuḷaṅkaḷil oṉṟu mokeñco-tarō kēṇiyiṉ aḷavil iruntatu. cintuveḷik kaṭṭiṭak kali perumpālum kaḷi maṇṇiyum nīriyum aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatu: eṅkē iyaṟkittari muṭintatu? eṅkē kaṭṭiṭa vēli toṭaṅkiyatu? eṉṟu pala iṭaṅkaḷil teḷivākat teriyavilli.
 24. neruppuṇṭākki cūṭāṉa ulikaḷil palvēṟu poruḷkaḷ uṟpattiyāyiṉa eṉpatit tulliyamākak kāṇa hārp HARP amippiṉar `puvik kāntap pulatti Geomagnetic techniques aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa āyvukaḷi mēṟkoṇṭatil  piṉvarum ceytikaḷ terintaṉa:-
kimu 2600 - 1900 kāla aḷavil cintu nakaraṅkaḷiṉ  harappā uṭpaṭa matiṟcuvarkaḷukku uḷḷēyē attakiya poruḷ uṟpatti naṭantatu. āṉāl vēṟu cila nakaraṅkaḷil maṭpāṇṭam tayārikkac cūḷikaḷi amikka kuṟippiṭṭa  iṭaṅkaḷ otukkappaṭṭaṉa; harappāvil ikkaṭṭuppāṭu illi; kāraṇam teriyavilli.
25. mokeñco- tarōvil āparaṇakkaṟkaḷ, kaṭaṟ cippikaḷ  (Shell chanks) cārnta vēlikaḷ orē iṭattil naṭantaṉa, aṅkē ulikaḷum amintaṉa. āṉāl maṭpāṇṭam tayārikkum cūḷikaḷ otukkuppuṟamāṉa piṟitōr iṭattil amintaṉa.  harappāvil ‘E’ aṭiyāḷamiṭṭa mēṭṭiṉ teṉ puṟa naṭuppakutiyil cempi urukki naṭattum toḻilkaḷ maṭṭumē naṭantaṉa; kāraṇam puriyavilli.
26. neruppiṉ veppatti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa ellāt tēvikaḷukkum orē vaki eriporuḷi māttiram payaṉpaṭutta muṭiyātu; mara viṟaku cila tēvikaḷukku maṭṭumē  utavum; cintunākarikak kāla uyarnta uṟpattit tiṟaṉukku payaṉpaṭṭa ciṟanta eriporuḷkaḷ eviyeṉṟu aṟiya HARP tiṭṭam muyalkiṟatu.
27. āparaṇam ceyyum peyṉ kal appaṭiyē iyaṟkiyil kiṭikkātu! pūmiyil āṅkāṅku kiṭakkum  mūlap poruḷkaḷ cilavaṟṟik kalantu kaṭuñcūṭēṟṟit tayārippārkaḷ. cintu nakaraṅkaḷiṉ mukkiya tayārippu āka atu iṭampeṟṟatu. mutirnta  harappā nākarikattiṉ toṭakka kālam mutal harappā nākarikam vīḻnta kālam vari maṭṭumaṉṟi, ataṟkup piṉṉar iṉṟuvari peyṉ tayārikkappaṭukiṟatu. cintu nākarika pakutikaḷil   ellām peyṉ uṟpatti ceytaṉar. harappā ati oṭṭiya vaṭakku / kiḻakkup pakutikaḷ ivaṟṟil peyṉ uṟpatti atikam. kivaḷiyal, ciṟiya cimiḻ, ciṉṉañciṟiya pāttira vaṭivil aḻakup poruṭkaḷ, `veḷippuṟattil cōṭaṉiyāka moṭṭukkaḷ niṟinta ciṟanta ki vaḷiyalkaḷ, kaḻuttu māli aṇikaḷ ivi ellām atil uruvākiṉa.
28. toṭakkak kāla maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷic ceytavuṭaṉ vikkōl, pul , kaḻivukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟāl mūṭit tī mūṭṭic cūḷiyiṭṭaṉar. kālap pōkkil iraṇṭu aṭukkuc cūḷiyum uruvākiyatu. kīḻ aṭukkil neruppu takatakaveṉa erintu koṇṭirukkum; mēlē oru taṭṭu (veppam cellum ōṭṭikaḷuṭaṉ); antat taṭṭil pāṉi caṭṭikaḷ,  maṉitap pommikaḷ (ciṟiyavi) pōṉṟavi aṭukkiyirukkum. ivaṟṟi  mūṭi veppam veḷiccellāmal taṭukkum periya mūṭiyik kaviḻppar. (mūṭiyi eṭuttu, vēṟiṭattil vikkavum muṭiyum).  ittakiya cūḷi aṭuppi mokeñco- tarō, harappā, lōtal, kōṭ ṭiji, palakkoṭ pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.
29. cintunākarika kālattil cuṭṭa ceṅkal viliyuyarnta  kaṭṭumāṉap poruḷ. eṉavē uṟpatti toṭaṅkiyatiliruntu ataṉ tiruṭṭum toṭaṅki viṭṭatu eṉkiṟār  pocēl! ellāp perunakaraṅkaḷiṉ kaṭṭumāṉattilum cuṭṭa ceṅkal payaṉpāṭu illi. perumaḷavil cuṭṭaceṅkal payaṉpaṭuttiya iṭam mokeñco- tarō maṭṭumē. uṟpattic celavu mika atikamākiyāl cuṭṭa ceṅkalliyē muḻuvatum vittu kaṭṭaṭam kaṭṭa iyalavilli. mokeñco- tarōvil kūṭa veyilil kāynta kaḷimaṇ kaṟkaḷum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. harappāvil cuṭṭa ceṅkal potuk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷilum cila vīṭukaḷilum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa pōtilum kaḷimaṇṇum, cuṭāta kaḷimaṇ ceṅkaṟkaḷumē atikam payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
30. tāṉiyak kaḷañciyam (Granary) eṉṟu mutalil tavaṟutalākak kuṟippiṭṭa kaṭṭiṭattil kūṭa cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷum, cuṭāta ceṅkaṟkaḷum oru cērap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ikkaṭṭiṭattil kāṟṟōṭṭattiṟkākac cuvarkaḷil mikavum nuṭpamāṉa kāṟṟu vāyilkaḷ (ventilation gaps) amikkappaṭṭiruntati aṇmi āyvukaḷ kāṭṭiṉa. cuṭṭa ceṅkali (nīṭitta āyuḷ, valu, nīr aripput taṭuppu ākiya kāraṇaṅkaḷukkāka) harappā caṉkutaro, kōṭ ṭiji, kālipaṅkaṉ pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil cākkaṭi, kiṇaṟu, kuḷiyal mēṭikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟkkaṭṭa cuṭṭa ceṅkal payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. tāṉiyaṅkaḷip pirittu eṭukkum harappā nakar mēṭikaḷilum (husking floors-‘F’ eṉṟa aṭiyāḷamiṭṭavi) cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷ patikkappaṭṭaṉa.
31. rōjti (Rojdi) pōṉṟa corat harappā miyaṅkaḷil ceṅkaṟkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭavēyilli. kulli mēṭṭup pūmiyilum ceṅkaṟkaḷ payaṉpāṭilli.  cintu nākarika mēṟkuk kōṭi cutkākeṉ-torilum cuṭṭa ceṅkal illi. tolavirā, curkotaṭā, ivaṟṟilum ataṉ payaṉpāṭu illi eṉalām. (ivi iraṇṭilum vīṭukaḷ, kiṇaṟukaḷ, cuvarkaḷ, kaḻivunīr vāykkālkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum kaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭiṉar). karācci nakarukku arukiluḷḷa allāṭiṉōvilum (Alladino) appaṭittāṉ.
nakaramipput tiṭṭamiṭal (Layout)
32. cintu nākarika nakaraṅkaḷik kaṭṭa muṉṉarē tīrmāṉittu tiṭṭam layout uruvākkiṉar eṉṟu colvatu muḻuvatum cariyalla. atu, mokeñco- tarōvukku oruvēḷi poruttamāka irukkalām; āṉāl ellā nakaraṅkaḷukkum poruntātu, citadel eṉṟa mēṭṭup pakutiyi `akropoli (Acropolis) eṉṟu aḻikkiṟārkaḷ. atu kirēkka moḻic col. kirēkka nākarikam ceḻittōṅkiya kimu 500 - 300 kālattil kirī nāṭṭil pirapalamāka irunta irācciyaṅkaḷ (āteṉ, pārṭā, mutaliyavi) ovvoṉṟum mutalil nakaram city aḷavil valuppeṟṟup piṉṉar aṇṭip pakutikaḷiyum kālaṉikaḷiyum piṭittu irācciyaṅkaḷ state āṉapiṟaku city - state  eṉa aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. anta city - state  kaḷ palavaṟṟiṉ nakarappakutiyil uyarnta kuṉṟu iruntatu. atāvatu kuṉṟukaḷic cuṟṟi nakaraṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. mukkiya aracu pīṭaṅkaḷ, kōyilkaḷ kuṉṟukaḷil amintaṉa. iṉṟum akropāli eṉṟāl nakariṉ uyarnta pakuti eṉṟu poruḷ.  cintu nākarika nakaraṅkaḷil kāṇum mēṭṭup pakutiyip pātukāppu araṇ eṉṟa arttattil Citadel allatu kirēkka nakara mēṭṭup pakutiyi aḻippatu pōl akropāli eṉṟum  tolliyalāḷar cuṭṭalāyiṉar. (kirēkka nākarika kālam cintuveḷi nākarikattiṟku mikap pintiyatu.)
33(i). cintup perunakaraṅkaḷiṉ mēṭṭup pakutikaḷ maṇ, ceṅkal, kaḷimaṇ ceṅkal koṇṭu kaṭṭiyavi. ivaṟṟiṉ tōṟṟam paṟṟiya karuttukkaḷil mukkiyamāṉatu: natikaḷ veḷḷam varum pōtu pātukāppāṉa uyarnta iṭam mēṭṭuppakuti. mokeñco- tarōvil mēṭṭup pakuti - akropāli eṉṟu cuṭṭiṉar. annakariṉ mēṟkup puṟattil atu uḷḷatu. [všyh cintu nakaraṅkaḷilum appaṭi illi. amri, allāṭiṉō, lōtal, rōjti, curkotaṭā, rōpar, hūl ākiyavaṟṟiṉ mēṭṭup pakutikaḷ mēṟkil ïšiy.]
(ii). cintu nākarikattil muntit tōṉṟiya harappāvil mēṭṭup pakuti [F gFâ] mēṟkil iruntapōtilum mēṭṭup pakutikku vaṭakkēyum harappā nakariṉ mukkiya pakutikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. cintu nakaraṅkaḷil mēṭṭup pakuti enta ticiyil iruntālum kīḻ nakaramum (Lower town) iruntē tīrum. kīḻnakarap pakutiyiltāṉ potumakkaḷ kuṭiyiruntaṉar. mokeñco-tarō, atupōṉṟatē, kālipaṅkaṉ maṟṟum ēṉiya 2000 (ciṟitum peritumāṉa) cintunakaraṅkaḷ iruntapōtilum mokeñco-tarō, harappā iraṇṭu maṭṭumē ivvakiyil oṉṟu pōl uḷḷaṉa.
(iii). kirēkkarkaḷ akropāli pakutiyi puṉitamāka (sacred or High city) karutiṉar. taṅkaḷuṭiya mēṭukaḷi cintu nakara makkaḷ eppaṭik karutiṉar eṉat teriyavilli. (cintu nākarika makkaḷ nūlakam, takaval kaḷañciyam etiyum eccamāka viṭavilliyē! viṭṭiruntālum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭavilli.)
34. putitāka amikkum navīṉa nakaraṅkaḷiṉ neṭuñcālikaḷ ‘Grid planning’ paṭi amikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. terukkaḷ vaṭakku teṟkākavum, kiḻakku mēṟkākavum oṉṟiyoṉṟu veṭṭic  celkiṉṟaṉa. terukkaḷiṉ cantippukaḷ 900 il irukkum. ivvāṟu veṭṭic celvatāl catura vaṭivilāṉa nilapparappukaḷ tōṉṟum. avi ‘Blocks’ eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. inta caturap pakutikaḷi mēlum ciṟiya pākaṅkaḷākap pirittup perum teruvuṭaṉ iṇiyum ciṟiya terukkaḷi uruvākkuvārkaḷ. (ciṟiya terukkaḷi ‘Lanes’ eṉpar).
34. cuyampuvāka (taṉṉicciyāka) vaḷarnta ilaṇṭaṉ nakaril  terukkaḷ ippaṭit tiṭṭamiṭṭaṉavalla. āṉāl putitāka eḻunta  niyūyārkkil ittakiya tiṭṭattip pārkkalām. kimu 2600ilēyē mokeñco- tarōvil ittakiya amippu  muḻu vaṭivam peṟṟatu viyakkattakkatu. (āṉāl ciṟu kuḷaṟupaṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa; kīḻē pārkkalām). kālipaṅkaṉ, navṣarō nakaraṅkaḷilum  terut tiṭṭamiṭal ōraḷavukku iruntatu.
35. cīṉarkaḷ kimu 1500 liruntē inta vakiyāṉa teru amipput tiṭṭatti taṅkaḷ nakaraṅkaḷil amalpaṭuttiṉar. avarkaḷ kimu 770 - 476 aḷavil teru amipput tiṭṭatti eḻuttil pativu ceytuḷḷaṉar. atil varum vācakam varumāṟu: 
oru talinakaram catura vaṭivattil irukka vēṇṭum. nakaratti oṉṟiyoṉṟu veṭṭik koṇṭu cellum oṉpatu mukkiya terukkaḷiyum, catura vaṭivattiṉ iru puṟattē amikkappaṭṭa mummūṉṟu perum vāyilkaḷ toṭuttirukkum. oru puṟam mūṉṟu vāyilkaḷ maṟupuṟam mūṉṟu vāyilkaḷ eṉṟa oḻuṅku pēṇappaṭum.
36. kāntārap pakutip perunakaraṅkaḷilum mata pīṭa nakaraṅkaḷilum - Gandhara cities and monasteries - kimu 1100 mutal kipi 1100 vari mēṟkūṟiya teru amippu oḻuṅku ceyalpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. cirkap, takṣacilā (Sirkap, Taxila) eṉpaṉa mukkiya utāraṇaṅkaḷ. pākitāṉ nāṭāṉatu ilāmapāt eṉṟa peyaril putiya nakaratti 1959il amittapōtu cirkap nakariṉ terut tiṭṭatti piṉpaṟṟiyatu. (cirkap iṭipāṭukaḷ ilāmapāt  nakarukku arukil uḷḷaṉa).
37. mokeñco- tarō nakaril vaṭakku - mēṟkut terukkaḷ iraṇṭu mēṭṭup pakutiyil iruntu kīḻ nakarukkuc celkiṉṟaṉa; mutal teruviṉ akalam 7.6 mīṭṭar, iraṇṭām teruviṉ akalam 9.1 mīṭṭar. āṉāl kiḻakku-mēṟku terukkaḷil oru cikkal irukkiṟatu. mattiya teru (Central street) akalam 5.5 mīṭṭar. atu mutalteruvi veṭṭicceṉṟu oru kaṭṭiṭattōṭu niṉṟu viṭṭatu! piṟitoru kiḻakku - mēṟkut teru ( 3.8 mī. akalam)  mutal teruvil vantu appaṭiyē niṟkiṉṟatu! iviyellām grid planning oḻuṅki mīṟuvaṉa! puttamputiya kaṉṉi nilattil kaṭṭiyeḻuppiya mokeñco-tarōviṉ terukkaḷ (icciṟu muraṇkaḷ tavira), mikac ciṟanta muṟiyil amikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu potuvāṉa karuttu.
38. nakaraṅkaḷip putitāka amikkum pōtu cila mukkiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭa ticiyil irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu potu viti. cīṉac cakkaravarttiyiṉ āṇippaṭi uruvāṉa nakaramippu nūl irāca parampariyiṉariṉ kula teyvak kōyil kiḻakkilum, pūmik kaṭavuḷ, tāṉiyak kaṭavuḷ ākiyōr kōyil mēṟkilum, araca pīṭam teṟkilum, potuc canti vaṭakkilum amital vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.
39. mūrkkattaṉamāka muṭṭi mōtip pāyvatum tiṭumeṉat taṉatu ōṭṭap pātiyi māṟṟik koḷvatum āṉa cintu natip paṭukiyil aminta mokeñco-tarōvukku ennēramum tiṭīr veḷḷapperukku āpattu iruntuvantatu. varalāṟṟuk kālattil veḷḷapperukku 7 varuṭattiṟku orumuṟi nikaḻntatākat terikiṟatu. mokeñco-tarō nakaram muḻuvatiyum periya  maṇmēṭukaḷiṉmēl kaṭṭiyataṉ nōkkam nāṉku eṉpar jeṉcaṉ:
1) kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭuvataṟkāṉa aṭittaḷam amittal: periya nīccal kuḷam kaṭṭuvataṟkāka eḻuppappaṭṭamēṭu utāraṇam.
2) taṉik kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭuvataṟkāṉa maṇ mēṭṭu aṭittaḷam.
3) taṉik kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷi muḻumiyāka / pakutiyāka uyarttuvataṟkāṉa mēṭukaḷ.
4) paḻiya / kiviṭappaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷukku mēṟkut ticiyil putiyaṉavaṟṟi eḻuppuvataṟkāka uruvākkiya maṇ mēṭukaḷ.
40(i). mokeñco- tarō mēṭṭiṉ aṭiyil toṭakka harappā kālak kuṭiyiruppu ciṟiyatāka oṉṟu iruntirukkalā meṉṟum apperunakariṉ aṭiyil atu maṟintirukkum eṉṟum jeṉcaṉ pōṉṟavar karutukiṉṟaṉar. kīḻ nakaric cuṟṟip perum parappaḷavil maṇ mēṭukaḷ iruppati 1964il jārj ep. ṭēl nirūpittuḷḷār. anta mēṭukaḷ ‘HR’ eṉṟu aṭiyāḷamiṭappaṭṭa kaḷimaṇceṅkal kaṭṭiṭattiṟku arukil  ataṉmēṟkut ticiyil 183 mīṭṭarukku atikamāṉa tūrattiṟku paravik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.
(ii). mēṟkūṟiya HR pakutikkuk kīḻ mokeñco- tarōviṉ teṟku elliyil, kaḷimaṇ / kaḷimaṇ ceṅkal pōṉṟavaṟṟāl āṉa maṇmēṭu iruppati 1981-1982 il em. kurkārci naṭattiya puvi amivu (Geophysical) āyvu nirūpittatu. mokeñco- tarō tolliyal akaḻāyvukaḷilum (perumaḷavu kaḷimaṇ kaṭṭi aṭukkukaḷ mūlam eḻuppiya) mēṭukaḷ aṭimaṭṭattil iruntaṉa. mutaṟkaṭṭamēṭukaḷ veḷḷattaṭuppukkāka amintaṉa; avi annakari cintuveḷiyiṉ tarimaṭṭattil iruntu kaṇicamāka uyartti veḷḷa āpattit tavirttatu.
(iii). mēṭukaḷ eḻuppuvataṟkāṉa uṭaluḻippup paṇiyiṉ paḷu mikap periyatu; aṭittaḷam eḻuppuvataṟku māttiram 10,000 variyāṉa toḻilāḷarkaḷ 400 nāḷ vēli ceyya vēṇṭiyirukkum. (vēliyāḷarkaḷ 2,500 pēr tāṉ eṉṟāl atē paṇikku 1,600 nāḷ - 4 āṇṭu 4 mātam - ākiyirukkum). mokeñco- tarōvik kaṭṭu muṉṉarē taṉi naparō/ kuḻuvō cāṅkōpāṅkamāṉa (Comprehensive) tiṭṭam uruvākki ataṉpaṭi  kaṭṭumāṉap paṇikaḷi naṭattiyati itiliruntu aṟiyalām.
(iv). tiṭṭappaṭi kaṭṭiyatu eṉpatu kīḻnakara amippiṉ cāli oḻuṅkiliruntum terikiṟatu. vaṭakku - teṟkāka ōṭum mutalām / iraṇṭām terukkaḷum, kiḻakku mēṟkākac cellum kiḻakku / mattiya terukkaḷum tiṭṭa miṭṭuttāṉ amikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kimu 3000ttilēyē ippaṭit tiṭṭamiṭṭu, niṟivēṟṟakkūṭiya āṟṟal vāynta maṉitarkaḷ yār evar eṉṟu puriya villi.
41. nakaram oṉṟi amippataṟkāka mutalil uruvākkiya maṇ mēṭukaḷiṉ parimāṇa aḷavukaḷ, annakaram virivaṭivatiyum atika makkaḷ aṅku kuṭipukuvatiyum taṭuttirukkum eṉpar jeṉcaṉ. mēṭṭiṉmēl mēṭu eṉṟu aṭukkik koṇṭu pōṉa pōtu mokeñco-tarō nakaram kiṭitticiyil virivaṭi yāmal ceṅkuttāka vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. cuvarukku mēl cuvar, vīṭṭukku mēl vīṭu, mēṭṭukkumēl mēṭu eṉṟu eḻuppi uyarttum pōtu, citadel pōṉṟa ceyaṟkik kuṉṟukaḷ tōṉṟiyatil viyappilli. avaṟṟi aṉṟiya cintunākarika kaṭṭumāṉaṅkaḷil kāṇpatu tāṉ viyappu.
42. periya nīccal kēṇik kuṉṟu (The mound of the Great Bath) maṉitaṉ amitta maṇ mēṭēyākum. cintunati koṇarnta vaṇṭal maṇ (alluvium) ataṟkup payaṉpaṭṭatu. inta maṇṇiyum kuṉṟiṉmēl kaṭṭiya kēṇiyiyum pātukāppataṟkāṉa cuṟṟuc cuvari ammēṭṭiccuṟṟi eḻuppiṉar. ati mikavum iṟukkamāka, palamākak kaṭṭiyirunta pōtilum atu irāṇuva nōkkil kaṭṭiyatalla. (ēṉeṉṟāl kaḷañciyamākak kaṭṭiya (Warehouse) kaṭṭaṭamum mēṟkuppuṟa miyap pakutiyil kuṉṟiṉmēl tāṉ uḷḷatu; intak kuṉṟiyum citadel eṉṟa pātukāppu araṇākak kaṭṭiyiruntāl, antak kaḷañciya kaṭṭiṭam aṅkē irun tirukkātu . eṉavē cintu makkaḷ  mukkiyamāṉa tamatu kaṭṭaṭatti kampīramāka uyarattil amippataṟkākavē  inta ceyaṟkik kuṉṟi uruvākkiṉar eṉalām.

iyal - 5
mokeñco-tarō
veṇkala ūḻiyiṉ aṭiyāḷamāka, muṟpōkku amcaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟik koṇṭatāka, neṭuṅkālam nilipeṟṟa perunakaramāka mokeñco-tarō mutaṉmi peṟukiṟatu. ataṉ kaṭṭamippu mikavum kavarcciyāṉatu. (mēṟkil eḻuppiya 400mī.  X 200 mī. mēṭṭiyum cērttu) eṭṭu hekṭēr parappaḷavik koṇṭatu atu. inta mēṭṭiṟku vaḻaṅkiya peyarkaḷ pala: 
mārṣal ati tūpi mēṭu eṉṟār.  (anta mēṭṭiṉ mītu piṟkālap puttamatattiṉar puttamatatūpi oṉṟik kaṭṭiyatāl intap peyar). vīlar atik kāpparaṇ eṉṟa arttattil `ciṭṭāṭal (citadel) eṉṟār; mārṣal taṉatu cintu nākarikap perunūlil periya nīccal kēṇi mēṭu (mound of the Great Bath) eṉṟu aḻittār.
2. inta mēṭṭiṟkum ataṟkuk kiḻakkil uḷḷa kīḻ nakarattiṟkum iṭiyil tiṟantaveḷi oṉṟu uḷḷatu. akaḻāyvukaḷum, nilattuḷiyīṭukaḷum inta veḷiyil makkaḷ vāḻntatilli eṉpatik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷ kuṭiyirunta kīḻ nakariṉ aḷavīṭu 1100 x 650 mīṭṭar: atāvatu  71.50 hekṭēr. mokeñco-tarō parappaḷavu iṉṟu kāṇpativiṭap  peritāka, atāvatu 100 hekṭēr vari iruntirukkalām eṉṟum cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar.
3. mokeñco-tarōvic cūḻnta pakutiyil mūṉṟu iṭaṅkaḷil nilattaṭi āyvukaḷ ceytaṉar. oṉṟu mokeñco-tarōvi viṭa 2-3 maṭaṅku periyatu; teṟkē oru ki.mī. tūrattil uḷḷatu. 18.3 mīṭṭar āḻattil aṅku ceṅkaṟ kaṭṭiṭa eccaṅkaḷum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum iruppatu terintatu. 1987, 1988  āṇṭukaḷil pūmimaṭṭattiṟkuc caṟṟukkīḻē, kīḻ nakarattiṟkuk kiḻakkē, natiyōramāka mēlum iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷ  (veḷḷattaṭuppu aṇikku arukil) kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa.  nilamaṭṭattil iruntu caṟṟu uyarnta pakutiyeṉṟu varipaṭam 11.1 (Spur No. 3 pocēl pak. 186)il eṉa intappakuti cuṭṭap paṭukiṟatu. anta iṭattil nalla niliyiluḷḷa kaṭṭiṭa aṭikkal ceṅkaṟkaḷum cintu maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum, catura vaṭiva muttirikaḷum kiṭittaṉa.
4. mokeñco-tarōvil 1924-25il āyvu ceyta rāv pakatūr tīkṣit periya nīccal kēṇikkut teṉ - mēṟkil 1.5 kimī tūrattil cintu maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum ceṅkaṟ kaṭṭikaḷum irukkak kaṇṭār. mokeñco-tarō  nakaril iruntu oru kimī tūrattil hacaṉvataṉ kirāmattiṟkuc cellum teruppakutiyilum ivi pōṉṟavaṟṟik kaṇṭār. iṉṟiya mēṭṭuppakutiyiliruntu ivi otuṅki iruppatāl mokeñco-tarō parappaḷavu 100 hekṭēr maṭṭumē eṉpatē potuvāṉa karuttu.
5(i). periya nīccal kēṇi mēṭṭil putta matattiṉar aminta tūpikku vaṭakkē mārṣal āḻamākat tōṇṭiṉār. cintu nākarikat toṭakkak kāla allatu ataṟkup pintiya toṭakka kālam mutircci aṭinta kālamāka māṟiya (Transition)  kālattil (kimu 2600-2500) makkaḷ cintuveḷiyil tarimaṭṭattil vāḻntu vanta aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ kiṭittaṉa. ataṟku mēṟpakutiyil kaḷimaṇṇiyum, uṭinta kaṭṭikaḷiyum putta tūpi mēṭṭu uyaram vari nirappi anta uyarnta mēṭu uruvākkappaṭṭatu. cintuveḷi makkaḷukku mukkiyamāṉa periya nīccal kēṇi, kaḷañciyam pōṉṟavaṟṟi anta mēṭṭil amittaṉar.
(ii). inta uyarmēṭippakutiyip pātukāttiṭa atic cuṟṟip palamāṉa, ceṅkaṟkaḷālāṉa taṭuppuc cuvar kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. nīr arippit taṭuppatum accuvariṉ nōkkaṅkaḷil oṉṟu. iccuvar citadel (pātukāppu araṇ) pōṉṟa kāṭciyi ammēṭṭiṟkut tantatu. mēṭṭic cuṟṟik kaṭṭiya cuvar āṉatu, mēṭṭil aṭaṅkiya maṇ, ceṅkal tuṇṭukaḷ pōṉṟavi citaṟāmal niṟka vaki ceytatu. (pūcāri - maṉṉarukkō vēṟu evarukkō pātukāppu vaḻaṅkum nōkkam koṇṭatalla!) immēṭṭiṉ ārampakāla varalāṟu paṟṟi aṟiya muṭiyavilli. piṟkālavāyilkaḷil oṉṟu, veḷi nilattil iruntu mēṭṭiṉ ucci varic cellum 7 mīṭṭar akalap paṭikkaṭṭukaḷik koṇṭuḷḷatu.
(iii). oruvēḷi antap pātiyi, mēṭṭiṉ piṉpuṟattil mēṟkup pakutiyil uḷḷa kaḷañciyattiṟkuc celvataṟkāka amit tirukkalām. vīlar itit tāṉiyak kaḷañciyam (granary) eṉṟu peyariṭṭār. nakarp puṟattil iruntu varuvōrukku atu pātiyāka iṭampeṟṟirukka iyalātu. ammēṭṭiṉ teṉkiḻakku mūliyil piṉpuṟa vāyil oṉṟu ciṟitāka uḷḷatu. iṉṟu ilakuvāka immēṭṭil ēṟiccellap pala pātikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ceṅkuttāṉa vaṭakkup puṟap pātiyi tavirttuk koṇṭu celvōr, varipaṭattil ‘SD’ eṉṟa pakuti ‘L’, eṉṟa pakutikaḷukku iṭiyil cellalām (vīlar varinta varipaṭa ilakkam 11.3. pocēl pakkam 188)
periya nīccal kēṇi
6(i). periya nīccal kēṇikkuc cellum pātiyi `mattiya vīti (Main street) eṉṟu mēṟkūṟiya varipaṭam (ila. 11.3) kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. atu kēṇikkum ataṟkuk kiḻakkiluḷḷa kaṭṭiṭattiṟkum iṭiyil celkiṟatu. akkaṭṭiṭatti mekkē `pūcārikaḷ iruppiṭam eṉṟār. mattiya vīti akalamāṉatu, nērāṉatu: akalam 4-5 mīṭṭar. 100 mīṭṭar vari avvītiyi akaḻntu pārttil vāykkālkaḷ cila kāṇappaṭṭaṉa: oru vāykkāl periyatu, veḷḷic cuṇṇāmpukkal tuṇṭukaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭatu. (accuṇṇāmpukkal mokeñco - tarōvukku arukil uḷḷa rōri kuṉṟukaḷil (Rohri HIlls) iruntu koṇṭu vantatu).
(ii). anta vāykkālkaḷiṉ nīr naṉku payaṉpaṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. oru pakkattil nīr nirampiya periya nīccal kēṇi; maṟupuṟattil maṉita naṭamāṭṭamuḷḷa kaṭṭiṭam, mēṭṭiṉ miyap pakutiyil caṟṟu vaṭakku / mēṟkup pakkamāka kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  (atu iruppiṭamāka irukkalām. avarkaḷ caṭaṅku campiratāyaṅ kaḷōṭu toṭarpuṭiyavarkaḷum aṭikkaṭi nīrāṭupavarkaḷākavum iruntirukkalām). kēṇiyiṉ teṉmēṟku mūliyil kaḷañciyak kaṭṭiṭam amintuḷḷatu. kēṇiyum kaḷañciyamum iṇintu ceyaṟpaṭṭirukkalām eṉpar mārṣal. periya nīccal kēṇi yāṉatu nīriṉāl nōykaḷik kuṇap paṭuttum cikicci niliyattiṉ aṅkamāka iruntirukkalām. mokeñco -tarōvil akaḻntavaṟṟil atuvē miku uṉṉatamāṉatu eṉkiṟār mārṣal. [“The Great Bath was part of, what appears to have been, a vast hydropathic establishment and the most imposing of all the remins unearthed at Mohenjo-daro” Marshall 1931: (24-25): quoted by G.L. Possehl 2002. The Indus Civilization: A Contemporary Perspective: P 187]. 
(iii). varalāṟṟukku muntiya kālantoṭṭē ekiptu, cintu, cīṉa, jappāṉ nākarikaṅkaḷil nīril kalanta maruntuc carakkukaḷ allatu atil vēkavitta paṭṭikaḷ / ilikaḷ / mūlikikaḷ mūlam nōykaḷik kuṇappaṭuttum cikicci muṟi iruntu vantatu; iti hiṭrōpati (Hydropathy) eṉpar; iṉṟum im muṟi vātanōy, uṭalvalikaḷik kuṇappaṭutta utavukiṟatu; eṉavē mārṣal intakkēṇi paṟṟik kūṟiyatu niyāyamāṉatu,  varavēṟkat takkatu.
(iv). ikkēṇi catura veḷiyil amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu; nāṟ puṟamum naṭipātikaḷ (verandahs) uḷḷaṉa; mūṉṟu naṭipātikaḷukkup piṉpakkattil pala aṟikaḷum nīṇṭa tāḻvāraṅkaḷum (galleries) uḷḷaṉa. teṟkup puṟattil nīṇṭa tāḻvāram oṉṟum, ovvoru mūliyilum ciṟiya aṟikaḷum uḷḷaṉa. kiḻakkil orēvariciyil pala aṟikaḷum, (avaṟṟiloṉṟil oru kiṇaṟu) uḷḷaṉa. vaṭakkil oru tokutiyāka, pala kūṭaṅkaḷum caṟṟupperiya aṟikaḷum kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
(v). anta tiṟanta nāṟpuṟaveḷiyiṉ naṭuvē 39 aṭi nīḷam X 23 aṭi akalam, tariyiliruntu 8 aṭi āḻam, koṇṭa periya `nīccal - (kuḷiyal) kēṇi (Swimming Bath) iṭampeṟukiṟatu. intakkēṇiyiṉ iru pakkaṅkaḷil paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa;  paṭikkaṭṭu muṭivaṭiyum iṭattil tāḻnta mēṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa; kuḷippavar uyarattukku kēṇi nīr āḻamākat tōṉṟiṉāl avar inta mēṭiyip payaṉpaṭuttiṭa vacatiyāka mēṭiyik kaṭṭiyirukkalām.
(vi). ikkēṇikkuk kuṟintatu oru mēl māṭiyāvatu iruntirukkalām eṉpati mēlēcellum paṭikkaṭṭukaḷum, mēliruntukīḻvarum vaṭikālkaḷum terivikkiṉṟaṉa. mēlmāṭi kaṭṭum periya kaṭṭiṭa vēlikkup payaṉpaṭuttiya maraṅkaḷiṉ kariyum cāmpalum akaḻāyviṉ pōtu kiṭittatāka jāṉ mārṣal kuṟippiṭukiṟār.
7(i). nerukkamāka aṭukkiya ceṅkaṟkaḷik koṇṭu ikkēṇi kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. itu pōṉṟa iṟukkamāṉa ceṅkaṟ kaṭṭumāṉatti entap paḻaṅkāla nakarilum kaṇṭatilli eṉpar mekkē. kēṇiccuvar nāṉkum 1.36 mīṭṭar akalattil kaṭṭiyavi; avaṟṟiṉ mēl 2.4 cemī tār pūci nīrkkacivu taṭukkappaṭṭatu. kēṇiyiṉ tariyilum tār pūcappaṭṭu iruntatā eṉṟa nōkkil āyvu ceyyavilli;  pūcappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu. kēṇiyiṉ kiḻakkil ataṉ cuṟṟuk kaṭṭukkuḷ oru kiṇaṟu uḷḷatu; kēṇiyi nirappiṭa kiṇaṟṟu nīr payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām eṉpar mārṣal, ikkarutti mekkē ēṟkavilli.
(ii). kēṇiyiṉ uṭpuṟattil teṉ-mēṟku mūliyil vaṭikāl oṉṟu uḷḷatu. inta vaṭikāl ceṅkal aṭukkukaḷiṉ ūṭākac ceṉṟu ceṅkaṟcuvarkaḷ iraṇṭiṉ naṭuvē uḷḷa kaḷi maṇṇiyum cuvarkaḷiyum ūṭaṟuttuk koṇṭu veḷiyēṟiyatu. iru cuvarkaḷ, naṭuvirukkum kaḷimaṇ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ motta akalam 3.12 mīṭṭar. kēṇiyil iruntu nīṅkum iṭattil vaṭikāliṉ kuṟukkaḷavu 38 x 20 ce.mī. ākum.
(iii). vaṭikāl mūlam veḷiyēṟiya kēṇi nīr āṉatu ceṅkal vāykkāl vaḻiyē (carācari uyara maṉitaṉ tāṇṭic cellakkūṭiya) matakiṉ mūlam veḷiyēṟiyatu. inta matakiṉ uyaram 2.3 mī. akalam 0.71 mī. mataku mikavum aḻakāka `kārpel (Corbel) muṟippaṭi kaṭṭiyatu. [gSit mēl vaḷiviṉ mītu pōṭāmal irupakkaccuvar allatu tūṇ mītu paḷuvic cumattum muṟi kārpel vaḷivu v‹g®]. cintuk kaṭṭiṭak kaliyil inta muṟiyi āṅkāṅkē kāṇalām.
(iv). vaḷivukkup pakkattil nīr veḷiyēṟum ceṅkal vāykkālil iṟaṅki, ataṉ uṭpuṟattip pārviyiṭavum   iṟaṅkic cuttappaṭuttavum āṉa `mēṉ hōl (man hole) tuvāram mūṭiyuṭaṉ iruntatu; itu mokeñco-tarō vāykkālkaḷil kāṇum potu amcam; anta vāykkāl eṅku ceṉṟu muṭintatu eṉpati aṟiya muṭiyavilli.
(v). kēṇi amintirunta peru mēṭṭiṉ mēṟkup pakuti iṭintu karintu pōy viṭṭatāl. nīr veḷiyēṟuvataṟkāṉa immataki ēṉ oru maṉitaṉ naṭakkak kūṭiya aḷavil  kaṭṭavēṇṭum? āpattuk kālattil tappic cellum vaḻiyāka irukkumō eṉṟu mārṣal aiyam kiḷappiṉār. mekkēyiṉ viḷakkam matakaṭiyil oru taṭuppip pōṭṭu mēṉ hōl tuvārattiṉ mūlamāka, nīr ūṟṟum pōtu nīr mēleḻumpi kēṇiyil pōy viḻum eṉpatākum. iti nampuvatu kaṭiṉamāka irukkiṟatu!
(vi). kēṇiyic cuṟṟi (iṭiyē āṭkaḷ ceṉṟuvarak kūṭiya) tūṇ varicikaḷ iruntaṉa. ittūṇkaḷiṉ mītu irunta kūri (roof) aḻintu viṭṭapaṭiyāl, kēṇiyic cuṟṟiya pakuti iṉṟu tiṟanta muṟṟamāka uḷḷatu. kēṇiyiṉ kiḻakkuppuṟattil 7 allatu 8 kuḷiyal aṟikaḷ (bathing chambers) uḷḷaṉa; ivi mokeñco-tarō pāṇiyil kaṭṭiyavi; aṟikaḷ katavu poruttiyavi. (kaḻivunīri veḷiyēṟṟavum aṅku vaki ceyyap paṭṭiruntatu).
(vii). ikkēṇiyi vivarippavarkaḷ mēṭu eḻuppiya kālattiṟkuc caṟṟup piṟkālattil kēṇi amikkappaṭṭirukkalām eṉkiṉṟaṉar. iṭikkālattil atil māṟṟaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. mokeñco -tarō kaṭicikkāla iṟutiyil atu payaṉpāṭu iḻantirukkalām. kiviṭappaṭṭatu māttiramalla atik kaḷimaṇṇum kuppiyum pōṭṭu mūṭiviṭṭu, kēṇi irunta pakutiyē `kiviṉip poruḷkaḷ uṟpatti ceyyum iṭamāka māṟṟappaṭṭatu.
(viii). kēṇiyi mūṭuvataṟkup payaṉpaṭṭa `nirappikaḷiṉ (fillers) parimāṇam teriyavilli. kēṇiyiṉ pakka aṟikaḷ cumār mūṉṟu mīṭṭar āḻattiṟku nirappappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kaḷañciyap pakutiyi akaḻnta vīlar antak kaṭṭiṭattiṟku etiril irunta mēṭip pakuti 30 aṭi uyarattiṟku (taraṅkuṟinta kaṭṭiṭa iṭupāṭukaḷāl) niravappaṭṭiruntatu eṉpar. inta iṭi pāṭukaḷip piṟkālattil āynta jermaṉ kuḻuviṉar mokeñco-tarōviṉ `ucca niliyil ataṉ kaṭṭumāṉam muḻuc cintunākarikak kaṭṭiṭak kalikkē mātiriyāka iruntirukkum eṉṟaṉar.
(ix). kēṇiyi mūṭi 4000 āṇṭu ākiviṭṭa niliyilum, atu irunta mēṭāṉatu kāṇpōr neñcit toṭukiṟatu; ataṉuṭiya uyaram, parappu, ciṟappu ivi palvēṟu  eṇṇaṅkaḷi eḻuppukiṉṟaṉa. mēṭṭil vāḻntōri mēṭṭuk kuṭiyiṉar eṉṟum kīḻ nakaril vāḻntōri cātāraṇak kuṭiyiṉar eṉṟum uṟutiyākac colla muṭiyumā? eṉiṉum mokeñco-tarō peru nakarit tiṭṭamiṭṭōr, iraṇṭu vakiyiṉari maṉatil koṇṭu ceyalpaṭṭaṉar eṉṟutāṉ colla vēṇṭum.
(x). camūka, kali, araciyal taḷaṅkaḷil talimi niliyil iruntōriṉ atikāratti kāṭṭum vakiyil, uyarnta mēṭṭi eḻuppi atil uyar maṭṭattiṉar vacikkavum, āṭci ceyyavum vaki ceytaṉar eṉalām. maṉita varalāṟṟil araṇmaṉi / uyarnta kōpuram / piramiṭ kallaṟi / vāṉuyarnta kōyil ivipōṉṟavi āṭciyāḷariṉ valuviyum mutaṉmiyiyum  veḷikkāṭṭuvaṉavākavē amikiṉṟaṉa.
kaḷañciyam, kiṭaṅku (Warehouse)
8. icciṟanta kaḷañciyam perumaḷavukku marappalakikaḷāl uruvākkiyatākum. poruḷkaḷic cēmittu vittu eṭukkum kaḷañciyam atu. paḷuvāṉa, taṭitta, taṭṭiyāṉa kūriyoṉṟu ataṟku iruntirukka vēṇṭum. palamuṟi atu nāṇal, pul, maram, kaḷimaṇ ivi koṇṭu putuppittirukka vēṇṭum. kūrip pāram atikarikkat toṭaṅkiya pōtu mēlum muṭṭukaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. kēṇiyi viṭa (orumāṭi uyaram aḷavukku) kaḷañciyak kaṭṭiṭam uyaram kuṟintatu. kaṭṭiṭac cuvarkaḷum, tūṇkaḷum malikkāṭukaḷil viḷinta pāriya, valuvāṉa maraṅkaḷil ceytaviyākum.
9. ikkaḷañciyattil eṉṉa poruḷkaḷ cēmittaṉar? evvāṟu payaṉpaṭuttiṉar? eṉpatu pōṉṟavaṟṟi akaḻāyvu mūlam aṟiya iyalavilli. kēṇiyum kaḷañciyamum arukarukē  iruppatāl aṅkē cēmitta poruḷkaḷukkum kēṇip payaṉpāṭṭiṟkum iṭiyil ētō toṭarpu iruntirukkalām;  kēṇiyi nirvakittavarkaḷ tamatu poruḷkaḷi kiṭaṅkil vittiruntirukkalām; uṇavup poruḷ, āṭi, tuṇi, eriporuḷ, aṟuttuc ceppaṉiṭṭa maraṅkaḷ, kuḷiyalukkāṉa tilaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavi kaccāp poruḷkaḷāka allatu uṭaṉaṭiyākap payaṉpaṭuttum niliyil vittiruntiruppar. itupōla kēṇiyum kaḷañciyamum oṉṟiṇintavāṟu lōtalil maṭṭum kāṇalām. mokeñco-tarō, lōtal ivi tavira vēṟu cintu nākarika nakaram etilum ivi illi.
10. mokeñco-tarō kaḷañciyattiyum cintunatic camaveḷikkuc cellum pātiyiyum iṇikkum akalamāṉa ceṅkal paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ kaḷañciyattiṉ teṟkil mēliruntu kīḻāka amintiruntaṉa. mukkiya cēmippiṭamāṉa ikkaḷañciyatti pātukākka cuvarkaḷō vēṟu ēṟpāṭō illi. kēṇiyum kaḷañciyamum mokeñco-tarōviṉ pintiya kālattil orē kālakaṭṭattil kiviṭappaṭṭaṉa.
11. iraṇṭiyum kaṭṭiyatu orē nērattil tāṉ. kēṇiyiṉ teṟkuvācal mūlam veḷiyēṟi, valappakkam tirumpi, āḷ naṭamāṭṭam mikunta teru vaḻicceṉṟu, ciṟiya paṭikkaṭṭil ēṟik kaḷañciyattiṟkuḷ nuḻiyalām. iraṇṭu kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum avvaḷavu nerukkamāṉatu. [gh®¡f varipaṭam: 11.3 pocēl pakkam 188].
12(i). (vīlar varinta) avvaripaṭattil teṟku - vaṭakkākac celvatu `piratāṉa vīti. avvītiyiṉ teṟkil mukkiya piramukarkaḷ vāḻntiruppar eṉpar pocēl pōṉṟavarkaḷ. piratāṉa vītiyiṉ oru vāṭiyil periya nīccal kēṇiyum atiyoṭṭi kaḷañciyamum iruntaṉa. avvītiyiṉ maṟuvāṭiyil `pūcārikaḷ illam (College of priests) iruntatu. pūcārikaḷukkum nakara nirvākattiṟkum eṉṉa toṭarpu? anta illam, piratāṉa vītiyiṉ teṉ pakutiyil iruppatu maṭṭumaṉṟi nīccal kēṇikku etirāka (piratāṉa teruviṉ ōramāka) iruppatālum kēṇikkum pūcārikaḷukkum iṭiyilāṉa toṭarpu eṉṉa? pūcārikaḷiṉ kuḷiyalukkāka kēṇi kaṭṭappaṭṭatā? avarkaḷ avvaḷavu mukkiyamāṉavarkaḷā? eṉṟa viṉākkaḷum eḻukiṉṟaṉa.
(ii). nakara niruvāka amippilāṉa pūcārikaḷ iruppiṭa māṉatu mika perum patavi vakitta atikāri māḷiki allatu atiyuyar pūcārit talivariṉ māḷiki, allatu pūcārik kuḻuviṉar taṅkiṭam ākiya immūṉṟil oṉṟāka iruntataṉāl perumukkiyattuvam peṟṟatu eṉalām.
(iii). ikkaṭṭiṭattil ippōtu veṟum cuvarkaḷ maṭṭumē miñciyiruppatāl ippaṭip palavāṟu ūkikka vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. kaṭṭiṭam 70.3 mī.nīḷam 23.9 mī.akalam koṇṭatu. avvappoḻutu kaṭṭiṭa amippil pala māṟṟaṅkaḷ ceytuḷḷaṉar. ataṉ kāraṇam  pūcārikaḷiṉ ācārak kuḷiyalukku māttiramaṉṟi nakara camūkat talivarkaḷ / atikārikaḷ, (parampariyāṉavarkaḷā allatu terivu ceyyap paṭṭavarkaḷā teriyavilli) ākiyōrum kēṇiyip payaṉpaṭutti iruppārkaḷ; cātāraṇa makkaḷukku aṉumati vaḻaṅkappaṭṭirātu eṉpatu maṭṭum niccayam.
(iv). rōm cāmrācciya uccakaṭṭattil ittakiya kuḷiyal aṟikaḷ (Baths) rōm nakaril mika pirapalam; aṅku uyarkuṭiyiṉar oṉṟukūṭik kuḷittu kummāḷamiṭavum, aruvaruppāṉa pala ceyalkaḷic ceyyavum kuḷiyal iṭaṅkaḷ (pala kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ aṟikaḷik koṇṭatāka) virivaṭintaṉa. `rōmaṉ kuḷiyal eṉṟālē pala viparīta arttaṅkaḷ ataṟku uṇṭu! āṉāl mokeñco-tarō nīccal kēṇiyum appaṭiyāṉatu eṉa  ituvari evarum kūṟavilli.
13(i). pūcāri nirvākattiṉ talimip pīṭam itu eṉpatu vīlar karuttu. citadel pātukāppu araṇil vāḻnta varkaḷ cātāraṇa makkaḷ mītu carvātikāra āṭci celuttiṉar eṉpar avar.  kēṇiyip pūcārikaḷē payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉṟu vittuk koṇṭālum `avarkaḷukku elliyaṟṟa araciyal atikāraṅkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉṟu eppaṭik kūṟa muṭiyum? eṉpatu etirvātam.  pūcārikaḷ tam iruppiṭattil pātukāppāka vāḻntaṉar eṉpatālēyē pūcāri - maṉṉarkaḷ (Priest kings) tāṉ āṇṭaṉar eṉṟu muṭivu ceyvatu cariyā?! kirīṭ tīvil miṉo maṉṉaṉ atiyuyar pūcāriyākavum pēraracaṉākavum iruntāṉ. (King Minos of Crete was the High Priest and overall king) eṉpatālēyē mokeñco-tarōvilum pūcāri maṉṉar āṭci ceytaṉar eṉṟu colvataṟku ātāram illi eṉalām.
(ii). makkaḷik kaṭṭuppāṭṭil vittirukkavum taṅkaḷ āṭciyi niyāyappaṭuttavum paṇṭikkāla maṉṉarkaḷ tammiyē araciyal  talivaṉ / matattalivaṉ āka niyamittuk koṇṭaṉar; oru paṭi mēlēyum ceṉṟu tammik kaṭavuḷiṉ avatāramākavum, cila camayam kaṭavuḷākavē tammi ākkik koṇṭaṉar!
cintu araciyal nirvākam (Political organization of the Indus)
14. cintu nilapparappu muḻuvatum perum cāmrājyamāka (vast empire) iyaṅkiyatu; mokeñco-tarō, harappā, kālipaṅkaṉ eṉpaṉa muṟiyē vaṭakku / teṟku / kiḻakku nirvākat talinakaraṅkaḷ eṉpar cila āyvāḷar. harappā, mokeñco-tarō nakaraṅkaḷi iraṭṭit talinakaraṅkaḷ eṉṟum kuṟippiṭuvar.
15. cintu nakaraṅkaḷ tammittāmē āṭciceyyum. amippukaḷākac ceyaṟpaṭṭaṉa, avaṟṟiṟkiṭiyil toṭarpu iruntālum, perumpālāṉa atikāraṅkaḷ antanta nakara makkaḷ poṟuppiltāṉ iruntaṉaveṉṟutāṉ perumpālāṉa  āyvāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar. perunakaraṅkaḷ taṉittiyaṅkiya pōtilum, potu amcaṅkaḷāka nakara vaṭivamippu, niṟuttal / nīṭṭal aḷavu orumippāṭu, ceṅkaṟkaḷiṉ pariṇāmam kuṟittu cintup parappu muḻuvatum nilaviya oṟṟi aḷavīṭu eṉpaṉa amintaṉa. intap potumiyi amalākkiyatu yār / enta amippu eṉpatu kēḷvik kuṟiyākattāṉ uḷḷatu.
16. cintu nakaraṅkaḷ mikavum muṟpōkkāṉa nakaramippu vitimuṟikaḷik koṇṭiruntaṉa. avaṟṟi uruvākkiyōr yār, mīṟuvōrit taṇṭippatu yār eṉpatum teriyavilli. (cintu nakara vācikaḷ aṉivarum kuṟṟamaṟṟavarkaḷ caṭṭam, oḻuṅku vitimuṟikaḷukkup paṇintu naṭappavarkaḷ eṉṟum colla muṭiyātu!) cintu nakaraṅkaḷi `talimip poṟuppilirunta kuṭumpaṅkaḷ cila nirvakittaṉa eṉṟa karuttum uḷḷatu. inta āṭci muṟiyi Chiefdoms eṉpārkaḷ. [mu©kidfŸ, aracarkaḷ, paṭṭattu rāṇikaḷ cintu nākarikattil illātapaṭiyāl vēṟu ātāraṅkaḷ varum tiu] intak karuttukku ātaravu uṇṭu.
17. mokeñco-tarō perunakarkkup piṟavaṟṟukku illāta taṉi aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. mutaliliruntu muḻumi peṟumvari atu naṉku tiṭṭamiṭṭuk kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. kuṟinta paṭcam itaṟku 600 āṇṭukaḷ piṭittirukkavēṇṭum. ataṉuṭiya īṭiṇiyaṟṟa vaṉappum, mukkiya amcaṅkaḷāṉa
perumaḷavu cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷ,
eṇṇaṟṟa kuṭinīrk kiṇaṟukaḷ,
periya nīccal kēṇi, atu amintuḷḷa 12 mī.uyara mēṭu,
nērkkōṭṭil cellum cālikaḷ,
cuttam / cukam kavaṉippu oḻuṅkukaḷ
eṉpaṉavum inta aḷavu uṉṉata niliyil vēṟu ennakarilum illi. nakarik kiviṭāmal ēḻu taṭavi oṉṟiṉ mēl oṉṟāka aṅkēyē vacittatiliruntē annakaram appakutivāḻ makkaḷuṭaṉ  piṇintiruntatu eṉpati aṟiyalām. [Mdhš yār anta makkaḷ, enta iṉappeyar / kulam / kōttiram iviyoṉṟum bjÇaÉšiy]. `cintuveḷi `mokeñco-tarō eṉpavi yellām piṟkālattavarākiya nām vitta peyartām. aṉṟu vaḻaṅkiya paṇṭip peyar itu varit teriyavilli!
18. mokeñco-tarō celvac ceḻippuḷḷa perunakaram. celavip poruṭpaṭuttāmal antak kālattiṉ mikac ciṟanta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭa nakaramāka ataṉik kiviṭum vari mēmpaṭutti vittiruntaṉar.
19. mokeñco-tarō makkaḷ vacatiyuḷḷa,  ārōkkiyamāṉa, `naṉku uṇṭu uṟaṅkiya maṉitarkaḷākat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉar.  potuvāka atu mēṭṭuk kuṭiyiṉariṉ vāḻviṭam. vēḷāṇ makkaḷum āṭu, māṭu vaḷarppōrum aṅku vāḻnta cāṉṟukaḷ illi. iṉṟiya vācakattil kūṟuvatāyiṉ toḻilatiparkaḷ, kiviṉit tiṟaṉāḷikaḷ, nakara nirvākikaḷ, kaṭṭaṭak kaliñarkaḷ, poṟiyiyalāḷarkaḷ, celvantarkaḷ perum eṇṇikkiyil vāḻnta iṭamākavē atu iruntatu.
20. marmaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟik koṇṭa mokeñco-tarō kimu. 1900 aḷavil ceyaṟpāṭu iḻantu kiviṭappaṭṭatu tāṉ marmaṅkaḷiṉ cikaram. ivvakic citivi uḷcitivu (Implosion) eṉpar.
21. ucci mēṭṭil uḷḷa paṭikkaṭṭuk kēṇikku mīṇṭum ceṉṟāl ataṉ vaṭakkup pakkattil pārkka varipaṭam 11.3 pocēl: pak 188] Block 6 iṭattil pala kuḷiyal aṟikaḷ iruntatip pārkkalām. ivaṟṟi 1927-28 il mekkē kaṇṭupiṭittār. periya nīccal kēṇikkup patilāka ivi payaṉpāṭṭukku vantirukkalām eṉṟu mekkē karutukiṟār. itu cintu nākarikattiṉ piṟkāla nikaḻcciyākum. atē vaṭakkup pakutiyil piṟkālattil putta matattiṉar, tūpi amittatuṭaṉ tamatu vāḻviṭaṅkaḷiyum amittuḷḷaṉar. putta matattiṉar mokeñco-tarōviṉ mēṭṭukku vantataṟku iru kāraṇaṅkaḷ: (1) mēṭu kiviṭappaṭṭu  iruntatāl atil tūpiyi nāṭṭi vāḻat toṭaṅkiṉar. (2) anta mēṭṭiṟku ētō mata mukkiyattuvamum iruntirukkalām.
22. Block 6 eṉṟu varipaṭam kuṟippiṭum pakutikku arukiluḷḷa piratāṉa vītiyiṉ maṟu pakkattil periya tiṟantaveḷi muṟṟam uṇṭu. immuṟṟam kēṇi / kaḷañciyam kaṭṭiya kālattil amikkappaṭṭateṉalām. ataṉuṭiya cuṟṟuccuvar iraṇṭu mīṭṭar akalam; ceṅkaṟkaḷāl uṟutiyākak kaṭṭiyatu. immuṟṟam etaṟkāka uruvāṉatu? eṉṟu teriyavilli. āṉāl ataṟku mataccaṭaṅku mukkiyattuvam irukkalām eṉpar pocēl pōṉṟōr. 
23. kēṇiyil iruntu teṟku nōkkic ciṟitu tūrattil avvaripaṭattil L Area, eṉak kuṟippiṭṭa pakuti mukkiyamāṉa iṭamāka mokeñco-tarō ucca kaṭṭa kālattil iruntirukka vēṇṭum. vīlar ati Pillared hall, atāvatu tūṇkaḷ tāṅkum maṇṭapam eṉṟār. ataṟku muṉṉar anta iṭatti mārṣal `tuṟavikaḷ / pūcārikaḷ monks / priests kūṭumiṭam eṉṟār. immaṇṭapattil cintu nakaraṅkaḷ etilum kāṇa muṭiyāta taṉi vicēṭamāka, tūṇkaḷukkiṭiyil tariyil patitta ceṅkal varicikaḷi muṟṟilum vittiyācamāṉa muṟiyil aṭukkiyuḷḷaṉar; kāraṇam puriyavilli. mīṇṭum, akaḻāyvu ceyya vēṇṭiya avaciyatti icceṅkal varicikaḷ uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟār vīlar. piṟkālam cārnta pala ceyporuḷkaḷ (Artefacts) pala ic ceṅkal varicip pakutiyiṉ mēṟpuṟattil kiṭittaṉa. oṉṟu 61 X 54 X 37 cemī. aḷavuḷḷa kaṟuppukkal. itu cerupput tippavaṉ tōli veṭṭap payaṉpaṭuttum veṭṭuk kal pōṉṟatu. kēṇi, kaḷañciyam avaṟṟōṭu toṭarpuṭiya aṉittum kiviṭappaṭṭa piṟaku ammēṭṭiṉ mītu kiviṉit toḻilkaḷ iṭampiṭittaṉa eṉpati itu kāṭṭukiṟatu. itu kalmēṭṭiṉ kuṟikkōḷ māṟiyatik kāṭṭukiṟatu; vīḻccikkum cāṉṟākiṟatu.
mokeñco-tarōviṉ kīḻ nakaram (The Lower Town)
24(i). mārṣal, mekkē, vīlar ākiyōr mokeñco-tarō nakariṉ mēṭṭup pakuti, kīḻppuṟa nakaram ivaṟṟi akaḻāyvu ceytatuṭaṉ varipaṭaṅ kaḷiyum tayārittuḷḷaṉar. `kīḻ nakaram eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭa pakutikku mārṣal mikavum tulliyamāṉa varipaṭaṅkaḷ tayārittatōṭu viḷakkaṅkaḷiyum tantuḷḷār. kīḻnakarak kaṭṭumāṉaṅkaḷi matippiṭa mārṣaliṉ varipaṭaṅkaḷ utavukiṉṟaṉa.
(ii). mika atikamāka vīṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭum pakutiyi mārṣal HRA, HRB eṉṟu kuṟittār. kīḻ nakariṉ piṟitoru  pakutiyi avar VS-A, VS-B eṉṟum, kīḻ nakariṉ pintiya kālattik kuṟikkum kaṭṭumāṉaṅkaḷ uḷḷa pakutiyi  moṉīr ēriyā (Moneer area) (allatu DK -1] eṉṟum ataṉ eñciya pakutiyi DK-A, DK-B, DK-C maṟṟum DK-G eṉṟum avar kuṟittuḷḷār itē variciyil kīḻ nakar pakutikaḷip pārppōm.
(iii). HR eṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ House Residence, vīṭukaḷi / vāḻviṭaṅkaḷi kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. intap pakutikku ūṭāka mutalām teru (first street) celkiṟatu. mutalām teruvukku kiḻakkē irukkum vīṭukaḷ ciṟu terukkaḷ uḷḷa pakutiyi HR -A eṉṟum mutalām teruvukku mēṟkē uḷḷa atu pōṉṟa pakuti HR-B eṉṟum varipaṭam cuṭṭukiṟatu.  (varipaṭam ila. 11.12 pocēl pakkam 198].
(iv). mutalām teruviṉ vaṭakkup pakuti 10 mī akalam. vākaṉap pōkkuvarattiṟkup poruttamāṉa pakuti eṉṟālum vākaṉaṅkaḷ atteruvil pōy vanta aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ illi eṉpar mārṣal. (nakarap pakutiyil vākaṉap pōkkuvarattuk kaṭṭuppāṭu iruntirukkalām). mutalām teruviṉ veṟumi kaṇṇukkut terikiṟatu. vīṭukaḷ niṟiya iruntālum avaṟṟiṉ vācalkaḷ teruvip pārttapaṭi illi. uṭpuṟattil uḷḷa ciṟu terukkaḷip (lanes) pārttu uḷḷaṉa. mutalām teruvip pārkkumpaṭi vācal katavukaḷ, jaṉṉalkaḷ illi. itu mokeñco-tarōvil kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
(v). mutalām teruviṉ vaṭa-kiḻakkup pakutiyil vacatiyāṉōr vāḻum periya vīṭukaḷ iruntaṉa. ivaṟṟil vāḻvōr  piramukarkaḷāka (elite) irukka vēṇṭum eṉpar mārṣalum piṟarum. avvīṭukaḷ kaṭṭumāṉattil ceṅkaṟkaḷ perumaḷavu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ivi kimu 3000 kuriyavi. AH-R, Block 3, House No. viii, atāvatu HR-A pakuti 3ām catukkam, eṇ 8  vīṭṭi mārṣal virivāka ārāyntu varipaṭam tayārittuḷḷār. ēṉ inta vīṭṭiṟku ivvaḷavu mukkiyattuvam? iv vīṭṭiṉ `talivācal (min entrance) ciṟiya teruvi nōkkiyapaṭi uḷḷatu; acciṟiya teruvi mārṣal varipaṭattil High lane eṉpar. vaṭakkuc cuvarilum oru `piṉ katavu irukkiṟatu. vīṭṭiṉ piratāṉa vācalum, piṉ katavu nuḻivāyilum, perunteruvāṉa mutalām teruvil amikkappaṭātataṟku antak kāla vaḻakkam kāraṇam eṉalām. citadel il itu pōṉṟa periya mēlmāṭi vīṭukaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; atu mēṭṭuk kuṭiyiṉar vāḻviṭam eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
25(i). eṭṭām eṇ vīṭṭiṉ teṟkuc cuvar 26 mī.nīḷam ākum. accuvari ciṟiyateruvil tali vācal uḷḷatu. vīṭṭiṉ vaṭakku teṟku nīḷam 29.5 mī. vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷ (ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭiyavi) 1.2 mī.mutal 1.5 mī. akalamāṉavi.  cuvaril iru katavukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa; vēṟu tuvāraṅkaḷ illi. talivācal ākiya piratāṉa vāyil mutalil 3 mī.akalamāṉatu; vīṭṭi māṟṟi amittapōtu 2.3 mī. ākak kuṟintatu. vīṭṭiṉ muṉpakutiyil vīṭṭuk kāvaṟkāraṉiṉ ciṟiya aṟi uḷḷatu.
(ii). cuvarkaḷiṉ mēl 2 cemī aḷavu kaḷi maṇṇum pullum kalanta cāntu pūci ataṟkumēl kaḷimaṇ pūccut tarappaṭṭatu. oru muṟi vīṭṭil tīppaṟṟiyatāl intap pūccukaḷ ventu iṟuki viṭṭaṉa. vīṭṭukkuḷ nuḻintu valappakkam tirumpiṉāl tariyil ceṅkal  patitta tiṟanta veḷi muṟṟam; itutāṉ vīṭṭiṉ miyap pakuti. varipaṭam 18, 18a eṉṟu itik kuṟikkiṟatu. (vīṭṭiṉ varipaṭam ilakkam 11.13 pocēl pakkam 199il.)
(iii). muṟṟattiṉ kiḻakkilum teṟkilum ciṟiya aṟikaḷ (6-13) kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tiṟanta muṟṟam illāviṭṭāl kīḻ vīṭu muḻuvatum veḷiccam illāmal iruḷaṭintirukkum. muṟṟamum atic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa tarittaḷa aṟikaḷum ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭiyavi. 12,12a, 11m ilakka aṟikaḷiṉ veḷippuṟattil vaṭikālkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avi 12a yiluḷḷa kīḻ nōkkic cellum ceṅkuttāṉa cuṭṭamaṇ kuḻāyuṭaṉ iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itu pōṉṟa iṉṉoru kuḻāyum uḷḷatu. (paṭattil kāṭṭavilli.)
26(i). perumpālāṉa, cintu nākarika vīṭukaḷ, oṟṟi māṭi allatu iraṭṭi māṭi vīṭukaḷ. vaṭikālkaḷum, kaḻivu nīr vāykkālkaḷum teruviṉ aṭiyil cellum kaḻivu vāykkālkaḷuṭaṉ iṇikkappaṭṭavi. (itaṟku vitivilakku teṉpaṭavilli). eṭṭām eṇ vīṭṭiṉ ceṅkuttāṉa kuḻāykaḷ iraṇṭum nilattaṭiyil nirantaramākap patikkappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭattil ceṉṟu muṭikiṉṟaṉa - atāvatu kuḻāykaḷ vaḻic ceṉṟa poruḷ maṭpāṇṭattil viḻuntatu. iti viḷakkum pōtu mārṣal `antak kuḻāykaḷ vaḻiyāka kaḻippaṟi malak kaḻivu ceṉṟu maṭpāṇṭattil viḻukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiyatu cariyalla.   tiṟanta maṭpāṇṭattil malam cēkarikkappaṭṭirukkum eṉak kaṟpaṉi ceyyak kūṭa muṭiyavilli. cuttam, cukattiṟku muṉṉurimi koṭutta accamutāyattil atuvum mēṭṭuk kuṭip piramukarkaḷ vāḻum pakutiyil mārṣal kūṟuvatupōl, naṭantirukka vāyppilli; intak kuḻāykaḷil maḻi nīr / kuḷiyalaṟi nīr ceṉṟatu eṉalām. kūrntu nōkkumpōtu atu kūṭac cāttiyamilli.
(ii). itupaṟṟi ṣeriṉ ratnākar Studies in People’s History I-1 June 2014 itaḻil virivāka āyvu ceytuḷḷār. avar kūṟum karuttukaḷ cila: ūr, pāpilōṉ pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil ittakiya amippukaḷ illi; mokeñco-tarōvil kaḻivunīr kuḻāykaḷil vīṭṭuk kaḻinīr maṭṭum ceṉṟatā? allatu ciṟunīr, malam mutaliyaviyum (vīṭutōṟum kaḻippaṟi?) ceṉṟaṉavā? appaṭippaṭṭa nilimi camāḷittirukkak kūṭiyatā? palavakik kaḻivukaḷum pōkakkūṭiya aḷavukku akkuḻāykaḷiṉ vāṭṭamum parimāṇamum iruntirukkumā? atumaṭṭumaṉṟi māṭivīṭukaḷil moṭṭimāṭi nīrum akkuḻāykaḷil pāyntiruntāl, maḻi nāṭkaḷil terukkaḷellām turnāṟṟam aṭittirukkumē? eṉṟa pala aiyaṅkaḷi eḻuppiyuḷḷār eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. avar kaṭṭuriyiṉ peyar (The drinage system at Mohenjo-daro and Nausharo: A technological breakthrough or a stinking disaster?) eṉpatākum.
27. eṭṭām eṇ vīṭṭiṉ aṟikaḷ 6 - 13 pakkam mīṇṭum celvōm. mūṉṟu aṭukkup paṭikkaṭṭukaḷil ēṟicceṉṟu 6vatu aṟikkuc ceṉṟāl, ceṅkal patitta kiṇaṟṟik kāṇalām. 7, 8ām aṟikaḷukku kiṇaṟṟu nīrik kuṭaṅkaḷil nirappik koṇṭu celvataṟku vacatiyāka cuvaril iṭam viṭappaṭṭiruntatu. (7- 8ām aṟikaḷ kuḷiyal aṟikaḷ). inta aṟikaḷukku tiṟanta (muṟṟam pārtta) marac caṉṉalkaḷ iruntaṉa. 9ām aṟiyil mēl māṭiyiliruntu kīḻ nōkki varum paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avi kuḷiyal aṟikku varum mēl māṭi vācikaḷukkāka amikkappaṭṭavitām.
28. oṉpatām aṟiyil irukkum iṉṉoru vāyil mūlamum `tiṟanta muṟṟap pakutiyil iruntum uḷḷē cella muṭiyum. kuḷitta piṉṉar muṟṟattil āṟutalāka amaravō, allatu nīril tōynta āṭikaḷi ularaviṭavō inta ēṟpāṭu ceytirukkalām. 10,11,12,13ām aṟikaḷ mikavum ciṟiyavi; ivi paṇiyāḷar / vīṭṭu vēlikkārar taṅkum aṟikaḷ eṉalām. (avi kaḷañciyaṅkaḷākavum irukkalām). 10-12ām aṟikaḷiṉ katavukaḷ māṭṭiyirunta marakkālkaḷ tēyntu kāṇappaṭṭaṉa; aṭikkaṭi katavukaḷit tiṟantu mūṭum pōtu tēymāṉam ēṟpaṭattāṉē ceyyum.
29. mēlmāṭiyip pārviyiṭum muṉpu piratāṉa vāyilukku neruṅkiya 17ām aṟiyiyum ataṟkuḷ aminta 17a eṉṟa ciṟiya aṟiyiyum ativiṭac ciṟiya aṟiyiyum kavaṉikka vēṇṭum. aṟikkuḷ aṟiyāka aminta uḷ aṟikaḷukkuḷ, veḷi aṟi vaḻiyākattāṉ nuḻiya muṭiyum. (veḷiyil iruntu 17a ilakka aṟi teriyātu. mutiyōr aṟi/ taṉimiyi virumpum viruntāḷiyiṉ aṟiyāka itu irukkalām eṉṟu pala vacatikaḷ koṇṭa inta maṟiviṭam karutappaṭukiṟatu). atu vīṭṭuk kāvalkāraṉiṉ aṟi maṭṭumē eṉkiṟār pocēl.  kīḻ vīṭṭil vīṭṭu vāyilukku arukil iruppatāl avar appaṭik karutukiṟār pōlum.
30. eṭṭām aṟi vīṭu maṇ nirappi eḻuppiya mēṭiyil kaṭṭiyatu. (cintunati ōrattil aminta vīṭukaḷ, kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ mēṭikaḷil kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟum avvappoḻutu  mēṭikaḷi  uyarttik koṇṭaṉar eṉpatiyum kaṇṭōm). inta vīṭṭiṉ vaṭa pakuti mēṭiyāṉatu taṉicceṅkal mēṭikku mēl  uṟutiyāka amikkappaṭṭatu. veḷḷa āpattu vantāl kuṭumpam vacatiyākat taṅkuvataṟkāka ippakuti aṟikaḷi ellām immēṭi mītu kaṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.
31(i). mokeñco-tarōvil cila vīṭukaḷ aṭutta vīṭṭuc cuvariliruntu oru aṭi taḷḷi irukkiṉṟaṉa. cuvarkaḷiṉ kaṉattik koṇṭu vīṭukaḷukku 2 / 3 māṭikaḷ iruntati aṟiyalām; moṭṭi māṭiyum iruntirukkalām. cuvarkaḷil uyarattil catura tuvāraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa, avaṟṟuḷ marattuṇṭukaḷ cerukappaṭṭaṉa. tuvāraṅkaḷi iṇikkum nīḷamāṉa marappalakikaḷ kūri vēyap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ mēl nāṇalpulpāykaḷi virittu avaṟṟiṉ mītu oru aṭukku kaḷimaṇ parappi maṭṭañ ceyyappaṭṭatu; itu tāṉ kūri. cila vīṭukaḷil mēlē cellum paṭikkaṭṭukaḷi iṉṟu kāṇa muṭiyātataṟku avi marattiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭatē kāraṇam; ceṅkal paṭikkaṭṭukaḷi iṉṟum kāṇalām.
(ii). vīṭṭiṉ kūrikaḷ taṭṭiyāka iruntaṉa. kūriyil viḻum maḻi nīr ceṅkaṟ kuḻāykaḷ mūlam vītiyil viḻuntu nilattaṭi vāykkāl mūlam veḷiyēṟiyatu. vīṭutōṟum kiṇaṟu, kuḷiyal aṟi, kaḻippaṟi, camiyal aṟi, vīṭṭup poruḷkaḷ vippaṟikaḷ iruntaṉa; kuḷiyal aṟikaḷ teruppuṟamāka iruntaṉa. taṇṇīr veḷiyēṟuvataṟku vacatiyāka vīṭṭuk kaḻivu nīr ceṅkal patitta oru kuḻiyil vīḻnta piṉṉarē vītiyiluḷḷa vāykkālukkuc ceṉṟatu. (kuḻi nirampiya piṉṉarē kaḻivu nīr vāykkālukkuc cellum. tiṭap poruḷkaḷ kuḻiyil vīḻntu  kiṭantu, avvappoḻutu nīkkappaṭum; ikkuḻiyil malak kaḻivukaḷ viḻuntirukkātu eṉalām).
32. mokeñ-cotarō mutal teruvukku mēṟkil HR - B eṉṟu varipaṭam 11.12il kuṟitta kuṭiyiruppukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. itil 8ām vīṭu 2ām catukkattil (Block 2) uḷḷatu. itaṉ 17ām aṟiyil oru cūḷi (kiln) uṇṭu. nerukkamāṉa kuṭiyiruppukkuḷ cūḷi (kiln) iruntatu mikaviyappu; atu piṟkālattil niṟuvappaṭṭirukkalām. avvīṭṭiṉ 8ām aṟiyil tariyaṭiyil 1925-26 il piṉvarum putiyali tayārām cāhṉi kaṇṭupiṭittār: 
1.	oru paruttit tuṇip piyil cuṟṟi vitta, veḷḷic cāṭikaḷ (jars)  iraṇṭu.
2.	oru cempu kōṭari; uḷi uḷḷa ceppuccāṭi oṉṟu.
3.	periya keṭṭiyaṟṟa (hollow)  kātu kammalkaḷ nāṉku.
4.	vaṭṭa vaṭiva taṅka kammalkaḷ iraṇṭu.
5.	talip parivaṭṭaṅkaḷ (diadems) taṅkattil, mūṉṟu.
6.	curuṭṭi vitta talip parivaṭṭaṅkaḷ 13.
7.	akalamāṉa parivaṭṭaṅkaḷ, maṇikaḷ toṅka viṭṭavi mēlum iraṇṭu.
8.	kūr vaṭivāṉa neṟṟip paṭṭaṅkaḷ mūṉṟu.
9.	ari vaṭṭa vaṭivilāṉa āparaṇaṅkaḷ ēḻu.
10.	iraṇṭāka uṭinta veḷḷik kivaḷiyal oṉṟu.
11.	ki viral mōtiraṅkaḷ āṟu.
12.	(caṅku cippik) kiviral mōtiram oṉṟu. 
13.	kaḻuttu mālikaḷ oṉpatu; avi taṅkam, veḷḷi, peyṉ, malivāṉa āparaṇak kaṟkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil amintavi. (toṅkaṭṭāṉ, iṇippi, iṭiveḷi nirappi ivi uṭpaṭa).
veḷḷic cāṭikaḷ iraṇṭil paṭintirunta ākcijaṉ kaḷimpu (oxide salt) tāṉ avaṟṟic cuṟṟiyirunta paruttip piyic citiya viṭāmal pātukāttatu. parutti iruntati aṭiyāḷam kāṇa intap pi utaviyatu.
33. eṭṭām catukkattiṉ 5ām vīṭu piramāṇṭamāṉatu. vīṭṭiṉ kaṭṭumāṉam 1000 catura mīṭṭar; tiṟanta ceṅkal tari muṟṟam vīṭṭiṉ naṭunāyakamāka iruntatu. 14mī. X 19 mī.  muṟṟattiṟku vaṭakkē, cila aṟikaḷum, kiṇaṟum, uyarnta mēṭṭup pakutiyum uḷḷaṉa. ammēṭṭil māṭikkāṉa taḷam amikkappaṭṭatu. inta vīṭṭiṉ 49m aṟi 3.5mī. X 3.3 mī. nīṇṭu oṭuṅkalāṉatu. iv aṟiyil teṟkuc cuvarāṉatu vāyili aṭintirunta pōtilum, atil irunta vaḷivu (arch) periyatāka mokeñco-tarō vīṭṭu vaḷivukaḷil vittiyācamāṉatāka iruntatu. 49ām aṟi nalla niliyil uḷḷatu; cuvarkaḷ 5 aṭi uyaram; mēṟpakutiyil nīṇṭa marattuṇṭukaḷic cerukum tuvāraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. inta aṟiyiṉ mēṟpakuti perum eṭiyit tāṅkak kūṭiyatu.
34. aṟi 49ait tuppuravu ceytapōtu periya vaṭṭak kaṟkaḷ 18 aṟiyiṉ vaṭakkuc cuvar ōramākak kiṭittaṉa. itu pōṉṟa vaṭṭak kaṟkaḷi periya nīccal kēṇi mēṭṭil mekkē ‘L’ eṉṟu aṭiyāḷap pakutiyil kaṇṭeṭuttār. cāhṉi aṟikki kūṟuvatu:-
vaṭṭak kaṟkaḷukku arukil vaṭṭamēṟpakuti uḷḷa  vaṭṭakkaṟkaḷ iraṇṭik kaṇṭēṉ. ivi harappā, mokeñco-tarōvil kaṇṭa  palvēṟu parumaṉ kaṟkaḷiṉ mēl vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriyatāka niṟuttiya (liṅkam pōṉṟa) mēṟpakutikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. etaṟkāka niṟuttappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu carivarat teriyavilli. ivaṟṟip pōṉṟa kallil, (cūḷiyiṭṭa) maṇ pōṉṟa kūr uruḷi vaṭivaṅkaḷ, (mēṟpakuti nīṇṭirukkum vaṭṭa ōram illāmal) kiṭittaṉa. itu pōṉṟa 11 aṅkula uyara kūr uruḷi vaṭivatti nāṉ harappāvil 1924 - 1925-il mīṭṭēṉ. atu civaliṅka vaṭivamāka iruppatic cuṭṭiṉēṉ. atip pīṭattil niṟutti (aṭippākam karaṭu muraṭāṉa)  arukil uḷḷa ciṟiya ceṅkaṟ kaṭṭiṭattil vittu vaṇaṅkiyirukkalām.
35. aintām ilakka vīṭṭilum oru liṅkam kiṭittatu. cuṇṇāmpuk kal tūṇ talip pākaṅkaḷ (capitals) iraṇṭu 49ām aṟiyil kiṭittaṉa. akkampakka aṟikaḷ 47, 50 ivaṟṟilum kiṭittaṉa. ittakiya talippākaṅkaḷ mokeñco-tarōvil maṭṭum kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. (vaṭṭak kaṟkaḷ tūṇkaḷiṉ aṭippākamākavum payaṉpaṭṭatākat tolavirāvil terintatu.)
36. vaṭṭa vaṭivilāṉa mēṟkūṟiya kaṟkaḷiṉ naṭuvē, vaṭṭa vaṭivak kuḻikaḷi amittuḷḷaṉar. kūmpu vaṭivāṉa kaṟkaḷi kuḻikaḷil niṟutti liṅkam ākki mokeñco-tarō makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭaṉar eṉak koḷḷac cāṉṟāka itu amikiṟatu. [“A Lingam was also found in house V and it figured in the preliminary report in the same photograph as the objects noted in Sahni’s Quote” Gregory Possehl - p. 203 The Indus civilization: A Contemporary perspective: 2002.]
37. aintām vīṭṭic cuṟṟi kuṟippiṭattakkak kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ pala uḷḷaṉa. taṉittaṉiyākak kaṭṭiya cila kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum aṭaṅkum. appakutikku vaṭakkil 9ām ciṟu teruvil mēṟku nōkki 20 mī tūram ceṉṟāl 55ām vīṭu varum; avvīṭṭiṉ 40ām aṟiyiṉ tari ceṅkal parappic cīrākkiyatu. avvaṟiyil tāṉ pukaḻpeṟṟa veṇkala naṭaṉa mātiṉ ciliyi cāhṉi 26-1-1927 aṉṟu kaṇṭeṭuttār.
38. vīṭukaḷ eṇ. 50,30 ivi itē HR - B cūḻalil uḷḷaṉa. vīṭu 50 piramāṇṭamāṉatu; taṭitta cuvarkaḷ koṇṭatu. 30 ām vīṭu oru kōyilāka irukkalām eṉpar cāhṉi. Sahni says of house 30 “That the building, which stood on such massive foundations, had an exceptional character - probably sacred - cannot be doubted”. ikkaṭṭiṭam puṉitamāṉatu eṉpati maṟukka muṭiyātu. eṉiṉum puṉitap poruḷkaḷ eviyum avvīṭṭilō 50ām vīṭṭilō  kiṭikkavilli eṉpatu ippakutiyil cēkaritta poruḷ paṭṭiyalil iruntu terikikiṟatu.
39(i). mēṟcoṉṉa iru vīṭukaḷukkum iṭiyil, iraṭṭi variciyil cumār 15 ciṟiya maṉikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. oṉṟil kiṇaṟu uḷḷatu. ivi aṉittum oṟṟi māṭik kuṭiyiruppukaḷ. avi kaṭikaḷ irunta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ eṉṟu cāhṉi karutiṉār. kiṇaṟṟunīrik kuṭippataṟku vitta pāṉikaḷ, kuṭikkum kuvaḷikaḷ iviyum kiṇaṟṟarukē iruntaṉa. vīlar maṟṟum cilar inta oṟṟimāṭikkaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kūlit toḻilāḷaruk kāṉavi eṉak karutiṉar. (harappāvilum itu mātiriyāṉa toḻilāḷar vāḻviṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa).
(ii). vīlar mēlum oru paṭi ceṉṟu, ivi aṭimikaḷ, aṭimipōṉṟa toḻilāḷarkaḷiṉ iruppiṭam eṉṟār. āṉāl mokeñco-tarō camuka amippil aṭimikaḷ illi eṉpatu uṟuti. `kūlit toḻilāḷar kuṭiyiruppu eṉṟāl miyamāṉa iṭattil pala vacatikaḷuṭaṉ ciṟanta vīṭukaḷik kaṭṭi aṭimikaḷukku koṭuppārkaḷā! kiṇaṟum kūlikaḷukkāṉatalla; kiṇaṟṟil kuḷikka varuvōr taṟkālikamākat taṅkuvataṟku vacatiyāka oṟṟi māṭic ciṟuvīṭukaḷik kaṭṭiṉar. enta akaḻāyvup poruḷiyup paṟṟiyum nuṭpamāṉa aṟivuṭaṉ cintittu muṭivukku varuvatutāṉ tolliyal - Archeology is a composite of find and its interpretation” eṉṟār kārṭaṉ cilṭ.
40(i). ciṟitu vaṭakkil vāṭ 1923-24 il akaḻnta pakutiyākiya V S-A, V S-B uḷḷatu. avi muṟiyē mutalām teruvukku kiḻakku / mēṟkil uḷḷaṉa. akaḻviṉ pōtu kaṭṭaṭa aṭittaḷaṅkaḷiyum āḻamākacceṉṟa vaṇṭalmaṇ aṭukkukaḷiyum vāṭ kaṇṭār.
(ii). V S-A pakuti vītikaḷ nērkkōṭṭil illi. ip pakutiyil maṉitac caṭalaṅkaḷ pala orē iṭattil kiṭittaṉa. iti V S-A tuṉpiyal nikaḻcci tragedy eṉpar. caṭalaṅkaḷik kaṇṭa iṭam - 3ām catukkam, 4ām ciṟu teru vīṭukaḷ 18,33 ākiyavaṟṟiṟku iṭiyil ākum. muṉpu KD-1, eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭu vanta pakuti āyvāḷar peyarāl iṉṟu moṉīr pakuti (Moneer area) eṉappaṭukiṟatu. ivviṭattil 1924-25il akaḻnta  tīkṣit āṅkila eḻuttu U vaṭivil akalam kuṟinta kuḻiyi veṭṭiṉār: paṭṭi tīṭṭum toḻilakattik kaṇṭatōṭu malivāṉa āparaṇak kaṟkaḷ, ērāḷamāṉa maṇikaḷ,  ciṟiya eṭik kaṟkaḷ 16,  ciṟiyaraka tarāciṉ eṭit taṭṭukaḷ (weighing pans) oru cōṭi ivaṟṟi mīṭṭār. tīkṣit akaḻāyvu ceytatāl itaṉi KD-1 eṉac cuṭṭuvar.
41. DK-A eṉṟa pakutikkum tīkṣit peyartāṉ. mokeñco-tarōviṉ mikapperiya kiḻakku - mēṟku piṟkālat teru iṅku uḷḷatu. itaṟku nilattaṭi nīr vāykkāl uṇṭu. DK-B, DK-C pakutikaḷ vaṭakkē 100 mīṭṭar tūrattil irukkiṉṟaṉa. DK-B yiṉ kaṭṭumāṉa amippu tolliyal mukkiyattuvam vāyntatu. aṅku kiṭittavi mokeñco-tarōviṉ mikap piṟkālac cāṉṟukaḷ. vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷ akalam ciṟitākiyāl,  kīḻē vīḻntu kiṭantaṉa. ceṅkal aṭukkukaḷ varicip picaku, oḻuṅkup picakuṭaṉ iruntaṉa; kottaṉāriṉ acirattiyum kaṭṭiṭak kalit tara vīḻcciyum teriya vantaṉa.
42. pakuti DK-BÆš ceyta akaḻvil iraṇṭām teru veḷivantatu; ataṉ akalam 9 mī. (mutalām teruvip pōla) pūcāri maṉṉaṉ vaṭivam mutal teruviṉ mēṟkup puṟa HR-B pakuti, 2-m catukka, 2-m vīṭu, 2-m aṟiyil kiṭittatu.
43. DK - B yiṉ toṭarcciyāṉa DK-C pakuti 7,800 catura mī.  kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ koṇṭatu. ippakuti kiḻakku mēṟkut teruviṉ vaṭakkil catukkam 1,2,3 uḷḷaṉa. it teruviṉ kīḻ nilattaṭi cākkaṭi vāykkālkaḷ iruntaṉa. 16ām catukkam vīṭṭiṉ 2ām aṟiyil oru mī. āḻattil veḷḷic cāṭiyil aṭaṅkiya  āparaṇaṅkaḷ kiṭittaṉa.
44. DK-G pakuti: 1927 - 1931il mekkē mokeñco-tarōvukku vaṭakkil 2.8 hekṭēr parappi akaḻntār. ip pakutiyiṉ naṭuvē kiḻakku - mēṟkāka mukkiya vīti ceṉṟatu. atu mattiya vīti (Central street) eṉac cuṭṭappaṭṭatu. atteruviṉ iru pakkaṅkaḷilum irunta pakutikaḷi avar vaṭakku / teṟku eṉak kuṟittār. mutalām teruviṟku iṇiyākac cellum iṉṉoru vītiyiyum mekkē kaṇṭu  mēṟkut teru eṉap peyariṭṭār. mottap parappu 28,000 catura mī. (vaṭakku 12,000 +  teṟku 16,000).
45(i). DK-G teṟku: ip pakutiyiṉ mukkiyamāṉa kaṭṭiṭam araṇmaṉiyāka irukkalām. mekkē karuttuppaṭi itaṉ cuvarkaḷ ētōvoru potuk kaṭṭiṭattiṉ pakutiyāka irukka vēṇṭum, cāṉṟukaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil atu niccayamāka oru araṇmaṉiyāka / pēracacaṉukkuriyatāka iruntāka vēṇṭiya avaciyam illi, em mākāṇattiṉ tali nakaramāka iruntatō ammākāṇa nirvākat talimikkuriyatāka irukka vēṇṭum.
ikkaṭṭiṭam teṟkiṉ mutalām catukkam irukkum iṭattil uḷḷatu. itu araṇmaṉi / mukkiyatariṉ vāḻviṭamākattāṉ  iruntirukka vēṇṭiya avaciyam illi eṉpar mekkē [Further excavations at Mohenjo-Daro 1937 - 1938].
(ii). araṇmaṉik kaṭṭiṭattiṉ teṟkup pakutiyi mattiya naṭipāti yāṉatu aṟittokutikaḷ palavākap pirittatu. teṉ - mēṟku alakiṉ 33ām aṟiyiṉ kiḻakkup puṟattil 4 aṭi 3 aṅ āḻam koṇṭa iraṇṭu cūḷikaḷ: avaṟṟi paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ varumāṟu:-
ovvoru cūḷiyiṉ kuṟukkaḷavu mēṟpakutiyil 3 aṭi 3 aṅkulam; aṭippakutik kuṟukkaḷavil iraṇṭiṟkum vēṟupāṭu uḷḷatu. (2 aṭi 10 aṅ. / 3 aṭi 2 aṅ.) iccūḷikaḷ āppu vaṭiva ceṅkaṭṭikaḷālum kaḷimaṇ pūccālum kaṭṭiyavi. uṭpuṟak kaḷimaṇ pūccu cūḷiyiṉ kaṭum veppattiṟkuc cāṉṟu ākum. maram / kari payaṉpaṭutti uccaveppam peṟṟatic cūḷiyil kiṭanta veḷḷic cāmpal kāṭṭiyatu. cūḷiyiṉ uṭpuṟattil nīṭṭik koṇṭirunta 4 aṅkula viḷimpu (ledge) pōṉṟa pakutiyil ulōkam urukkum caṭṭi (crucible) / miṉukkam ceyvataṟkāṉa (glazing) poruḷi vittirukkalām.
kaṭuveppam uṇṭākkic ceyta uṟpattip paṇikaḷ ippakutiyil pala naṭantuḷḷaṉa. mokeñco-tarōviṉ kaṭici nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil vāḻ viṭaṅkaḷ toḻil pēṭṭiyāka māṟiyati ippakuti kāṭṭiyatu. paḻiya piramāṇṭamāṉa aṭittaḷaṅkaḷiṉmēl eḻuppiya putiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum paḻiyaṉa vāki toḻiṟkūṭamāka māṟiviṭṭaṉa. `araṇmaṉi eṉṟu muṉṉar karutiya kaṭṭiṭam kōvilāka irukkalām eṉṟa mekkē karuttip piṟa tolliya lāḷar ēṟpatilli.
46. DK-G vaṭakku: ippakutiyiṉ ellikaḷ kiḻakkil mutalām teru, teṟku mattiya vīti, mēṟkil mokeñco-tarō mēṭṭiṉ ōram, vaṭakkil elli teriyavilli. mattiya vītiyil iṟutivari perum māṟṟaṅkaḷ nikaḻavilli. (mokeñco-tarō mukkiya vītikaḷil oṉṟāka atu toṭarntatu. nilattaṭic cākkaṭi, nīr vīḻntu veḷiyēṟum kuḻikaḷum toṭarntu ceyaṟpaṭṭaṉa). mokeñ co-tarōviṉ piṟa pakutikaḷ cīraḻinta pōtu DKG vaṭakkil putiya kuṭiyiruppukaḷ, toḻil miyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
47. uccakaṭṭa kālak kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷukkum piṟkāla mokeñco-tarō kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷukkum iṭiyil pala vēṟupāṭukaḷ uṇṭu. piṟkālak kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ cintu nākarikattiṉ tēyvip potuvākavum mokeñco-tarō vīḻcciyik kuṟippākavum kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. kimu 1900kku muṉṉarē mokeñco-tarōviṉ vīḻcci toṭaṅki viṭṭatu.
48. piṉvarum mūṉṟum atāvatu
(i) perum nīrp pācaṉa amippukaḷ -- nīr vāykkāl / aṇi pōṉṟavi
(ii) ērāḷamāṉa uṇavu uṟpatti
(iii) nakaraṅkaḷiyum kaṭṭumāṉap paṇiyiyum ceyya pallāyiram aṭimikaḷ
ākiyavi paṇṭikkāla ulakil enta nākarikamum tōṉṟavō / mēmpāṭu aṭiyavō iṉṟiyamiyātavi eṉpar aṟiñar. (cintunākarika vaḷarccik kaṭṭap paṇpukaḷi taṉpāl koṇṭuḷḷa) mokeñco-tarōvil inta mūṉṟiṟkāṉa cāṉṟātāraṅ kaḷit teḷivāka, veḷippaṭiyākak kāṇamuṭiyavilli.

iyal - 6
cintuveḷi eḻuttup poṟippu 
peṅkuyiṉ ulaka varalāṟu (putiyatu) teṉṉin tiyāvil iṉṟum vaḻaṅkivarum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷōṭu iyipuṭiya oru moḻiyic cārntaviyāka (cintu muttiric coṟkaḷ) irukkalāmeṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu. jē. em. rāparṭ (2007)
It now seems at least likely that they are part of a language akin to the Dravidian tongues still used in Southern India (J.M. Roberts The New Penguin History of the World,) V, Edn (Revised) 2007, London.
cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷiṉ moḻi eṉṉa eṉṟu iṟutiyāka niṟuvappaṭumpoḻutu atu tirāviṭamoḻi cārntatāka amivataṟkē vāyppumika atikam.  - kamil cuvelapil (1990)
oru iṉam taṉakkuriya viḻumaṅkaḷil mutaṉmiyāṉatākap pōṟṟip pātukāppatu ataṉ varalāṟu ākum. varalāṟṟik kāṭṭa eḻuttup poṟippukaḷ perum paṅku vakikkiṉṟaṉa. eḻuttukkaḷ paṭikka iyalāmal marmamāka iruntāl varalāṟṟil pāriya veṟṟiṭam tōṉṟukiṟatu. kiṭṭattaṭṭa paṇṭi kāla moḻikaḷ pattu cārnta eḻuttukaḷ ituvari vācikkappaṭāmal uḷḷaṉa; cintu eḻuttup poṟippukaḷi vācikkāmal cintunākarika varalāṟu muḻumipeṟātu. ceṉṟa kālamtāṉ nikaḻ kālatti viḷakkuvatāl cintuveḷi eḻuttip paṭikka vēṇṭiyatu  avaciyam. cintu eḻuttiṉ marmattit tulakkuvatu moḻiyiyal aṟiñarkaḷiṉ veṟum poḻutupōkku alla; cintup poṟippukaḷi vācittāl teṟku āciya (intiya) araciyal maṟṟum paṇpāṭṭu varalāṟṟiṉ pala aṭippaṭik kūṟukaḷ mikunta teḷivum uṟutiyum aṭiyum.
2. mekcikkō nāṭṭil kimu 500 - kipi 1200 il nilaviya māyaṉ Mayan nākarika paṭa eḻuttukaḷi (Hieroglyphics) pala aṟiñarkaḷ muyaṟci ceytu paṭitta piṉṉar ariya varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷi veḷikkoṇarntuḷḷaṉar. mekcikkō kalvi niliyaṅkaḷil (puttuyir peṟṟa) māyaṉ moḻi pāṭamākavum vikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ekiptiṉ purātaṉa moḻiyum paṭa eḻuttu vaki cārntatē; āṉāl māyaṉ moḻiyi vācippatil irunta cikkal ekiptu moḻi vācippil illi. ekiptiṉ purātaṉa moḻiyi oppīṭṭiṉ mūlam culapamāka vācikka muṭintatu. nil mukattuvārattil kiṭṭiya  mummoḻik karuṅkal poṟippu (Rosetta stone) āṉatu ekiptu eḻuttip pakutikaḷiṉ mūlamāka paḻamperum ekiptu  ilakkiyam / nākarikam paṟṟi muḻumiyāka aṟivataṟku vaki ceytatu. tālami (Ptolemy V) maṉṉaṉ cārpil mempi (Memphis) nakaril kimu 196il veḷivanta anta āṇippoṟippāṉatu 
 (i) ekiptu moḻip paṭa eḻuttu, 
(ii) purātaṉa kirēkka moḻi eḻuttu
 (iii) aṭittaṭṭu makkaḷiṉ Coptic eḻuttu 
ākiya mūṉṟilum (kalmēl) poṟikkappaṭṭatu. 1799il ekiptu mītu pireñcu atipati neppōliyaṉ paṭi eṭuttapōtu piyari - pirāṉcuvā pūccāṭ (Pierre - Francois Bouchard) eṉṟa paṭivīraṉ nil mukattuvārattil roceṭṭā eṉṟa iṭattil akkallik kaṇṭeṭuttatāl atu roceṭṭā kal eṉappaṭṭatu. piṟkālattil itupōṉṟa mummoḻi poṟippukaḷ pala ekiptil kiṭitta pōtilum anta muṉṉōṭik kalliṉ perumiyi maṟukka muṭiyātu.
3. cintunākarikattil nilaviya moḻiyip paṭikka utavak kūṭiya 2 moḻi / 3 moḻikaḷ poṟitta cāṉṟu (rocaṭṭā pōṉṟatu) etuvum kiṭikkātatāl inta moḻiyi vācippatu mikavum kaṭiṉam; mēlum cila muṭṭuk kaṭṭikaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. cintuveḷip poṟippu ovvoṉṟum carācari  4 allatu 5 eḻuttukkaḷiyē  koṇṭatu. orē oru (mika nīḷamāṉa) poṟippu maṭṭumē 17 eḻuttu koṇṭatu. perumpālāṉa muttirikaḷ oru aṅkulam caturamāṉavi, ippoṟippukaḷi eḻuttukaḷ eṉṟu  ēṟkāmal veṟum kuṟiyīṭukaḷ eṉṟum cilar (tavaṟākac) collavum vāyppuḷḷatu. ulakiṉ entap paṇṭi moḻiyilum ivvaḷavu curukkamāṉa poṟippukaḷ illi; intac curukkam (brevity) cintuveḷi moḻi vācippil uḷḷa perum taṭaṅkalākum.
4. cintu eḻuttup poṟippukaḷ (Indus Script)  piṉvarum vakikaḷil namakkuk kiṭittuḷḷaṉa:-
1) 	cintuveḷi muttirikaḷ seals
2) 	muttirikaḷik kaḷimaṇṇil kuttippeṟṟa pativukaḷ sealings
3) maṭpāṇṭam, ceppu takaṭu, ciṉṉañciṟiya paṭṭayam, karuvi pōṉṟa pattukku mēṟpaṭṭa vakip poruḷkaḷil
ciṟanta poṟippukaḷi nalla niliyiluḷḷa 3000 muttirikaḷil kāṇalām. (muttirikaḷ kaḷimaṇ pōṉṟu - pativi ēṟkakkūṭiya poruḷkaḷil - kuttip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar).
5. muttirikaḷ mākkallil (Steatite) ceytu cūṭākki uṟutippaṭuttiyavi. perumpālāṉavaṟṟil vilaṅku, mīṉ, oṟṟik kompu `kāṇṭāmirukam, cīpu māṭu, vaṭivaṅkaḷum mēṟpakutiyil eḻuttukaḷ eṉṟu karutak kūṭiya kuṟiyīṭukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. varttakam, nāṭṭu niruvāka aluval pōṉṟavaṟṟukku am muttirikaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. ēṟṟumatip potikaḷ mītu kaḷimaṇṇil pativu ceyvataṟkum muttirikaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. (potikaḷik kaṭṭuvataṟkup payaṉpaṭuttiya nāṇal pul pāy, cippam kaṭṭiya kayiṟu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ cila kaḷimaṇ kaṭṭikaḷil pativākiyuḷḷatu).
6. cintuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷiṉ eṇṇikki cumār 400. inta 400il uḷḷa ciṟu vēṟupāṭukaḷi nīkkiṉāl aṭippaṭi eḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷ basic signs 200 kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. ik kuṟiyīṭukaḷ kimu 2600 - 1900 aḷavil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. kimu 1900kkup piṟaku payaṉpāṭṭil illi eṉṟu potuvākak karutiṉar. āṉāl pet tuvārakā (Bet Dwaraka) kaṭalaṭi āyvil inta eḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷ ataṟkup piṉṉarum cila kālam puḻaṅki yirukkalām eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟu kiṭittuḷḷatu. inta eḻuttup poṟippu caṭutiyil tōṉṟavilli; kimu 3000ām āṇṭu aḷavilēyē cintuveḷiyil vaḻaṅkiya moḻiyil iruntu tōṉṟi viṭṭatu eṉpar aṟiñar. 
7. cintu eḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷ paṟṟi vēṟupaṭṭa karuttukkaḷ nilavukiṉṟaṉa: avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu ippoṟippukaḷ moḻi eḻuttukkaḷē alla; vaṇikar paravalākap payaṉpaṭuttiya vaṇikak kuṟiyīṭukaḷtām Non - linguistic Sign system” eṉa 2004ām āṇṭu pārmar, purōṭ, viṭcal (Farmer, Sproat, Witzel) ākiyōr terivittatu ākum. accukaḷil vārtta ērāḷamāṉa cintu vaḻipāṭṭuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷil itē poṟippukaḷ uḷḷamiyāl immūvariṉ apattak karutti aṟiñar otukki viṭṭaṉar.  iṉṉoru apatta karuttu intap poṟippukaḷ `repu (Rebus) rakattic cērntavi; kaṇiṉi yukattil payaṉpaṭuvatu pōl oru colli allatu karutti veḷippaṭutta aṉṟu uruvākkiya kuṟiyīṭukaḷtām eṉpatākum. ituvum ēṟkapaṭavilli.
8(i). cintup poṟippukkaḷ moḻikkuriya eḻuttukkaḷtām eṉṟa karutti iṉṟu maṟukkum nallaṟiñar evarum illi. vālṭar ē. pēr cērvi (1921 - 1994) taṉatu 1969 - The Roots of Ancient India - nūlil cintu eḻuttup poṟippu harappā nākarikattiṟku muntiya kālattuk kuyavarkaḷiṉ maṭpāṇṭa aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷukku orupaṭi mēlāka, āṉāl ekiptiṉ paṭaveḻuttu, cumēriya karutteḻuttu (Ideograph) ākiyavaṟṟiṟkuk kuṟintatākavum uḷḷatu. atu vaḻaṅkiya kālattil (ciṟiya allatu periya māṟṟam etuvum aṭiyāmal) harappā nākarikam, muttirikaḷōṭu maṟintu viṭṭatu eṉṟār.
(ii) pala āṇṭu ciṟanta āyvukkup piṉṉar 1969kaḷil piṉlāntu, helciṉki palkalikkaḻaka intiyaviyal pērāciriyar akō parpōlā (Asko Parpola) cintu eḻuttup poṟippukkaḷ tol tirāviṭa moḻiyic cērntatu eṉṟu tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka niṟuvi aṟivittār. atē kāla kaṭṭattil raṣya moḻiyiyalāḷarkaḷum atē karuttit terivittaṉar. ataṉ piṉṉar pērcarvi-m taṉatu muntiya karutti māṟṟik koṇṭu cintup poṟippukaḷi taṉi moḻiyāka ēṟṟu ati vācikka taṉatu eñciya vāḻnāḷiṉ irupatuāṇṭukaḷic celaviṭṭār. (Walter A Fairservis:(1992) The Harappan Civilization: A Model for the decipherment of the Indus Script).
(iii). ulakiṉ cintu eḻuttup poṟippu vācippu nipuṇarkaḷil mutaliṭam vakippavar ākiya akō parpōlā cintup poṟippu vācippiyē taṉatu vāḻnāḷ paṇiyākak koṇṭuḷḷavar. intiyā, pākitāṉil kiṭitta pallāyiram cintuveḷippoṟippukaḷ, muttirikaḷ uḷḷiṭṭa aṉittiyum niḻal paṭam piṭittu mūṉṟu pāriya maṭalaṅkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār.  Corpus of Indus seals and Inscriptions: 1987 and 1991. cintunākarikam, ataṉ eḻuttup poṟippi vācittal ivi toṭarpāṉa muḻumiyāṉa ātāra nūliyum veḷi yiṭṭuḷḷār: Deciphering the Indus Scripts: Cambridge University Press (1994).
(iv). cintu nākarikattiṟku mecapotāmiya nilapparappu nākarikaṅkaḷil oṉṟākiya cumēriya makkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya peyar mē-la-kā (Me-la-ha) / Meluhha / mēlūkā eṉpatākum. cintuveḷiyiṉ tirāviṭamoḻip peyar mēṭakam eṉak koṇṭāl cumēriyarkaḷiṉ mē-la-kā poruḷ potintatāka uḷḷatu eṉpar parpōlā. mēṭukaḷ uḷḷa nāṭu eṉṟu tol tirāviṭa moḻiyil mēṭakam vaḻaṅkiyatu. mēlum āriyar allātāric cuṭṭum `milēcca eṉṟa camakirutac collum mēṭakam eṉpatiliruntu tōṉṟiyirukkalām eṉpar parpōlā.
9. airāvatam makātēvaṉ cintu eḻuttup poṟippu vācippil 45 āṇṭukaḷāka āḻnta ārāyccikaḷi ceytu varupavar. avar tol -tirāviṭa moḻikkuriyatākavē avaṟṟik karutukiṟār. oḻuṅkāṉa, tiṟamāṉa muṟiyil kavaṉamāka āyvu ceypavar;  nuṭpamāṉa āyvumuṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟi taṉatu karuttukaḷi mikakkavaṉamāka veḷippaṭuttupavar; taṉ muṭivukaḷiyum putiya ceytikaḷ, karuttukaḷukkēṟpa nayamāka ēṟkattakka vakiyil uruvākkik koḷḷat tayaṅkātavar. nāṉ colvatē cari; muṟṟāṉa muṭivu eṉpatu makā tēvaṉ  pāṇi alla eṉkiṟār pocēl: The Indus Age: The Writing - System. page 130.
10. raṣya moḻiyiyalāḷar yūri nārācāv (Yuri Knorozov) cintup poṟippukaḷ aciyeḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭu (Logosyllabic) vakiyic cērntavi; kaṇiṉi mūlam āyvu ceytatil avi kaṇṭippāka koḷuvunili (Agglutinative) moḻiyākiya tirāviṭa moḻiyākattāṉ irukkavēṇṭum eṉṟum kūṟiṉār.
11. cintu muttiri eḻuttukkaḷum (piṟa cintuveḷip poṟippukaḷum) kaṇṭippāka toltirāviṭa moḻitāṉ eṉṟu parpōlā, pērcarvi, nārācāv, makātēvaṉ pōṉṟa ciṟanta aṟiñarkaḷ ēṟṟuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷ tol - tirāviṭa moḻi eṉpatu toltamiḻiyē kuṟippatākum. iti aṭuttuvarum pattikaḷil kāṇalām.
12. vāṣiṅṭaṉ palkalik kaḻakam, mumpāy ṭāṭṭā aṭippaṭi ārāycci miyam ivaṟṟiṉ aṟiñarkaḷ rājēṣ rāv talimiyil iṇintu, kaṇiṉiyip payaṉpaṭutti cintu eḻuttup poṟippu moḻi evvaki moḻiyāka irukkalām eṉpati aṭiyāḷam kāṇa viḷintaṉar.  iṉṟu vaḻakkiluḷḷa intiya / āciya moḻik kūṟukaḷ, (kaṇiṉi moḻi uṭpaṭat) takaval cātaṉa ēntukaḷ, ākiyavaṟṟi iṇittunōkki, cintup poṟippukaḷi kaṇiṉi mūlam pakuppāyvu ceytaṉar. atil cumēriya moḻi, paḻantamiḻ ākiyavaṟṟiṟku cintu eḻuttup poṟippu moḻi nerukkamāka iruppatu teriyavantatu. iyaṟkiyākat tōṉṟiya moḻikaḷiṉ taṉmikaḷip pōṉṟē cintuppoṟippu moḻiyiṉ taṉmikaḷum iruppati uṇarntōm eṉpatu  muṭivu. ‘Entropic evidence for Linguistic structure in the Indus Script’: Rajesh P.N. Rao, Nisha Yadav, Mayan K. N. Vahia, Hrishikesh Joglekar, R. Adhikari, & Iravatham Mahadevan: Science Volume 324, Issue 5926, April 24, 2009.
13. cintu eḻuttup poṟippukkaḷ intō - airōppiya moḻik kuṭumpattic cērntavi tirāviṭa moḻiyalla veṉṟu iṉṟum cilar kūṟuvati ēṟkum nallaṟiñar yārum illi! car jāṉ mārṣal aṉṟē itupaṟṟik kūṟiyatu:  tirāviṭa moḻi oṭṭu nili moḻi; tirāviṭa moḻiyōṭu ivvakiyil iṇiyum moḻikaḷ, cintunākarika moḻiyum cumēriya moḻiyumākum - Mohenjo-Daro and the Indus Civilization: Sir John Marshal (1931) eṉpatākum. atu iṉṟu meyppikkappaṭṭu viṭṭatu.
14. cintu eḻuttiṉ moḻiyi nirṇayippataṟkuc ciṟanta āvaṇaṅkaḷāka muttirikaḷ māttiramē uḷḷaṉa. eḻutappaṭṭa   cintu ilakkiyaṅkaḷ oṉṟumē illi. āṉāl kalvi aṟivu cintuveḷi makkaḷukku iruntati maṟukka muṭiyātu. itaṟkut tolavirā aṟivippup palaki māttiramalla; āyirakkaṇakkāṉa  maṭpāṇṭap poṟippukaḷum cāṉṟākum. caṭṭi pāṉikaḷil kuyavarkaḷ ceytikaḷik kīṟal vaṭivattil tīṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. ivaṟṟip purintu koḷḷum tiṟami maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷip puḻaṅkum makkaḷukku iruntuḷḷatu; maṭpāṇṭak kuṟikaḷukku arttam irukka vēṇṭum; atip purintukoḷḷac cintu moḻi aṟivu tēvi. cintuppoṟippu moḻiyi vācikkum muṟiyik kaṇṭaṟintu viṭṭatākap palar kūṟiṉum, yārum taṉṉuṭiya vācipputāṉ cariyāṉateṉṟu niṟuvavō, piṟa aṟiñari ēṟkac ceyvō iyalavilli.
15. ituvari naṭanta akaḻāyvukaḷil kaḷimaṇ villiyilō, piṟa aḻiyāp poruḷilō pativu ceyta  cintuveḷip poṟippu etuvum ituvari kiṭikkātatālēyē `appaṭi oṉṟumē illi eṉṟu muṭivu kaṭṭakkūṭātu. akaḻāyvukaḷ muḻu vaṭivam peṟavilli eṉṟu kūṭac collalām.  aciriyāvil niṉavē māmaṉṉaṉ acurpaṉipāl (kimu 669 - 626) (Ashurbanipal of Ninevah the last of the Great Assyrian kings) kālattil 22,000 kaḷimaṇ kaṭṭikaḷil patiyappaṭṭa āppeḻuttu nūlkaḷ 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiltāṉ tarikku aṭiyil iruntu mīṭkappaṭṭaṉa. cintuveḷiyilum atu pōṉṟu varuṅkālattil nikaḻa vāyppu uṇṭu.
16. airāvatam makātēvaṉ paṭṭiyaliṉpaṭi cintumoḻip poṟippuḷḷa aṉittuc cāṉṟātāraṅkaḷil 87 viḻukkāṭu mokeñco -tarō harappāvil kiṭittaṉa.

17. makātēvaṉ 1977il tayāritta `muttirikaḷil poṟitta vilaṅkukaḷ vivaram varumāṟu: I. Mahadevan (1977: 793)

18. cintu muttirikaḷ catura / nīḷcatura vaṭivaṅkaḷil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. pakkaṅkaḷ nīḷa, akalam 3x2 ce.mī. itiviṭac ciṟiya  muttirikaḷum cila kiṭittaṉa. cīl vaṭivamippum poṟippum oḻuṅkupaṭa amintuḷḷaṉa; eṉiṉum mēṟpakuti eḻuttup poṟippukaḷil cila oru ticiyil nerukkip poṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. muttiriyil uruvaṅkaḷum eḻuttukaḷum kūrmiyāṉa karuṅkal allatu cempu - veṇkalak karuvi koṇṭu cetukkappaṭṭavi. muttirikaḷi ēṟkeṉavē veṭṭic ceppaṉiṭṭu vittirunta uṟpattiyāḷarkaḷ, vāṭikkiyāḷar tēvikkēṟpa vilaṅku, eḻuttu ākiyavaṟṟi cetukkit tantiruppārkaḷ eṉkiṟār mekkē.
19. cintu muttirikaḷil perumpālāṉavaṟṟil poṟittuḷḷa vilaṅku, oṟṟikkompu kāṇṭāmirukam (Unicorn). ivaṟṟi oṟṟikkompu erutāka ēṉ karutak kūṭātu? eṉpar cila āyvāḷarkaḷ. kāṇṭāmirukam cintup pakutiyil iyaṟkiyil perum eṇṇikkiyil iruntatā? avvilaṅkiṉ paṭatti maṭṭum muttirikaḷil  ēṉ mikapperum eṇṇikkiyil payaṉpaṭuttiṉar? eṉṟa kōṇaṅkaḷum karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
20. cintu makkaḷ aṟinta vilaṅkukaḷ cīpu raka timil vittu erutu, atē iṉap pacu, kuṟuṅkompu māṭu, erumi, yāṉi, oṭṭakam, nāy, kaḻuti, āṭu, maliāṭu, puli, kuraṅku, kāṇṭāmirukam, māṉ, paṉṟi, mutali, kīri, pāmpu, aṇil, kiḷi, kōḻi, mayil, muyal mutaliyavi. kāṇṭāmirukam `riṉōcara (Rhinocerous) iṉattic cērntatu. āṉāl muttirikaḷil perum eṇṇikkiyil kāṇum kāṇṭāmirukam vēṟu iṉattic cērntatu. iraṇṭiṟkum neṟṟik kompu uṇṭu. āṉāl muttirikaḷil peruvāriyāka kāṇum vilaṅkiṉ kompu iyaṟkikkup puṟampāka muṉṉōkki mikavum nīṇṭirukkiṟatu. ippaṭi oru vilaṅku vāḻntatā eṉṟu teriyavilli. ataṟku  ēṉ ivvaḷavu mukkiyattuvam tarappaṭṭatu eṉṟum teriyavilli. pi. rimāṉ (P. Rissman) kāṇṭāmiruka muttirikaḷi āyvu ceytu avaṟṟil iruvaki uḷḷatākak kaṇṭuḷḷār. ivvilaṅkiṉ tali, kaḻuttuc cōṭaṉi, ataṉuṭiya talippākattiṟkuk kīḻ vittirukkum tīttaṭṭu (brazier) ākiyavaṟṟi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu avar iraṇṭuvakik kāṇṭā miruka muttirikaḷi iṉaṅkaṇṭār:
(1)	(vaṭakkil harappā, colitāṉ pakutikaḷil veḷiyiṭṭa) `kuṟumukamuḷḷa kāṇṭāmiruka muttirikaḷ.
(2)	(teṟkil mokeñco-tarō pakutiyil) catic curukkamuḷḷa kaḻuttu, vittiyācamāṉa vaṭivamuḷḷa tīttaṭṭu ākiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ iṉṉoru vaki.
mokeñco-tarōvil immuttirikaḷ eṅkeṅkē kāṇappaṭṭaṉa eṉpati yū. pirāṅkē - vākṭ (U. Franke - Vogt) āyntār: 25 viḻukkāṭu muttirikaḷ periya nīccal kēṇi mēṭṭil kiṭittavi.
