(HR, VS pakutikaḷil ōraḷavum DK-G, DK-1 pakutikaḷil ērāḷamākavum kiṭittaṉa. - pārkka iyal 5). mokeñco-tarō iṭipāṭukaḷiṉ mēṟ pakutiyil atikamākac ceppup paṭṭayaṅkaḷum kīḻppakutiyil muttirikaḷum kiṭittaṉavām. muttiri tayārippup pāṇi cintu parappil pirāntiyattiṟkup pirāntiyam vēṟupaṭṭatu eṉkiṟār vākṭ. mokeñco-tarō, caṉku-taro, harappā, cinti, colitāṉ muttirikaḷ oru pāṇiyilum, lōtal pirāntiya muttirikaḷ caṟṟu taram kuṟintaviyākavum iruntaṉa. paṉavāliyil Unicorn muttirikaḷ 20 viḻukkāṭu āṭu / māṉ muttirikaḷ 53.3 viḻukkāṭu āka iruntaṉa. āṅkāṅ kirunta muttiri uṟpatti niliyat tayārippukaḷ vēṟupaṭṭavi. (muttirikaḷiṉ kīḻ / mēl pakuti mūlikaḷil uḷḷa poṟippukaḷi oppiṭum āyvukaḷum uṇṭu: - A short Introduction to the study of the Indus Script, with comments on the corner symbols. by Dontal B. Buchanan: The Epigraphic Society Occasional Papers: no. 28.]	
muttirikaḷ patippataṟku (kuttuvataṟku) maṭṭumē payaṉpaṭṭaṉavā? 
(Were the seals used for Stamping?)

21. muttirikaḷ ērāḷamākak kiṭittālum `muttiri kuttiya kaḷimaṇ pativukaḷ mikak kuṟivākavē kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. kiṭitta muttirikaḷilum perumpālāṉavi puḻaṅkappaṭāmal, tēymāṉam iṉṟi meruku keṭāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa! potikaḷil vaṇikat taravukaḷip poṟippataṟku māttiramā am muttirikaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉa?
22(i). ituvari cintup pakutiyil kiṭitta kaḷimaṇ pativukaḷiṉ eṇṇikki varumāṟu: mokeñco-tarō-4; harappā - 4; pāṉavāli -1; kālipaṅkaṉ -5; lōtal -37; (vaṭa cavurāṣṭirāviṉ) pākarā (Bagasra) -3. iviyellām poti, vaṇikapporuṭkaḷ aṭaṅkiya cāṭi, pōṉṟavaṟṟi tiruṭṭut taṉamākat tiṟattal / pirittal ākiyavaṟṟit  taṭuppataṟkāka avaṟṟiṉ mītu kuttiya muttirip pativukaḷ (estampages) ākum.
(ii). muttirip pativukaḷic ceyvataṉ mūlamāvē pala tāyattukaḷum vaḻipāṭṭu villikaḷum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa! avi inta āyvukku uṭpaṭavilli. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu pativu ceyta muttiri aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷum avvāṟē.
23. mokeñco-tarōvil 1926 - 1927il āyvu ceyta mekkē, kaḷimaṇ pativukaḷ (estampages) etuvum kiṭikkavilli eṉṟār. muttirikaḷ eṉṟu collappaṭum inta vaṭivaṅkaḷ tāyattukkaḷāka irukkalām eṉpar mekkē.  eṉ pārviyil varttaka / ēṟṟumatip poruḷkaḷip pātukāttiṭavē kaḷimaṇ pativukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa; (potikkayiṟṟiṉ muṉikaḷi muṭiccuppōṭṭa piṉṉar) potikaḷiliruntu tiruṭuvatit taṭuppatē aṭippaṭi nōkkam; āvaṇattukkāka (record purposes) cintu muttirikaḷ payaṉpaṭavilli eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.
24. mecapotāmiyā uṭpaṭa muṉṉāḷ varttaka miyaṅkaḷākat tikaḻnta nāṭukaḷilum mekkē akaḻāyvu ceytavar. avar kiṣ (Kish) iṭipāṭukaḷil kaḷimaṇ pativukaḷ palavaṟṟi mīṭṭār; āṉāl mokeñco-tarōvil attakiya pativukaḷ kāṇappaṭātati kaṇṭu viyantār.  cintu muttirikaḷ ceyta nērattil eppaṭi iruntaṉavō appaṭiyē kaṉṉi kaḻiyāta putumiyuṭaṉ iruntaṉa. ṭiyaṭṭiṭ (mākkal) mikavum meṉmiyāṉatu. cūṭupaṭutti uṟutippaṭuttiṉāl kūṭa muttirikaḷ neṭunāḷpuḻakkattil tēymāṉam aṭiyattāṉ ceyyum. mecapotāmiyāviṉ uruḷi vaṭiva muttirikaḷi, kaṟkaḷ, viṭūriyam pōṉṟa kaṭiṉamāṉavaṟṟil ceytaṉar; avi kūṭap puḻakkattil tēyntuḷḷaṉa.
25. cintu muttirikaḷ `aṭiyāḷa aṭṭikaḷ pōl iruntirukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇuvatil tavaṟilli.  vaṇikattukkup payaṉpaṭṭaṉa eṉpati viṭa araciyal / nirvāka aṭiyāḷat tēvikkup payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām. mattiya aracō, maṉṉar āṭciyō illāta cintu nakaraṅkaḷil immuttirikaḷ payaṉpāṭu itaṟkāka iruntirukkalāmō eṉṟu eṇṇat tōṉṟukiṟatu.
26. cintu muttiri moḻiyi vācikka muyaṟci ceyyu muṉṉar,  aṭippaṭi viṣayamāṉa atu enta moḻikkuṭumpattic cērntatu (atāvatu tirāviṭa moḻik kuṭumpam). eṉpatu tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka terintuḷḷatu aṭuttu, patitta eḻuttup poṟippukkaḷip (seal impressions) paṭippatu valamiruntu iṭamākavā, iṭamiruntu valamākavā eṉpati aṟiya vēṇṭum. muttiriyip patitta piṉṉar valamiruntu iṭamāka  (LR)  vācikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttu paravalāka ēṟkappaṭukiṟatu.
27. uḻavu māṭu pōl, mutalil `iṭamiruntu valamākavum aṭuttu `valamiruntu iṭamākavum varikku vari vittiyācamāka māṟimāṟi eḻutum muṟiyi pūtirōpeṭoṉ - Boustrophedon eṉpar.  (āti kirēkkak kalveṭṭukkaḷ immuṟiyil  iruntaṉa  Bous = māṭu; strophedon = uḻuvatu). cintup poṟippukkaḷ  muḻumiyāka vācikkappaṭavilli eṉiṉum avaṟṟuḷ cila pūtiropeṭoṉ muṟiyilum eḻutiyirukka vāyppuṇṭu. [Writing Systems: A Linguistic Introduction: Stanford University Press: Geoffry Sampson (1985)].
28. cintu eḻuttu paṭaveḻuttu eṉkiṉṟaṉar. mecapotāmiya eḻuttu paṭaveḻuttuk kaṭṭattiliruntu muṉṉēṟiyatu. cintu / mecapotāmiyā paṭaveḻuttukkaḷ pala oṉṟupōlavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. īrāṉ pīṭapūmiyiṉ tol - elām eṉappaṭum Linear elām moḻiyum paṭaveḻuttu muṟiyic cārntatu. tol elām, cintumoḻi iraṇṭum, kiṭṭattaṭṭa camakālattavi. cumēriya moḻikkum cintu moḻikkum uḷḷa orumippāṭṭi viṭa atikamāṉa  orumippāṭu elām, cintu moḻikaḷiṭiyē irukka vāyppuṇṭu. āṉāl tol - elām moḻiyum ituvari vācikkappaṭavilli. G.R. haṉṭar kūṟuvatu:
iraṇṭiṟkum iṭiyil toṭarpu illi; eḻuttup poṟippukkaḷiṉ oli vaṭivaṅkaḷ vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa, uṟpatti mūlam vēṟu, eṉṟālum avaṟṟiṟku iṭiyil uḷḷa oṟṟumikaḷit taṟceyalāṉavi eṉṟu otukkiṭa iyalātu Script of Harappa and Mohenjo-Daro and its connection with other scripts: G.R. Hunter (1934) Kegan Paul, London.
iṉṉum vācikkappaṭāmal irukkum moḻikaḷil mikap paḻamiyāṉatu tol-elām (Proto-Elamite). ataṉ kālam kimu 3200 - 2900. cintuveḷi eḻuttup poṟippiṉ kālam kimu 2600 - 1900.
cintunākarika moḻiyiṉ varivaṭiva eṇṇikki
29. cintuveḷi eḻuttu aṭippaṭi varivaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki paṟṟi karuttu vēṟupāṭu uḷḷatu. eṉiṉum eḻuttup poṟippukaḷ niccayam 100kkumēl irukkalām eṉṟē aṉivarum karutukiṉṟaṉar:-
     lāṅṭaṉ (Langdon) 1931	288
haṉṭar (Hunter)  1932	149
vāṉ meriki (Von Merrigi) 1934 	270
tāṉi (Dani) 1963	537
kokeṉṉiyemi, akō parpōlā 
(Koskenniemi, Asko Parpola) 1982	396
airāvatam makātēvaṉ (Iravatham. Mahadevan) 1977	417
pērcarvi (Fairservis) 1992	419
30. vēṟupāṭṭukku mukkiya kāraṇam, `etu aṭippaṭiyāṉa eḻuttup poṟippu, etu tēvikkēṟpac caṟṟu māṟṟiya poṟippu eṉa aṟiya iyalātatutāṉ. ettaṉi eḻuttu eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ payaṉpāṭu paṟṟiyum aṟiyāmal vācippatu mikak kaṭiṉam.
31. āṅkila moḻiyil 26 eḻuttukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avi alpapeṭ (alphabet) vaṇṇamāli eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. (kirēkka, moḻiyiṉ mutal iru eḻuttukkaḷiyum iṇittatu `alpapeṭ - Alpha + Beta = Alphabet. cīṉa eḻuttu ki.mu. 1000 āṇṭiṟku muṟpaṭṭatu: 4000 variyāṉa karutteḻuttukaḷi  (Ideograph) koṇṭatu.
32. cintuveḷi eḻuttiṟku vaṇṇamāli iruppatāka teriyavilli; veṟum kuṟiyīṭukaḷum alla; ākiyāl vallunar aṉivarum cintueḻuttup poṟippukaḷi Logosyllabic collaciyaṉ aciyeḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷ ākak karutukiṉṟaṉar.
33. makātēvaṉ kūṟum 417 vaṭivaṅkaḷil 113 eḻuttup poṟippukaḷ orē orumuṟi; 47 poṟippukaḷ  iraṇṭu muṟi; 59 poṟippukaḷ aintu taṭavikkuk kuṟivāka, eṉṟavāṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. mikap pala taṭavi varum  eḻuttup poṟippu 	cāṭik kuṟi, atu 1,395 taṭavi varukiṟatu.  harappā kālap piṟpakuti cārnta rōjti (kimu 2000 - 1700) uṭpaṭap piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum atikkāṇalām. 
34. iraṭṭic ceṅkuttu aṭiyāḷam ākiya || eṉpatu (iraṇṭām niliyil) 649 taṭavi varukiṟatu; itu eḻuttā - eṇṇā eṉpatu teriyavilli. mīṉ kuṟikkup pakkattil inta aṭiyāḷam 6 taṭavi varum pōtu aṟumīṉ eṉṟum 7 taṭavi varum pōtu `eḻumīṉ eṉṟum vācippar. motta eḻuttup poṟippup payaṉpāṭṭil 67 kuṟiyīṭukaḷ 80 viḻukkāṭṭip piṭittu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. makātēvaṉiṉ 417 kuṟiyīṭukaḷil 200 maṭṭumē atika aḷavil payaṉpaṭṭaṉa eṉpar. atikamāṉa eḻuttukaḷik koṇṭa cuṭṭa maṇ ōṭukaḷ iraṇṭu mokeñco-tarō DK pakutiyil kiṭittaṉa.
35. harappā nākarika ucca kaṭṭa kālattil payaṉpaṭuttiya cintu eḻuttup poṟippil vaḷarcciyiyō,  mēmpāṭṭiyō kāṇa muṭiyavilli. inta eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷiṉ tōṟṟamum nakarppuṟa nākarika eḻucciyum cama kālattavi eṉṟu karutiṉālum eḻuttu caṟṟu muntiyē tōṉṟiyirukkalām eṉpar pocēl mutaliya aṟiñarkaḷ. taṉittaṉik kuṟiyīṭukaḷāka iruntavi oru oḻuṅkamippil varac cila kālam piṭittirukkum. itu (māṟṟak kālam (transition) eṉappaṭum) kimu 2600 - 2500 kāla aḷavil naṭantirukkalām eṉpar pocēl. (2003)
36. maṭpāṇṭak kuṟiyīṭukaḷāka irunta eḻuttup poṟippukkaḷ paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarcci aṭintu mēmpaṭṭa nili eytiṉa eṉpati niṟuvum tolliyal cāṉṟu ituvarik kiṭṭavilli. mecapotāmiyāvil kimu 6000 il āppu eḻuttāka (Cuneiform) ārampittu kimu 4000 aḷavil eḻuttu nilikku vantu `purātaṉa vācakaṅkaḷāka (Primitive texts) vaṭiveṭuttaṉa. itu cintu eḻuttu varalāṟiliruntu māṟupaṭṭatu. cintu eḻuttup poṟippukaḷ nilaviya kālamuḻuvatum avi māṟṟamē aṭiyavilli eṉṟum kūṟuvatu kaṭiṉam. kālap pōkkil emmoḻiyilum māṟṟam varum; ammāṟṟaṅkaḷ antanta moḻit taṉmikkēṟpa ēṟpaṭum, eṉpatum potuvāṉa vitikaḷ; eṉṟālum vitivilakkukaḷum uṇṭu.
37. mutircci aṭinta muḻu vaṭivattil cintu moḻi tiṭīr eṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākak koḷḷa muṭiyātu. ataṉ vaḷarccip pāti teḷivākumvari appaṭittāṉ (tiṭīrt tōṟṟam!) koḷḷa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu! atē camayattil, māṟṟaṅkaḷē ēṟpaṭāta moḻi eṉṟu koḷvataṟkum tayakkamāka uḷḷatu. cintu muttirikaḷ vaṇikam, nāṭṭu nirvākam, taṉi napari aṭiyāḷappaṭuttal pōṉṟa tēvikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. muttirikaḷiṉ piṉṉaṇiyil oru kaṭṭuppāṭu iruntirukkum. avaṟṟi viniyōkittavar / aṉumatittavar / aṅkīkarittavar yār yār eṉṟa viṉā eḻukiṟatu. muttirikaḷ toṭarpāṉa ceytip pativukaḷ kaḷimaṇ / paruttit tuṇi / pataṉiṭappaṭṭa tōl /marappaṭṭi pōṉṟavaṟṟil ceyyappaṭṭirukkalām eṉpar.
38. muttirip poṟippukaḷum piṟavum, cintu moḻiyiṉ / moḻikaḷiṉ payaṉpāṭṭup pulaṅkaḷil oru ciṟiya pakutiyākat tāṉ iruntirukkum. vēṟupala kāriyaṅkaḷukkāka tōl, paṭṭi mutaliya palavakic cātaṉaṅkaḷ akkālattil iruntu piṉṉar aḻintirukkalām. kaḷimaṇṇil patiyappaṭṭavi aḻiya vāyppilli. periya cintunākarikap parappil oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkiyirukkalām. ellōrum iṇippu moḻiyāka ēṟṟuk koṇṭa Lingua Franca moḻiyākavum oṉṟu iruntirukkalām; appaṭiyāṉāl eḻuttu poṟippukkaḷ aṉittum orēyoru moḻiyic cērntavi eṉṟu koḷvatukūṭa muṟṟilum uṟutiyāṉatu alla. appaṭi iraṇṭu /mūṉṟu moḻikaḷiṉ eḻuttup poṟippukaḷ cintuveḷiyil payaṉpaṭṭiruntālum avi eṉṉaveṉṟu namakku ippoḻutu teriyātu!
39(i). aṟiñar mek alpiṉ (Mc Alpin) cintunākarika moḻiyāṉatu, tirāviṭa moḻiyum  elām moḻiyum kalanta kalappu moḻi eṉṟa karutti 1974il terivittār. [Toward Proto - Elamo - Dravidian: Language Volume 50, No. 1]. inta munti nilik kūṭṭu moḻiyi avar tol-elām-tirāviṭam eṉṟu aḻittār.
(ii). cintup poṟippukaḷiṉ moḻi tirāviṭak kuṭumpam tāṉ eṉṟu heṉṟi hīrā 1940- 50kaḷilē uṟutiyākak kūṟiṉār.  cuvalapil, parpōlā, makātēvaṉ ākiyōrum ik karuttiṉarē.
(iii). mikkēl viṭcel (MichelWitzel) taṉatu putukkaruttāka cintu moḻi ātirō - āciya (Austro - Asiatic) kuṭumpa moḻi eṉṟu kūṟi ataṟku `paramuṉṭā Para - Munda eṉap peyar vittār.
40. cek nāṭṭu aṟiñar cuvalapil (Kamil Vaclav Zvelebil 1927 - 2009) tamiḻ, camakirutam, tirāviṭa moḻiyiyal ākiya palatuṟi viṟpaṉṉar; tamiḻ ilakkiyatti avar tuṟi pōkak kaṟṟavar. cek viññāṉak kaḻakattiṉ kiḻakkiyal tuṟi vallunar. cintu eḻuttup poṟippu paṟṟiya cuvalepil (1970) nūlil kūṟum karuttukkaḷ  varumāṟu:
i)	potuvāka avaṟṟi valam iruntu iṭamāka vācikka vēṇṭum. muttirikaḷi iṭamiruntu valamākavum [LR] muttirip pativukaḷi valamiruntu iṭamākavum [LR] vācikka vēṇṭum.
ii)	mikaccila iṭaṅkaḷil iṭamiruntu valamākavum eḻuttup pativukaḷip paṭikka nēralām. pūṭarapeṭāṉ muṟiyilum oru cila poṟippukkaḷ irukkalām.
iii)	cintu moḻiyil perumaḷavu piṉṉoṭṭukkaḷ (marks of agglutination) uḷḷaṉa.  muṉṉoṭṭukkaḷ, iṭiyoṭṭukkaḷ, eṇ / pāl / vēṟṟumikku amivākac coṟkaḷiṉ vikuti māṟṟam Inflection kāṇappaṭavilli.
iv)	cintu eḻuttup poṟippi aciyeḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭākak karutalām.
kaṭiciyāka tamatu 1990 nūlil cuvalepil mēlum virivākak kūṟiyatu varumāṟu:
i)	eḻuttup poṟippukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki mika atikam; avaṟṟi eḻuttukaḷākavō eḻuttup pōṉṟaviyākavō, koḷḷa muṭiyātu. (Not alphabetic or quasi-alphabetic).
ii)	kimu 3000 - 2000 kālam cārnta (piṟanāṭṭu) eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷukkum cintup poṟippu eḻuttu vaṭivattiṟkum neruṅkiya uṟavu illi. tol -elām moḻiyuṭaṉ maṭṭum cila oṟṟumi iruppiṉum, uṟavu illi.
iii)	piṟkāla intiya eḻuttu vari vaṭivam (lipi) etaṉuṭaṉum (pirāmi, karōti uṭpaṭa) lipi cintu eḻuttu uṟavuṭiyatalla.
iv)	ituvari palarum uruvākkiyuḷḷa cintumoḻic col = piṟamoḻic col oppumip paṭṭiyal oṉṟu kūṭa tiṭṭavaṭṭa māka vācikka utavakkūṭiyataṉṟu.
v)	intō - airōppiya moḻi etaṉuṭiya eḻuttu vaṭivākavum cintu moḻi irukkavē muṭiyātu. kāraṇam Inflection moḻikkuṟiya muṉṉoṭṭu, iṭiyoṭṭu, coṟkaḷiṉ vikutimāṟṟam pōṉṟavi cintu moḻiyil illavē illi.
vi)	cintu poṟippukkum mēṟku āciya eḻuttu etaṟkum toṭarpē illi; kāraṇam mēṟku āciya moḻikaḷil uriccol / peyarccol ākiyavi toṭariṉ muṟpakutiyil varukiṉṟaṉa. (eṉiṉum elām moḻiyuṭaṉ oruvēḷi uṟavu iruntirukka kūṭum; āṉāl atu nirūpikkappaṭavilli).
vii)	cintup poṟippukaḷil `cāṭi eḻuttu; `ampukkuṟi ivi                                                                                                   vēṟṟumi urupu; colluruvam peṟṟa eḻuttu eṉṟu cilar karutuvati makātēvaṉ Concordance pōṉṟavi ātarikkavilli.  
viii)	ituvarip palarum ceytuḷḷa vācippukaḷil oṉṟu kūṭa  uṇmiyāṉatu eṉṟu nirūpikkappaṭavilli. “none of the published clims is valid”.
cuvelapil muṭivukaḷi cintup poṟippu toṭarpāṉa potuvāṉa nilippāṭāka iṉṟum karutalām; avaṟṟil māṟṟam ēṟpaṭuttum nikaḻcci ituvari nikaḻavilli. cintu eḻuttup poṟippukkaḷ iṟutiyil tirāviṭa moḻi cārntavi tām eṉṟu nirūviṭa vāyppuṇṭu eṉṟa  cuvalepil uṭiya uṟutiyāṉa muṭiviyum maṟantu viṭakkūṭātu. “The most probable candidate is and remins some form of Dravidian” Dravidian Linguistics - An Introduction Pondicherry: (1990): Dravidian and Harappan, Chapter VI.
41. ji. ār haṉṭar 1929il cintup poṟippukaḷi vācikkum muyaṟciyit toṭaṅkiṉār. ituvari pattukku mēṟpaṭṭa vallunarkaḷ hīrā, akō parpōlā, ai. makātēvaṉ, irā. mativāṇaṉ, pūrṇa cantira jīvā, ār. pālakiruṣṇaṉ, (pōlantu) cālek Szalek ākiyōr intat tuṟiyil ceyalpaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. āṉāl yāṉiyit taṭavip pārtta kuruṭarkaḷ pōl, ovvoruvar vācippum ovvoru vitamāka irukkiṟatu! orē eḻuttup poṟippiṟku avarkaḷ vitavitamāṉa arttaṅkaḷik kāṇkiṉṟaṉar! oru eḻuttup poṟippiṟkuk kūṭa aṉivarum orē potu arttatti vaḻaṅkavilli. (utāraṇattiṟku `mīṉ kuṟiyip perumpālāṉa aṟiñarkaḷ `viṇ mīṉākap pārkkiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl vālṭar pērcarvi ati `muṭiccāka (knot) pārkkiṟār!) ovvoruvarum taṉ cātaṉiyē cari eṉṟa nōkkuṭaṉ taṉi vaḻi celkiṉṟaṉar.
42. pacupik mākkaṭalil uḷḷa īṭar tīvil marak kaṭṭikaḷil poṟitta vaṟṟi cintuveḷi eḻuttup poṟippukaḷuṭaṉ iṇittu oppiṭum muyaṟciyi kirakari pocēl kēlik kūttu eṉṟu cariyākavē kaṇṭittuḷḷār. (īṭar tīvup poṟippukaḷ kipi 18 - 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭu cārntavi, īṭar tīvukkum cintuppakutikkum iṭiyil tūram 21,000 kimī. iṭiyil iraṇṭu camuttiraṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉālum ji.ār. haṉṭariṉ harappā mokeñco-tarō eḻuttukkaḷum avaṟṟōṭu toṭarpuṭiya piṟa eḻuttukkaḷum eṉṟa nūlukku muṉṉuri eḻutiya pērāciriyar lāṅṭaṉ (Langdon), iru eḻuttup poṟippukaḷukkum iṭiyiluḷḷa oṟṟumikaḷ paṟṟi aiyamē illi eṉṟu terivittiruntār!)
43. iṉṟiya teṟku āciyāvil eḻuntuḷḷa paṇpāṭṭut tēciyavātam Cultural Nationalism (atāvatu tattam moḻi, iṉam, kuḻutāṉ taliciṟanta paṇpāṭṭi mutaṉ mutalil uruvākkiyatu eṉṉum uratta kūccal) pala kēṭukaḷi viḷivittuḷḷatu. varalāṟṟit tirippatu  allatu putitāka uruvākkuvatu; ivaṟṟiṉ mūlam antanta nāṭṭil / pakutiyil perumpāṉmi yiṉarāṉa kuḻukkaḷ, moḻiyiṉar, iṉattavar tattam mēlātikkatti uṟuti ceytu, araciyal ātāya / ātikkam peṟuvatum iṉṟu nām paravalākak kāṇpavi. cintunākarikam cār muṭivukaḷit tirittu vētakāla āriyac cāyam pūcum pōkkum tali tūkkiyuḷḷatu. cintunākarikac cāṉṟukaḷ tolliyal ārāycci mūlam peṟṟavi. irukku vēta varalāṟu muṟṟilum ilakkiyam cāṉṟatu. iraṇṭum oppiṭāta muṭiyātavāṟu muraṇpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl mōtal valukkumē oḻiya iṇivu ēṟpaṭa vāyppilli. eṉṟu intiyāviṉ paṇpāṭṭut tēciya eḻucci paṟṟi amartyā ceṉ taṉatu (piṉvarum) nūlkaḷil eḻutiyuḷḷatiyum kāṇka:
(i)	The Argumentative Indian :Writings on Indian Culture, History and Identity (2005)
(ii)	Identity A Violence: The Illusion of Destiny (2006)
44(i). cintu eḻuttup poṟippukaḷiṉ moḻiyāṉatu tol-tirāviṭam  eṉṟum, intiyā - mattiya āciyā uḷḷaṭaṅkiya aṇmik kiḻakku (Near East) vari tol-tirāviṭap paṇpāṭu nilaviyatu eṉṟum ēṟattāḻa tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka niṟuvappaṭṭuḷḷa tuṭaṉ kūṭutalāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ iṉṉum vantavāṟu uḷḷaṉa. iruntālum iti maṟutalikkum muyaṟcikaḷum 1950kaḷ mutal naṭakkiṉṟaṉa! āriyac cārpā - tirāviṭac cārpā eṉṟa tēviyillāta vātaṅkaḷil   īṭupaṭuvati intiya aṟiñarkaḷil cilarum iṉṉum viṭṭuviṭavilli!!
(ii). cintunākarikattiṉ peyariyē cintu - caravati nākarikam eṉṟu māṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa kuralum olikkiṉṟatu. cintu, kaṅkā ākiya iraṇṭiviṭavum periyatu (irukku vētattil varum) caravati āṟu eṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu. intiyāvil ippaṭiyeṉṟāl (1947il uruvākkappaṭṭa) pākitāṉil 7000 āṇṭukaḷ paḻami vāynta `pākitāṉ nākarikam eṉṟu kūṟuvōrum uḷḷaṉar!
(iii). intiyāvilum, pākitāṉ pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷilum pal tuṟi aṟiñar naṭunilimiyāṉa nērmiyāṉa muṭivukaḷiyē ātarikkum nili illi, uriya aṟivup pulaṅkaḷil (relevant disciplines of sciences and Humanities) -- akaḻāyvu, kiṭittuḷḷa eccaṅkaḷiṉ kālakkaṇippu, mānta marapaṇuviyal, AMH taṟkāla mānta iṉattōṟṟam, palvēṟu moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷiṉ varalāṟu; pōṉṟavaṟṟil -- antantak kuḻuviṉar tamakkē mutaṉmiyum uyarvum eṉṟu vallaṭi vaḻakku muṭivu ceytukoṇṭu ātāraṅkaḷit tirittō, pōli ātāraṅkaḷic ciruṣṭittō  niṟuva muyalum pōkkukaḷ uḷḷaṉa; innili māṟuvatākavum tōṉṟavilli. iccūḻniliyil, tolliyal mutaliya tuṟikaḷil tamiḻk kāppāḷar āḻntu īṭupaṭa vēṇṭiyatu  iṉṟiyamiyātatu. 

iyal - 7
a) cintu nākarikak kalikaḷ
palvēṟu intiyakkalikaḷiṉ mukkiyamāṉa aṭippaṭip paṇpukaḷ cintunākarikak kalip paṭippukaḷ kālam toṭaṅki, muriyar āṭcik kālam ataṟku pintiya kālaṅkaḷ, ivaṟṟiyellām tāṇṭi iṉṟuvarit toṭarntu vantuḷḷaṉa. - ṭellā krāmriṣ 1898 -1993.
“The quintesential characteristics of Indian Art could be discerned as much in Indus Valley artefacts, as in the Mauryan period to the very present” - Stella Kramrisch 1898-1993. 
intiyāviṉ (teṉ āciyāviṉ) mikap paḻiya ciṟpa vaṭivaṅkaḷi cintunākarikak kālattil kāṇalām. maram, kal, kaḷimaṇ, ariya kal vakikaḷ - kārṉīliyaṉ, jāpar, akēṭ āṉik, lāpi lāculi, kuvārṭ, mākkal pōṉṟavaṟṟil uruvākkiyaviyum mākkal eṉpaṉavaṟṟil ceyyappaṭṭaviyum, cempu / veṇkalap poruḷkaḷum tolliyal akaḻāyvil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. (marattil ceytavi muṟṟāka aḻintuviṭṭaṉa) perunakaraṅkaḷāṉa harappā, mokeñco-tarōvil tāṉ atikamāṉa ciṟpaṅkaḷ kiṭittaṉa.
2. cintu nākarikattiṉ muṉṉōṭiyāṉa mekarkāril teṟku āciyāviṉ mikap paḻiya cuṭṭa maṇṇālāṉa peṇ pommikaḷ kiṭittaṉa; uruvattil mikac ciṟiyavi eṉpatāl avi ciṟiya pāvikaḷ (figurines) eṉappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. caṭṭi, pāṉi pōṉṟa vīṭṭu upayōkap  poruḷkaḷi uṟpatti ceyyumuṉṉarē intap peṇ vaṭivaṅkaḷi mekarkār vācikaḷ ceyyat toṭaṅki viṭṭaṉar; ivaṟṟik kalipporuḷkaḷāka māttiramalla vaḻipāṭṭup poruṭkaḷākavum pārkka vēṇṭiyatu avaciyam. mekarkār kuṭiyiruppiṉ kālam kimu 7000 - 2500 ākum. (ikkālatti eṭṭuk kaṭṭaṅkaḷākat tolliyalāḷarkaḷ vakuttuḷḷaṉar).
3. ellāk kaṭṭaṅkaḷilum `ciṟiya peṇ vaṭiva maṇ pommikaḷ ērāḷamākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. kālappōkkil avaṟṟukku varṇam tīṭṭi kaḷimaṇ āparaṇaṅkaḷiyum porutti (uruṇṭu, tiraṇṭa uyaramāṉa) tali alaṅkāramum ceyyappaṭṭatu; mārpakaṅkaḷ puṭittu nimirntu niṉṟaṉa. āparaṇaṅkaḷit tavira vēṟoṉṟum antap piṟanta mēṉi uṭalil kāṇappaṭavilli. ivaṟṟiṉ kali nayatti uṇarnta pireñcut tolliyalāḷar, kimu 3000 cārnta cuṭṭa maṇ pāvi oṉṟit terivuceytu tam nāṭṭut talinakar paḻamporuṭ kāppakattil vittuḷḷaṉar.
4. kimu 7000 - 2600 āṇṭukaḷil atikamākap peṇ vaṭivaṅkaḷ tāṉ amikkappaṭṭaṉa. kimu 2600ttukkup piṟaku tāṉ  cuṭṭa maṇ / āṇ pommikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. paṭippaṭiyāka  āṇ pommi eṇṇikki perukiyatu. maṇ pāvikaḷ /maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷukku civappu niṟam tīṭṭuvataṟkāṉa irumpu ākciṭ (Iron - Oxide) kaṟkaḷ iyaṟkiyil kiṭittaṉa; culapamāka iṭittup poṭiyākki nīril kalantu Ochre (ākkar) civappu niṟat tiravattic ceytu pūci caṭṭi, pāṉi, pāvi mutaliya maṇ pommikaḷi aḻakupaṭuttit tamatu kali uṇarvik kāṭṭiṉar.
5. lāpi lāculi (Lapis Lazuli) eṉṟa karunīlak kalliyum cintumakkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; karunīlattōṭu taṅka varṇamum intak kallil uṇṭu; itu intiyāvil kiṭippatu aritu. paṇṭu toṭṭu lāpi lāculi kaṟkaḷukkut taṉi matippuṇṭu. vaṭa kiḻakku apkāṉiṉ, car-ī-caṅ (Sar - I - Sang) curaṅkaṅkaḷil atu kiṭikkum. kimu 7000 mutal iṉṟuvari vaṭa - kiḻakku āpkāṉitāṉ, paṭakṣāṉ (Badakshan) mākāṇac curaṅkaṅkaḷ  tām lāpi lāculi kaṟkaḷi ulakiṟkē tarukiṉṟaṉa.
6. ekiptu rāṇi kiḷiyōpatrā lāpi lāculi kallip poṭi ceytu mikavum meṉmiyākki kaṇṇukku aṭiyil (mukattil) pūci aḻakupaṭuttik koḷvārām. piṟkāla airōppiya ōviyarkaḷ alṭṭirāmariṉ Ultramarine eṉṟa karunīla ōviya miyi akkallil iruntu uruvākkip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. cintunākarika ucca kālattil aḻaku cātaṉap poruḷkaḷum āparaṇaṅkaḷum ikkallil tayārikkappaṭṭaṉa. intiya vaṇikarkaḷ paṭakṣāṉ mākāṇattil kuṭiyiruppu uruvākki lāpi lāculi kal ēṟṟumati vaṇikam naṭattiṉar. mekarkāril  ikkal āparaṇaṅkaḷ iṭukāṭṭuk kuḻikaḷil kiṭittaṉa. kilkāmeṣ itikācattilum intak kal paṟṟiya kuṟippu uṇṭu.
7. kalciṭōṉi (Chalcedony) eṉṟa maṇ kal (Silica) jāpar, āṉik, kārṉīliyaṉ pōṉṟa paṭivaṅkaḷil kiṭikkiṟatu. jāpar (Jasper) kal āṉatu maṇ niṟam (brown), mañcaḷ, pacci, civappu ākiya niṟaṅkaḷil kiṭikkum. civappu jāpar mika atikam; nīla niṟa jāpar ariyatu. intak kalli miṉukki aḻakāṉa poruḷkaḷic cetukkic ceyyalām. kimu 5000 - 4000 aḷavil mekarkār toḻil viṟpaṉṉar pacci jāpar kalli veṭṭit tuḷik karuvikaḷil tuḷippāṉākap porutti āṉik (Onyx) āparaṇak kallil tuḷiyiṭṭaṉar. kalciṭōṉi kuṭumpa varṇaṅkaḷpala varivariyāka aminta āparaṇakkal āṉik ākum; ataṉi kaḻuttu māliyāka aṇintaṉar.
8. kalciṭōṉi kuṭumpattiṉ iṉṉoru āparaṇak kal kārṉīliyaṉ (Carnelian). itu intiyāvil kiṭikkiṟatu. mēṟkūṟiya aṉittum (lāpi lāculi tavira) intiya upa kaṇṭattil kiṭippaṉatāṉ. intiyāvil pallāyiram āṇṭākap payaṉpaṭum iṉṉoru kal ṭiyaṭṭiṭ (Steatite) `mākkal ākum. `cavarkkārak kal (Soapstone) eṉṟu kūṟuvatu mikap poruttam. teṉ - kiḻakku īrāṉiṉ teppē yāhyā (Tepe Yahya) vaṇika niliyam 5000 - 3000 kimu āṇṭukaḷil mākkal viṟpaṉi miyamāka iruntatu. mākkal 67% cilikkā, 33% makṉīciyā (Magnesia) aṭaṅkiyatu. 	Some aspects of the Harappan Technological tradition: K.K. Bhan, M. Vidale, & J.M. Kenoyer - Indian archeology in Retrospect: Edited by S. Settar and Ravi Korisettar: Manohar Press: New Delhi (2002)
9. ēṉiya āciyanākarikam etaṟkum illāta toṭarcciyāṉa 5000 āṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa āparaṇat tayārippup pārampariyam intiyāvukku uṇṭu. intiyāvum cuṟṟiyuḷḷa nāṭukaḷum āparaṇat tayārippukkup poruttamāṉa ulōkaṅkaḷ / ariya kaṟkaḷiyum maṭṭumaṉṟi, ciṟanta kaliñarkaḷik koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. cintu āparaṇa uṟpattiyāḷarkaḷē ivarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōṭikaḷ. cintuk kalikaḷip pakuppāyvu ceytāl avi mikappala ākum. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil  tīṭṭiya ceṭi, koṭi, malar, vilaṅku ākiyavi iyaṟkikkāṭci cārntavi. eṇṇikki, kalinayam ivaṟṟilum cintu muttirikaḷ iraṇṭām iṭampeṟum.
10(i). cintunākarika kālattil maṉitarkaḷiyō, vilaṅkukaḷiyō acappil / appaṭiyē uṇmiyāṉa uyaram, parumaṉil ciṟpamāka amikkum pārampariyam illi. ituvari appaṭi nija vaṭivam akaḻāyvil kiṭikkavilli. maṉita vaṭivaṅkaḷ aritākavum ciṟiyatākavumē ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. iti  `ciṟu vaṭivak kali Miniature art eṉpar vimarcakar. harappā - mokeñco-tarōvil kiṭitta maṉita uruvaṅkaḷ cila cemī. uyaramtāṉ; āṉāl avi ciṟanta nayamikkavi. uyaram ivvaḷavu kuṟivāka iruppatu ēṉeṉṟu teriyavilli.
(ii). ciṟiya uruva āṇ - peṇ vaṭivaṅkaḷ (maṇṇil ceytu cuṭṭavi) kimu 7000 mutal cintunākarika iṟutik kālam varit toṭarkiṉṟaṉa. annākarittiṉ toṉmiyāṉa, iṟutivari nīṭitta kalivaṭivaṅkaḷ ivi. mokeñco-tarōvil kiṭitta peṇpommiyiṉ iṭuppil mikac ciṟiya (nīccal uṭi)yik kāṇalām. kaḻuttu niṟiya āparaṇaṅkaḷ; tōḷil toṅkum āparaṇaṅkaḷ; aṭivayiṟu vari āparaṇaṅkaḷ ivaṟṟik kāṇalām. mekarkār mutal mokeñco-tarō variyāṉa maṇ pāvi nallāḷ payaṇam ippaṭi amikiṟatu.
(iii). `mekarkār - mokeñco-tarōvil 5000 varuṭaṅkaḷ toṭarntu nilaviya maṇ pāvikaḷ mūlam aṟivaṉa:
(a) kalip poruḷāka iruntālum immaṇ pommikaḷ ētō oru vitattil mata vaḻipāṭṭuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavi.
(b)  mukam, uṭal, uruvam ākiyavaṟṟiliruntu cintupparappil vāḻnta makkaḷ pala iṉattavar, pala paṇpāṭu cārntavar
(c) antakkāla makkaḷ enta āparaṇaṅkaḷi aṇintaṉarō avaṟṟiṉ nakalkaḷittām pāvikaḷil nām kāṇkiṟōm. cilar kaḻuttil iṟukkamāka uḷḷa āparaṇaṅkaḷiyum aṇintirukkalām.
(iv). nīḷamāṉa kaḻuttit taṉiaḻakākak karutum paḻiya iṉaṅkaḷ iṉṟum uḷḷaṉa. miyaṉmāriṉ (parmā) kāyaṉ lākvi (Kayan Lahwi) peṇkaḷiṉ kaḻutti nīṭṭuvippataṟkāka iru vayatuc ciṟumiyāka irukkumpōtē kaḻuttu vaḷiyaṅkaḷi aṇiyat toṭaṅkuvar. ivarkaḷi `nīḷak kaḻuttu iṉam eṉpar. teṉṉāpirikkāviṉ nīṭapelē (Nedebele) iṉappeṇkaḷum kaḻutti nīṭṭuvikkum āparaṇaṅkaḷi cempu - veṇkalattil ceytu aṇikiṉṟaṉar.
11(i). harappā, mokeñco-tarō nakaraṅkaḷi `iraṭṭi nakaraṅkaḷ (Twin cities) eṉṟu aḻippar; avaṟṟiṟ kiṭiyil pēraḷavu oṟṟumi iruppiṉum, ciṟpak kali cārntu niṟiya vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu; itu paṟṟi pocēl tarum kuṟippu varumāṟu:
(1) harappāvil (muḻumi peṟāta) maṉita vaṭivaṅkaḷākiya  civappu jāpar cāmpal niṟam kal (torso) iraṇṭu kiṭittaṉa. ivi pōṉṟa nayamāṉa kalippaṭivac celvaṅkaḷ mokeñco-tarōvil kiṭikka villi. 
(2) naṭaṉa mātuc cilikaḷ iraṇṭum, mēlum patiṉoru mukkiya uruvaṅkaḷum mokeñco-tarōvil kiṭittaṉa; avi pōṉṟavi harappāvil kiṭikkavilli.
(3) harappā civappu jāpar uruvamum, mokeñco-tarō naṭaṉa mātu veṇkalac ciliyum cintu nākarikattiṉ tali ciṟanta paṭippukaḷ ākum. mutal uruvattiṉ ciṟpattiṟaṉ cintu muttirikaḷ cilavaṟṟiṉ tiṟaṉ pōṉṟa nayam vāyntatu.
(ii). harappā civappu jāpar (torso) uyaram 9.5 cemī; kālkaḷ iraṇṭum toṭikkukkīḻ uṭintuviṭṭaṉa; taliyum iru kikaḷum avvāṟē. ivvuruvam āṭikaḷiṉṟi nirvāṇamāka muṉpuṟam kāṭṭi niṟkiṟatu; āṇ uṟuppit teḷivākac cetukkiyuḷḷaṉar. tali, kikaḷi muṇṭattuṭaṉ (torso) poruttiṭa tuḷi iraṇṭu pōṭṭuḷḷaṉar. tōḷiṉ muṟpakutiyil vaṭṭa vaṭivil āḻam kuṟinta paḷḷaṅkaḷit tuḷik karuvi mūlam ceytuḷḷaṉar; etaṟkāka eṉṟu teriyavilli. (āṭikaḷ aṇivippataṟkāka eṉṟu cilar colluvatu cari eṉat tōṉṟavilli). ciliyiṉ mulik kāmpu (nipple) irukkum iṭattil kuḻi ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aṅku vēṟu niṟak kal poruttak karuti iruntirukkalām. (itik kaṇṭeṭuttavar em.e. vāṭ; 1928-29il periyatāṉiyak kaḷañciyattiṟkut teṟkil uḷḷa, vaṭṭa vaṭivamāṉa, tāṉiyaṅkaḷip piritteṭukkum mēṭikaḷukku arukil kaṇṭupiṭittār) ataṉ kālam kimu 2200 - 1900 ākum. ciṟiya ciṟpa vaṭivaṅkaḷip `pākam pākamākac ceytu poruttum vaḻakkam potuvāka intiya ciṟpa pārampariyattil illi. mēlum jāpar kallil ciṟpam ceyyum vaḻamiyum illi.
(iii). nalla catip piṭippuḷḷa intiya iḷiñaṉiṉ uṭali  nērttiyāka paṭam piṭittuk kāṭṭum vaṭivam civappu jāpar  ciṟpam eṉpar kali vimarcakarkaḷ. maṉita uṭalamippic citikkāmal mēmpaṭṭa ciṟpakkali nuṭpam kivarappeṟṟa vallavaṉ inta ciliyic ceytuḷḷāṉ. 
(iv). kirēkka ciṟpakkaliyil, maṉita uṭalukku nikarāṉa vaṭivam iyaṟkiyilō, ceyaṟkiyilō kāṇa muṭiyātu eṉṟa tattuvam nilavukiṟatu. carācari āṇiṉ uyarattil (āṇ kuṟi naṉṟākat teriyum vitamāṉa) nirvāṇac cilikaḷik kirēkkarkaḷ kimu 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉ/piṉ kālattil ceytaṉar. āṉāl cintuveḷic ciṟpa viṟpaṉṉar kirēkkari munti viṭṭārkaḷ. orē vēṟupāṭu: kirēkkarkaḷuṭiyatu muḻu uyaram, intiyaruṭiyatu mikacciṟiyatu. `naṉṟāka vaṭivamitta āṇ vaṭivam cātāraṇa maṉitaṉākavum, uyarnta kaṭavuḷākavum irukkalām eṉṟu paṇṭik kirēkkarkaḷ eṇṇi iraṇṭiyum oṉṟupōla nōkkiṉar.
12. intiya ciṟpak kaliyiṉ tōṟṟam maṇ pāvikaḷil ārampittu mokeñco-tarō - harappā civappu jāpar  cili, naṭaṉa mātu veṇkalac cili ākiyavaṟṟil ucca vaṭivam peṟṟatu. cintu nākarikam vīḻcci aṭinta kālattiṟkum muriyap pēraracu kimu 321- 185 tōṉṟiya kālattiṟkum iṭiyil intiya ciṟpakkaliyil veṟṟiṭam uḷḷatu. 
13.  jāpar kal uruvam kiṭitta iṭattiliruntu 150 mīṭṭar vaṭakkil naṭaṉamāṭum āṇiṉ karumiyāṉa cāmpal kallil vaṭitta cili torso vi vāṭ kaṇṭeṭuttār. ataṉ kāl iraṇṭum uṭintu tolintu viṭṭaṉa. taliyum, kikaḷum  (poruntu tuḷikaḷ pommiyil iruntālum) kiṭikkavilli. intac cili naṭaṉamāṭum civaṉōṭu toṭarpuṭiyatu eṉṟu mārṣal, vīlar ākiyōr karutiṉar. jāpar (uṭinta) cili pōṉṟa nayam illāviṭṭālum intac ciliyum uyirōṭṭam uḷḷatu eṉpar.
veṇkala naṭaṉamātu cili
14(i). cintuveḷic cilikaḷ aṉittilum mikavum kavarcciyāṉatu (10.8 cemī. uyara) `veṇkala naṭaṉamātu (Bronze Dancing girl). iti mokeñco-tarōvil teṉ-mēṟku kuṭiyiruppup pakutiyil 1926-27il cāhṉi kaṇṭeṭuttār. ciliyiṉ uṭalil catip piṭippu illi. kucci pōṉṟa anta vaṭivattil kalivimarcakarkaḷ pala ciṟappamcaṅkaḷik kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar:
tali caṟṟē piṉṉukkuc carintum, iṭatu kāl muṭṭiyil maṭintum, valatu kāl piṉpuṟam ūṉṟiyum, avaḷ āṭiyiṉṟi niṟkiṟāḷ,  valatu ki maṭintu, iṭuppup piṉpuṟattil ūṉṟiyuḷḷatu; iṭatuki muṉṉukkuc ceṉṟu iṭatu toṭiyit toṭukiṉṟatu; vayatu 15-16 irukkalām. kaḻuttil oru māli; iṭatu kiyil cumār 25 vaḷiyalkaḷum valatu kiyil 4 vaḷiyalkaḷum - (iraṇṭu maṇikkaṭṭil; iraṇṭu muḻaṅkikkumēl).
(ii). ivi aṉittum aṭaṅkiya intac ciṉṉa vaṭivam orē vārppil (Lost wax Process) mutalil meḻukuc cili; ataṉi urukiyōṭac ceytapiṉ kiṭṭum veṟṟiṭattil veṇkalam eṉṉum muṟiyil ceytatu.  taliyil iṟukkamaṟṟa koṇṭi, avaḷ niṟkum nili uṇmiyil naṭaṉamāṭukiṟāḷā? eṉṟu aiyappaṭa vikkiṟatu. paṇṭic ciṟpa vaṭivamippu varalāṟṟil ivaḷip pōl iṉṉoruttiyi nāṉ kaṇṭatilli eṉpar vīlar (1973). intiya ciṟpa pārampariyattil intac cili pukaḻ peṟṟatu. 2004-05il (mokeñco-tarōvukkuc cila ki.mī tūrattil uḷḷa - hariyāṉā mākāṇa-) pirāṇā (Bhirrana) pāṉi ōṭṭil ivaḷuṭiya vaṭivam poṟikkappaṭṭiruntatu.
15. iṭatu kiyi iṭuppil ūṉṟi, valatu kiyi uṭalōṭu oṭṭi vittu nērāka niṟkum iṉṉoru naṭaṉap peṇ veṇkalac ciliyi 1930-31il mekkē avarkaḷ mokeñco-tarō DK-G pakutiyil kaṇṭeṭuttār. `vaṭivamippil itu taraṅkuṟintatu eṉkiṟār mekkē. maṇṇukku aṭiyil kiṭantapaṭiyāl atu citintum viṭṭatu. naṭaṉak kali mokeñco-tarōvil uṉṉata iṭam vakittati ivviru cilikaḷum kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. enta vaki naṭaṉa pārampariyattic cērntavarkaḷ eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu. ciliyiṉ mukattiyum taṭitta utaṭukaḷiyum ātāramākak koṇṭu avaḷ palucci iṉappeṇ eṉpar mekkē. vēṟu cilar avaḷ tirāviṭa / āppirikka / tol - ātirēliyāp paḻaṅkuṭi ākiya mūṉṟil oṉṟic cērntavaḷ eṉkiṉṟaṉar.



pūcāri maṉṉaṉ cili
16(i). naṭaṉa mātuc cili pōlavē pukaḻ peṟṟa `pūcāri maṉṉaṉ (Priest - King) cili mokeñco-tarō DK-B pakutiyil, catukkam 2, aṟi 1 il 1.37 mīṭṭar āḻattil 1925 - 1926 tīkṣit  mīṭṭatu ākum. atu mokeñco-tarō varalāṟṟiṉ piṟkālap pakuti cārntatu. aṭippākam uṭinta icciliyiṉ uyaram 17.5 ce.mī akalam 11 ce.mī. itu pūcāri maṉṉaṉ cili eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭālum pūcāri eṉpataṟkō, maṉṉaṉ eṉpataṟkō ātāram illi! itu (ṭiyaṭiṭ Steatite) mākkallil ceytu kuṟinta veppattil cūḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. 
(ii). inta uruvam caṭṭi aṇiyavilli. orē kāmpil mūvaṭṭa ili koṇṭa vēlippāṭum, iṭiyiṭiyē taṉivaṭṭa alaṅkarippumuḷḷa pōrvi oṉṟu ciliyiṉ iṭatu tōḷukku mēlāka vantu, valatu puyattiṟkuk kīḻākac celkiṟatu mutukiyum maṟittapaṭi. ivvuruvattiṟkuk kuṟukiya, nīḷat tāṭiyum, muṟṟāka mīci maḻikkappaṭṭa taṭitta mēlutaṭum uḷḷaṉa. pōrviyil civappu varṇam tīṭṭiya aṭiyāḷam terikiṟatu. pōrvi vaṭṭaṅkaḷ, vicēṭat tuḷikkaruvi mūlam tuḷiyiṭṭu attuḷikaḷ piṉṉar uḷi mūlam cīrākkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; etaṟkāka ippaṭic ceytaṉar eṉṟu teriyavilli.
(iii). akaṉṟa mukamum, paranta neṟṟiyum koṇṭa icciliyil  neṟṟip paṭṭamum (nīḷ vaṭṭa vaṭivil) poṟittuḷḷatu. paṭṭatti nāṭāvil kōrttu, nāṭāviṉ iru muṉikaḷit talip piṉpuṟattil muṭiccup pōṭṭuḷḷaṉar. talimayir oṭṭa vāri (naṭu vakiṭuṭaṉ) piṉpuṟamākac celkiṟatu. talip piṉpuṟam taṭṭiyāka iruppatāl ciṟpi, koṇṭiyit taṉiyākac ceytu poruttak karuti irukkalām. neṟṟippaṭṭa nāṭāviṉ piṉpuṟamuṭicciṉ iru muṉikaḷum aḻakuṟat toṅkukiṉṟaṉa. neṟṟippaṭṭam kaṭṭum vaḻakkam paḻantamiḻariṭam iruntati niṉivu kūra vēṇṭum. āticcanallūr parampu āyvil neṟṟip paṭṭaṅkaḷ pala veḷippaṭṭaṉa. (Catalogue of the pre-historic antiquities from Adichanallur and Perumbir: A. Rea (Madras) 1915).
(iv). ciliyiṉ valatu ki muḻaṅkikkuc caṟṟu mēlē uṭintu, uṭinta pakuti kiṭikkavilli. iṭatu kiyiṉ mēl pakutiyi pōrvi mūṭiyuḷḷatu. valatu kiyiṉ mēṟ pākattil ciṟiya paṭṭam kaṭṭiyuḷḷatu. ciliyiṉ mūkku uṭintuḷḷatāl mukattic cariyāka uṇara muṭiyavilli. kaṇkaḷ iraṇṭum oṭuṅkalākavum nīṇṭaviyākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa. (kaṇkaḷiṉ uṭpuṟattil viḻiyi pōṉṟa kal vikkappaṭṭirukkalām. avaṟṟil oṉṟu kiṭittuḷḷatu). kātukaḷ ovvoṉṟiṉ kīḻp pakutiyil tuḷikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kaḻuttumāli allatu vēṟu aṇikalaṉ poruttuvataṟkākat tuḷikaḷ pōṭappaṭṭirukkalām. itu arikuṟiyākac ceyyap paṭṭa cili eṉṟu cilarum, mika nērttiyākac ceyta cili eṉṟu vēṟucilarum kūṟuvar. karācci tēciya paḻamporuḷ kāppakattil iccili kaṇṇāṭip peṭṭiyil uḷḷatu.
(v). kimu 4000 aḷavil iraṇṭām pātik kālattil teṉ  mecapotāmi yāvil uruk (Uruk) nakara nākarika āṭciyāḷari  pūcāri - maṉṉaṉ (Priest - king) eṉṟu tolliyalāḷar kuṟittaṉar. āṭci, matam iraṇṭum orē napar kaṭṭuppāṭṭil iruntaṉa eṉpatu poruḷ. kirīṭ tīviṉ miṉōvā nākarikat tilum aracaṉtāṉ talimip pūcāri. teṉ mecapotāmiyāvilum pūcāri maṉṉaṉ cilikaḷ iraṇṭu kiṭittaṉa. atiloṉṟu pāri nakar luvrē (Louvre) paḻam poruḷ kāppakattil uḷḷatu.
mokeñco-tarōvil kiṭitta mēlum cila maṉita vaṭivac ciṟpaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ varumāṟu:
uṭkārntirukkum maṉitaṉ (Seated man L - 950)
17(i). ācaṉattil amarntirukkum inta taliyaṟṟa ciṟpam periya nīccal mēṭṭiṉ teṉ puṟattē ‘L’ eṉṟu kuṟitta pakutiyil kiṭittatu. atu mokeñco-tarō piṟkālattiṟku uriyatu; kaṇṭeṭuttavar mekkē. ivvaṭivattiṉ uyaram 29.2 cemī. pūcāri- maṉṉaṉ ciliyil pōrttiyatu pōṉṟa pōrvi intac ciliyilum `iṭatu tōḷiṉ mēṟpakutiyil iruntu valak kiyiṉ kīḻppuṟam vari mūṭiyuḷḷatu. ciliyiṉ iṭatu ki iṭatu muḻaṅkālilum, valatu ki valatu muḻaṅkālilum vikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. `piṉṉiya nīṇṭa talimayirkkaṟṟi valatu pakkat tōḷiṉ mēlākacceṉṟu mutukup puṟattil toṅkukiṟatu, ciṟiya koṇṭi oṉṟum iruppatu pōl terikiṟatu. ciṟpi carivara itic cetukkavilli.
(ii). intac cili oṉṟu maṭṭumē naṉku pārkkakkūṭiya vitattil amarntu irukkiṟatu. (pūcāri - maṉṉaṉ ciliyiṉ muḻu vaṭivam kiṭittiruntāl atuvum ippaṭittāṉ amarntirukkum eṉalām). pocēl muṭivu viṉōtamāṉatu:
ippaṭi iṭatu tōḷukku mēlāl eṭuttu, valatu kikkuk kīḻāl pōrviyik koṇṭu varum vaḻakkam mattiya āciya nākarika vaḻakkam. vaṭa āpkāṉitāṉ, kiḻakku turkmeṉitāṉ, teṉ upekkitāṉ, mēṟku tajikkitāṉ aṭaṅkiya piratēcattil kimu 2300 - 1700il nilaviya veṇkala ūḻi nākarikattiṉ tākkam āṉatu, piṟkāla mokeñco-tarōvil iruntatu; ittākkam kāraṇamākat tāṉ pōrvi aṇiyum vaḻakkam cintup pakutiyilum ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām. ivviraṇṭit tavira vēṟu enta (cintuveḷic) ciliyilum pōrvi ippaṭi aṇiyappaṭavilli. itu vātattiṟkuriyatu.
`kaṟār pārvikkāra maṉitaṉ (The Stern man L - 898)
18. immaṉitat talic ciṟpatti mekkē ‘L’ pakutiyil 60 cemī āḻattil eṭuttār; uyaram 19.7 mī. ; tali mayir kuṭumipōla uḷḷatu. mukattil `eḷḷum koḷḷum veṭippatu pōl kaṇṭipput terikiṟatu. tāṭi maṉitaṉ tali (Bearded man’s head) mokeñco-tarō kuṭiyiruppup pakutiyil vīṭu 1, HR -A pakutiyil 1925 - 26 il āṇṭil oru vīṭṭil kiṭittatu; itu vīṭāka illāmal kōyilākavum irukkalām. taliyiṉ uyaram 17.5 cemī. (pūcāri maṉṉaṉ ciliyiṉ uyaramum inta taliyiṉ uyaramum camam) pūcāri maṉṉaṉ ciliyic ceyta ciṟpiyē intat taliyiyum ceytirukkalāmeṉak karutukiṉṟaṉar; tāṭi maṉitaṉ taliyil kuṭumi irukkiṟatu; kuṭumiyik kayiṟu pōṉṟa tuṇiyāl kaṭṭi, ulōka ūciyum kuṭumiyil cerukap paṭṭuḷḷatu. nāṭiyil ciṟiya tāṭi  kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. mēl utaṭu maḻikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. maṉitat talikku etir māṟāka itic ceytirukkalām. kaṇkaḷ naṉku tiṟantuḷḷaṉa. (māṟāka pūcāri - maṉṉaṉ kaṇkaḷ tiyāṉattil iruppatu pōl `pāti mūṭi uḷḷaṉa). pūcāri - maṉṉaṉiṉ mūkku pōla intac ciliyiṉ mūkkum uṭintuḷḷatu!
cintuveḷi muttirikaḷiṉ aḻakiyal ciṟappu:  (Aesthetic quality of Indus seals)

19. cintuveḷi aḻakuk kalikaḷil muttiri uṟpattiyum oṉṟeṉalām. muttirikaḷi vēṟu kāriyattukkāka  ceytaṉar. avaṟṟil kalinayam miḷirvatik kāṇalām. cintuk kaliñarkaḷ cilikaḷi mikac ciṟiya vaṭivaṅkaḷākavē vaṭittaṉar. eṉavē muttirikaḷil poṟitta vaṭivaṅkaḷukkut taṉi matippaḷittaṉar. cintuveḷi muttirikaḷiṉ parappu 2.5 catura ce.mī mutal 5 catura ce.mī. pacupati muttiri eṉṟu jāṉ mārṣal kuṟitta muttiriyiṉ parappu 3.5 catura cemī. āṉāl ataṉ kali nayamum (piṉṉaṇiyil uḷḷa arttaṅkaḷum) iṉṟu vari āyvu ceyyappaṭuvaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa.
20. cintu muttirikaḷ puriyāta putirākavum, kavarntiḻukkum taṉmi vāyntaṉavākavum iruppatāka kali varalāṟṟaṟiñar (Art historians) kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avi kali nuṇukkat tiṟaṉukku aṭiyāḷamākavum amikiṉṟaṉa; cīrāka amiya vēṇṭum eṉpatil mikak kavaṉañ celuttiṉar. aṉittu muttirikaḷiyum potuvāka āyvu ceytāl vēṟupaṭṭa iraṇṭu pāṇikaḷ teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa: oru vakiyil meṉmiyāṉa aḻaku miḷirkiṟatu; iṉṉoru vakiyil kōṭukaḷ, vaḷivukaḷ iṇinta kaṇitamuṟip paṭimaṅkaḷ mēlōṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉa. cintu makkaḷiṉ mata nampikki, kaṟpaṉi, paṇpāṭṭu aṭiyāḷam ivaṟṟiyellām muttirikaḷ tāṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉa. - A concise history of Indian Art: Royc. Craven.
21. muttiriyiṉ vaṭivam ciṟitāyiṉum tatrūpamāṉa  uyirōṭṭatti ivi veḷippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu vīlar 1968il kūṟiṉār. muttirikaḷil poṟitta vilaṅkukaḷiṉ aṅka vaṭivaṅkaḷum, acivukaḷum uṇmikkuk koñcamum picakillāmal amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. vāl pakutiyi, kuṭṭiyāka / nīḷamāka / aṭip pakutiyil mayirk kuñcam iruppatupōl eṉṟellām tatrūpamākak kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. entac ciṟu  vivaramum viṭupaṭātavāṟu, ciramappaṭṭup poṟit tuḷḷaṉar. cīpū erutiṉ kālkaḷ, atu niṟkum nili, pārkkum pārvi, kaḻuttuc cati eṉpaṉa mikavum nuṭpamāka cittarikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cintu makkaḷ kompukku mukkiyattuvam koṭuttaṉar. tammip periya `kompaṉkaḷākak karutiṉar pōlum! muttirikaḷiṉ kompukaḷ vaḷintum, neḷintum, curuṇṭum mūṉṟu vakiyākak kāṭci tarukiṉṟaṉa.
22. muttiri cipu  (zebu) 337jāṉ mārṣali viyappūṭṭiyatu: ciṟanta ciṟpi kaḷik kēṭṭa poḻutu cīpu erutip poṟitta ciṟpi civappu jāpar kal ciliyiyum vaṭikka vallavaṉā yiruntiruppāṉ eṉṟaṉar. (uṇmiyil illāta) muḻuk kaṟpaṉi vaṭivaṅkaḷum muttirikaḷil uḷḷaṉa. iraṇṭu kompu, nīṇṭa vāl, vilaṅkiṉattiṉ pātam ivi koṇṭa nirvāṇap peṇ orutti `kompu vitta pulikaḷ iraṇṭuṭaṉ mōtum kāṭci poṟitta muttiri mārṣalukku 1931il āṇṭu kiṭittatu. iṉṉoru muttiriyil kuṟuṅ kompu ovvoṉṟuḷḷa `kāṇṭā mirukaṅkaḷ (Unicorns) iraṇṭu marattukkuḷḷiruntu veḷiyē varukiṉṟaṉa. (vaḻakkamāṉa mēṟpakkattil poṟippati viṭṭu immuttiriyil aṭippākattil eḻuttukkaḷ poṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa)
23. ēṟattāḻa erutupōṉṟa uṭalum neṟṟiyil muṉṉōkki nīṇṭa curuḷ kompum uḷḷa vilaṅki `yuṉikārṉ  (kāṇṭāmirukam) eṉṟu aḻippar. mikavum nērttiyākap paḻutiṉṟi uḷḷa  mokeñco-tarō muttiri HR 743, harappā muttiri HR 9 ākiyavaṟṟil ivvilaṅku poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu cintu nākarika makkaḷ kaṟpaṉi vilaṅkākat tāṉ irukka vēṇṭum. tolliyal āyvil eṅkum itaṉuṭiya elumpukaḷ kiṭikkavēyilli. mēṟku airōppiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil inta vilaṅku paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ uṇṭu. ivvilaṅku paṟṟiya ceyti cintup pakutiyil tōṉṟi, īrāṉ, kirēkkam vaḻiyāka airōppā varic  ceṉṟirukkalām eṉpar ilakkiya āyvāḷar. inta vilaṅku intiyāviṟku uriyatu eṉpatil aiyamilli. irāmāyaṇa, makāpārata itikācaṅkaḷil taliyil oṟṟik kompuḷḷa riṣyaciruṅkaṉ eṉṟa oru iḷiñaṉ varukiṟāṉ. `(riṣya = kompu).
24. cintu muttirikaḷ cilavaṟṟil vilaṅku vaṭivam oṉṟi ūrvalamākac cumantu cellum kāṭci uḷḷatu. pacupati muttiriyip pōl ituvum cintu makkaḷiṉ mata nampikki cārntatākalām. ekiptu, mecapotāmiyā, kirīṭ nākarikaṅkaḷil (uṭal valu, iṉavirutti ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ciṉṉamāka) eruti vaṇaṅkum vaḻakkam paṇṭu iruntatu. kirīṭ tīvil nācā (Knossus) araṇmaṉiyil māṭṭut taliyum ataṉuṭiya iru kompum puṉitamākap pēṇappaṭṭaṉa. ekiptiyar api (Apis) eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ poli kāḷiyi vaṇaṅkiṉar. mecapotāmiyarkaḷ erutukkompu iraṇṭum iṇintu piṟiccantira (Crescent moon) vaṭivam eṭuppatāka nampiṉar. civaṉuṭiya vākaṉam nanti  (erutu) . paṉṉiru rācikaḷilum oṉṟu iṭapam ākum.
25. pala vilaṅkukaḷiṉ uṭaṟ pākaṅkaḷi oṉṟiṇittu putiya `kaṟpaṉi vilaṅki uruvākki muttirikaḷil poṟittuḷḷaṉar. kaṟpaṉi vilaṅkāṉa kāṇṭāmirukattiṟku yāṉit tumpikkiyip poruttip pārttiruk kiṟārkaḷ; ataṉuṭiya mukam kiṭṭattaṭṭa maṉita mukam pōl uḷḷatu; kūrmiyākap pārkkumpōtu tantaṅkaḷ terikiṉṟaṉa. vēṟu cila muttiri kaḷil ataṟkup pulikkālkaḷ poruttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. (Femd Seal no. 411). mokeñco-tarō muttiri oṉṟil pāti maṉita uṭalum pāti erumi uṭalum koṇṭa irāṭca vilaṅku oṉṟu puliyit tākkum kāṭci uḷḷatu. (cumēriya kilkāmeṣ itikācattil peṇ teyvam aruṟu Aruru ippaṭippaṭṭa payaṅkara vilaṅku oṉṟi uruvākki  parākkiramacāli kilkāmeṣ maṉṉaṉik kolvataṟku ēvukiṟāḷ. mokeñco-tarō muttirip poṟippiṟkum itaṟkum toṭarpu irukkalām eṉpar kirakari pocēl). 
26. iṉṉumoru cintuveḷi muttiriyil oruvaṉ taṉatu kikaḷ ovvoṉṟilum oru puliyip piṭittirukkum kāṭci poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. (puṟanāṉūṟṟil varum iruṅkōvēḷ oliyal kaṇṇip pulikaṭimāl = puṟanāṉūṟu 201 - 202] kilkāmeṣ varalāṟṟil avaṉum kikku oru ciṅkattip piṭittuk kollum nikaḻcci varukiṟatu. cintu muttiriyil ciṅkattiṟkup patilākap puli poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. (cintu veḷiyil ciṅkam vāḻntiruk kiṟatu; anta makkaḷukkum ciṅkam paṟṟit teriyum; āṉāl oru muttiriyil kūṭac ciṅkattik kāṭṭavilli. mavuriya cāmrācciya kālattil kimu 321-185 -tāṉ ciṅkattiṟku antatu tarappaṭṭatu. acōkaṉ  ciṅkattit taṉatu tūṇkaḷiṉ ucciyil amittāṉ) 
27. cintu makkaḷukkup `pēy - picācu nampikki iruntatu. 1931 il mokeñco-tarōvil mārṣal eṭutta muttiriyil picācu vaṭivam poṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. am muttiriyiṉ iru pakkattilum eḻuttup poṟippuṭaṉ vilaṅkum uḷḷatu. iru pakkattilum naṉṟāka nimirntuniṟkum picācu vaṭivaṅkaḷ ōrattil teḷivākap poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ataṟkut taliyil iru kompukaḷ; iṭuppil nīḷamāṉa vāl uṇṭu; atu taṉatu valatu kiyi uyartti yāriyō aḻippatupōl uḷḷatu. āḻamāṉa mata nampikki uḷḷa cintu makkaḷukku pēy picācu nampikkiyum iruntataṟku immuttiri cāṉṟākum.
28. erutum pacuvum cintu makkaḷ vāḻvil mukkiya iṭam vakittaṉa. cūriyak kaṭavuḷiṉ aṭiyāḷam erutu. The bull was the symbol of the Sun - the father. cūriyaṉit tantiyākavum pūmiyit tāyākavum karutiṉar. pacu pūmiyiṉ aṭiyāḷamākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. pūmiyit tāyk kaṭavuḷāka pōṟṟum vaḻakkam (pūmit tāy) vēḷāṇ camūkaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟil uṇṭu. ekiptunākarikat tāyakkaṭavuḷiṉ peyar hatōr (Hathor), avarkaḷukkut tamatu tāykkaṭavuḷum pacuvum oṉṟē. They equated their Mother Goddess with cows.
29. cintu makkaḷiṉ paṇṭi - mummūrttikaḷ - cūriyaṉ, pūmi, cantiraṉ ākum. cumēriyariṉ mata nampikkiyilum itu pōṉṟa mummūrttik kōṭpāṭu iruntatu: cūriyaṉi utu (Utu), pūmiyi niṉkurcak (Ninhursag) cantiraṉi ciṉ (Sin) eṉṟu cumēriyar aḻittaṉar. ekiptiyarkaḷiṭamum itu pōṉṟa nampikki iruntatu; avarkaḷ cūriyaṉi amuṉ (Amun), pūmiyi māṭ (Maat), cantiraṉik kuṉcu (Khunsu) eṉṟaḻittaṉar. kirīṭ tīviṉ miṉōvaṉ nākarikattilum mummūrtti nampikki iruntatu.
30(i). caṉku-taro muttiri 13il erutu oṉṟu maṇṇil kiṭakkum peṇṇip puṇarum kāṭci uḷḷatu; peṇ kuṟiyum erutiṉ āṇ kuṟiyum tulliyamākap poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; antap peṇ puṇarcciyi virumpuvatu pōlavum tōṉṟukiṟatu. cittirip putuāṇṭu ovvoṉṟum cūriyaṉ pūmiyip puṇarvatōṭu toṭaṅkukiṟatu eṉṟa cintu makkaḷiṉ nampikki mekkē 1943il caṉku -tarovil  peṟṟa muttiriyil veḷippaṭukiṟatu. cūriyaṉi erutākak kaṇṭa makkaḷ `pūmiyi uḻutu payiriṭum ceyali puṇarcciyāka uruvakittaṉar. [putuvaruṭat toṭakkatti cumēriyarkaḷ akiṭṭu (Akitu) viḻāveṭuttuk koṇṭāṭiṉar. ekiptiyarkaḷ putu varuṭa viḻāviṉ peyar opeṭ (Opet). cumēriya nakar uruk akaḻvukaḷil kiṭitta cāṭiyil akiṭṭu viḻā koṇṭāṭum kāṭci uḷḷatu)
(ii). cūriyaṉ tantik kaṭavuḷ, avaṉ vaṭivam erutu. pūmi tāyk kaṭavuḷ, avaḷ vaṭivam pacu. uḻavu vitippuk kāriyaṅkaḷ, cūriyaṉum pūmiyum puṇarvatu pōṉṟaṉa eṉṟu nampiya makkaḷ mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu muttiriyip paṭittaṉar.
(iii). cumēriyarkaḷ erutic cantira kulamākak karutiṉar. (The bull is lunar in Mesopotamian culture.) cintuveḷiyiṉarō erutic cūriya kulamāka varittaṉar. eppaṭi iruppiṉum varalāṟṟukku muntiya paṇpāṭukaḷil māttiramalla iṉṟum matam, matam cārnta nampikkikaḷ, viḻākkaḷil erutukku mukkiya iṭam uḷḷatu. miṉōvar kirīṭ tīvil putu varuṭak koṇṭāṭṭattiṉ pōtu iḷavayatu āṇum peṇṇum ēṟu taḻuvum vīra viḷiyāṭṭi araṅkēṟṟiṉar. miṉōvā cuvar ōviyaṅkaḷ, maṭpāṇṭa ōviyaṅkaḷil akkāṭcikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ekiptiyarkaḷ  teyvaṅkaḷukkup pala peyar iṭṭu vaṇaṅkiṉar. kep (Geb) eṉpatu vāṉaveḷik kaṭavuḷ, naṭ (Nut) eṉpatu pūmik kaṭavuḷ. vāṉaveḷiyiyi āṇākavum pūmiyip peṇṇākavum ekiptiyar kaṇṭaṉar.
(iv). ekiptiyarkaḷ eruti api (Apis) eṉṟu peyariṭṭu vaṇaṅkiṉar. taṅkaḷ varalāṟṟiṉ muṟpakutiyil api eruti  ptā (ptah) kaṭavuḷiṉ vaṭivamākavum piṟpakutiyil aciri (Osiris) kaṭavuḷiṉ vaṭivamākavum vaṇaṅkiṉar. ekiptiṉ nīṇṭa  (cila āyiram āṇṭukāla) varalāṟu muḻuvatilum ērāḷamāṉa perum erutukaḷ kōyilkaḷil vaḷarttu vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. kōyil pūcārikaḷ aḻakiya erutukaḷit terivu ceytu kōyilil vaḷarttaṉar. kōyil erutu cettāl `mammi ākki oṟṟik kal pēḻikaḷil vittu aṭakkañceytaṉar. 1851il akaḻāyvu naṭattiya akatē mēriyaṭ (Auguste Mariette) cakkārā (Saqqara) eṉṟa iṭattil erutu mammikaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittār.
(v). iṉṟu turukkiyil uḷḷa kiḻakku aṉatōliyā akaḻvāyvukaḷil kimu 8000 cārnta māṭṭuttali elumpum ataṉ iraṇṭu kompum kiṭittaṉa. avi vaṇakkap poruḷākap pātukākkappaṭṭavi. cempuk kāla makkaḷ (Chalocolithic people) iraṇṭām rācikkuriya naṭcattiraṅkaḷi erutu vaṭivil kaṇṭaṉar. ilattīṉ moḻiyil tāra (Taurus) eṉṟāl erutu. veṇkala ūḻi makkaḷ (Bronze age people 4000 - 1700 BC) vacanta kālattil toṭaṅkum putu varuṭa aṭiyāḷamāka erutik kaṇṭaṉar. (The bull was seen in the constellation Taurus by the Chalcolithics, and marked the New Year at Springtide by the Bronze Age people of 4000 - 1700 BC). cintu nākarika makkaḷ putiya kaṟkālam, cempuk kālam, veṇkala kālam mūṉṟilum toṭarntu vāḻntavarkaḷ 


(ā) cintunākarikap pakuti makkaḷ matam
31(i). periya kōyiṟ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ, kaṭavuḷ cilikaḷ, tiraḷāṉa pūcāri vakuppiṉar ivi illāta cintuveḷiyil vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ mata nampikkikaḷ paṟṟi aṟivatu kaṭiṉam. cintup poṟippukaḷ muḻumiyāka vācikkappaṭāmal irukkum niliyil avarkaḷ matattiṉ peyarkūṭat teriyavilli. cintu akaḻvil kiṭitta kaṭavuḷ paṭimaṅkaḷ mikaccilavē. accilavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil ceyyakkūṭiya ūkaṅkaḷ: uyirkaḷip pali koṭukkum vaḻakkam iruntatākat teriyavilli; pala teyvaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭāmal oṟṟitteyva vaḻipāṭu naṭantirukkalām eṉpataṟku (kaṭavuḷ paṭimam eṉṟu collakkūṭiya) oṟṟi muttiri vaṭivamum, peṇ teyvamākak karutakkūṭiya cuṭṭa maṇ pommikaḷum cāṉṟukaḷākum.
(ii). muttirikaḷ, muttirip pativukaḷ; ceppup paṭṭayaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil uḷḷa pimpaṅkaḷ; tāykkaṭavuḷik kuṟippuṇarttum cuṭṭamaṇ pommikaḷ; ākiyavaṟṟil iruntu nām cila muṭivukaḷukku varalām Marshall identified a great Male God and Mother Goddess; deification or veneration of animals and plants; use of baths and water in religious practices; symbolic representation of the phallus (linga) and vulva (yoni) as prominent features of the Indus religion eṉpar aṟiñar airāvatam makātēvaṉ. cintu nākarika matam paṟṟi jāṉ mārṣal 1931 nūliṉ muṉṉuriyil tantuḷḷa karuttu ciṟantatu. ammatattiṉ nāṉku mukkiya amcaṅkaḷ piṉvaruvaṉa eṉpār mārṣal:
1.	mutaṉmi āṇ kaṭavuḷ oṉṟu, mutaṉmit tāykkaṭavuḷ oṉṟu.
2.	puṉita vilaṅkukaḷ cilavaṟṟiyum, puṉita maraṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟiyum teyvamāka vaṇaṅkutal. 
3.	kuḷiyalukkum nīrukkum matak koḷkikaḷilum ācāraṅkaḷilum mukkiyamāṉa paṅku.
4.	āṇ uṟuppu (liṅkam) peṇ uṟuppu (yōṉi) ivaṟṟiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷi vaṇaṅkutal.
mārṣaliṉ ikkarutti aṟputamāṉatu eṉṟu pocēl uṭpaṭap palar karutukiṉṟaṉar. namkālattiya intu mata nilavaram cintu nākarikak kālattil iruntatākak karutiṭum avaruṭiya pōkki kaṇṭippar vēṟu cilar. eṉiṉum ivviṣayattil mārṣalukku nikarāṉatoru valuvāṉa karutti vēṟu evarum veḷiyiṭavilli eṉpatu uṇmi.
32(i). mārṣaliṉ mutaṉmi `āṇ kaṭavuḷ (a great male god) muttiri eṇ. 420il uḷḷatu. mokeñco-tarō 1928-29 akaḻāyvil DK- G eṉak kuṟippiṭṭa pakutiyil mekkē itik kaṇṭeṭuttār. itil poṟitta āṇ uruvatti `tol-civā (Proto - Siva) eṉṟum, `piṟkālattil vaḻipaṭappaṭṭa civaṉ eṉṟum mārṣal aṟivittār. yōkāppiyācam ceyta civaṉ vaṭivam atu eṉṟu avar kūṟiṉār. uyarnta pīṭattiṉ mītu avvuruvam kālkaḷi maṭakki amarntuḷḷatu. kutikālkaḷ iraṇṭum oṉṟiyoṉṟu toṭukiṉṟaṉa; kāl peruviralkaḷ kīḻ nōkki uḷḷaṉa. `iru muḻaṅkālkaḷ vari nīṭṭi vittirukkum iru kikaḷilum vaḷiyalkaḷ; muḻaṅkālkaḷit toṭṭu maṭakkiya kiviralkaḷiṉ peru viralkaḷ iraṇṭum veḷiyē terikiṉṟaṉa. ivvaṭivattiṟku mūṉṟumukam iruppatākac cilar kūṟiṉālum  maṟuppōrum uḷar. (uṭaliṉ kīḻppakuti āṭiyiṉṟi nirvāṇamāka uḷḷatākavum āṇ kuṟi veḷiyē terivatākavum cilar coṉṉālum `āṇ kuṟi eṉappaṭuvatu iṭuppup paṭṭiyiṉ muṉiyāka irukkalām eṉpar cilar). tali uḷḷa iru erumik kompukaḷ nīṇṭu vaḷintu naṭut taliyil irukkum uyarnta talic cōṭaṉiyi cantikkiṉṟaṉa. erumik kompukaḷ  eṉpatil aiyam illi; (avvilaṅkiṉ muṟṟiya kompil irukkum `vaḷiyaṅkaḷ ivaṟṟilum uḷḷaṉa).
(ii). inta uruvattic cuṟṟik kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷ nāṉkiyum  (yāṉi, kāṇṭāmirukam, erumi, puli), pīṭattil māṉ iraṇṭiyum poṟittuḷḷaṉar. muttiri mēl pakutiyil ēḻu eḻuttup poṟippu oṉṟum, valatu puṟattil (yāṉikkum pulikkum iṭiyē) oru maṉita vaṭiva kōṭṭuuruvum outline  iṭampeṟukiṉṟaṉa. iṭampaṟṟātatāl maṉita vaṭivatti eḻuttuppoṟippu kaḷōṭu cērkkāmal vilaṅkukaḷukku iṭiyē poṟittirukkalām, allatu antak kuṟippiṭṭa iṭattil iruppataṟku ciṟappu arttamum irukkalām. eḻuttup poṟippi vācikkumvari oṉṟum uṟutiyākak kūṟa muṭiyātu.
33. civaṉ eṉṟa kaṭavuṭ peyar kimu 200kku mutal paravalākap payaṉpaṭavilli eṉṉum āyvāḷarkaḷ, āṇ kaṭavuḷāṉa inta vaṭivatti eppaṭi `āticivaṉ eṉṟu cuṭṭalām eṉkiṉṟaṉar.   420 muttiri vaṭivatti mārṣal tol - civaṉ / makāyōki eṉṟu iruvitamāka aḻikkiṟār. vētakāla rutraṉ eṉṟa ciṟu teyvamum cintuveḷiccivaṉum oṉṟutāṉ eṉṟu mārṣal karutiyatu vēṟu viṣayam. civā eṉum col oḷiyik kuṟikkum; paṇṭiyōr appeyarāl cūriyaṉi vaṇaṅkiṉar. aṉittu vaḻipāṭukaḷukkum muntiyatum  mēlāṉatum cūriya vaḻipāṭu eṉpatip paṇṭiya nākarikaṅkaḷ aṉittiṉ varalāṟukaḷum kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. (yōkikaḷukkellām talivaṉ eṉṟa karuttil makāyōki eṉṟu mārṣal kūṟiyatil tavaṟilli; āṉāl mūṉṟumukam uṭiyavaṉākak kaṇṭati cilar maṟuttu appoṟippil `mūṉṟumukam teriyavilliyē eṉṟu aṅkalāykkiṉṟaṉar).
34(i). yōkak kaliyil kipperuviral civaṉiyum cuṭṭu viralkaḷ uyirkaḷiyum kuṟippaṉa. cuṭṭu viralip `peru viraliṉ kīḻ aḻuttamākak cērkkumpōtu civaṉ muttiri piṟakkiṉṟatu. mēlum kip peruviral, cuṭṭuviral, naṭuviral, mōtira viral, ciṉṉa viral eṉpaṉa muṟiyē tī, kāṟṟu, ākāyam, pūmi, nīr eṉpavaṟṟukku aṭiyāḷamākum; yōkak kuṟiyīṭukaḷ ivvaṭippaṭiyil iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. civacittāntattiṉpaṭi iṟivaṉ oruvaṉē, uyirkaḷ pala; inta arttattil civaṉip pacupati eṉpar.
(ii). kirakari pocēl civacittāntattiṉ `pati, pacu, pācam paṟṟit teriyātatāl, vētakālattiṟku muṉṉar civaṉ kālnaṭikaḷiṉ talivaṉ eṉṟum, piṉṉar avaṉ kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷiṉ talivaṉ ākiṟāṉ eṉṟum kūṟuvar! civaṉtāṉ pati eṉṟum, uyirkaḷ pacu; taḷi pācam eṉṟu civa cittāntam cuṭṭum. `uyirkaḷ aṉittu uyirkaḷiyum  aṭakkiyatu. mārṣal civaṉi vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷiṉ talivaṉ - Lord of the Animals - eṉak kuṟippiṭṭatu tavaṟu.
35(i). intiya mataṅkaḷ palavaṟṟiyum cuṭṭum potuvāṉa collāka, nirvāka vacatikkāka āṅkilēya āṭciyar intu matam Hindu religion eṉṟa colli uruvākkiṉar. civaṉi muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākak koṇṭa civa cittāntam ciṟu teyva vaḻipāṭṭi ūkkuvippatilli. civaṉ uruvamaṟṟavaṉ (arūpi); civaliṅkam civaṉuṭiya aruvuruvam (illātatum iruppatumāṉa uruvam). civacittāntak kōṭpāṭu tirāviṭa (tamiḻiyac) cintaṉiyiṉ uccavaṭivam eṉpatil aiyamilli. tamiḻar civaṉi āṇākavum peṇṇākavum (mātoru pākaṉākak) kaṇṭaṉar. caktiyum civaṉum muṟiyē jaṭap poruḷum, ataṉ iyaṅku caktiyumākac ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa; oṉṟillāmal maṟṟatu illi. civaṉit talivaṉākak koṇṭa cintuc camayattiṉ cāyalkaḷ piṟkāla `intu matattilum, pavuttam, camaṇam ākiyavaṟṟilum uḷḷaṉa.
(ii). pocēl `vētamē muḻu ātāram eṉat tavaṟākak karutupavar: Gregory Possehl has a Vedic fixation. māṟāka civam paṟṟiya mikacciṟanta, cariyāṉa ñāṉam uṭiya mārṣal, ataṉi naṉku veḷippaṭuttiyuḷḷār; pārāṭṭiṟkuriyavar.
(iii). mokeñco-tarōvum harappāvum namakku veḷippaṭuttiyuḷḷa pala ceytikaḷuḷ civa camayattiṉ varalāṟāṉatu, cempuk kālam variyum (ēṉ ataṟku muntiyum) celvatākum eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. iṉṟum ulakil nilavivarum aṉittu mataṅkaḷilum civa matamē miku toṉmi vāyntatu. “Among the many revelations that Mohenjo-daro and Harappa have in store for us, none perhaps is more remarkable than this discovery that Saivism has a history going back to the chalcolithic age or perhaps, even further still, and thus it takes its place as the most ancient living faith in the world” - Mohenjodaro and the Indus Civilization: Vol. 1, p vii: Sir John Marshall (1931). tamatu (2007 - V vatu patippu) The New penguin History of the World nūlil jē.em. rāparṭ kūṟuvatum atē iṉṟu ulakattil niṉṟu nilavivarum camayaṅkaḷil civaṉ vaḻipāṭē aṉittiyumviṭat toṉmi vāyntatu eṉa uyttuṇarac cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
36. muttiri 420iṉ naṭuvil kāṇpatu teyvam tāṉ;   taliyil iruppatu erumik kompukaḷ iraṇṭu; cintumakkaḷiṉ matanampikki cārntavi ivi eṉpati pocēl ēṟkiṟār. erumiyōṭu pōriṭum maṉitar cintu muttirikaḷ cilavaṟṟil uḷḷaṉar. kaṭavuḷarukku erumik kaṭā pali koṭukkum vaḻakkam iruntirukkalām eṉa eṇṇat tōṉṟukiṟatu. itaṟku 1995il meṭō, keṉōyar ākiyōr harappāvil eṭutta cuṭṭamaṇ paṭṭayam cāṉṟākiṉṟatu. 
37. yākjavalkayarum patañcaliyum yōkak kaliyi kaṇṭu piṭittu `yōka cūttiram nūli eḻutiyatākac collappaṭṭālum yōkak kali cintunākarika makkaḷ tamatu uṭal / maṉa nalattiṟkākap payiṉṟatu eṉpatē varalāṟṟu uṇmi; avarkaḷ tam teyvatti yōka niliyil irutti vaṇaṅkiṉar eṉpataṟku mokeñco-tarō muttirikaḷ 5-m, harappā muttirikaḷ / paṭṭayaṅkaḷ 11-m cāṉṟākum. cintuvil toṭaṅkiya yōkak kali īrāṉ varip paraviyiruntatu. teṉ - kiḻakku īrāṉiṉ teppē yāhyāvil (Tepe Yahya) kimu 2400 -  1800 kāla maṭpāṇṭa ōṭṭil `yōka niliyil amarnta oruvariṉ vaṭivam poṟittiruppati akō parpōlā cuṭṭiyuḷḷār.
38(i). meṭō, keṉōyar kaṇṭeṭutta harappā miyūciyap poruḷ eṇ H95 - 2486 nīḷcatura cuṭṭamaṇ paṭṭayam 3.91 ce.mī nīḷam 1.5- 1.62 akalam koṇṭatu ataṉ vaṭivam piḷēṉō kāṉvek (plano convex) - atāvatu oru pakkam taṭṭi (flat) maṟupakkam puṭittirukkum (convex). harappā cārnta mikavum aṟputamāṉa kaṇṭupiṭippukaḷil oṉṟu (one of the most dramatic finds from Harappa) eṉṟu itu karutappaṭukiṟatu. ippaṭṭayattiṉ muṉpakkappoṟippu ceṅkuttākavum piṉpakkappoṟippu kiṭiyākavum uḷḷatu. muṉ pakkattil oru peṇ teyvam iraṇṭu pulikaḷuṭaṉ pōriṭukiṟār; `teyvam eṉpataṟku uruvattiṉ talic cōṭaṉiyē ātāramākum. avvaṭivattiṟku mēṟpakutiyil cakkara vaṭivilāṉa oṟṟi eḻuttup poṟippu irukkiṟatu. paṭṭayattiṉ kīḻp pakutiyil yāṉi poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṟkum mēlēyuḷḷa peṇ teyvam x pulikaḷ pōrukkum toṭarpu illāta vitattil iṭiveḷi kāṇappaṭakiṟatu!
(ii). ippaṭṭayattiṉ piṉ pakkattiṉ oru puṟattē maṉita uruvam  oṉṟu yōka niliyil uḷḷatu. taliyil mūṉṟu kiḷikaḷ  (ilikaḷ niṟinta, ciṟiya marak kiḷi) uḷḷaṉa. kaṇṇi mūṭik kikaḷi nīṭṭik, kāli maṭakki avvaṭivam āḻnta tiyāṉattil uḷḷatu. caṟṟut taḷḷi `kiyil nīṇṭa vēlēntiya maṉitaṉ oruvaṉ erumiyit tākkukiṟāṉ; avaṉ tūkkiya kāl erumiyiṉ neṟṟiyil patintuḷḷatu; avaṉatu maṟṟoru ki iṉṉoru erumiyiṉ kompu nuṉiyip paṟṟiyuḷḷatu; mottattil pōrāṭṭam mūrkkattaṉamāka naṭappatik kāṭṭukiṟatu. piṉpakkattil eḻuttup poṟippu oṉṟum illi. āṉāl caṇṭikkāṭcikku mēṟpuṟattil intiyā cārnta mīṉ iṉaṅkaḷi viḻuṅki, nati nīril vāḻum kariyāl (Garialis Gangeticus) raka mutalip paṭam uḷḷatu. cintu, kaṅki, airāvati natikaḷil kariyāl raka mutali vāḻkiṉṟatu; vāy mikavum oṭuṅkalāka, nīḷamāka, mīṉvēṭṭikkut tōtāka amintuḷḷatu; maṉitarkaḷukku āpattillāta mutali rakam atu  eṉpar.
(iii). ippaṭṭayattiṉ irupuṟap poṟippukaḷiṭiyē toṭarpu irukkiṟatā illiyā eṉṟu teriyavilli. ivaṟṟukkup piṉṉāl cuviyāṉa `purāṇak kati irukkalām. 420 muttiriyil iruppavar amarntirukkum ācaṉamum, intap paṭṭayattil uḷḷavar uḷḷa ācaṉamum orē mātiri uḷḷaṉa. iru vaṭivaṅkaḷumē tāmari ācaṉam (patmācaṉam) ākum.  iṟimit tamiḻ ñāṉacittar tirumūlar, eṇṇilā ātaṉam eṉṟu tirumantirattil kuṟippiṭuvār. (cittarkaḷ ācaṉatti ātaṉam eṉpar. ātmā cakajaṉam, itaṉ curukkam ātaṉam eṉappaṭum.)
39. cintu nākarika ucca kaṭṭa kālattil 1.25 milliyaṉ catura ki.mī nilapparappiyum 5 ilaṭcattiṟkum kūṭutalāṉa makkaḷ tokiyiyum koṇṭiruntatu eṉṟu kaṇṭōm. ivvaḷavu periya cintu nākarikattil kaṭavuḷar paṭimaṅkaḷum, kōyilkaḷum perumaḷavukkuk kāṇappaṭātatu peruviyappu.
40. iravīntiranāt tākūr 1930 mē mātattil ākpōrṭu palkalik kaḻakattil hiperṭ (Hibbert) niṉivup pēruriyi maṉitaṉiṉ matam eṉṟa talippil nikaḻttiyapoḻutu maṉita iṉam uruvākiya pōtē avaṉiṭam mata uṇarvum piṟantu viṭṭatu eṉṟār. appaṭiyāṉāl cintu makkaḷ mata uṇarviṉ vaṭivam eṉṉa? ekiptu / cumēriyā pōl eṇṇilaṭaṅkāta teyvaṅkaḷ iruppataṟkup patilāka muttiriyil oru āṇ teyvamum, ciṟu eṇṇikkiyil peṇ teyvaṅkaḷum  maṭṭumē kāṇappaṭuvatu paṇṭik kāla nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ taṉmikku nērmāṟāka irukkiṟatē?
41(i). erutu, araca maram ākiyavaṟṟic cintuveḷi makkaḷ vaḻipāṭṭiṟku uriyaṉavākap pōṟṟiyirukkalām. piṟkālattil civaṉiyē kallālaṭiyil amarntavaṉ / ālamar celvaṉ eṉṟu aḻittaṉar. cintup paṇpāṭṭil araca maram taṉiyiṭam peṟṟatu. avarkaḷuṭiya kalivaṭivaṅkaḷil aracamara ili mukkiya iṭampeṟukiṟatu. putta matattiṉarum inta amcatti tamatu matattil cērttuḷḷaṉar. cintukkali vaṭivaṅkaḷil erutu / erumik kompukaḷiyum mara ilikaḷiyum oṉṟiṇitta putiya uruvaṅkaḷ, pāṉi ōviyaṅkaḷil tōṉṟiṉa. curāṣṭirāvil tāṉiyamcēmikkum periyacāṭippāṉi akaḻāyvil kiṭittatu; ippāṉi mēl tīṭṭiya ōviyattil tāvara vaṭivattiṉmēl erumik kompu poruttiyuḷḷatu! kālipaṅkaṉil (toṭakka kāla harappā nākarika kaṭṭattil) vilaṅkum tāvaramum iṇintu putiyatōr vaṭivam uruvākkiya utāraṇaṅkaḷ pala irukkiṉṟaṉa. kompukaḷil ilikaḷum, malar moṭṭukaḷum eḻil vaṭivam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. kulli, kālipaṅkaṉ ākiya nakara eccaṅkaḷil varum nīr vāḻ tāmari moṭṭukaḷukkum itaḻkaḷukkum itu putiya arttam tarukiṟatu; peṇ kuṟikku ammoṭṭi oppiṭum pōkkiyum kāṇalām.
(ii). tāvaraṅkaḷip peṇṇākavum vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷi āṇākavum car jāṉ mārṣal pārttār. cintu  makkaḷukku araca maram `uyiriṉ uruvakamāṉa maramāka (Tree of life) tōṉṟiyatic cintup poṟippukaḷ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. civaṉum caktiyum iṇanta artta nārīvara vaṭivatti tāvaramum vilaṅkum iṇinta kalip paṭippukaḷil mārṣal kaṇṭuṇarntār. iṟivaṉum iṟiviyum iṇinta civacaktiyi aṉṟē mārṣal kaṇṭupiṭittu viṭṭār eṉalām.
(iii). iṅku muttiri 430 mukkiyamāṉatu. atil varum  kāṭciyil maram oṉṟiṉuḷ oru maṉita vaṭivam aikkiyamāki irukkiṟatu; marattiṟku muṉṉāl iṉṉoru maṉitaṉ maṇṭiyiṭṭu vaṇaṅkukiṟāṉ; avaṉukkup piṉṉāl oru āṭṭuk kaṭā (virinta nīṇṭa kompu iraṇṭuṭaṉ) nimirntu niṟkiṟatu; ivaṟṟiṉ kīḻ ēḻu maṉita vaṭivaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. immuttiriyiṉ mēṟpakutiyil poṟitta kuṟiyīṭukaḷil mīṉ vaṭivam teḷivākat terikiṟatu.
(iv). veṇkalakkālanākarikaṅkaḷil eṇ 7 mikavum mukkiyamāṉatu. kirēkkarkaḷ piḷiyāṭi (Pleiadis) naṭcattiraṅkaḷi ēḻu cakōtarikaḷākak kaṇṭaṉar. talimuṟi ēḻu eṉpatu tamiḻar kōṭpāṭu. kaṭal koṇṭa kumarināṭu, ēḻ teṅku nāṭu pōṉṟu mottam 7 x 7 ciṟiya nāṭukaḷi koṇṭatu eṉpar paḻantamiḻar. vaṭamoḻiyil ēḻu naṭcattiraṅkaḷ riṣipattiṉikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. pūmikku mēlē ēḻu ulakum pūmikkuk kīḻē ēḻu ulakum iruppatākak kaṟpaṉi. civacaktiyāṉavaḷ `makiṣācuraṉ eṉṟa erumivaṭiva irākkataṉi aḻippataṟku turkki vaṭiveṭuttāḷ. irattapījaṉ eṉṟa iṉṉoru irākkataṉiaḻikka vanta turkkiyum ēḻu peṇ vaṭivaṅkaḷ eṭuttāḷ. (inta ēḻil cāmuṇṭi turkkiyiṉ akōra vaṭivam; irattapījaṉiṉ ovvoru tuḷi irattattil iruntum meṉmēlum irākkatarkaḷ tōṉṟik koṇṭē iruntaṉar! eṉavē avaṉuṭiya uṭalil irunta muḻu irattattiyum cāmuṇṭi orē mūccil kuṭittu viṭuvāḷ!!)
(v). kaṇitattil puṉita eṇṇāka ēḻu karutappaṭukiṟatu. cintu nākarika mukkiyakkūṟukaḷil oṉṟāka, kuḷittu ācāramāka iruppatum, nīrukku mata mukkiyattuvam aḷippatum, paṇṭikik kāla nīrāṭutalum iṭampeṟukiṉṟaṉa. itaṟkāka eṉṟu kūṟa muṭiyāviṭṭālum ovvoru vīṭṭilum kiṇaṟum, kuḷikkum aṟiyum, potu iṭaṅkaḷil kuḷikkum vacatikaḷum  amikkap paṭṭaṉa. cintuveḷi makkaḷ uṭal tūymiyi `matam cārnta kaṭamiyākak karutiṉar eṉalām. mokeñcō-tarō periya nīccal kēṇi (poḻutu pōkkiṟku allāmal) ētōvoru vakit teyvīka / mata nōkkuṭaṉ amintatu eṉalām. ekiptiyak kurumār pakalil iru muṟiyum iravil irumuṟiyum nīrāṭiṉar eṉṟum, `kaḻuvi ularttiya āṭikaḷi aṇintaṉar eṉṟum ekiptu āvaṇaṅkaḷiliruntu aṟiyalām.
(vi). cintu nākarika nakaraṅkaḷ mēṭukaḷ niṟintavi. 12 mīṭṭar (39 aṭi) uyara mokeñco-tarō mēṭṭiṉ ucciyil apperiya nīccalkēṇiyip (toṭarpuṭiya piṟa kaṭṭumāṉaṅkaḷum) potumakkaḷ perumaḷavil vāḻnta kīḻ nakarattil kaṭṭāmal mēṭṭil kaṭṭiyataṟku kāraṇam eṉṉa? aṅkunīrāṭum urimi  cilarukku maṭṭumā? eṉṟa viṉā eḻukiṉṟatu; atu ācārak kuḷiyalukkāka uyaramāṉa mēṭṭil amikkappaṭṭu atu kuṟippiṭṭa cilarukku maṭṭumē uri yatāka iruntirukkalām eṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu. intak kēṇiyē kōyilā allatu atit tāṅki niṟkum uyarnta mēṭutāṉ kōyilā teriyavilli. kōpura taricaṉam kōṭi puṇṇiyam eṉpārkaḷē atupōl ituvum oṉṟā! cintu nirvākikaḷiṭam kōyilkaḷ kaṭṭat tēviyāṉa celvam, poṟiyiyal tiṟaṉ, maṉita vaḷam maṟṟum piṟa ēntukaḷ aṉittum iruntum, avarkaḷ kōyilkaḷi kaṭṭāmal iruntatu putirtāṉ! kipi 6 - 12 nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil erāḷamāṉa kōyilkaḷāka kaṭṭiyum pūcakarkaḷukkum pirāmaṇarukkum pirammatēyaṅkaḷi vaḻaṅkiyum pukaḻ peṟṟa tamiḻnāṭṭu maṉṉarkaḷiyum ēṉ teṉṉāṭṭiṉ piṟpakuti maṉṉarkaḷiyum pōla cintumakkaḷ ceyalpaṭavilli!
42. cintuveḷi matam paṟṟi cila aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷavi poruttamāyuḷḷaṉa:-
pocēl: kiṭitta paṭivaṅkaḷiyum avi veḷippaṭuttum nampikkikaḷiyum pārkkumpōtu valuvāṉatoru matamum, ataṉ piṉṉaṇiyil oru cittāntamum iruntati ēṟka vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. āṉāl avaṟṟip paṟṟiya uṇmiyi teḷivāka aṟivatu cikkalāṉatu; itu cārntu āḻnta āyvukaḷi uriya aṟiñarkaḷ toṭarātatāl ittuṟi āyvukaḷ `mēltariyic curaṇṭip pārttatuṭaṉ niṉṟu viṭṭatiyum ottuk koḷḷavēṇṭum. 
ārṉālṭ jōcap tāyiṉpi (1889 - 1975) māperum varalāṟṟaṟiñar: ekiptiṉ aṭiyāḷamākap piramiṭukaḷum cumēriyāviṉ aṭiyāḷamākac cikurāt-kaḷum uḷḷaṉa. atupōla cintuveḷi aṭiyāḷamāka mokeñco-tarō/ harappā mēṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
43(i). ekiptu makkaḷ iṟanta aracarkaḷ, kuṭumpattiṉar  uṭalkaḷip pātukāppāka vippataṟkum, avarkaḷuṭiya (cetta piṉṉar mēlulakil) maṟuvāḻvukkut tēviyāṉa aṉittup poruḷkaḷiyum, avarkaḷuṭiya celvaṅkaḷuṭaṉ putittu vippataṟkākavum `piramiṭ vaṭivilāṉa kūrnutik kōpuraṅ kaḷik kaṭṭiṉar. (atupōṉṟa  piramiṭukaḷ  vēṟu iṭaṅkaḷilum -- māyaṉ nākarika piramiṭukaḷ pōṉṟavi uḷḷaṉa). aviyum  mēṟcoṉṉa kāraṇaṅkaḷuṭaṉ aracāṭciyiṉ atikāra āḷumiyik kāṭṭi potu makkaḷ miraṭṭu vataṟkākavum amikkappaṭṭaṉa. 
(ii). piramiṭukaḷi ekiptiyarkaḷ kaṭṭu muṉṉarē cumēriyarkaḷ cikurāt (ziggurat) vaki piramiṭukaḷi yūpirēṭī - tikirī āṟṟuppakutiyil kaṭṭiṉar; avi piṇam putikkum iṭaṅkaḷalla; kōyilkaḷētāṉ! (paṭikkaṭṭup piramiṭukaḷ eṉalām). cumēriyarkaḷ vaṇaṅkiya teyvaṅkaḷukku kōyilkaḷ cikurāṭ ucciyil amintaṉa cātāraṇap potu makkaḷ akkōyilkaḷukkuc cella muṭiyātu; paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ mūlam mēlē ceṉṟu ucciyil uḷḷa teyvattip pūcikkum urimi pūcārikaḷukku maṭṭumē uṇṭu; aracarkaḷukku nikarāṉa cakti uḷḷavarkaḷākap pūcārikaḷik karutiṉar; mecapotāmiya atikāra varkkaṅkaḷil pūcārikaḷ mukkiya iṭampeṟṟaṉar.
(iii). cumēriyarkaḷukkup piṉṉar mecapotāmiya āṭci ceyta pāpilōṉiyar, elām nāṭṭavar, akkātiyar, aciriyar ākiyōr tattamakkuc contamāṉa cikurāt-kaḷi amittaṉar. cumēriyarkaḷ, pāpilōṉiyarkaḷōṭu neruṅkiya vaṇikat toṭarpu koṇṭu (ataṉ mūlam peruñcelvamum īṭṭiya) cintuppakutit talivarkaḷ cikurāt pōṉṟa kōyilkaḷi ēṉ amikkavilli? eṉṟa viṉāvukku viṭi illi. cikurāt kōpuraṅkaḷi uyaramākak kaṭṭi ucciyil kōyilkaḷ amittataṟkum kāraṇamuṇṭu: teyvaṅkaḷ vāṉulakattavar; avarkaḷuṭiya vāṉulakuṭaṉ āṉa cikurāt ucciyil iruntu eḷitil toṭarpu koḷḷalām!
(iv). māyaṉ (Mayan) pōṉṟa mattiya amerikka (Meso - American) nākarikattukkuriyōrum paṭikkaṭṭuk kōpuraṅkaḷi ‘Stepped Pyramid’ (cikurāt pōṉṟu) amittu ucciyil tamatu teyvaṅkaḷik kuṭiyiruttiṉar. māyaṉ kōyilkaḷukkum tamiḻnāṭṭukkōyilkaḷukkum iṭiyil viyattaku oṟṟumikaḷ uṇṭu. mecapotāmiya cikurātkaḷil teyvattiṟku maṭṭumē iṭamuṇṭu; āṉāl māyaṉ kōpuraṅkaḷilō ucciyil teyvaṅkaḷ iruntatōṭu aṭippākattil iṟanta aracarkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷum putikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
44. aracarkaḷukkuriya āṭampara araṇmaṉikaḷō teyvaṅkaḷukkuriya peruṅkōyilkaḷō illāta cintunākarikam piṉtaṅkiyatāka karutat takkatalla! cintu araciyal nilimiyum oḻuṅkum, matap pārampariyamum ēṉiya (mēṟ coṉṉa) piṟa nāṭṭu nākarikaṅkaḷil iruntu māṟupaṭṭatu eṉpati uṇarka. cintup paṇpāṭṭu taṉmikaḷip purintu koṇṭāl, araṇmaṉikaḷum kōyilkaḷum ēṉ illi eṉpataṟku viḷakkam kiṭikkum: kampīramāṉa pūcārikaḷ iruppiṭa kaṭṭaṭam (College of Priests) periya nīccal kēṇi arukil iruntālum iraṇṭiṟkum toṭarpu iruntatākat teriyavilli. collappōṉāl, antap pāriya kaṭṭiṭatti kōyilāka karutuvataṟkē tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa ātāram illi.
45. periya nīccal kēṇi mēṭṭil irukkum putta tūpikku aṭiyil cintu nākarikak  kālakkōyil oṉṟu irukkalām eṉa vīlar eṇṇiṉār. `oru kōyilukku mēl iṉṉoṉṟik kaṭṭuvārkaḷā? eṉṟataṟku `mokeñco-tarō perunakaramē ēḻu taṭavi oṉṟukkumēl oṉṟāka kaṭṭappaṭṭatutāṉē  eṉṟu maṟumoḻi vantatu! putta tūpikkuc cētam iṉṟi atic cuṟṟi naṭattiya akaḻāyvukaḷil (ceṅkal pāviya tarikaḷ uḷḷa) vīṭṭu aṟikaḷ, vīṭṭu muṟṟaṅkaḷ, kuḷiyalaṟikaḷ, kaḻivunīr vāykkālkaḷ pōṉṟa perunakara aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ maṭṭum veḷippaṭṭaṉa. iv akaḻāyvukaḷ tūpikkaṭiyil kōyil oṉṟum illi eṉṟu kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.



kōyilkaḷākak(?) karutak kūṭiya 
mokeñco-tarō kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ aintu 

1) HR-A pakuti vīṭu - 1
46(i). itu mikavum vittiyācamāṉa amippu. makkaḷ kuṭiyirunta vīṭāka itik karutamuṭiyātu. ērāḷamāṉa muttirikaḷum kiviṉip poruḷkaḷum inta kaṭṭiṭa akaḻvil kiṭittaṉa. tāṭi maṉitaṉ tali (HR 910), muṟittup pārkkum maṉitaṉ tali (L 898), iviyum iṅkutāṉ kiṭittaṉa. jāṉcaṉ ceyta āyviṉpaṭi itil kiṭitta muttirikaḷiṉ eṇ 12; avaṟṟil kāṇṭāmiruka vaṭiva muttirikaḷ atikam.
(ii). intak kaṭṭiṭattil mūṉṟu taṉittaṉik kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ iṇintuḷḷaṉa; paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ mūlam mūṉṟiyum iṇittu oṉṟupōl ākkiyuḷḷaṉar. intak kaṭṭiṭa vaṭa pakutiyāṉatu periyanīccalkēṇi mēṭṭuk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ, HR-A pakuti vīṭu viii pōṉṟu uyarttappaṭṭuḷḷatu; kāraṇam puriyavilli. iti kōyilākavō kōyiluṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyatākavō karuta muṭiyātu; āṉāl kuṭiyiruppākavum eṇṇa muṭiyātu! evvakiyil nōkkiṉālum itu acātāraṇak kaṭṭiṭam eṉpatu muṭivu.
2) HR-B pakuti, vīṭu - XXX
47. piramāṇṭamāṉa inta ceṅkal kaṭṭiṭattil cintuveḷi nākarikap piṟkāla, iṭikkālap pakutic cuvarkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. veḷic cuvarkaḷiṉ akalam 1.37 mīṭṭar. iṭiyāmal irunta cuvarkaḷiṉ uyaram 2 mīṭṭar. uṭpuṟa aṟikaḷiṉ vāyilkaḷ kāṇappaṭavilli. cuṭappaṭāta kaḷimaṇ ceṅkal  mēṭikaḷ vāyilkaḷ irukka vēṇṭiya iṭattil iruntaṉa. teṟkuppuṟa aṟikaḷukku oru potuk kiṇaṟu amintiruntatu. periya kaṭṭiṭam oṉṟi eḻuppum nōkkil aṭittaḷam iṭṭa aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa; āṉāl akkaṭṭiṭam aṭittaḷamā allatu kaṭṭappaṭṭu aḻintu viṭṭatā teriyavilli. ikkaṭṭiṭattiṉ parappaḷavum amippum ati oru kōyilākak karuta tūṇṭiṉālum kōyil eṉṟu nirūpikka uriya ātāram illi.
3) HR-B pakuti, vīṭu - ‘L’
48. mēṟkūṟiya kaṭṭiṭattiṟku arukilum ataṟku nikarāṉa amippilum uḷḷa iṉṉoru kaṭṭiṭamāka L eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭa 50ām eṇ vīṭṭic cilar kōyilākak karutiṉālum, ataṟkāṉa ātāraṅkaḷ illi.
4) DK-G pakuti,catukkam 11 (BLock 11)
49. akaḻāyvu ceyta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷil intak kaṭṭiṭattik kōyil eṉṟu collalām eṉṟu mekkē terivittār. kāraṇaṅkaḷ eṉṉavāka irukkalām? kōyilukku varuvōriṉ kuḷiyal tūymikkāka variciyāka mūṉṟu kiṇaṟukaḷ iruntaṉa. muṟṟattiṟkut teṟkuppuṟa aṟikaḷil cuṭṭamaṇ pāvikaḷ cila kiṭantaṉa; vēṟu poruṭkaḷ illi. (intak kaṭṭiṭat tokutiyi muṟṟāka akaḻāyvu ceyyavilli eṉpatiyum kuṟippiṭa vēṇṭum) akaḻnta pakutiyil muṟṟam, ataṟkut teṟkē pala aṟikaḷ, kiṇaṟukaḷ mūṉṟu, nīr vāykkāl mēl pālam, nīr pōkkik kuḻāykaḷ, aṟikaḷil cuṭṭamaṇ pommikaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ciṟitu kālam kaḻittu mekkē  toṭarntu akaḻvu ceytapiṉ `intak kaṭṭiṭam  kōyil eṉṟa tam muntiya karutti viṭṭuviṭṭu ētō oruvaṉ taṅkum viṭutiyāka irukkalām eṉṟu muṭivukku vantār.
5) catukkam 8A, DK-G  pakuti
50(i). ippakutiyiṉ ūṭākac cellum mattiya vītiyum (central street) paḷḷamāṉa ciṟiya muṭaṅkut teruvum (low lane) cantikkum iṭam catukkam 8A (Block 8A) ākum. iccatukkattil tamiḻ eḻuttu ‘l’ vaṭivattil uṟutiyākak kaṭṭiya kaṭṭiṭam uṇṭu. cuvarkaḷ akalamākavum uṭpuṟattē maṇ miṇṭukaḷ koṭuttum iruntaṉa; mēlmāṭi iruntirukka vāyppu uṇṭu. mokeñco-tarō ēṉiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷip pōl itaṉuṭiya vāyilum `ciṟiya teruviltāṉ iruntatu. (vāyilukku vaṭakkē kiṇaṟum atic cuṟṟi aṟiyum amintiruntaṉa.)
(ii). eṉṉa kāraṇamō teriyavilli mutalil amitta vāyili kaḷimaṇ cuvarāl aṭittuviṭṭu mattiya vītiyip pārttavaṇṇam putiyavāyil tiṟakkappaṭṭatu; im māṟṟattiṟkup poruntiya vaṭikāl kuḻāykaḷ, nīr ēntum kuḻic cāṭikaḷ ivaṟṟip poruttiyatōṭu kaṭṭiṭattiṉ mēl/kīḻp pakutikaḷi iṇikkum (cuṭṭamaṇ) nīrk kuḻāykaḷum poruttappaṭṭaṉa. kaḻippaṟi vacatikaḷum iruntaṉa. intap pakuti muḻuvatiyum veḷḷattiliruntu pātukākka piṟkālattil maṇ pōṭṭu kaṭṭiṭa maṭṭatti  uyarttiṉar; kiṇaṟu irunta aṟikkuc cella paṭikkaṭṭukaḷum amittaṉar. ikkaṭṭiṭam eṉṉa tēvikku utaviyatu eṉṟu teriyavilli; atu viṭutiyum alla; kōyilum alla; puriyāta putir!
(iii). mārṣal, mekkē, vīlar ākiyōr kōyil kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ irukka vēṇṭumeṉak karutittāṉ tēṭiṉārkaḷ, āṉāl kiṭikkavilli! periya nīccal kēṇikkum, cintunākarika makkaḷ matattiṟkum toṭarpu iruppatāka ammūvarum karutukiṟārkaḷ. aimpūtaṅkaḷil iraṇṭāṉa nīriyum, neruppiyum vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉṟu colvatil arttamuṇṭu. aimpūtaṅkaḷi avarkaḷ iṟimiyiṉ kūṟukaḷāka vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ, (aimpūtaṅkaḷē kaṭavuḷ eṉṟu karutavilli). eṉṟu cilar vātiṭuvar. āṉālum car jāṉ mārṣal kūṟiyavi tāṉ iṉṟiya niliyilum ēṟpuṭiyatāka uḷḷaṉa.
51. cintu makkaḷ `civaṉ eṉṟa peyar cūṭṭāviṭṭālum,  periya āṇ kaṭavuḷ oṉṟi vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉpatu uṟuti; avaṉ yōkak kali aṟinta `makāyōki, avaṉukku iṇiyāṉa vēṟu āṇkaṭavuḷ cintuveḷi makkaḷukku illi eṉpati muttirikaḷ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. mekarkār mutal mokeñco-tarōvari kiṭitta (cuṭṭamaṇ) peṇ pommikaḷiṟ pala perum tāy Great Mother” kaṭavuḷ āka irukkalām. āṉāl inta ātāram pōtātu eṉkiṟār pocēl (It is not robust). mokeñco-tarō `cuṭṭamaṇṇālāṉa peṇ pommikaḷ tāytteyva vaṭivaṅkaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟu tolliyalāḷar cilar nampukiṉṟaṉar. Terracotta female figurines unearthed by Archeologists in Mohenjo-daro are believed to be that of the Mother goddess.
52(i). kālipaṅkaṉil mūvakiyāṉa tī vaṇakka vakikaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa: (i) mēṭṭuppakutiyil potu makkaḷukkāṉa   akṉi kuṇṭaṅkaḷ (ii) nakariṉ kīḻp pakutiyil vīṭukaḷ tōṟum vaḻipāṭṭu amippukaḷ (iii)  nakariṉ kiḻakkil pirattiyēkamāṉa tī vaṇakka miyam tāykkaṭavuḷ vaṇakka aṭiyāḷam kālipaṅkaṉil illi)
(ii). lōtal makkaḷum tī vaṇakkattil īṭupaṭṭirukkalām. aṅku ācārak kuḷiyalukkup payaṉpaṭṭa mēṭikaḷ iruntataṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ  uḷḷaṉa. (matam cārnta cāṉṟukaḷ oṉṟum rōjtiyilkiṭikkavilli) 
53(i). airāvatam makātēvaṉ kaṇakkuppaṭi 1,159 cintu muttirikaḷil yūṉikārṉ eṉṟa oṟṟikkompu vilaṅkiṉ vaṭivamum, ataṉuṭiya talikku aṭiyil kampattil niṟuttiya iraṇṭaṭukkup pāttiramum poṟittuḷḷaṉa. appāttirattiṉ mēlaṭukku uruḷi vaṭivilum (cylindrical) kīḻppakuti arik kōṇavaṭivilum (hemispherical) uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ aṭiyil  tāṅkik kampam uḷḷatu. mārṣal iti kaṟpūram / cāmpirāṇi pōṉṟavi erikkum tūpakkāl (incense burner) allatu puki tarum nerupputtaṭṭu (brazier) eṉṟu karutiṉar.
(ii). āyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa immuttirikaḷik kūrmiyāka āyvu ceyta piṟaku makātēvaṉ māṟupaṭṭa putiya karuttiyum kūṟukiṟār: appāttiratti avar `puṉita vaṭikaṭṭi (sacred filter) eṉkiṟār, mēṟpāttiram oru vaṭikaṭṭi; kīḻppāttiram vaṭitta tiravatti ēntuvatu. I. Mahadevan: The sacred filter standard facing the unicorn: more evidence: South Asian Archeology 1993: Editors Asko Parpola and another: Helsinki Suomalinen Tiedeakatemia: pages 435 - 445.
(iii). anta vaṭikaṭṭiyi ūrvalaṅkaḷil kampattil porutti mukkiya aṭiyāḷam pōl tūkkic celvārkaḷ. mokeñco -tarō, harappāvil ippaṭi naṭantirukkalām eṉṟum, intak kāṭci, kaḷimaṇ pativukaḷ iraṇṭil kāṇappaṭuvatākavum mārṣal kūṟukiṟār. (1931: 111  104, no. 38). nāṉku maṉitarkaḷ āḷukku oṉṟāka, nāṉku nīṇṭa kampaṅkaḷit tūkkic celkiṉṟaṉar; kampaṅkaḷiṉ mēl nuṉiyil poruttiya vaṭivaṅkaḷil yūṉikārṉ vaṭivamum, aṭuttu vaṭikaṭṭiyiṉ kīḻppakutiyum uḷḷaṉa.
(iv). harappāvil kiṭitta kaḷimaṇ villiyil oru maṉitaṉ iru kikaḷālum talikku mēl avvaṭikaṭṭiyi vittirukkum kāṭci uḷḷatu. vaṭikaṭṭiyiṉ parimāṇam āṉatu tūkki vittirukkum maṉitaṉiviṭap periyatu. vaṭikaṭṭiyiṉ mukkiyattuvatti ippaṭi uṇarttiṉar pōlum eṉpar vāṭ.
(v). tuvajam (standard) ākattūkkikkoṇṭu cellak kūṭiyatākiya inta vaṭikaṭṭiyi harappā makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpar makātēvaṉ. airōppiya  nākarikaṅkaḷil (koṭikaḷ (flags) payaṉpāṭṭiṟku varumuṉṉar) vilaṅkukaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, kaṟpaṉi vilaṅkukaḷ pōṉṟa vaṭivaṅkaḷi (standards) nīṇṭa kampaṅkaḷil poruttip pōrukkuccellum paṭi aṇiyiṉ muṉṉaṇiyil  tūkkic celvārkaḷ. attaku perum mukkiyattuvam vāynta avvaṭivaṅkaḷi uyirik koṭuttup pātukāppārkaḷ. rōm cāmrācciyap paṭikaḷ veḷḷi / veṇkalattil ceyta `ciṟaku viritta kaḻuku vaṭivatti appaṭit tūkkic celvārkaḷ; intak kaḻuki avarkaḷ `akkiyūlā (Aquila) eṉpar; atit tūkkic cellum perumikkuriya paṭivīraṉ `akkiyūlipar (Aquilifer). rōm cāmrācciyattil palanūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka ivvaḻakkam iruntatu. antak kampatti standard eṉa aḻippar. ippoḻutu koṭiyiyum appeyarāl aḻikkum vaḻakkam uṇṭu. makātēvaṉ standard eṉṟa collit taṉatu kaṭṭuriyil payaṉpaṭuttukiṟār. iti vaṭamoḻiyil tuvajam eṉpar. makātēvaṉ cāṉṟākac cuṭṭum muttirikaḷ varumāṟu:
a). A Harappan sealing showing a procession of men holding portable standards - (Marshall 1931; p 116, no. 8 Mohenjo-daro.)
b). A Harappan sealing showing a priest holding the sacred filter standard above his head - (Vats 1940; ii pl 93. no. 309. Harappa).
(vi). yāṉit tantattil ceyta ciṟiya puṉita `vaṭikaṭṭiyi  hārp (HARP) āyvāḷarkaḷ harappāvil mīṭṭaṉar. ataṉ  mēlpākattil āḻamāṉa tuḷikaḷ iruntaṉa. itiliruntu puṉita vaṭikaṭṭiyiṉ uṇmiyāṉa nōkkam terikiṟatu; atu taṇal taṭṭu alla eṉpatum nirūpaṇamākiṟatu.
(vii). vaṭikaṭṭi mūlam ētō oru tiravattit tayārittaṉar eṉṟum, ataṟkum avarkaḷuṭiya mata nampikkikkum toṭarpu iruntatākavum karutappaṭukiṟatu. mēlum puṉita vaṭikaṭṭi vaṭivam poṟitta `peyṉ āparaṇak kaṟkaḷ, `mākkaṟ paṭivaṅkaḷ pōṉṟaṉavum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. taliyil aṇiyum taṅkap paṭṭi oṉṟilum vaṭikaṭṭiyip poṟittuḷaḷaṉar. āka harappā nākarika makkaḷ peritum matitta mukkiyap poruḷāka puṉita vaṭikaṭṭi iruntirukkalām eṉṟa kōṭpāṭu valuvāṉatutāṉ. cintu makkaḷiṉ matam paṟṟiya āyvil atu iṉṟiyamiyāta iṭam peṟukiṉṟatu. aḷakkuṭi cītārāmaṉ tamiḻakattil karūril kaṇṭeṭutta veṇkala muttiriyilum inta vaṭikaṭṭi vaṭivam poṟittuḷḷatu.
54. `vēta āriyarkaḷ tamatu cōmapāṉa marapi harappā nākarikam makkaḷiṭam iruntu peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa makātēvaṉ muṭivu tavaṟalla. vētaṅkaḷil uḷḷa tutip pāṭalkaḷiṉpaṭi intiraṉ cōmapāṉak kuṭiyi virumpupavaṉ. taṉatu tuvajattil avaṉ cōmapāṉak kiṇṇattit taṉ aṭiyāḷamākak koṇṭiruntāṉ. intiraṉ vēta kālap pōrk kaṭavuḷ; avaṉ intira tvajam eṉṉum cōmapāṉak kiṇṇa vaṭivam poruttiya kampattip pōrukku eṭuttuc celvāṉ. atē vētakāla mataccaṭaṅkukaḷilum cōmapāṉattiṟku mukkiya iṭam uṇṭu.
55. cintu makkaḷ uḷnāṭṭup pōr, veḷināṭṭup pōr etilum īṭupaṭṭatākat teriyavilli. eṉavē puṉita vaṭikaṭṭi poruttiya tuvajattip pōril payaṉpaṭuttiyirukka vāyppilli; matamcārnta nikaḻvukaḷiltāṉ payaṉpaṭuttiyirukka vēṇṭum. cōmapāṉam aruntum vaḻakkam, (cōmapāṉa) vaṭikaṭṭiyit tuvajattil poruttum marapu, ivaṟṟi harappā makkaḷiṭamiruntutāṉ vētakāla āriyar peṟṟaṉar eṉpatu intiyap paṇṭi varalāṟṟil peruntākkatti uṇṭākkak kūṭiyatu.
56. matac caṭaṅkukaḷil payaṉpaṭṭa poruṭkaḷum, pali pīṭap poruṭkaḷum akaḻāyvukaḷil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. caṅku, cippik karaṇṭikaḷ shell ladles (ūtavum, nīr coriyavum payaṉpaṭuttiyavi) pala kiṭittuḷḷaṉa, cintu muttirikaḷil teyvaṅkaḷukkuc cāṭikaḷi arppaṇikkum kāṭciyāṉatu (jars offered to deities) cintu muttirikaḷil (harappāvil kiṭitta oṉṟu uṭpaṭa) poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. accāṭikkuḷ tiravamō tāṉiyamō iruntirukkum eṉpar jēṉ mekkiṉṭoṣ ammiyār - The Ancient Indus Valley: New Perspectives: Jane Mcinthosh (2008). 
57. cintu matattōṭu toṭarpuḷḷa mantira tantiraṅkaḷ māyājāla vittikaḷ, naṉṉimittam kūṟutal, āvikaḷōṭu pēcutal, pukipaṭinta veṇkalak kaṇṇāṭiyil etirkālattik kaṇṭu kaṇippiṭutal, pōṉṟa acātāraṇa viṣayaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya āyvukaḷ cila āṇṭukaḷāka naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟukkāṉa ātāraṅkaḷ mokeñco-tarō, harappā mutaliya nakaraṅkaḷil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.
58(i). kippiṭiyuṭaṉ  (ēṟattāḻa vaṭṭa vaṭivamāṉa) veṇkalak kaṇṇāṭikaḷ kulli, mokeñco-tarō, harappāvil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa; paḷic eṉa miṉukkum ikkaṇṇāṭiyil mukam pārkkalām. āṉāl ataṉ nōkkam atuvalla. paḷapaḷappāṉa antak kaṇṇāṭikkuc ciṟitu karumpukiyi ūṭṭi (caṟṟu vittiyācamākat tōṉṟum itaṉit) taṉatu toḻilukkup payaṉpaṭuttupavari (āvikaḷuṭaṉ pēcum) `ṣāmaṉ (Shaman) eṉpar. (māyak kaṇṇāṭi mūlam avar naṉmi, tīmikkup poṟuppāṉa āvikaḷuṭaṉ pēcuvār! anta āvikaḷum ṣāmaṉ mūlamāka utavi kōrupavaruṭaṉ pēcum eṉpatu nampikki!)
(ii). tiruṭṭup pōṉa poruṭkaḷi mīṭṭal, tiruṭarkaḷip piṭittal, vīṭṭil teḷikkum puṉita nīr `ōtik koṭuttal, tīya āviyi viraṭṭutal, tīya āvi utaviyōṭu cūṉiyam vittal(!) pōṉṟa vēlikaḷi ṣāmaṉ ceyvār. iṉṟum āciya - āppirikka nāṭukaḷil ṣāmāṉicam (Shamanism) matam peyarāl naṭakkum ittakiya naṭavaṭikkikaḷik kāṇalām; nam kaṇṇukkut teriyāta āvikaḷ, tēvatikaḷ uṭaṉ ṣāmāṉ toṭarpuḷḷavar(!) ātāram: Elizabeth During Caspers - Another face of the Indus Valley Magico - Religious system: South Asian Archeology: (1993) Editors Adalbert J. Gail and another pp 65 - 89 Stuttgart, Germany.
(iii). naṉṉimittam kūṟupavar payaṉpaṭuttum (yāṉit tantattil ceytu puḷḷikaḷ veṭṭiya) tāyak kaṭṭikaḷ, puḷḷikaḷ / vaṭṭaṅkaḷ veṭṭiya (yāṉit tanta) mantirak kōlkaḷ iviyum mokeñco-tarō, harappāvil kiṭittaṉa. vili uyarnta maṇik kaṟkaḷil ceytu uṭalil aṇivataṟkāṉa keṭṭa caktikaḷi viraṭṭum tāyattukkaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. hārp (HARP) amippu cārpil āyvukaḷi harappāvil ceyta meṭō, keṉōyar  ērāḷamāṉa ciṟiya tāyattukkaḷi eṭuttuḷḷaṉar.
(iv). mokeñco-tarōvil kiṭitta cāṭiyil pukiyi veḷiyēṟṟum tuḷikaḷ iruntaṉa. maṉitari niṉiviḻakka vittup paravaca nilikku iṭṭuc cellum pukiyi antac cāṭi veḷiyēṟṟum vitamāka atu ceyyappaṭṭiruntatu, cāṭikkuḷ maruntuc carakkukaḷip pōṭṭut tīyiṭṭup puki kiḷappuvar; (pukiyic  cuvācikkum pūcāri paravacam aṭintu āruṭam colluvāṉ). mantira tantiram, pēy picācu nampikki, āvi ulaka vaḻikāṭṭal pōṉṟavi harappā nākarikattōṭu iṇintavi eṉkiṟār ṣeriṉ iratnākar ammiyār.
59. cintuveḷi muttirikaḷiṉ payaṉpāṭu vaṇikam, nāṭṭu  nirvākam ākiyavaṟṟōṭu toṭarpuṭiyatāka iruntālum, avaṟṟi vittiruppavar, payaṉpaṭuttupavar, taṉṉōṭu koṇṭu celpavar ākiyōrukku avi pātukāppumvaḻaṅkiyatākak    karutappaṭṭirukkalām  eṉpar ṣerīṉ iratnākar: atāvatu muttirikaḷum oruvaki tāyattukaḷ talisman tāṉ eṉpatu avaruṭiya karuttu. [Shereen Ratnagar: Understanding Harappa (2001)]
60. amerikka āyvāḷar ṭīv pārmar (Steve Farmer) orupaṭi mēlē ceṉṟu cintu muttirikaḷil naṉṉimittak kuṟiyīṭukaḷ (Auspicious symbols) tām poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; avi eḻuttup poṟippukkaḷē alla eṉṟu vātiṭat toṭaṅkiṉār. intak kūṟṟi nallaṟiñar ēṟkāviṭiṉum, muttirikaḷ aṉukūla vāyppukkaḷi favourable fortune peṟṟut tarupavi; allatu cakti tarupavavi āka karutap peṟṟirukkalām eṉṟum cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar.
61(i). cintu muttirikaḷil mikak kūṭutalāṉavi oṟṟikompu yūṉikārṉ erutukaḷākum; avaṟṟiṟku muṉṉāl puṉita vaṭikaṭṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. cōmapāṉam eṉṟu vēta āriyarāl aḻikkappaṭṭālum, vaṭikkaṭṭappaṭṭatu `veṟiyūṭṭum matuc carakku eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. yūṉikārṉ nija ulakattiṟku uriyatalla; atu harappā nākarika makkaḷ uruvākkiya kaṟpaṉi vaṭivam; inta vilaṅku cintuveḷiyiṉ mikavum cakti vāynta iṉak kuḻu /  vaṇikak kuḻumattiṉ kuṟiyīṭāka iruttal vēṇṭum; puṉita vaṭikaṭṭi poṟikkāta yūṉikārṉ muttiriyik kāṇpatu aritu. intap piṉṉaṇiyil, yūṉikārṉ erutu, vaṭikaṭṭi ivi iraṇṭiyum oṉṟākap poṟikka vēṇṭiya avaciyam eṉṉa eṉṟu kēṭṭāl: ataṟku yūṉikārṉ erutum vaṭikaṭṭiyum iṇintatu veṟum kuṟiyīṭutāṉ! eṉṟu patilaḷikkappaṭukiṟatu.
(ii) ērāḷamāṉa yūṉikārṉ muttirikaḷ mokeñco-tarō akaḻvukaḷil kiṭittaṉa. 1922 - 1927 kaḷil naṭattiya akaḻāyvil mārṣal 588 muttirikaḷiyum, 1927 - 1931 kaḷil mekkē 388 muttirikaḷiyum peṟṟaṉar. mekkē mīṭṭa  muttirikaḷil oṉṟu kalipōrṉiya palkalik kaḻaka eṭvarṭ kāṉ tokuppil (Edward Gans collection) catura vaṭivil (29 X 29 cemi) uḷḷatu; ṭiyaṭṭiṭ mākkallil veṭṭi taṇaliliṭṭu valuvūṭṭappaṭṭatu; puttam putiyatupōl irukkum inta muttiriyil vilaṅkum vaṭikaṭṭiyum tulliyamākat terikiṉṟaṉa.
(iii). vēṟu enta cintu nakarilum kiṭikkāta muttiri oṉṟu kujarāt mānila kola - torovil Golo Doro  kiṭittatu. inta muttiriyil oru akalamāṉa kuḻi oṉṟu uḷḷatu; nīḷ caturamāṉa akkuḻikku mūṭi iruntirukkalām; akkuḻikkuḷ curuḷ tāyatti aṭittu vittirukkalām eṉṟu āyvāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar. kola torovil yūṉikārṉ muttirikaḷ aintu kiṭittaṉa eṉpatu mukkiyamāṉatu (pārkka iyal 3).
62(i). ērāḷamāṉa yūṉikārṉ muttirikaḷ mokeñco-tarōviltāṉ (1920kaḷil) kiṭittaṉa; avaṟṟil iraṇṭu veḷḷiyāl āṉavi. 1977 makātēvaṉ paṭṭiyalil cintunākarika  yūṉikārṉ muttirikaḷ eṇṇikki 1,159 eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. (āṉāl ataṟkup piṉṉarum pala iṭaṅkaḷil yūṉikārṉ muttirikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa). makātēvaṉ paṭṭiyaliṉpaṭi yūṉikārṉ muttirikaḷukku aṭutta eṇṇikkiyil  amintavi kuṟuṅkompu erutu muttirikaḷ ākum; avaṟṟiṉ eṇṇikki 95; ivaṟṟil erutiṟku muṉṉāl oru toṭṭi uḷḷatu; atu uṇavut / taṇṇīrt toṭṭiyāka irukkalām. erutiṉ tali antat toṭṭiyi nōkkic cāyn tirukkiṟatu. inta erutu cīpu rakamalla. cīpu raka erutu poṟitta muttirikaḷiṉ eṇṇikki 54 ākum. (1977kkup piṟakum cīpu erutu muttirikaḷ cila kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. 1990kkup piṟpakutiyil HARP āyvāḷarkaḷukkum  harappāvil ivvaki muttirikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. cīpu muttirikaḷiṉ eṇṇikki kuṟintiruppatu viyappāka uḷḷatu).
(ii). cintuk kalip poṟippukkaḷil kāṇum cīpu erutu vaṭivam matac caṭaṅkukaḷil payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām. cintuveḷi nakaraṅkaḷ tōṉṟu muṉpē maṭpāṇṭa ōviyaṅkaḷil cīpu uḷḷatu. cuṭṭamaṇ pommikaḷākavum cīpu erutu kiṭittuḷḷatu. (cintu nākarikam aḻintu) varalāṟṟuk kālam toṭaṅkiya piṟakum kūṭa inta erutu ōviyamākavum, ciṟpamākavum, ēṉ ilakkiyattilum kūṭa iṭam peṟukiṟatu.
63(i). muttirikaḷil poṟitta vilaṅkukaḷ cintuveḷi vāḻ makkaḷuḷ mukkiya kuṭumpaṅkaḷiṉ aṭiyāḷac ciṉṉamā (totem) eṉṟa kēḷvi eḻukiṉṟatu. paṇṭikkālattil vilaṅku / paṟavikaḷ pūrva kuṭikaḷiṉ marapuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷāka iruntaṉa. avaṟṟi  amerikkattolkuṭi (cevvintiyar) moḻikaḷil `ṭōṭṭam (Totem) eṉpar. itupaṟṟi māṉiṭaviyal aṟiñarkaḷ  kūṟuvatu:
ṭōṭṭem eṉpatu puṉitamāṉa aṭiyāḷam. atu kuṭumpam, iṉam, iṉakkuḻu, camutāya amippukkaḷ, vaṇikap perumakkaḷ, araca parampariyiṉar pōṉṟōri atu valintu taṉpāl iḻukkiṉṟatu, avarkaḷuṭiya paḻamip pārampariyatti niṉivuṟuttukiṟatu. perum nilapparappil parantu vāḻum oru kūṭṭattiṉari oṉṟiṇikkum valu ataṟku uṇṭu. ṭōṭṭem eṉṟāl vicēṭa uṟavu muṟi eṉṟu arttam.
(ii). cintuveḷiyil ātikkak kuṭumpam / vaṇika amippiṉ ciṉṉam eṉṟu yūṉikārṉ vilaṅku karutappaṭukiṟatu. atu kaṟpaṉi vilaṅkāka iruppiṉum erutu pōlavē muttirikaḷil nimirnta nērpārviyuṭaṉ uḷḷatu. āḷum varkkamāka irukkalām icciṉṉattiṟku uriyōr eṉpar tolliyalaṟiñar ṣerīṉ iratnākar. taṉatu nūlil avar cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷiṉ vil / puli / mīṉ ciṉṉaṅkaḷ paṟṟiyum avar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. - Ideology and the nature of political consolidation and expansion: An archeological case: Shereen Ratnagar.
(iii). muttiriyiṉ vaṭivattik koṇṭu atu enta nāṭṭiṟku uriyateṉṟu tīrmāṉikkalām. cintu muttirikaḷ caturamākavum, pāracīka vaḷi kuṭāvil (Persian gulf - Barbar) vaṭṭamākavum, mattiya āciyāvil (pakṭrīyaṉ) (Central Asia - Bactrian) perumpālum vaṭṭamāka āṉāl atil cēttira kaṇitap poṟippukkaḷ (Geometric designs) uṭaṉ irukkum. kimu 3000 ām āṇṭaḷavil mecapotāmiya nāṭukaḷiṉ muttirikaḷ uruḷi vaṭivil (cylindrical) iruntaṉa.
(iv). eḻuttu vari vaṭivam (lipi - script) tōṉṟum muṉṉarē muttiri patikkum vaḻakkam tōṉṟiyirukkum. poti kaṭṭiya kayiṟṟu muṭiccu, tiravam aṭitta cāṭi mūṭi ākiyavaṟṟiṉ mītu kaḷi maṇṇi vittu ataṉmēl muttiriyip pativu ceyvārkaḷ. muttirip pativukaḷ, urimi aṭiyāḷamākavum pātukāpput tāyattākavum iṭam peṟṟaṉa. uruḷi muttiriyik kaḷimaṇ mītu uruṭṭip pativukaḷi ceyvārkaḷ. - Seidenberg A. (1975) The ritual origin of geometry: Archive for the History of Exact Sciences: 1 - 488 - 527.
