64. cintuveḷi matam paṟṟiya āyvukku mokeñco-tarō ciṟanta vāyppu aḷikkiṟatu; ataṉ muḻu vaṭivattiyum aṅku kāṇalām. aṅku kiṭitta eccaṅkaḷtām cintunākarikap parappu muḻuvatum paravalāka vaḻaṅkiya potuvāṉa matattiṉ vaṭivatti  viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatuṭaṉ, piratēca rītiyākavum, taṉi napar rītiyākavum nilaviya vaṭṭāra mataṅkaḷ, potumakkaḷ nampikki marapukaḷ ivi aṉittiṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷum mokeñco-tarō vil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. (vaṭṭāra mataṅkaḷum, palvēṟu nampikki marapukaḷum, cintup perumparappil nilaviya  perumatattiṟku māṟāṉaviyalla; avi perum matattōṭu mūraṇpaṭāmal iṇintu ceyaṟpaṭṭaṉa eṉalām).
65. mokeñco-tarō akaḻvukaḷil puki paṭinta cuṭṭa maṇ pāvikaḷ mīṭkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi vīṭṭil tūpa tīpam kāṭṭi vaṇaṅkiya peṇ teyva vaṭivaṅkaḷāka irukkalām. ati kāliyil nīrāṭi, camiyalaṟi aṭuppip paṟṟa vikkum muṉṉarum, pakal poḻutu maṟiyum māli nērattilum, vīṭṭil viḷakkēṟṟik kaṇ mūṭit tiyāṉikkum vaḻakkam matattiṟku appāṟpaṭṭa potuvāṉa mānta iṉa vaḻakkamākum.
66. mokeñco-tarō, harappāvil kiṭitta cuṭṭa maṇ pommikaḷil cilavaṟṟil mēḷattōl karuvi ceytu poruttappaṭṭuḷḷatu. potuvāṉa mata vipavaṅkaḷ naṭantatiyum aṅku mēḷavāttiya oli eḻuppappaṭṭatiyum ivi kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. cintunākarika matam pala vaṭivaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkiyirukkavēṇṭum; mataṅkaḷil uṭpirivukaḷ kālaṅ kālamāka kāṇpavi; itaṟku cintu nākarikamum vitivilakkalla; jāṉ mārṣal cintunākarika matam paṟṟi eḻutiyavaṟṟi paṭittāl itu teḷivākum.


(i) cintu nākarika makkaḷ iṟantōrip
putitta marapukaḷ 

67. iṟantōrip putippatutāṉ mika paṇṭiyakāla marapu. tolliyalil putippiṭaṅkaḷ perum mukkiyattuvam vāyntavi; paṇṭik kāla putippukkaḷ antanta makkaḷ paṟṟiya ariya ceytikaḷi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa; varalāṟṟukku muntiya vāḻkki muṟi, paṇpāṭṭu nampikkikaḷ avarkaḷ maraṇam paṟṟik koṇṭirunta koḷkikaḷ, iṟantōr viṭṭuc ceṉṟa uṭal paṟṟiya cintaṉi, maṟuvāḻvut tattuvaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa pala ceytikaḷi avi uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. putippuṭaṉ toṭarpuḷḷa caṭaṅkukaḷi aṟiya muṭiyātu; avaṟṟit tolliyal āyvu kāṭṭātu. putikuḻikkuḷ uṭalōṭu iṭappaṭum poruṭkaḷ (grave goods) elumpuk kūṭṭu eccaṅkaḷōṭu cērntu, nilittu niṉṟu tolliyal āyvil pala ceytikaḷit tarukiṉṟaṉa.
68. tolliyal tēṭalil kiṭitta ulakiṉ mikap paḻiya iṭukuḻi ekiptil kiṭittuḷḷatu. 55,000 āṇṭu paḻamiyāṉa intak kuḻiyil ciṟuvayatuccaṭalam oṉṟiṉ elumpuk kūṭu kiṭittatu; maṇal pāṅkāṉa piratēcattil kuḻi tōṇṭi antac caṭalattip putittiruntaṉar. 1994il ekiptiṉ vaṭa pakutiyil (Upper Egypt) kaṇṭeṭutta iccaṭalam irutti vikkappaṭṭatu;  muḻaṅkālkaḷ iraṇṭum neñcōṭiṇintu iruntaṉa; mukam kiḻakku nōkki irukkum vakiyil putittiruntaṉar. piṟkāla ekiptilum inta vaḻakkut toṭarntu, atāvatu mukam kiḻakku nōkki vikkum vaḻakkam  uḷḷatu. [55,000 varuṭap paḻamiyāṉa inta ekiptuc caṭalam paṟṟiya nūl:- Predynastic Egypt: Oxford, Shire publications: (2000) Adams B. - Van peer, Vermeers and Paulson (2010) pp 216 - 217]
69. piramiṭukaḷiṉ kālam 4000 āṇṭukku muṟpaṭṭatu. mika uyaramāṉa piramiṭukaḷ kimu 3000kkup piṟaku kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. ataṟku muṉṉar mēṭṭuk kuṭiyiṉar nīḷcatura vaṭivilāṉa kallaṟikaḷil aṭakkam ceyyapaṭṭaṉar; intak kallaṟikaḷ matapā mastaba eṉappaṭṭaṉa. pala matapā vaṭivaṅkaḷi oṉṟiṉmēloṉṟāka aṭukki ucciyil kūrnutiyi amittu imkotep (Imhotep) eṉṟa ekiptiyak kaṭṭiṭakkaliñaṉ piramiṭ vaṭivatti uruvākkiṉār. ataṉāl avaṉiyum oru teyvamākavē ekiptiyar vaḻipaṭṭaṉar.
70(i). putippu cārpāka tolliyalāḷar mattiyil oru kōṭpāṭu nilavukiṟatu: aṭakkam ceyyum muṟikkum paṇpāṭṭu nampikkikaḷukkum nēraṭit toṭarpu uḷḷatu; iti ekiptiya nākarikattil maṭṭumaṉṟi cintu nākarikattilum pārkkalām. ekiptiyarkaḷ rā (Ra) eṉṟa peyaril cūriya vaṇakkam ceytaṉar; cūriyaṉ cāvatilli; mīṇṭum mīṇṭum atikāli utayamākiṟāṉ; ekiptiyarkaḷiṉ maṟuvāḻvu nampikki itiliruntu piṟantatu. avarkaḷ piramiṭukaḷi cūriya oḷi paḷicceṉa viḻum iṭamāṉa pāli nilattilum, mēṟkup pakutiyilum kaṭṭiṉar. avarkaḷukku mēṟku iṟappiṟkuriya tici; caṭalaṅkaḷ cūriya utikkum ticiyākiya kiḻakkip pārttapaṭi vikkappaṭum.
(ii). ekiptiyarkaḷōṭu oppiṭum pōtu cintu makkaḷ iṟantavar uṭalukku atīta matippaḷikkavilli eṉpati uṇaralām. mūtātiyarkaḷukku vaṇakkam celuttiya pōtilum, avarkaḷuṭiya elumpukaḷiyē pōr āyutaṅkaḷ, puḻaṅkum poruḷkaḷ ceytiṭap payaṉpaṭuttiya iṉaṅkaḷum uṇṭu! iṉṟu amerikkāvukkuc (USA) contamāṉa, mēṟku pacupik peruṅkaṭalil uḷḷa kuvām (Guam) tīvil 4,000 āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ kuṭiyēṟiya cāmōro (Chamorros) paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṭam ivvaḻakkam iruntatu. avarkaḷ, caṭalaṅkaḷiṉ kāl, kaṭaṟkariyi nōkki irukkumāṟu putittaṉar; tici paṟṟik kavali illi; kaṭal evvaḷavu tolivil iruntālum paravāyilli -- kālukku nērāka iruntāl pōtumāṉatu! palakālam ceṉṟapiṟaku uṭaliṉ cati maṟṟum tiravappakutikaḷi maṇ tiṉṟu aḻittapiṟaku avarkaḷ mūtātiyiṉ elumpuk kūṭṭi veḷiyē eṭuppar. kāl / ki elumpukaḷil iruntu, īṭṭi muṉi, `mīṉit tākkik kollum kompil poruttum kūriya āyutam, tūṇṭil, vīṭṭuk karuvikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟic ceytu payaṉpaṭuttuvar! maṇṭi ōṭṭi maṭṭum vīṭṭil vittu vaḻipaṭuvar; eñciya elumpukaḷi mīṇṭum putittu viṭuvar. Ancient Chamorros society Cunningham, Lawrence J. The best press, Honolulu (1992) cāmōro paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉar teṉkiḻakku āciyāvil iruntu kaṭalvaḻic ceṉṟu mēṟkupacupik tīvukaḷil kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ.
71. cintuveḷiyil iṟantōr uṭalkaḷi oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa muṟikaḷil aṭakkam ceytaṉar. mukkiyamāka, cintu makkaḷil perumpālōr iṟanta uṭalukku acātāraṇa matippaḷittatākavē teriyavilli. putipaṭiva āyvukaḷiṉ pōtu aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟumāka elumpukaḷ, paṟkaḷ kiṭittaṉa; kuṟippāka rōjtiyil. kiṭṭattaṭṭa 7000 varuṭam iṭiviṭātu nīṭitta mekarkār toṭṭu mokeñco-tarō variyāṉa cintu nākarika varalāṟṟil peruṅkallaṟiyō niṉivuc ciṉṉamō eḻuppappaṭavilli.
72. palvēṟu iṉa makkaḷ vāḻnta cintu nilapparappil cāvuccaṭaṅkukaḷ palavakiyil nikaḻntaṉa. nīḷamāka, mutukuppuṟam nilattil allatu peṭṭiyiṉ aṭippakutiyil paṭumāṟu putippukaḷ iruntaṉa. piṇaṅkaḷuḷ, cila cavap peṭṭikkuḷ vittum, cila cavappeṭṭi iṉṟiyum, nīḷcaturak kuḻiyil putikkappaṭṭaṉa. (akaḻviṉ pōtu marap peṭṭikaḷ cila aḻintu viṭṭapōtilum avaṟṟiṉ eccam kiṭṭiyuḷḷatu; piṇatti veḷiyiṭattil pōṭṭu vittuc cila kālam kaḻittu elumpuk kūṭāṉa piṉṉar putikkum / erikkum vaḻakkamum iruntirukkalām. orē kuḻiyil oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa caṭalattip putikkum vaḻakkam mokeñco-tarō, harappāvilum; peṭṭi allatu maṇṇil putikkum vaḻakkam mekarkār, navṣarō, kālipaṅkaṉ ākiya nakaraṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.

iyal - 8
iṟantōri aṭakkam ceyta muṟikaḷum; cintu nākarika makkaḷ palvaki iṉattavar eṉpatum
cintu nākarika makkaḷ iṉa vakiyil  palavakiyiṉar eṉpatu cuṭṭamaṇ pommikaḷ mūlamākavum, akaḻvukaḷil kiṭitta maṉitelumpu mutaliya eccaṅkaḷ mūlamākavum teriya varukiṉṟatu. appommikaḷiṉ āṭi alaṅkāram, tali muṭi oḻuṅku, talic cōṭaṉi ivi, avarkaḷ pala iṉattavar eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟākum. cintuveḷic cuṭṭamaṇ pommikaḷ perumpālum aṉṟāṭa vāḻkki muṟiyik kāṭṭuvaṉa; āṇkaḷ taliyaṇi headscarf vitavitamāka iruntuḷḷatu; talippāki, irukompu koṇṭa talimuṭi (head - cover with two horns), viciṟikaḷ, uruḷi vaṭivat talik kavacaṅkaḷ, maṭittuc curuṭṭiya toppikaḷ pōṉṟa pala vaṭiva pommikaḷip pārkkalām.
2. peṇkaḷiṉ talimuṭi amippāṉatu uruṇṭi yākkit tūkki vittapaṭiyum, āṇkaḷukku nikarāṉa (allatu kūṭutalāṉa) taliyaṇi kaḷuṭaṉum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. peṇ pommikaḷ tārāḷamākak kaḻuttu aṇi, kivaḷiyal, iṭuppu oṭṭiyāṇam pōṉṟavi poruntiyavi. toṭakkak kālattil avi nirvāṇamāka iruntālum piṟkālattil ciṟiya tuṇi iṭuppil aṇiyappaṭṭatu; mārpup pakuti mūṭappaṭāmal viṭappaṭṭatu. ērāḷamāṉa peṇ pommikaḷ mekarkāril kiṭittaṉa. kālkaḷit taṉiyākac ceytu iṭuppuṭaṉ iṇittaṉar; pommiyiṉ kālkaḷukku valuvūṭṭuvataṟkāka toṭakka kālattil kālkaḷ iraṇṭum oṭṭiyiruntaṉa; piṟkālattiltāṉ kālkaḷ pirintu niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
3(i). āṇ pommikaḷukku oṟṟic caṅkili kaḻuttil aṇiyap paṭṭatu. nirvāṇamāka iruntālum taliyil uyaramāṉa taliyaṇi vittiṭāta āṇ pommi illi. orē pommiyil - āṇ, peṇ aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷi (āṇ kuṟi + peṇ mārpu) oruṅkē koṇṭaviyum - mokeñco-tarōvil kiṭittaṉa; pommikaḷil pala, kiyil kuḻantiyi ēntiyuḷḷaṉa. tāy tālāṭṭum pāvaṉiyum uṇṭu. harappāvil kiṭitta peṇpommi roṭṭikku māvu picikiṟatu! ip pommikaḷil veḷippaṭum `iṉap perukkaviḻivuk karuttit tolliyalāḷar kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar.
(ii). nirvāṇa āṇcilikaḷiṉ āṇ kuṟi veḷippaṭi yākat terikiṟatu; taliyil toppi, kaḻuttil oṟṟic caṅkili; āṉāl iṭiyil tuṇi illi. ippaṭi oru pommiyi mārṣal mokeñco-tarōvil 1931il kaṇṭeṭuttār. harappā civappu jāpar kal uruvattilum āṇkuṟi nīṇṭiruntatu. āṉāl peṇpommikaḷ nirvāṇamā yiruppiṉum peṇ kuṟi `maḻuppi viṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu (atāvatu tulliyamāka kāṭṭavilli); naṭaṉamāṭum veṇkalac cili ciṟanta utāraṇam.
4. innāṭṭiyakkāriyiṉ iṉam paṟṟiya carcci toṭarkiṟatu. 1991il HARP āyvāḷarkaḷ harappāvil ariya cuṭṭamaṇpommi oṉṟik kaṇṭaṉar; atu peṇ ākiyāl `cintu nākarika rati `Venus of Indus’ eṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar. avaḷ kaḻutti  iṟukkum nakikaḷ mūṉṟu cōṭi (chokers), aṭṭikikaḷ (necklaces) ivaṟṟi aṇintiruntāḷ; viciṟi pōṉṟa vaṭivattil talic cōṭaṉi iruntatu; anta viciṟic cōṭaṉikku irupuṟamum ovvoru kiṇṇam poruttappaṭṭiruntatu. iṭuppil oṭṭiyāṇamum, oṭuṅkalāṉa tuṇiyum aṇintiruntālum, mārpukaḷ veḷiyil teriyum vakiyil iruntaṉa. ivaḷ neṟṟi uyarntu āppirikkap peṇpōṉṟa tōṟṟam iruntatu; āṉāl ivaḷ iṉamum tīrmāṉikkappaṭavilli.
5. āṇ kuṟiyit teḷivākak kāṭṭum nirvāṇa maṇ pommikaḷ caṉku-taro, mokeñco-tarō, lōtal ivaṟṟil kiṭittaṉa; mokeñco-tarōvil aintu kiṭittaṉa. (harappāvil oṉṟum kiṭikkavilli). ippommikaḷukku `itipālik (Ithyphallic) eṉṟu peyar. carācariuyaram 12 cemī. itu pōṉṟavi mecapotāmiyāvilum kiṭittaṉavākiyāl avi cintup pakutiyil iruntu  ceṉṟirukkalām eṉṟum cilar karutuvar. pommi ceyta maṇṇip paricōtaṉi ceytatil, inta itipālik cilikaḷ cintuveḷik kaḷimaṇṇil ceytavi eṉṟu terintatu. iccilikaḷukkuc ciṟiya vālum (irukka vēṇṭiya iṭattil) iruntatu. cintuveḷit tayārippāyiṉum mecapotāmiya nākarikat tākkam ivaṟṟil uḷḷatu eṉpar. 
6. palucitāṉ malippakutiyiluḷḷa kulli kuṭiyiruppukaḷil kiṭitta kaḷimaṇ pommikaḷ cintu makkaḷ palvakimiyiṉar eṉpatik kāṭṭuvaṉa. vaṭivam, muka pāvam pōṉṟa amcaṅkaḷilum cintup pāvikaḷil iruntu ivi muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭavi; kikaḷ mikac ciṟiyaṉavāka uṭalōṭu oṭṭiyuḷḷaṉa; kālkaḷ illi (vaṭṭa vaṭivamāka viṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa). kulliyil maṉita pommikaḷiviṭa  vilaṅkiṉa pommikaḷtām ērāḷamākak kiṭittaṉa. pala iṉa makkaḷum, palavakip paṇpāṭukaḷum koṇṭa paranta nilapparappu cintuveḷi ākum.
7. cintuveḷi maṉitarkaḷ aṇivataṟkuriya mukamūṭikaḷ (masks) kiṭittaṉa; āṉāl payaṉpāṭṭu vivaram teriyavilli. muṭṭi vaṭivil kaṇ, mūkku, vāyt tuḷikaḷ viṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; tali ucciyil nīṇṭa kātu allatu kompu pōṉṟa vaṭivam, ivi ammukamūṭikaḷil uḷḷaṉa. enta iṉam eṉṉa tēvikku inta mukamūṭikaḷi payaṉpaṭuttiyatu eṉat teriyavilli.
8. iccuṭṭamaṇ pommikaḷiṉ payaṉpāṭu, nōkkam ivi orupuṟam irukka, tolliyalāḷar kaṇṇōṭṭattil avi palvēṟu iṉamakkaḷ uṟpatti ceytavi eṉṟa karuttu uḷḷatu. pommi uṟpattip pārampariyam mikavum paḻiyatu, cintu nākarikat toṭakkam mutal iṟuti vari iṭiviṭātu pala vaṭivaṅkaḷil ippārampariyam toṭarntatu. jāṉ mārṣal, mekkē pōṉṟa mūtta tolliyalāḷar karuttum ituvē.
9. tolliyal akaḻvukaḷiṉ pōtu cuṭṭamaṇ pāvikaḷ muḻumiyāka kiṭittaṉavaṟṟiṉ eṇṇikkiyi viṭa (ētō kāraṇattiṟkāka) aṭittu noṟukkiya pāvit tuṇṭukaḷtāṉ ērāḷamākak kiṭittaṉa. (payaṉpāṭu muṭinta piṉṉar kaḻivupporuḷāka eṟiyappaṭṭaviyum irukkalām). kaḷimaṇ pommikaḷ paṟṟiya nilippāṭu nākarikattiṟku nākarikam vēṟupaṭukiṟatu; cilanākarikaṅkaḷil avi viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷāka māttiram iruntaṉa. cintu nākarikattilum (cuṭṭamaṇ pommi) vilaṅkukaḷum, erutu pūṭṭum vaṇṭi, ūtum vicil (Whistle) pōṉṟa viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. cuṭṭamaṇṇāl āṉa peṇ pommikaḷum atu pōṉṟavi eṉa eṇṇuvatu tavaṟu. avi perumaḷavukkut tāyt teyva vaḻipāṭṭup paṭimaṅkaḷ / iṉap perukkatti ūkkuvikkum ciṉṉam / taṉimaṉitar vīṭukaḷil aṉṟāṭam vaṇaṅkum poruḷ āka irukkalām eṉpar.  mantiratantiram, ceyviṉi cūṉiyaṅkaḷil payaṉpaṭuttiya poḻutu avaṟṟi aṭittu noṟukkiyirukkalām eṉṟa karuttiṟkum iṭamuṇṭu. marattaṭiniḻalil kirāmap puṟaṅkaḷil `kati collunar tamatu coṟkaḷukku valuvūṭṭa ivvaṭivaṅkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiyirukkavum vāyppuṇṭu.
10(i). peṇ pommikaḷip poṟutta variyil  perumpālāṉa cuṭṭamaṇ pommikaḷiṉ (pālūṭṭutal, makappēṟu cārnta) mārpukaḷum iṭuppup pakutiyum piṟa uṟuppukaḷiviṭap peritākavum kavarcciyākavum ceytaṉar. 
(ii). ivi pommalāṭṭa pommikakaḷ tām eṉṟum cilar kūṟuvar: intiya pommalāṭṭap pārampariyam cintuveḷiyilēyē tōṉṟiviṭṭatu eṉṟa karutti ‘Traces of the origins of puppetry in India to the Indus civilization’ - Ghosh, Massey and Banerjee ākiyōruṭiya, 2008 puttakattil kāṇalām. 
11. cuṭṭamaṇ pommikaḷi ēṟṟumatiyum ceytirukkalām: lōtal akaḻvukaḷil (1955 - 62) maṉita maṟṟum vilaṅkiṉap pāvikaḷ, viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ, erutu, vaṭivaṅkaḷ cuṭṭamaṇ, cempu mākkal ākiyavaṟṟil (tayārittu muṭinta niliyil) kiṭittaṉa.
cintuveḷi maṉita elumpukaḷ paṟṟiya āyvu A survey of the Indus skeletal record
12. cintuveḷi cār elumpuk kūṭukaḷ, piṇam eritta cāmpal, ākiyavi uṭalkaḷip putitta / eritta iṭaṅkaḷ, mēṭikaḷil, kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. elumpuk kūṭukaḷ cila kaḻivaṟikaḷ, kiṇaṟu (kuḷiyal iṭaṅkaḷ) pōṉṟa vaṟṟilum etirpārāta vitamākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. mokeñco-tarōviṉ ucca kaṭṭattil 50,000 makkaḷ vāḻntirukkalām eṉpar. āṉāl peruviyappāka mokeñco-tarōvil iṉṟuvari iṭukāṭu  etuvum veḷivaravilli. “Mohenjo-daro was in every sense a Metropolis” eṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu;meṭrōpāli eṉṟa kirēkkaccolliṉ arttam nāṭṭiṉ / pirāntiyattiṉ poruḷātāra - araciyal - camūka mukkiyattuvam vāyntatum, piṟa nakaraṅkaḷ/ pirāntiyaṅ kaḷōṭu talimi cāṉṟa toṭarpuḷḷatum āṉa mukkiya perunakaram eṉpatākum. (vērccoṟporuḷ tāy nakaram piṟa nakaraṅkaḷ / pirāntiyaṅkaḷukku ip perunakaril iruntutāṉ makkaḷ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟuvar; eṉavē vērccoṟporuḷ valuviḻantu ciṟanta mukkiyattuvam vāynta perunakaram eṉṟa arttam  uruvāki iṉṟuvari nīṭikkiṟatu.
13(i). `meṭrōpāli nakar amintuviṭṭāl caṟṟut tolivil, annakaramakkaḷil iṟantōrip putikka nekrōpāli eṉṟa iṭukāṭum  amiyum. kirēkka moḻiyil “Necropolis” iṟantōr nakaram. paṇṭi nakaram ovvoṉṟilum ivvaki iṭukāṭukaḷ iruntaṉa. piramiṭu, matāpā, iṭukuḻik kuṉṟu ivi niṟinta ekiptiṉ kīcā (Giza) oru nekrōpāli tāṉ. purātaṉa kāla etrakaṉ makkaḷ tamatunāṭṭu elliyōrak kuṉṟukaḷik kuṭintu vīṭu / teru pōṉṟu amittu aṅkē iṟantōrip putittaṉar; atuvē oru iṟantōr nakaramāka āyiṟṟu!
(ii). rōm nāṭṭu ātimakkaḷ, mūtātiyar vaṇakkattik koṇṭi runtatāl uṟaviṉar caṭalaṅkaḷi vīṭukaḷukkuḷḷēyē putittaṉar. kimu449il tāṉ caṭṭappaṭi itit  taṭi ceytu, potu iṭattil putikkum vaḻakkam toṭaṅkiyatu. -Death in Antiquity and its legacy: I.B. Tauris: London (2012).
14(i). ivvakiyil mokeñco-tarō vitivilakkākum. kimu 2600 aḷavil niṟuvi, kimu 1900il (namakku teriyāta kāraṇaṅkaḷiṉāl) kiviṭappaṭṭa, veṇkala ūḻicārnta  innakarattiṟku nirantara iṭukāṭu oṉṟum irukkavilli! `ataṉuṭiya camūka amippu mika uṉṉatamāṉatu; eṉiṉum iṟantōriṉ uṭali aṭakkam ceyvatu paṟṟi kavaṉam celuttiyatākat teriyavilli.
(ii). [1986-š HARP amippi uruvākkiya ṭēl mokeñco-tarōvil neṭuṅkālam āyvukaḷ ceytavar; iṟutiyāṉa āyvi 1964-65 āṇṭukaḷil naṭattiṉār; (ataṟkup piṟaku āḻamāṉa akaḻāyvukaḷip pākitāṉ taṭi ceytu É£lJ)]. aṅku kiṭitta elumpu kūṭukaḷ paṟṟi ṭēl kūṟuvatu: cintu nākarika kālattiṟkuṟiya motta maṉita elumpuk kūṭukaḷ 37 maṭṭumē, 9āṇṭukāla mokeñco-tarō akaḻāyvukaḷil kiṭittaṉa. oṉṟāvatu citadel eṉappaṭum kāpparaṇ vāynta mēṭṭup pakutiyil illi. caṇṭi ētāvatu naṭantiruntāl aṅkutāṉ iṟutit taṟkāppup pōr naṭantirukkum...... harappā nākarika nakaraṅkaḷil naṭattiya virivāṉa akaḻāyvukaḷil āyutam tāṅkiya ākkiramippu cārnta teḷḷatteḷivāṉa ciṉṉañciṟiya cāṉṟum (āriya moḻipēcunar paṭiyeṭuppi nirūpippatākak) kiṭikkavilli. The mythical massacre at Mohenjo-daro : George F. Dales (1979): Ancient cities of the Indus: G.L. Possehl Pp 293-296.
(iii). harappā nākarika nakaraṅkaḷil kiṭitta maṉita elumpuk kūṭukaḷ 4000 āṇṭukaḷ paḻiyaviyāka iruppiṉum iṉṟum pañcāp, kujarāt pakutikaḷil vāḻntu varum iṉattavarkaḷip pōṉṟa iṉaṅkaḷukku uriyaṉavākavē uḷḷaṉa. veṇṇiṟa (Nordic) makkaḷ nuḻintu apperunakaraṅkaḷik kippaṟṟiyiruntāl ataṟkāṉa marapaṇu ātāram (genetic evidence) kallaṟi eccaṅkaḷil kiṭittirukkum. uḷḷūr makkaḷōṭu veḷi iṉam iṉakkalappuc ceytataṟkāṉa ātāramum illi. cintu nākarikam maṟinta piṉṉark kūṭa cintu camutāyak kaṭṭamippil periya māṟṟam ēṟpaṭavilli. ikkaruttittāṉ ṭēl, keṉōyar, jim ṣāpar, kāliṉ reṉpirū ākiyōr koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. - No change in the social structure even after the decline of the civilization: an idea shared by Dales, Kenoyer, Jim Shaffer and Colin Renfrew.
15(i). camakālap piṟanākarikaṅkaḷōṭu oppiṭukiyil cintuveḷik kallaṟi iṭuporuṭkaḷil (grave goods) oru cila tavirap perumpālāṉavi vilimatippaṟṟa cātāraṇap poruṭkaḷē. vilimatippāṉavaṟṟip  pōṭāmal iruntataṟku cikkaṉam kāraṇamākalām. cuṭṭamaṇ pommikaḷi iṭukuḻikkuḷ pōṭṭa kāraṇam puriyavilli. mekarkār kimu 7000 - 5500 kālattil kūṭi, kaṟkaruvi, elumpuk karuvi, maṇi, ki vaḷiyal pōṉṟavi kallaṟiyil iṭappaṭṭaṉa. vilaṅkukaḷ cilavaṟṟiyum iṭukuḻikku arukil paliyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. putitta uṭalkaḷukku irumpu ākciṭ (Iron oxide) maṇṇil ceyta civappu niṟam pūcappaṭṭiruntatu. (piṟkālac cintu iṭukuḻikaḷilum inta marapu iruntatu).
(ii). mecapotāmiya nākarikaṅkaḷil cuṭṭa maṇpommikaḷ iṭuporuḷāka iruntaṉa; ivvaḻakkam cintuveḷiyil paravalāka illi. cintuvil uṟpattiyāṉa cuṭṭamaṇ pommikaḷil atikamāṉavi peṇ pommikaḷ tām. potuvāka matam paṟṟiya ceytikaḷi iṭukuḻip poruḷkaḷ aṟivikkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl harappā iṭukuḻikaḷil matam paṟṟiya ceyti etuvum illi, iṟantōr  uṭalukku atika matippaḷittārkaḷ eṉṟukūṭac colla muṭiyātu! (ekiptiyarkaḷō pūṉikaḷit teyvamāka matittaṉar. iṟanta pūṉi marapukaḷ pōṉṟatu cintuveḷi nākarikattil illi!!).
16. pocēl matippīṭṭiṉpaṭi mokeñco-tarō nakariṉ kīḻp pakutiyil kiṭitta elumpuk kūṭukaḷ 42. avaṟṟi avacara avacaramāka aṭakkam ceytuḷḷamiyāl, atu `paṭukoli nikaḻcci (Massacre); mokeñco-tarō iṟuti nāṭkaḷil atu naṭantirukkalām eṉpatu vīlar karuttu. akkarutti ṭēl, keṉōyar pōṉṟōr `ātāram aṟṟatu; `anta naparkaḷ cāvukkum pōrukkum toṭarpilli eṉṟu nirūpittuḷḷaṉar. āka, `mokeñco-tarō nakaratti kiviṭṭataṟku palātkāra nikaḻcci etuvum kāraṇamilli eṉpatu nirūpaṇamāki uḷḷatu.
17(i). peṇkaḷiṉ (caṅkiṉāl āṉa) ki vaḷiyalkaḷ cārnta āyvu  muṭivukaḷ cila varumāṟu: iṭakkiyil māttiram vaḷiyalkaḷ aṇintapaṭi iṟanta peṇkaḷip putikkum vaḻakkamāṉatu peṇṇiṉ iṉakkuḻu (race) aṭiyāḷamāka irukkalām; allatu tirumaṇamākāta kaṉṉiniliyik kuṟittirukkalām. tirumaṇamāki kuḻanti peṟṟa peṇkaḷ irukikaḷilum vaḷiyal aṇintaṉar. tirumaṇamākāta / tirumaṇamākiyum piḷḷi peṟāta peṇkaḷ oru kiyil  maṭṭum (perumpālum iṭakki) vaḷiyalkaḷ aṇintaṉar.
(ii). `irukikaḷilum vaḷiyal maṇamākik kuḻantip peṟṟatiyum, uyarkuṭippiṟappiyum oruṅkē kāṭṭiyirukkalām. `oru kiyil maṭṭum vaḷiyal tirumaṇamākātatiyum, tāḻnta kulattiyum oruṅkē kāṭṭiyirukkalām eṉṟa karuttum uṇṭu. mokeñco-tarō  veṇkala nāṭṭiyakkāriyiṉ iṭatu kiyil maṭṭum 20kku mēṟpaṭṭa vaḷiyalkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaḷ tirumaṇam ākātavaḷākavum irukkalām, allatu tirumaṇamākiyum tāymi aṭiyātavaḷākavum irukkalām; avaḷ āppirikka iṉap peṇṇāka irukkalām; eṉpatum āyvāḷar karuttu. Durin Caspers E.C.L. (1987) : Was the dancing girl from Mohenjo-daro a Nubian? : Annali 47: pp 99 - 105.
18. makāyōki muttiriyil iruppavariṉ iru kikaḷilum vaḷiyalkaḷ aṭukkiyuḷḷaṉa; eṉavē, atu āṇ vaṭivamalla peṇtāṉ eṉṟu vātiṭuvar cilar. vēṟu cila uruvaṅkaḷuṭiya ki vaḷiyalkaḷil eḻuttup poṟippukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. ēṉ appaṭi, viḷakkam eṉṉa? eṉpatic cintu moḻiyi muḻumiyāka vācikkum vari aṟiya iyalātu. Fran Vogt (1989) ‘Inscribed bangles: An inquiry into their relevance’: South Asian Archeology 1985 : Eds. K. Frifelt and P. Sorenson pp237 - 246, London.
19(i). jāṉ mārṣal taṉatu 1931 perunūlil iṟanta uṭalkaḷ cārntu piṉvaruvaṉavaṟṟiyum kuṟippiṭukiṟār:-
1)	 piṇatti veḷiyiṭattil pōṭṭu atu muḻuvatum elumpukkūṭu ākiya piṉṉar aṭakkam ceytal (fractional burial)
2) uṭali eritta piṟaku kiṭikkum cāmpalit tāḻiyil iṭṭup putittal (Post-cremation urms).
(ii). mokeñco-tarōvil pala iṭaṅkaḷil mutal vakip putippu naṭantirukkalām, (utāraṇam Vs pakuti vīṭu eṇ XXVII eṉṟu mārṣal kuṟip piṭṭiruntālum anta iṭattil kiṭittatu maṉita elumpu alla. atē pōl avar `cāmpal tāḻi eṉak kuṟittavaṟṟil maṉitap piṇac cāmpal illi. ittakiya tāḻikaḷ mokeñco-tarō, harappāvil kāṇappaṭṭālum, oṉṟilum maṉitap piṇac cāmpal illi).
(mokeñco-tarō periyanīccalkēṇi vāyilil kiṭitta 2 tāḻikaḷil elumpō cāmpalō illi. iviyiraṇṭumē erittapiṉ putittaviyum alla. avaṟṟukku maṉitap piṇap putippōṭu toṭarpum illi eṉpar vīlar.  aṭikkal nāṭṭum pōtu allatu etiyō niṉivu kūrntu  iraṇṭiyum putittirukkalām eṉat njh‹W»wJ].
20. Funerary urns eṉṟu mārṣal cuṭṭum tāḻikaḷ ulakiṉ mutircci aṭinta nākarikaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟil (tol tamiḻar nākarikam uṭpaṭa) kāṇalām. itaṉ varalāṟṟik kūṟum nūl hiṭriyōtapiyā allatu tāḻip putippu. Hydriotaphia or urn Burial: Sir Thomas Browne, 1658 il eḻutiyatu. car tāma piravuṉ āṅkila nāṭṭu maruttuvarum pēraṟiñarumāvār. (nārpōk (Norfolk) pakutiyil avar kālattil veṇkalak kālattiṟkuriya 40-50 erikkappaṭṭa maṉitavuṭal eccaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkiya tāḻikaḷ kiṭittaṉa; ataṉ aṭippaṭiyil icciṟanta nūli eḻutiṉār. maṉita uṭali appaṭiyē putippatu tāṉ mikap paḻiya vaḻakkam; atiyaṭuttu uruvāṉa  pārampariyam eriyūṭṭutal eṉpar piravuṉ. iṭuvōr, cuṭuvōr eṉṟu tāṉ maṇimēkaliyum piṇa aṭakka muṟikaḷip paṭṭiyaliṭukiṟatu. maṉita uṭalil perumaḷavu nīr iruppatāl ati erippataṟku eriporuḷ tēvi eṉṟum avar kūṟuvar)  eṇṇey, tāṉiyaṅkaḷ ivaṟṟic cēmikka kirēkkar `pito (pithos) eṉṟa periya cāṭikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; maṉita uṭal eccaṅkaḷi accāṭikaḷil iṭṭu avarkaḷ putittaṉar. tirāy (Troy) perumpōril (kimu 2000) iṟanta vīrarkaḷ uṭaṉukkuṭaṉē eriyūṭṭappaṭṭaṉar. 
21(i). cintu nākarika kāla (kimu 7000-1900) iṟantōr uṭal iṟutic caṭaṅku cārnta eccaṅkaḷ mottam 53ai aṭiyāḷam kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar; mukkiyamāṉavi mekarkār, kulli, kālipaṅkaṉ, lōtal, mokeñco-tarō, harappā, caṉku-taro, rākkikārki, rōjti, curkōtaṭā, cuktākaṉ-tor.
(ii). mokeñco-tarō kīḻppakutiyil piṉvarum iṭaṅkaḷil maṉita elumpuk kūṭukaḷ muḻumiyākavō pakutiyākavō kiṭittaṉa. uṭal citintu elumpuk kūṭāṉa piṉ putitta cāṉṟukaḷum, cāmpal tāḻikaḷum kiṭittaṉa.
I HR - B pakuti catukkam 2, aṟi 74:
22(i). elumpuk kūṭukaḷ patiṉāṉku (13 āṇ, peṇ caṭalaṅkaḷ, 1 ciṟuvayatuc caṭalam) tarimaṭṭattiṟku mika nerukkamākak kiṭittaṉa -- oru caṭalam 30 ce.mī. āḻattil. iṟantavar caṭalaṅkaḷ vīḻnta iṭattilēyē viṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; avarkaḷ orē nērattil iṟantirukkalām;  maraṇa vētaṉiyuṭaṉ iṟantaṉar eṉpati accaṭalaṅkaḷ kiṭanta vitam kāṭṭiyatu. āriyar / ākkiramippup paṭiyiṉar ivarkaḷi kōṭari, vāḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟāl koṉṟirukkalām eṉṟu karutiya āyvāḷarkaḷil vīlar mutaṉmiyāṉavar; intap paṭukoli mokeñco-tarō muṭivuṟṟa kālam cārntatu; intiraṉē kuṟṟavāḷiyāka niṟkiṟāṉ (Indra stands accused) eṉṟār vīlar.
(ii). caṭalaṅkaḷil (aṇintirunta) āparaṇaṅkaḷ, piṟa poruḷkaḷ ivaṟṟuṭaṉ yūṉikāṉ muttiri oṉṟum kiṭittatu. oru elumpuk kūṭṭil aṇikalaṉil iruntirukkak kūṭiya 75 peyaṉ maṇikaḷ 2 ceppu mōtiraṅkaḷ, 2 ceppu maṇikaḷum piṟitoru ceppu aṇikalaṉ pākaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa. aṉittum cintup pakutit tayārippukaḷ.
(iii). itu paṟṟit tīrppuk kūṟiṭa ivveccaṅkaḷip paricōtitta  keṉṉat keṉaṭi (Kenneth Kennedy) takuti vāyntavar; avar karuttu elumpuk kūṭukaḷil kaṇṭa veṭṭukaḷ pōṉṟavi cākum pōtu ēṟpaṭṭavi alla; muṉṉarē ēṟpaṭṭaviyeṉalām; veṭṭukaḷāl iṟantaṉar eṉṟu kūṟa muṭiyātu eṉpar avar. inta 14 naparkaḷum cāvataṟku 30 mutal 70 variyāṉa nāṭkaḷukku muṉṉarē kāyamaṭintiruntu, piṉṉar iṟutiyāka akkāyaṅkaḷ cāvil muṭintirukkum eṉṟu (nipuṇarkaḷukkē iyalpāṉa eccarikkiyuṭaṉ) kūṟukiṟār. oṉṟu maṭṭum teḷivu: aṭakkam mikat turitamāka ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivarkaḷ ēṉ iṟantaṉar cāvukkuk kāraṇam yār eṉpatu teriyātu. avaṟṟiṟkāṉa tolliyal cāṉṟukaḷ illi eṉpatē cariyāṉa  muṭivu.

II iṟantavaṉ pāti - HR-A pakuti:
23. inta (Dead man’s lane) eṉṉum ciṟu teruvil āṇ elumpukkūṭu oṉṟu 1.25 mīṭṭar āḻattil kiṭantatu. kiṭanta iṭattiṉmēl mikappiṟkālattil kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭiyatāl akkūṭu vīṭṭiṉ tarikkaṭiyil  kāṇappaṭṭatu. antak kūṭu aṅkutāṉ mutalil kiṭantatu eṉpatē cantēkam eṉpar pocēl, eṉiṉum mārṣal vitta `cettavaṉ  pāti peyar nilittu viṭṭatu! (elumpukkūṭṭiṟku mēṟkil 15 aṅkulattil ciṟiya maṇṭi ōṭṭiṉ tukaḷkaḷum kiṭittaṉa.)
III tākkappaṭṭuc cetta cāṉṟukaḷ aṭaṅkiya 6 elumpuk kūṭukaḷ Vs pakuti: 
24. mēṟkaṇṭa pakutiyiṉ oṭuṅkalāṉa pāti 4; vīṭukaḷ XVII maṟṟum XXXIII ivaṟṟiṟku iṭiyil 6 elumpukkūṭu kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. intac caṭalaṅkaḷil oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu ciṟuvarkaḷāka irukkalām:
elumpuk kūṭukaḷ kiṭanta vitam tākkutalil cettatik kāṭṭiyatu; 4 elumpuk kūṭukaḷ mallākkak kiṭantaṉa -- 2 oṉṟiyoṉṟu pārttapaṭi.  2 elumpuk kūṭukaḷ vīḻnta iṭattil mukam kuppuṟak kiṭantaṉa. oṉṟu nīṭṭi nimirntu ki, kālkaḷic caṟṟu vaḷittapaṭi kiṭantatu. entap poruḷum, āparaṇamum kiṭikka villi. elumpuk kūṭukaḷ (ceṅkaṟkaḷ illāta) kaḷimaṇ aṟṟa, meṉpaṭala maṇ mūṭik kiṭantaṉa. mokeñco-tarō caṭalaṅkaḷ aṉittum iṟantavuṭaṉ mūṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa, allāviṭiṉ vilaṅkukaḷ avaṟṟic citittirukkum. avacarapputippi uṟaviṉar / naṇpar ceytirukkalām. cettavaruḷ oruvar acātāraṇa uyaramāṉavar: 1.8 mīṭṭar.
IV paṭukoli (massacre) eṉṟu collakkūṭiya 9 elumpuk kūṭukaḷ:
25. ivi muḻumiyāṉa yāṉit tantaṅkaḷ iraṇṭu uṭaṉ DK-G pakuti, catukkam 10 A, nīḷamāṉa oṭuṅkal pātiyil kiṭantaṉa. cintu kālattiya talimuṭivārum cīppu, peyaṉ maṇikaḷ, yāṉit tantaṅkaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ eccaṅkaḷiliruntu,  kollap paṭṭavarkaḷ tiruṭarkaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇac ceykiṉṟaṉa. elumpuk kūṭu oṉṟiṉ kiyil ceppu vaḷiyam iruntatu. elumpuk kūṭukaḷ nerukkamākavum, tāṟu māṟākavum kiṭantaṉa; oḻuṅkāka alla. palātkārac cāṉṟukaḷ teḷivākat terintaṉa; oru maṇṭi ōṭṭil irunta kaṭuṅkāyam uṭaṉaṭiyāka koṉṟirukka kūṭiyatu; maṇṭi ōṭṭip piḷakkak kūṭiya aṭiyiṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa kāyam atu; ivaṟṟil maraṇa avatic cāṉṟik kāṇpatāka mekkē kūṟukiṟār.
V DK-G pakuti, catukkam 8A kiṇaṟṟu aṟi 42 (DK-G Area, Block 8A,well room 42)
26(i) . iṅku kuṟippiṭavirukkum elumpuk kūṭukaḷum mēl eṇ I, III, IV, ākiyavaṟṟil kuṟitta kūṭukaḷum āka mottam 31-m paṭukoli naṭantirukkalām eṉṟa karuttiṟku iṭamaḷikkiṉṟaṉa. innikaḻvukaḷ mokeñco-tarō kaṭici nāṭkaḷil naṭantirukkalām. mattiya teru, mutalām teruc cantippup pakutiyil kiṇaṟṟiṟkuc cellum paṭikkaṭṭil (mēṭṭuppakutiyil iruntu tāḻnta pakutiyil irukkum kiṇaṟṟukku cellum paṭikkaṭṭu) iruvar iṟantu kiṭantaṉar -- (vīḻnta iṭattilēyē paṭikkaṭṭukaḷik kikaḷāl tāvip piṭittu mēlē cellum muyaṟciyil tōṟṟuc cettirukkiṉṟaṉar).
(ii). elumpuk kūṭu oṉṟiṉ tali, tākkutalil nacuṅkiya niliyil mēlpaṭikkaṭṭil kiṭantatu; iṭuppu elumpu kīḻppaṭiyil; iṭatukālum atē paṭikkaṭṭil, valatu kāl nērāka nīṭṭiyapaṭi iruntatu. iraṇṭāvatu elumpuk kūṭu muṟṟākac cētamaṭintu viṭṭapaṭiyāl atu kiṭanta vitam paṟṟi etuvum teriyavilli. iṟakku muṉṉarē kīḻē vīḻntirukkalām eṉṟum karutalām. itu āṇā peṇṇā eṉṟum teriyavilli. (mutal elumpuk kūṭu oru āṇuṭiyatu eṉalām). enta āparaṇamum piṟaporuḷkaḷum iṅku kiṭikkavilli.
27. mēlē I-V ākiyavaṟṟil kuṟitta paṭukolikaḷi nikaḻttiyavar yār? āriya moḻi pēcunarā allatu vēṟṟiṉamā? āriya moḻi pēcunarē  ivaṟṟiyum vēṟu paṭukolikaḷiyum nikaḻttiyirukkalām eṉṟu muṉṉar paravalākak karutappaṭṭa pōtilum, iṉṟu iti aṟiñar yārum ēṟpatilli.  itaṟku mukkiyamāṉa kāraṇam kāla vittiyācam tāṉ. mokeñco-tarō iṉṉaveṉṟu teriyāta kāraṇaṅkaḷukkāka kimu 1900lēyē  kiviṭappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. (rikvētap pāṭalkaḷil mikap paḻamiyāṉavi kūṭa kimu 1000 muṟpaṭṭavi alla; appāṭalkaḷ kimu 1900kku 900 āṇṭu piṟpaṭṭavi ākum).
28. intiyāviṟkuḷ āriyamoḻipēcunar nuḻintatiyum, paṭi palattāl nāṭṭik kippaṟṟiyatiyum paṟṟik kuṟippiṭum āvaṇam alla rik vētam. āriyarkaḷukkiṭiyilēyē naṭanta pōrkaḷiyum uḷḷūr makkaḷuṭaṉ nikaḻnta pōrkaḷiyum paṟṟi atu vivarikkiṟatu. atu cuṭṭum nilapparappukaḷil cintu mākāṇam veṟum ellip pakutiyāka maṭṭumē uḷḷatu. ataṉ miyap piratēcam pañcāp ākum; vētattil varum ceyti etuvum harappāvilō piṟa cintuveḷippakutiyilō nikaḻnta paṭukolikaḷ paṟṟiyatu alla.


rikvēta hariyūpiyā pōrum harappā nakarum
29(i). rik vēta āṟām maṇṭalam VI 27.5 hariyūpiyāvil naṭanta pōrip paṟṟi piṉvarumāṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟatu:-
apyavarttiṉ cāyāmaṉaṉukku utaviya intiraṉ,
varacikāviṉ cantatiyi aḻittāṉ
hariyūpiyāvil avaṉ virccivaṉkaḷiṉ,
muṉ paṭiyaṇiyi noṟukkiṉāṉ;
piṉ paṭiyaṇiyiṉar accattil ōṭiviṭṭaṉar 
(hariyūpiyā paṟṟi rikvētam vēṟu eṅkum kuṟippiṭavilli).
`hariyūpiyā eṉpatu harappā nakarāka irukkalām eṉṟu palar -- kōcampi, mālati ceṇṭukē, pi.el. cāmi, kuruvikkarampi vēlu pōṉṟōrkarutiṉar. `appaṭi pōrnaṭipeṟṟa cuvaṭu etiyum tolliyal āyvil kāṇavilli eṉṟum pōr naṭantiruntāl harappā nakarukku veḷiyil naṭantirukkalām eṉṟum keṉōyar aṇmiyil karutiyuḷḷār. `Hariyupiya and the Rg Veda’ by J.M. Kenoyar in The Indo - Aryan Controversy: edwin F. Bryant and Laurie L. Patton (eds) 2005 Routledge, London.
(ii). keṉōyariṉ 2005 kaṭṭuriyiṉ curukkat tamiḻākkam varumāṟu: rikvētam kuṟippiṭum hariyūppiyātāṉ harappā eṉṟu pala aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar (Datta 1961, Wheeler 1968, Sen 1974,  Singh 1995). iru paṭikaḷukku iṭiyil naṭanta pōr paṟṟik kuṟippiṭum rik vētam, cāyamāṉaṉ makaṉ apayavarttiṉ (puru) oru paṭiyiyum, etirppaṭiyit turuvācakula varacika vamcattavaṉ vircivant paṭittalivaṉum, vaḻinaṭattiṉar eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. ravi natiyiṉ (?) kiḻakkē uḷḷa hariyūppiyāvil intap pōr naṭantatu. tākkutal naṭattiya paṭiyil pāti mēṟkil citaṟiyōṭiyatu; mikutip paṭiyaṇiyi intiraṉ utaviyōṭu apayavarttiṉ tōṟkaṭittāṉ eṉpar ciṅ Singh (1995).
(iii). harappā nākarika kāla allatu (ataṟkup pintiya kālap) pōrt taṭayaṅkaḷ etuvum harappāvil kiṭikkavilli.  nakarukkuveḷiyē pōr naṭantirukkalām. appaṭiyāṉāl anta hariyūppiyā etu eṉat teriyavilli. 
(iv). nilimi ivvāṟu  irukkiyil taṟkāla aṟiñarkaḷ āriya moḻipēcunar paṭiyeṭukkavēyilli eṉkiṉṟaṉar. apyavarttiṉ, turvācaṉ ākiyōr peyarkaḷi rikvētam kuṟippiṭṭālum appōr cārnta ātāram puṟanakarp pakutiyil kūṭa kiṭikkavilli. pōriṉ viḷivu maṟṟum avviḷivāl ēṟpaṭum maṉita iṉakkuḻuk kalappāl uṇṭākum marapaṇu māṟṟam, paṇpāṭṭu māṟṟam pōṉṟaṉavum kāṇappaṭa villi. rik vētattil varalāṟāṉatu nēraṭi allatu kaṟpaṉi mikunta itikācam alla eṉṟu  ceṉeyṭē ē.rakōciṉ 1895 il colliyuḷḷār. - “The Rg Veda does not contin history in the direct narrative or epic form”. The story of the nations: Vedic India: Zenide Ragozin: T. Fisher Unwin, London. 1895.
30(i). aṇmikkāla ārāyccikaḷ harappā nākarika vīḻccikkum ataṉ piṉ nikaḻnta āriyar varukikkum iṭiyil kuṟintatu 300-250 āṇṭu iṭiveḷi irukku meṉak kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa; mokeñco-tarō `paṭukolikaḷukku āriya moḻi pēcunar paṭiyeṭuppu kāraṇamalla eṉpatutāṉ iṉṟiya palatuṟi aṟiñaruṭiya oṭṭumotta nilippāṭu. āyiṉ avaṟṟic ceytavarkaḷ yār? malivāḻ iṉakkuḻukkaḷākavō cintu ellippuṟak kumpalkaḷākavō irukkalām eṉpar mekkē. 
(ii). mokeñco-tarōvil āṭci, oḻuṅku cīraḻinta niliyil etuvēṇṭumāṉālum naṭantirukkalām eṉpar mekkē. oru nakaram cīraḻiya veḷiyār tākkutaltāṉ avaciyam eṉṟu karuta iyalātu. atu tāṉākavē pala vakikaḷil aḻivukaḷiyum tēṭik koḷḷum eṉpataṟku utāraṇam mokeñco-tarōviṉ iṟuti nāḷ naṭappukaḷ ākum:
`paṭukoli ceyyappaṭṭatākak karutiya 31 elumpuk kūṭukaḷuṭaṉ mēlum 6 elumpuk kūṭukaḷi ṭēl 1968 il akaḻnteṭuttār. avi iyaṟki maraṇam cārntavi eṉappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mokeñco-tarōvil piṇattiṉ cati aḻuki aḻinta piṉṉar piṇatti putittati 5 iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇalām.  avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟil maṇṭi ōṭu māttiramē iruntatu.
eritta cāmpali tāḻikaḷil iṭṭup putikkum muṟippaṭi putitta 7 tāḻikaḷ mokeñco-tarōvil kiṭittaṉa. avaṟṟil iraṇṭu (maṉita elumpō cāmpalō iṉṟi) periya nīccalkēṇi vāyilil iruntaṉa.
oru tokuti erintu kariyākiya maṉita elumpukaḷi mārṣal (1931) Vs pakuti, catukkam 4, kaṭṭiṭam, xxi, aṟi 3 l kaṇṭeṭuttār. taṉit tali ōṭukaḷ 3 kiṭittaṉa; avi oṭuṅkalāṉa teru, kuppik kuḻi (Cess pit), maṇṇukku aṭiyil, ākiya etirpārāta iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭittaṉa.
pocēl karuttu: mokeñco-tarōvilum piṟa cintu nakaraṅkaḷilum iṟanta uṭalkaḷi erikkum vaḻakkamum iruntirukkalām; cāmpal maṇṇōṭu kalantirukkalām. paṟkaḷ, cila elumpukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi  erittuc cāmpalākkuvatu kaṭiṉam. ākiyāl avaṟṟi ōṭum nīril / kuḷattil pōṭṭirukkalām (iṉṟum intukkaḷ ceyvatupōla) eṉṟum  cilar kūṟuvar. āka ētō oru vakiyil iṭittup poṭiyākki maṇṇil kalantirukkalām. ikkaruttu ātaramaṟṟa kūṟṟākat tōṉṟukiṟatu.
31(i). harappāvil R 37 iṭukāṭṭil 200 elumpuk kūṭukaḷ kiṭittaṉa. (orē iṭattil mika atika elumpuk kūṭukaḷ kiṭittatu iṅkutāṉ). harappāvilum mūṉaṟu vaki marapukaḷ terintaṉa. 
(1) maṇṇil muḻu uṭalum (vaṭakku - teṟkākap) putippu.
(2) elumpuk kūṭākkiyapiṟaku putippu 
(3) erittapiṉ cāṭiyil iṭṭup putippu.
iṭukāṭu R 37 (Cemetrery R 37) eṉṟa peyaril uḷḷa kuṟiyīṭukaḷi aṭuttu, akaḻāyvu aṭiyāḷa eṇkaḷ ataṟku vaṭakkē iṭukāṭu H (Cemetery H) kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. aṅkum iṅkumāka cila maṉita elumpukaḷ kiṭittaṉa. (elumpuk kūṭukaḷākkiya piṟaku putitta kūṭukaḷum kiṭṭiṉa). [uṭali iṭṭup putikkum vaḻakkamum harappāvil iruntatu. (eritta cāmpali / elumpi) tāḻiyil iṭṭup putittataṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl intac cāṭikaḷukkuḷ maṉita elumpō cāmpalō illātiruntatu viyappāṉatu. mokeñco-tarōvilum inta mātirit tāḻikaḷ kiṭittaṉa) 
(ii). harappāvil 230 cuṭṭamaṇ tāḻikaḷ pala vaṭivaṅkaḷ, parimāṇaṅkaḷil kiṭittaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ ciṟiya pāṉi, muttiri, karuvi, cuṭṭamaṇ viḷiyāṭṭup pommi, tāṉiyam, marac cāmpal, kari pōṉṟavi kiṭittaṉa. orēyoru tāḻiyil maṭṭumē maṉita elumpum cāmpalum kiṭittatu.
(iii). iṭukāṭu H pakuti akaḻvil kiṭitta tāḻikaḷil maṉita elumpukaḷ iruntaṉa. tāḻikaḷ mikac ciṟiyaṉa, 25 - 60 ce.mī. vari uyaram. maṉita elumpukaḷip piṟitōr iṭattil cēkarittu it tāḻikaḷil nirappiyiruntaṉar. avaṟṟi viṭa atikamāka vilaṅku elumpukaḷ, (kuṟippāka māṉ kompu, paṟavi elumpu) eṉpaṉavōṭu eli pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ eccaṅkaḷum tāḻikaḷil iruntaṉa.
(iv). āṇ, peṇ, ciṟuvar, ciṟumi - ākiyōriṉ oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭavar kaḷiṉ eccaṅkaḷi orē tāḻiyil nirappiyiruntaṉar. cila iṭukāṭu H tāḻikaḷil (vayatu kuṟinta) ciṟuvar eccaṅkaḷ maṭṭum iruntaṉa. tāḻikaḷukku iṟukkamāṉa cuṭṭamaṇ mūṭikaḷ pōṭṭu veḷippuṟattil vaṇṇam, ōviyam tīṭṭiyiruntaṉar. eṅkō oru iṭattil perum eṇṇikkiyil maṉita elumpukaḷ kuvintu kiṭantatik kaṇṭavarkaḷ avaṟṟic cēkarittu ittāḻikaḷil nirappi eṭuttu vantu iṅku putittirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉpar cila āyvāḷar. eṉavē ivaṟṟi eritta piṟaku miñcum eccaṅkaḷip putitta  tāḻikaḷāka (Post cremation urns) eṉṟu karutuvatu kaṭiṉam. ittāḻikaḷi nirappum pōtu peyar vivaram teriyātavarkaḷuṭiya eccaṅkaḷi nirappum maṉanili tāṉ mēlōṅki niṉṟirukkum. tam conta uṟavukkārar eccaṅkaḷi ippaṭi evarum nirappiyirukka māṭṭārkaḷ. iṭukāṭu H pakutiyil kimu 1900 - 1500 cārnta 54 tāḻikaḷ kiṭittaṉa.
(v). maṉita eccaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkāta tāḻikaḷip putikkum vaḻakkam iruperum nakaraṅkaḷāṉa mokeñco-tarō, harappāvukkup potuvāṉa amcam. itaṉ nōkkam eṉṉaveṉṟu teriyavilli. cintunākarika marmaṅkaḷil ituvum oṉṟu.
32(i). cintu nākarika ucca kaṭṭa kālam cārnta iṭukāṭu R 37 parappaḷavu 50 x 50 mī. iṭukāṭu G (ataṟkup pintiya) vīḻcciyuṟṟa kālam cārntatu. iraṇṭu iṭukāṭukaḷum arukarukē (ciṟu iṭiveḷiyuṭaṉ) iruntālum pāriya vittiyācaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ippaṭi oru cōṭi iṭukāṭukaḷ mokeñco-tarōvil illi. (R 37 iṭukāṭu pōṉṟa) harappā nākarika ucca kāla  iṭukāṭukaḷi lōtal, kālipaṅkaṉ ivaṟṟil kāṇalām.
(ii). R 37 iṭukāṭṭil muḻu uṭaliyum tarikkaṭiyil vaṭakku -teṟkākap putikkum vaḻakkamtāṉ atikam. uṭali naṉṟāka nīṭṭi maṇṇil mutuku aḻuntum vakiyil putittaṉar. cila putippuk kuḻikaḷukku uṭpuṟam ceṅkal patittiruntatu. cila uṭalkaḷ peṭṭiyil iṭṭup putittavi; peṭṭikaḷ aḻintu viṭṭālum avaṟṟiṉ eccam uṇṭu. puti kuḻikkuḷ pala ciṟiya pāṉikaḷip pōṭṭatōṭu vāḻntapōtu aṇinta āparaṇaṅkaḷ, kaḻuttu māli, maṇikaḷ, ceppu āparaṇaṅkaḷ pōṉṟaviyiyum (peṇkaḷ elumpuk kūṭṭukaḷiṉ kikaḷil maṭṭum caṅku vaḷiyalkaḷ)  cērttup putittaṉar.
(iii). H iṭukāṭṭil muḻu uṭaliyum maṇṇil putikkum vaḻakkam iruntatu. āṉāl anta iṭukāṭṭiṉ kiḻakku, mēṟkup pakutikaḷukku iṭiyil vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. kiḻakkil perumpālum uṭali nīṭṭi, mutuku maṇṇil aḻunta putittaṉar; mēṟkil uṭali veḷiyil viṭṭu vittu (cati nīṅki) elumpuk kūṭāṉa piṟaku putittaṉar. iraṇṭil etilum  tāḻip putippu illi. tāḻip putippukaḷ (mottam 54) iṭukāṭṭiṉ vaṭikōṭip pakutiyil maṭṭumē  iruntaṉa.
(iv). kiḻakku, mēṟkup putikuḻikaḷil vilaṅku elumpukaḷum piṟa poruḷkaḷum iruntaṉa. āṭu pali koṭutta aṭiyāḷam kiṭittatu. kiḻakkup pakutip puti kuḻikaḷil uṭalkaḷ kiḻakku -mēṟkākak iruntaṉa. oruvakik kuḻappamum iruntatu. cila mēṟku - kiḻakkākavum, vēṟu cila vaṭakku - teṟkākavum, vēṟu cila teṟku vaṭakkākavum putikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. kāraṇam teriyavilli.
(v). vēṟu iṭattil cēkaritta noṟuṅki urukkulinta maṉita elumpukaḷ mēṟkup pakutiyil putikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa; cila pāṉikaḷum antak kuḻikaḷil kiṭantaṉa. R 37, H iṭukāṭukaḷ ēṉ ippaṭi vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa? mutalāvatāka iraṇṭum vevvēṟu kālam cārntavi. R 37l vacati, celvākku, mēṭṭukkuṭi vāḻkki ivaṟṟukku uriyavarkaḷip putittu irukkalām. H iṭukāṭṭil vacati kuṟinta ēḻikaḷ, āpattu / kuḻappak kālaṅkaḷil cettavarkaḷi putittirukkalām. elumpukaḷum putippu muṟikaḷum ōraḷavukku iti uṇartta muṭiyum.
33(i). maṉitaṉ tōṉṟiyanāḷtoṭṭu vaḷarntacūḻal, vāḻkkiccumi, uṇavu pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ tākkaṅkaḷic camāḷittukkoṇṭu avaṉ naṭattiya vāḻkki paṟṟiya ceyti avaṉ uṭaṟkūṟṟiyal varalāṟāka ākukiṟatu. ivvakit  tākkaṅkaḷi aṉivar elumpukaḷilum kāṇalām. (taliyil perum paḷu cumakkum āṇ, peṇkaḷiṉ kaḻuttu elumpil periya māṟṟaṅkaḷ irukkum).
(ii). cintuveḷiyil kiṭitta veṇkalak kāla elumpuk kūṭukaḷi keṉaṭi (Kenneth K.R. Kennedy) āyvu ceytu  iṟantavarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟi, uṭal ārōkkiyam, uṇavu, nōykaḷiṉ tākkam, uṭal vaḷarcci pōṉṟa vivaraṅ kaḷi aṟiya utaviṉār; intat tuṟiyiṉ tanti ivar eṉalām. Hemphill B.E., Lucas J.R. & Kennedy K.A.R, (1991): “Biological Adaptations and affinities of the Bronze Age Harappans”: Harappa excavations 1986 - 1990: Ed. R. Meadow Pg.137 - 182.
34(i). iṭukāṭu R 37 il putikkappaṭṭavarkaḷiṉ uṭal ārōkkiyam, vaḷarcci ivi mika naṉṟāka iruntaṉa;taramāṉa uṇaviṉmi ataṉāl ēṟpaṭum nōykaḷ ivi illi; toṟṟu nōykaḷum illi; uyirāpatti viḷivikkum kāyaṅkaḷ uṭalil illi. mūṉṟu caṭalaṅkaḷiṉ uṭal vaḷarcci maṭṭum naṉṟāka illi. vātanōy  pīṭittavar; mutuku / kaḻuttu vali, uḷḷōr iruntaṉar; cilar paṟkaḷ citintu ārōkkiyamaṟṟu iruntaṉa. R 37, H iṭukāṭukaḷ paṟṟiya elumpuk kūṭu āyvukaḷukku ivvāṟu keṉṉaṭi ṭēl iruvarum vittiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
(ii). harappāvil iraṇṭu iṭukāṭukaḷilum kiṭitta elumpuk kūṭukaḷ paṟṟi mūṉṟu mukkiya viṉākkaḷ eḻukiṉṟaṉa. 
(1) iraṇṭu iṭukāṭṭu elumpuk kūṭukaḷukku iṭiyilāṉa uyiriyal / uṭaṟ kūṟṟiyal uṟavu paṟṟiya karuttu? iṭukāṭu H ṉ kīḻppakutic caṭalaṅkaḷum iṭukāṭu R 37ṉ elumpuk kūṭukaḷum ottuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl iṭukāṭu H‹ mēṟpakutic caṭalaṅkaḷ R 37, H‹ kīḻppakutic caṭalaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiliruntu vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. putippu muṟi vēṟupāṭukaḷum uṇṭu.
(2) cintuveḷip piṟa iṭukāṭukaḷil kiṭitta elumpuk kūṭukaḷukkum R 37,  iṭukāṭukaḷil kiṭittavaṟṟukkum  kūṭukaḷukkum iṭiyilāṉa uyiriyal (biology) uṭaṟ kūṟṟiyal anatomy vēṟupāṭu, oṟṟumikaḷ eṉṉa? vaṭakku harappā, teṟku mokeñco-tarō ākiyavaṟṟiṟkiṭiyil uyiriyal vēṟupāṭu uḷḷatu. inta vaṭakku - teṟku vēṟupāṭu muttirikaḷilum uḷḷatu. piṟa intiya iṉam oṉṟōṭum mokeñco-tarō elumpuk kūṭukaḷ ottirukkavilli. (āṉāl kiṭitta mātirikaḷ cila; avaṟṟil kūṭukaḷiṉ kāla nirṇayamum uṟutiyilli eṉpati maṟakkak kūṭātu).
(3) intiya mātirikaḷi (samples) piṟavaṟṟuṭaṉ, kuṟippāka intiyāvukku mēṟkil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ oppiṭumpōtu cintuppakuti makkaḷiṉat toṭarcci nīṭittatā? cintu nākarikam maṟinta piṉṉar attakiya toṭarpu nīṅki Biological discontinuity ēṟpaṭṭataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. āṉāl atu kimu 200kku muntiya caray kōlā (Sari Khola) viṉ irumpuk kālam cārntatu. toṭarpaṟṟa piṟa cāṉṟukaḷ kimu 200kkup pintiyavi eṉpar hemphil, lūkkā, keṉṉaṭi ākiyōr tamatu 1991 nūlil.


lōtal (Lothal)
35. lōtal tuṟimukattiṉ mukkiya kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷiṉ  kaṟpaṉi uruvākkatti ivviyal iṟutiyil kāṇalām. iṅkuḷḷa oru ciṟiya iṭukāṭṭil mīṭṭa elumpuk kūṭukaḷi keṉṉaṭi āyvu ceytu kūṟiya karuttukaḷ varumāṟu:
1)	mokeñco-tarō, harappāvuṭaṉ oppiṭumpōtu lōtal -il māṉiṭa iṉap palvakimi heterogeneity atikam. (atāvatu lōtalil vāḻnta mānta iṉa eṇṇikki muṉṉiraṇṭi viṭa atikam)
2) paṇṭi lōtal makkaḷ camakālac cintu makkaḷi ottavarkaḷ;  cintumakkaḷil iruntu vēṟupaṭṭavarkaḷ allaveṉṟa kūṟṟi itu ātarikkiṟatu. 
3) iṉṟiya intiyāvil uḷḷa intap pakuti makkaḷukkum paṇṭu vacittavarkaḷukkum iṭiyil uyiriyal toṭarcci uḷḷatu. (atāvatu mānta iṉa vakiyil vēṟupāṭu illi)
4) cila lōtal elumpuk kūṭukaḷil kāṇum vēṟupāṭukaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil putikkappaṭṭavarkaḷ vēṭṭi, tāvara uṇavu ākiyavaṟṟit tēṭiya paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ pōṉṟavar eṉalām; ittakiya paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉariṉ cantatiyiṉar iṉṟum  intiyāvil kuḻukkaḷāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉar.
36(i). varalāṟṟukku muntiya kujarāt - harappā makkaḷukkum appaḻaṅkuyiṉarukkum iṭiyil marapaṇut toṭarpu uḷḷati  āyvu kāṭṭukiṟatu.
(ii). lōtal iṭukāṭṭil (orē putikuḻiyil 2 caṭalam putitta) nāṉku kuḻikaḷ iruntaṉa. itu uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟum catiyi niṉivūṭṭuvatu. āṉāl lōtalil oṉṟāka eritta caṭalaṅkaḷ putikkappaṭavilli. cetta iruvar uṭalum eriyūṭṭappaṭṭavi alla; ākavē, itu cati nikaḻcci alla. orē kuḻiyil iruvarum āṇākavō / peṇṇākavō maṭṭumē  irukkiṉṟaṉar. (appaṭi orē pāliṉattic cērntavarkaḷ alla eṉpavar caṅkāliyā!)
(iii). mokeñco-tarō, harappā uḷḷiṭṭa aṉittuc cintu kuṭiyiruppukkaḷilum piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum perumpālum piṇaṅkaḷ erikkattāṉpaṭṭaṉa eṉpar mekkē; lōtal marapum ippaṭittāṉ iruntirukkum. cintu nākarika makkaḷum lōtaliṉ kiḻakkup pakutikaḷil vāḻnta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷum kalantataṉ kāraṇamāka putiya marapaṇukkaḷ uṇṭāṉati āyvu kāṭṭukiṟatu.
(iv). lōtal cintuveḷiyiṉ teṉ - kiḻakkil amintatu;  vaṇikam, ēṟṟumatikkāṉa āparaṇat tayārippu nakaram; eṉavē lōtal makkaḷ āciyak kaṇṭattiṉ kiḻakku / teṟkup pakutikaḷōṭum toṭarpu  koṇṭiruppar. ippōtu karnāṭakam irukkum teṉ pakuti aṉṟu paranta tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭapakutitāṉ.  intap pakutiyil kiṭitta taṅkam, ariya kaṟkaḷ, neruppu ākka utavum cikkimukkik kaṟkaḷ (Flint stones) pōṉṟavi lōtal toḻilakaṅkaḷukku ēṟṟumati ākiyirukkavēṇṭum. lōtal vaṇikarkaḷ `paṇiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar; itu vaṇikar eṉṟa tamiḻc colliṉ tiriputāṉ. kāvirip paṭukiyil kāṇpatu pōṉṟa vaṭṭak kiṇaṟukaḷ lōtalilum uḷḷaṉa.


kālipaṅkaṉ (Kalibangan)
37. kālipaṅkaṉ iṭukāṭṭiṉ amiviṭam mikavum nuṇukkamākat terivu ceytatākum. makkaḷ vāḻnta pakutikaḷil iruntu 300 mīṭṭar tolivil, turnāṟṟam, maḻi veḷḷam pōṉṟavi makkaḷip pātikkāta ticiyil amikkappaṭṭu iruntatu. kālipaṅkaṉil mūvakip putippukkaḷ iruntaṉa:  mutal rakattil māttiramtāṉ maṉita eccaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa;
(1) nīḷcatura / muṭṭivaṭivak kuḻiyil caṭalatti nīṭṭip putittal (inta vakip putippukaḷ 88)
(2) vaṭṭamāṉa kuḻikaḷil pāṉikaḷi māttiram putittal, (maṉita eccaṅkaḷ oṉṟum iṉṟi).
3) nīḷcatura / muṭṭi vaṭivak kuḻikaḷil pāṉikaḷi maṭṭum (maṉita eccam iṉṟip) putittal; maṉita eccaṅkaḷ kiṭiyātu.
38. mutal rakap putippukaḷil iruvar-mūvar putikkap paṭṭiruppatāl avi kuṭumpaṅkaḷukkuc contamāka irukkalām eṉpar. mutal iraṇṭuvakip putippukaḷ kālipaṅkaṉil atikam. mūvakip putippukaḷiṉ motta eṇṇikki 102. kālipaṅkaṉiṉ makkaḷ toki mika atikamākiyāl, caṭalaṅkaḷ perumaḷavil erikkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum. kālipaṅkaṉ kuṭiyiruppiṉ 600 āṇṭu kāla aḷavil 2500 pērāvatu cettirukka vēṇṭum āṉāl cuṭukāṭṭu mēṭu / mēṭi ivi oṉṟum veḷivaravilli. iraṇṭām, mūṉṟām vakip putippukaḷil maṉita eccaṅkaḷ illāmiyāl, avaṟṟil kāṇāmaṟ pōṉavar, tūratēcaṅkaḷil iṟantu iṅku aṭakkam ceyyappaṭṭavar ākiyōri niṉivu kūrum nōkkil amintirukkalām. orē iṭukāṭṭil immūṉṟu vakikaḷum kāṇappaṭṭālum, mutalrakap putippukaḷ ciṟitu tolivil iruppatāṉatu oruvaki vēṟupāṭṭik kāṭṭuvatākalām.
39. kālipaṅkaṉukku mēṟkē 90 ki.mī. tolivil (nīrvaḷamuḷḷa caravati āṟṟaṅkariyil) tarkkaṉvālā tērā (Tarkanwala dhera) eṉṟa kimu 2600 - 2500  cārnta ciṟiya kuṭiyiruppil naṭanta akaḻvil eriyūṭṭum mēṭi oṉṟum, atil aintu variyāṉa eriyūṭṭalkaḷ naṭanta cāṉṟukaḷum teriyavantaṉa. cintunākarikakkāla nakaram oṉṟil cantēkattiṟku iṭamaṟṟa eriyūṭṭal cāṉṟu kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa iṭam itu. iṅku kaṇṭatu pōṉṟa mēṭi ituvari eṅkum cintuppakutiyil kiṭṭavilli. 
40. cintu makkaḷi palvēṟuvaki māntiṉaṅkaḷiṉ kūṭṭamippu - Union of  ethnic types eṉṟu kūṟuvār pocēl (2002 iyal 6):
cintunākarikam paṟṟiya inta āyviṉ aṭippaṭik karutukōḷkaḷil oṉṟu, uṇavuuṟpattit toṭakka kālam mutal, mutircciyuṟṟa harappā nākarikak kālam, nakarppuṟa nākarikattiṟkup pintiya kālam ivi īṟāka, ellāk kālaṅkaḷilum, intap pirāntiyattil palatarappaṭṭa mānta iṉaṅkaḷ vāḻntaṉar eṉpatākum. One of the assumptions in this study of the Indus civilization is that there were many kinds of people in the region, from the beginnings of Food Production through the Mature Harappan and Post-Urban times. 
palvaki iṉa makkaḷ vāḻntataṟkāṉa ātāram tolliyal cāṉṟukaḷil uḷḷaṉa eṉpar pocēl. toṭakka kāla harappā nākarikattiṉ nāṉku pirāntiyaṅkaḷiṉ iṇippākac cintu nākarikam uruppeṟṟatu eṉpar avar. nāṉku pirāntiyaṅkaḷumē 
-- amri nal, koṭṭiji, coti - civāl, ṭāmp catāt - cama kālattavi: kimu 3200 - 2600. pocēliṉ 2002 nūlit tiṟaṉāyvu ceyta ṭi.pi. akarvāl (Review: D.P. Agarwal www. Infinity. com). cintunākarikam oru palvakimi amippu - paṇpāṭṭu, makkaḷ, puviyiyal palvakimi - The Indus civilization was an organization of diversity -- diversity of cultures, people and Geography eṉṟu pocēl kūṟiyati ātarikkiṟār. cintu  nilapparappu iṇintu iyaṅkiyatu; pirāntiya aḷavil moḻi, mata, paṇpāṭṭu vēṟupāṭukaḷ ciṟcila iruntirukkalām; eṉiṉum aṭippaṭiyil oru oṟṟumi Unity in diversity - iruntatu. cintu nākarikattiṉ ciṟappum valuvum atutāṉ. cintu nākarika nalivuk kālattil vēṟupāṭukaḷ mika atikamākiyatōṭu putupputu vaṭivaṅkaḷum eṭuttaṉa. `cintu nākarikam aḻiyavilli, putiya vaṭivam eṭuttatu eṉṟa  kūṟṟiṉ arttam itutāṉ.
41(i). akaḻāyvil kiṭitta maṉita elumpuk kūṭukaḷum eccaṅkaḷ, neṭuṅkālam toṭarnta cuṭṭamaṇ pommip pārampariyam, vaḻipāṭukaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟil vēṟupāṭukaḷ cila avvappoḻutu ēṟpaṭṭati kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
(ii). elumpuk kūṭukaḷi āyvu ceyta aṟiñar cintu makkaḷil perum eṇṇikkiyiṉar uṭaṟ kūṟu teṉṉintiya tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ uṭaṟ kūṟukaḷukku mika neruṅkiyatu; eṉiṉum naṇṇilak kari makkaḷ, ātiralāyṭ, maṅkōliyac cāyalkaḷ cila kalantirukkalām eṉpar. Based on skeletal records, a conclusion is reached that the Indus people were in the majority, similar in features to the Dravidians of South India, with an admixture of Mediterranean, Australoid and Mongolian types.
(iii). elumpukkūṭṭu āyvil eṭṭiya iru muṭivukaḷ mukkiyamāṉavi: (mokeñco-tarō, lōtal ākiya iru miyaṅkaḷ tavirtta) piṟa cintuveḷi miyaṅkaḷil vāḻnta makkaḷaṉivarum mānta iṉa vakimiyil mika neruṅkiyavarkaḷ. piṟa cintuppakutu makkaḷi viṭa mokeñco-tarō makkaḷ uṭaliyal cārnta vēṟupāṭu atikamāka koṇṭavarkaḷ. tirāviṭa moḻi pēcum makkaḷ paranta intiyā muḻuvatum aṉṟiya mēṟku intiyā uṭpaṭap parantu vāḻntaṉar. cintu nākarikamē tol tirāviṭa nākarikamtāṉ. mokeñco-tarō uḷḷa cintu mākāṇattil maṭṭumaṉṟip paluccitāṉilum tirāviṭa moḻi pēcum pirākuy (Brahui) makkaḷ iṉṟum vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. pirākuy makkaḷil perumpālōr cintu mākāṇattil tāṉ vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. paluccitāṉ vāḻ pirākuy makkaḷ eṇṇikki kuṟivu. iru pakutikaḷilum vāḻum pirākuy makkaḷ toki 1990 l 25 ilaṭcam. mikac ciṟiya eṇṇikkiyiṉar īrāṉ, āpkāṉitāṉ, nāṭukaḷil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. 
(iv). tirāviṭa moḻi iṉṉoṉṟip pēcum makkaḷ ākiya kantar Gond makkaḷ 93,19,000 pēr (1991) mattiya intiyāvil palamānilaṅkaḷil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar (ivarkaḷiṉ talivaṉ tāṉ tacaratarāmaṉ koṉṟa irāvaṇaṉ eṉṟu tolliyalāḷar caṅkāliyā iṟutivari vitaṇṭāvātam purintavar!)
(v). mēṟku vaṅkāḷam, vaṅkāḷa tēcam ākiyavaṟṟil kuruk (Kuruk) eṉṟa tirāviṭa moḻiyip pēcum iṉam vāḻkiṟatu.
42. cintuveḷi, tol-tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉar vāḻnta iṭamtāṉ. tol tirāviṭam, tol- tamiḻ iraṇṭum oṉṟē; vēṟupāṭu illi. āciyā kaṇṭattil perum pakutiyil tol-tirāviṭar paravi vāḻntaṉar eṉpatu aṟiñar ēṟṟuḷḷa uṇmi;  cintumoḻi, nākarikam, paṇpāṭu ivaṟṟit tol - tamiḻiyamākattāṉ pārkka vēṇṭum. intō - āriyamoḻi vaḷarccikkum tol-tamiḻ ātāramāka iruntuḷḷatu. erṭōci (Erdosy) eṉṟa āyvāḷar intō - āriya moḻikaḷiṉ moḻiyamippilum tirāviṭat tākkam iruppataṟku intō - āriya moḻi pēcuvōr muṉṉāḷil tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunarāka iruntatē mukkiya kāraṇam eṉpar. moḻiyiyal, oliyiṉiyal pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil intō - āriya moḻikaḷ iṉṟum tirāviṭa moḻi pōṉṟē irukkiṉṟaṉa. tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ paṅkaḷippu aḷappariyatu. tirāviṭac coṟkaḷālum pārampariyattālum intō - āriya moḻi ilakkiyam ciṟappuṟṟuḷḷatu. mēlum oliyamippu (Phonology) coṟṟoṭar (syntax) coltoki (Vocabulary) eṉṟa mūṉṟu tuṟiyilum intō - āriya moḻikaḷ perumaḷavukkut tirāviṭak (tamiḻiyak) kūṟukaḷiyē koṇṭuḷḷaṉa.
43. mokeñco-tarō, harappā nakara iṭipāṭukaḷil kāṇpatu paṇṭit tirāviṭak kaṭṭumāṉak kalitāṉ. vīṭṭup poruḷkaḷ, eṭi - aḷavukaḷ, āparaṇaṅkaḷ, karuvikaḷ, āṭikaḷ, parutti necavukaḷ aṉittum tirāviṭam cārntavi. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ ciṟṟilakka muṟiyiyē cintuveḷi nākarika eṭi aḷavīṭukaḷil kāṇkiṟōm eṉpatic ceṅkam vēṅkaṭācalam maṟukkoṇātavāṟu niṟuviyuḷḷār (ēṉi aṟiñar karuttum atutāṉ). piṟkālattil intumatam eṉap peyarpeṟṟa kūṭṭu matattiṉ mutaṉmik kūṟukaḷ tamiḻar kaṇṭa civattiṉ kūṟukaḷē. pavuttam, jiṉam eṉpaṉa āriya matattiṟkut tirāviṭar kāṭṭiya etirppil tōṉṟiya mataṅkaḷākum. tamiḻar vaḷartta āṭal, pāṭal, nuṇkalikaḷ tām iṉṟu intiyak kalikaḷāka vaṭiveṭuttuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻiya makkaḷiṭamiruntē intō āriya moḻipēcunar nākarikam peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉpataṟku cintuveḷit tol tamiḻiyac cintu nākarikam: 2014 : pi. irāmanātaṉ, tamiḻmaṇ patippakam, ceṉṉi. 17 nūl mēlum virivāṉa mūla ātāraṅkaḷit tarukiṟatu.
44. uruva vaḻipāṭu tamiḻarkaḷukkē uriyatu. uruva vaḻipāṭṭi āriyar, cintuveḷittamiḻarkaḷiṭam iruntē peṟṟār; aṟiñar carvapaḷḷi irātākiruṣṇaṉ karuttum atuvē; (kirēkkarkaḷiṉ tamiḻakat toṭarpukkup piṟakutāṉ tamiḻariṭam uruva vaḻipāṭu tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟa kūṟṟit tolkāppiyam tavaṟu eṉak kāṭṭukiṟatu; aivaki teyvaṅkaḷik kuṟippiṭum tolkāppiyam ovvoru teyvattiṟkum taṉi uruvam amittut tēvarp parāya muṉṉilik kaṇṇē (tol. cey. 138) eṉkiṟatu).
45. toltamiḻarkaḷ teṟkiliruntu vaṭakkākap paravic cintunākarikatti uruvākkiṉar; cintuveḷip parappaḷavu perum eṇṇikkiyāṉa makkaḷik koṇṭiruppiṉum, palvēṟu paṇpāṭṭu viḻumiyaṅkaḷiyum koṇṭiruppiṉum mēlōṅki irunta moḻi, mata, paṇpāṭṭuk kūṟukaḷ tol - tamiḻarkaḷuṭiyaṉavē. iṉṟiya intu matam cintuveḷiyil tōṉṟiyatu. iṉṟu kaṭalaṭiyil uḷḷa peṭ-tuvārakā (Bet - Dwaraka) cintunākarikattiṉ perum tuṟimukaṅkaḷil oṉṟu. kōṭṭi aminta tuvari eṉṟa nakar āṉatu, vēḷir iṉat tamiḻar āṇṭatu; tuvari  tuvarakā eṉṟum vēḷ  peṭ / pēṭ eṉṟum māṟiyuḷḷatu.uvarā īkit tuvari āṇṭu 49 vaḻimuṟi nta vēḷiruḷvēḷ ākiya iruṅkō vēḷ paṟṟiyavi puṟanāṉūṟṟil 201-202 pāṭalkaḷ (kapilar eḻutiyavi) 1iruṅkōvēḷ vaḻiyiṉar eṉat tammik kūṟik koḷpavarkaḷ iṉṟum tūtuttuk kuṭīmāvaṭṭattil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. (BSOAS: XXXII - 2 June 1969).
46(i). putiya kaṟkālat (Neolithic) tamiḻarkaḷum, cintuveḷit tamiḻarkaḷum tol-tamiḻ moḻiyip (Pre/Proto Tamil) pēciṉar eṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu. cintu eḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷiṉ payaṉpāṭu kimu 1900kkup piṉ niṉṟuviṭṭatākak karutiṉālum (tuvārakā akaḻvukaḷiliruntu) ataṟkup piṉṉarum inta eḻuttup poṟippukaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiyataṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. pūmpukāriṉ mēlapperumpaḷḷattil tamiḻnāṭu tolliyal tuṟi mē 2007il `ampuk kuṟiyiṭṭa pāṉi ōṭukaḷi akaḻnteṭuttuḷḷatu. inta ampuk kuṟi pōṉṟavi cintuveḷi muttirikaḷilum uḷḷaṉa.
(ii). kaḻi toṉmi vāynta poṟippukaḷ (cintup poṟippukaḷiṉ maṟuvaṭivam pōṉṟē) tamiḻnāṭu tiṇṭivaṇam pakuti cāṉūr, kōvi māvaṭṭam cūlūr, kēraḷa mānilam muciṟi ākiyavaṟṟil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. (harappā nākarika kālattiṟkup pintiya kallāyutam kimu 2000-1500 cārntatu) mayilāṭutuṟiyil 2007-l kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭatu. atil poṟitta eḻuttukaḷ cintumoḻi eḻuttup poṟipputāṉ eṉpati ai.makātēvaṉ tiṭṭavaṭṭamāka niṟuviyuḷḷār. pākitāṉil cintunākarika iṭaṅkaḷ āyirakkaṇakkil iruntum āyvu etuvum mēṟkoḷḷap paṭuvatilli; iṉiyum annilitāṉ. intiyāvil uḷḷa cintunākarika iṭaṅkaḷilāvatu intiya aracu mēlum virivāka āyvu ceyya vēṇṭum. pirrāṉā pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil virivāṉa akaḻāyvukaḷ iṉṟum (2015) toṭarkiṉṟaṉa.
47. harappā nākarikam maṟinta piṉṉartāṉ intō- āriya moḻi pēcunar vaṭa mēṟku intiyāvukkuḷ nuḻintaṉar eṉpar ṭi. ṭapiḷyū. antōṉi - The horse, the wheel and language (2007) Princeton University. atē kālakaṭṭattil āriya moḻi pēcunarō, piṟarō perum eṇṇikkiyil vaṭa intiyāviṟkuḷ nuḻintataṟkāṉa cāṉṟu eviyum ituvarik kiṭṭiyuḷḷa elumpuk kūṭukaḷ evaṟṟilum illi eṉṟu keṉṉat keṉṉaṭi teḷivāka viḷakkiyuḷḷār..


iyal - 9
cintunākarikattil poruḷātāram 
- ēṟṟumati iṟakkumatikaḷ

toṭakkam mutal iṟuti vari cintunākarikap pakutiyi vāḻa vittatu vēḷāṇmiyum kālnaṭi vaḷarppumē. cintu nākarika uccakaṭṭa kālattil vāḻnta makkaḷ toki 5 ilaṭcattiliruntu 10 ilaṭcam varitāṉ iruntirukkum eṉpatu aṟiñarkaḷ muṭivu ākum (appōtu ulaka makkaḷ motta tokiyē 8 kōṭitāṉ!). cintunākarikam celvacciṟappu mikuntu iruntataṟku ataṉ veḷināṭṭu vaṇikamē aṭippaṭik kāraṇam. cintut toḻilaka uṟpatti perumaḷavil ēṟṟumati nōkkil naṭantatu. cintu muttirikaḷiṉ mutaṉmip payaṉpāṭu ēṟṟumatip potikaḷip pativu ceyvatākum. ērāḷamāṉa yūṉikārṉ muttirikaḷ iruppataṟkuk kāraṇam cintuveḷiyiṉ mikap periya ēṟṟumatik kūṭṭamippiṉ vaṇika aṭiyāḷamāka (Trade mark) atu (yūṉikārṉ) iruntirukkalām eṉpatākum.
2. paṉṉāṭṭu vaṇikam kimu 3000 aḷavil toṭaṅkiyatāka verē kārṭaṉ cilṭ  Vere Gordon Childe karutukiṟār; atu tari, kaṭal ākiya iru vaḻiyākavum naṭantatu. tari vaḻi vaṇikam kaṭalvaḻi vaṇikattiṟku muntiyatu. 3000 varuṭaṅkaḷukku mēlākak mattiya kiḻakku nāṭukaḷiyum cīṉāviyum paṭṭu vaṇikap pāti iṇittatu. veṇkalak kālattiṉ mukkiya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷil oṉṟu kaṭal kaṭanta vaṇikam. paṭṭup pāti (Silk road) eṉṟa peyar vara, cīṉā tayārittu ēṟṟumati ceyta paṭṭut tuṇikaḷum āṭikaḷum tām kāraṇam. ippeyari uruvākkiyavar jermaṉ puviyalāḷar pērṭiṉaṉṭ riktōpaṉ pirapu (Baron Ferdinand Richtofen) āvār.
3. (National Geographic) itaḻ kūṟuvatu pōla Near East (aṇmik kiḻakku) eṉpatum Middle East mattiya kiḻakku eṉpatum piṉvarum nāṭṭut tokutiyi kuṟippaṉavēyākum:  ekiptu, īrāṉ, īrāk, irēl, jārtaṉ, lepeṉāṉ, pālatīṉam, ciriyā, turukki, cavutiarēpiyā, ōmāṉ, pahriyiṉ, kirīṭ, cipira. innāṭukaḷ veṇkalak kālattil tamakkuḷḷum cintuveḷiyuṭaṉum varttakam ceytaṉa. aṉṟiya mecapotāmiyāvāṉatu iṉṟiya īrāk, ciriyā, turukkiyiṉ ciṟiya pakuti ivaṟṟik koṇṭatu. iṉṟiya īrāk nāṭṭil veṇkalakkālattil, cumer Sumer, akkāṭ Akkad, pāpilōṉ Babylon, aciriyā Assyria ākiya aracukaḷ oṉṟiṉ piṉ oṉṟāka āṇṭaṉa.
4. teṟku mecapotāmiyāviṉ paḻampeyar cumer / cumēriyā. mukkiya nakaraṅkaḷ uruk, ūr, eritu (Eridu) kiṣ (kish),  lakāṣ, akēṭ (Agade), nippūr, akcāk (Akshak), lārcā (Larsa) pōṉṟaṉa. cumēriya nākarikattil uḷḷūr maṟṟum veḷiyiṭa  amcaṅkaḷ kalantuḷḷaṉa. tamatu muṉṉōr kiḻakkiliruntu vantavarkaḷ eṉṟa cevivaḻicceyti cumēriyariṭam uṇṭu. avarkaḷ palateyvaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar; teyva vāḻviṭamākac cikurāt pīṭaṅkaḷi amittaṉar. kaṇitam, vāṉiyal, nīr mukāmittuvam ākiyavaṟṟil vallavarkaḷ. eṭi, nīṭṭal aḷavukaḷi uruvākkiyiruntaṉar. ceṅkal tayārippil orē mātiri  (uniform) aḷavīṭu eṭiyik kaṭip piṭittaṉar; aḷavu 11.5 x 11.5 x 2.75 aṅkulam; eṭi 33 irāttal.
5. paccikkaḷimaṇ kaṭṭikaḷil kucciyāl eḻuti āppeḻuttu (cuneiform) muṟi allatu (ampukkuṟi eḻuttu Arrow writing) muṟiyi uruvākkiṉar. vāḻvātāra mara nampikki (The tree of life) cumēriyariṭam iruntatu. aracarkaḷi muṭicūṭṭutal toṭarpāṉa caṭaṅkukaḷi uruvākkiyiruntaṉar. vēḷāṇmi mūlam perumaḷavu vālkōtumi (pārli) payiriṭṭaṉar. veḷināṭṭu varttakattil nāṭṭam koṇṭavarkaḷ. ulakiṉ mutalmutalāka nāṭuviṭṭunāṭu kaṭalvaḻivaṇikam mecapotāmiyā - cintuveḷikku iṭiyil kimu 3000 āṇṭaḷavil naṭipeṟṟatu eṉpar varalāṟṟāciriyar: The first sea - borne trade in the world, occured between the Mesopotamians and the Indus people around 3000 BC. atu kariyōrap payaṇamtāṉ (naṭukkaṭal vaḻip piṉṉar vantatatu). kariyōraṅkaḷil taṅkiṭam amittuk koṇṭaṉar; avi kālappōkkil vaṇika miyaṅkaḷāka, uṟpatti iṭaṅkaḷāka māṟiṉa.
6. kaṭaṟ payaṇatti cumēriyar kimu 4000 - 3000 aḷavil  kaṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar. yūpirēṭṭi - tikiri natikaḷil mutaṟkaṇ paṭaku celutti inta aṉupavam vaḷarntatu. avarkaḷukkum neṭuṅkāla muṉṉar iruntē teṉṉintiyat tol - tamiḻarkaḷum, cīṉarkaḷum kaṭalil marakkalam ōṭṭiyavarkaḷē. cintu natiyil kimu 5000 aḷavil, cintuveḷi makkaḷ viññāṉa pūrvamākak paṭakōṭṭum tiṟaṉi vaḷarttuk koṇṭatākat terikiṟatu: It is believed that navigation as an Applied Science originated in the river Indus in 5000 BC. ekiptu nāṭṭavar ceṅkaṭal vaḻi paṉṭ (Punt) nāṭṭuṭaṉ  kaṭalvaḻi vaṇikam kimu 4000 liruntē ceytatākat terikiṟatu.
7. teṉṉintiyat tamiḻarkaḷ kaṭaṟpayaṇap pārampariya uccatti piṟkālac cōḻamaṉṉar kālattil eytiṉar. intiya / āciyak kaṇṭa aḷavil mikapperiya, palamvāynta, neṭuntūram cellum tiṟampeṟṟa kaṭaṟpaṭi irājarājacōḻaṉ kālattil iruntatu; cōḻarkaḷ vaṇikattiṟku mutaliṭam koṭuttaṉar; cōḻar āṭciyil vaṇika amippukaḷ valuppeṟṟaṉa. aracum vaṇika amippukaḷum oruvarukkoruvar uṟutuṇiyāka iruntaṉar.  tam paṭikaḷi neṭuntūram koṇṭu ceṉṟu kaṭal vaḻiyil orē nērattil pala iṭaṅkaḷil tākki vellum tiṟaṉi avarkaḷ peṟṟiruntaṉar eṉṟu `ulaka kaṭal pōkkuvarattu varalāṟu kūṟukiṟatu: - [Oxford Encylopedia of Maritime History: (2007): John B. Hattendorf (Oxford)].
8(i). mecapotāmiyāvum meluhāvum mika nerukkamāṉa vaṇikap paṅkāḷikaḷ āyiṉa. mecapotāmiya āvaṇaṅkaḷil tilmuṉ (Dilmun), mākaṉ (Makkan), melukā (Meluhha) eṉṟa mūṉṟu nāṭṭup peyarkaḷ aṭikkaṭi varukiṉṟaṉa. kimu 3000 aḷavilum kimu 2000 muṟpakutiyilum immūṉṟum mattiya kiḻakku, pakrayiṉ (Bahrin) tīviyum, ataṟku arukilirunta arēpiyak kari nilapparappiyum kuṟittaṉa.
(ii). `mākaṉ ōmāṉ nāṭṭiyum hārmu nīriṇik kariyōranilattiyum orucērak kuṟittatu. ōmāṉ cempukkup peyar peṟṟatu; atiliruntē veṇkalam tayārittaṉar; īrāṉil kiṭitta takarattik kalantu veṇkalam tayārittaṉar. mecapotāmiya pakutiyil pōrkaḷ aṭikkaṭi naṭantaṉa;avaṟṟil veṇkala āyutaṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. melukā eṉpatu cintuveḷiyittāṉ kuṟippiṭṭatu eṉpar varalāṟṟu āciriyar.  mecapotāmiya āvaṇaṅkaḷ melukāvuṭaṉ kimu 2350 mutal vaṇikam tīviramāka naṭantati viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa. mecapotāmi yāvukkum (annāṭṭavar melukā eṉṟu aḻitta) cintuveḷikkum iṭiyilāṉa tūram 1,500 kaṭal mil. 
ittakiya neṭuntūra vaṇikam naṭantataṟku iru pakutiyilum camakālattil kappal kaṭṭum toḻilnuṭpam vaḷarntatē kāraṇam. perumpālum pāymarak kappalkaḷ, kaṭaṟkariyit taḻuviyapaṭi cātakamāṉa kāṟṟu, kaṭal nīrōṭṭam ivaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttic ceṉṟaṉa. vaṇikap poruṭkaḷil perumaḷavukku mēṭṭukkuṭiyiṉariṉ ullāca vāḻkkikkut tēviyāṉa āṭamparap poruḷkaḷum, avarkaḷuṭiya matat tēvikaḷukkāṉa vaḻipāṭṭu cātaṉaṅkaḷum iṭam peṟṟaṉa. potu makkaḷiṉ tēvikkāṉa poruḷkaḷ antak kālattil kaṭal vaṇikattil illi. perum vili koṭuttu vāṅkum vacati cātāraṇa makkaḷiṭam ētu! mēlum, neṭuntūrak kaṭaṟpayaṇam mātakkaṇakkil iruppatāl, virivil keṭṭuppōkum uṇavup poruḷkaḷi vaṇika nōkkil eṭuttuc cella iyalātu.
(iii). cilṭu coṉṉavāṟu veṇkala yukakkāla kaṭalvaḻi / tarivaḻi vaṇikam āṭamparap poruḷ vaṇikamtāṉ. -Trade in luxury goods marks the long distance trade of the bronze age V. Gordon Childe.
9. arapuk kaṭal nāṭukaḷiyum cintuveḷiyiyum iṇikkum kaṭalvaḻi vaṇikam paṟṟiya āyvuk kaṭṭuri 1954 il ūr(nakar) vaṇikar kaṭalvaḻic celavu eṉṟa talippil vantatu. - The seafaring merchants of Ur - Adolf Leo Oppen Heimer: Journal of the American Oriental Society 74 pp 6 - 17 (1954).  ūr nakara araca parampariyiṉar vittirunta kaḷimaṇkaṭṭi clay tablets nūlakam akaḻāyvil veḷivantu avaṟṟip paṭitta piṉṉartāṉ ariya takavalkaḷ kiṭittaṉa. mecapotāmiya akkāṭ, tilmuṉ, mākaṉ meluhā ākiyavaṟṟiṟku iṭiyil naṭanta kaṭal vaṇikam, (tilmuṉ eṉappaṭṭa pahrayiṉ tīvu vakitta mukkiya paṅku), ivi paṟṟi terintatu. kiḻakkuāppirikkakkariyōram mutal teṟku āciyak kariyōram vari ekiptu, tilmuṉ, intiya mēlikkari ivaṟṟi uḷḷaṭakkiya kaṭal vaṇikap pāti iruntati piṟitōr āyvuri niṟuvukiṟatu. itaṉ mūlam vaṟaṭciyit tākkup piṭikkum cōḷam, kampu, rāki pōṉṟavi āppirikkāvil iruntu āciyāvukku vantati aṟikiṟōm.
10. melukā eṉpatu cintuppakutiyi, (paluccitāṉ malip piratēca kulli uṭpaṭak) kuṟippiṭṭatu eṉak koḷvataṟku ātāraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa (oru cilarē māṟṟuk karuttiṉar). mecapotāmiyāviṉ akkāṭ araciṉ melukā moḻi peyarppāḷariṉ muttiri kiṭittatu. cintup poruḷkaḷ, (apporuḷkaḷil cila cintu eḻuttup poṟippukaḷuṭaṉ) mecapotāmiyāvilum, atupōla mecapotāmiyap poruḷkaḷ cintuvilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. cintuppakuti vaṇikarkaḷ kaṭalvaḻittoṭarpi mecapotāmiyāvuṭaṉ ōmāṉ ūṭāka vittiruntaṉar eṉpataṟku ātāraṅkaḷ ōmāṉil uḷḷa rā alyuṉāy -lum arapukkaṭal ōraṅkaḷilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa; iti niṟuvum kaḷimaṇkaṭṭi eḻuttu clay tablets āvaṇaṅkaḷ paṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
11(i). mecapotāmiyap poruḷātāram, veḷināṭṭu vaṇikam, kaṭal mārkkac celavum varttakamum, pōṉṟavaṟṟik kūṟum aracu āvaṇaṅkaḷum, piṟeḻuttup poṟippukaḷum melukā toṭarpu paṟṟi aṭikkaṭi kūṟukiṉṟaṉa; cumār 76 variyāṉa āvaṇaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa; avi, mecapotāmiya toṭakkak kālamutal pāpilōṉ maṉṉaṉ hamurāpi kālamvaric cārntavi. melukāvil iruntu iṟakkumatiyāṉa ariya kaṟkaḷ, muttu, kaṭṭiṭam kaṭṭappayaṉpaṭum maraṅkaḷ, tāvaraṅkaḷ, ulōkaṅkaḷ, taṅkam, piṟa meluhā uṟpattikaḷ paṟṟi avi kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa.
(ii). ṣerīṉ iratnākar, mecapotāmiyat tolliyal kalviyi ilaṇṭaṉ tolliyal kaḻakattil peṟṟavar. avaruṭiya  1981 nūlil mākaṉ paṟṟik kūṟuvatu: mākaṉ paṭakukaḷ paṟṟi ēḻu āvaṇaṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. mākaṉ poruḷ uṟpattikkum piṟa iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu koṇarnta poruḷkaḷic cilakālam cēmittu vippataṟkum (entrepot) uriya iṭamāka iruntatu. cintup pakutiyil iruntu koṇṭu vanta poruḷkaḷi mākaṉil iruntu tilmuṉukku allatu nēraṭiyāka mecapotāmiyāvukku aṉuppiṉar. `melukā, mākaṉ, tilmuṉ mecapotāmiya caṅkilit toṭar vaṇikam paṟṟi ērāḷamāṉa ceytikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. Encounters; the westerly trade of the Harappan civilization: Oxford University press, New Delhi; Shereen Ratnagar (1981)
12. mākaṉōṭu oppiṭukiyil tilmuṉ atika mukkiyattuvam vāynta vēṟṟiṭap poruḷ (taṟkālika) vippiṭamāka (entrepot) iruntatu. mikappaḻiya āvaṇaṅkaḷ kūṭa, vaṇika miyamāka, peru vaṇikar vāḻviṭavamāka tilmuṉ-ai kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. āka aṉṟu arapukkaṭaliṉ kaṭal vaṇikatti iyakkiya kēntira miyam tilmuṉ. enta āvaṇattilum mecapotāmiyap poruḷkaḷi meluhāviṟku nēraṭiyāka aṉuppiyatāka oru cāṉṟum illi eṉpatu viyappukkuriyatu. (tilmuṉ mūlamākavō, mākaṉ mūlamākavō āvaṇaṅkaḷ  aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟutāṉ nām karuta vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu).
13. cintunākarikam kaṭal vaṇikam cārnta nākarikam eṉpati uṇarntāltāṉ, ataṉ taṉicciṟappu teriyum. annākarika ucca kaṭṭa kālattil ataṉuṭiya uṟpattikaḷ mēṟku āciyap pakutiyil mikap pirapalamāyiṉa. (ataṟku muṉpu ippakutikaḷiṭiyē irunta tākkam mikac ciṟiya aḷavil tāṉ.) kaṭaṟpayaṇattiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttiya kappalkaḷ nāṉku poṟippukaḷ cittarikkiṉṟaṉa: appoṟippukaḷil mūṉṟu mokeñco-tarōvilum, oṉṟu lōtalilum kiṭittaṉa.
14. arapukkaṭalōrattil mekarkārukku aṇmiyil uḷḷa palakāṭ mukkiyattuvam peṟukiṉṟatu. melukā - mecapotāmiya kaṭal vaṇikap pātiyil kēntiramāṉa iṭattil irukkum palakāṭ caṅku cēkarippil mukkiya iṭam vakittatu. cintu makkaḷiṉ mukkiya uṇavāṉa mīṉ, palakāṭ kaṭalil kiṭittatu. 1963 - 1976 kālattil jārj ep. ṭēl naṭattiya palakāṭ akaḻvu mūlam kimu 2500 - 1900 kālattiṟkuriya uliyum (furnace), ataṟkup pintiya kālattiṟkuriya kaṇappu aṭuppum (Oven) kiṭittaṉa. caṅku veṭṭi, kivaḷiyal pōṉṟavi ērāḷamāka (uḷḷūrt tēvikkum ēṟṟumatikkum) palakāṭṭil (Balakot) uṟpattiyāyiṉa.
15. tuṟimukamāka illāviṭṭālum ōmāṉiṉ rā al-juṉāyi Ras-Al-Junis melukā mālumikaḷiṉ taṅkiṭamākavum kappalkaḷip paḻutupārkkum iṭamākavum iruntatu. ērāḷamāṉa  cintu maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ aṅku kiṭittaṉa. iṅku kiṭitta iyaṟkittār (bitumen) melukā kappalkaḷukkup pūcappaṭṭatu. (kappaṟpayaṇattiṉ iṭiyil cila nāḷ taṅkum iṭamāṉa) al juṉāyi kaṭal vāṇikappātiyil mukkiya iṭam vakittatu.
16. palvēṟu poruḷkaḷi uṟpatti ceyvataṟkāṉa mūlap poruḷkaḷi kaṭal / tari mārkkamāka cintu miyaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭu varuvataṟkāka palakāṭ pōṉṟa miyaṅkaḷi cintu veḷi uṟpattiyāḷar uruvākkiyiruntaṉar. palakāṭ pōṉṟē makkaraṉ (Makran) kaṭaṟkariyil uḷḷa cutkākaṉ ṭor (Sutkagen Dor) mukkiyamāṉatu. kaṭumiyāṉa pātukāppu vacatikaḷuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiya inta iṭatti 1927-l ṭiṉ akaḻntār. pāracīka vaḷikuṭā vaṇikap pātikaḷukku cutkākaṉ ṭor neruṅkiyatu.
17. mokeñco-tarōviliruntu mēṟkil 1000 kimī. tolivil  irunta ṣorttuki (Shortugi) āpkāṉitāṉiṉ lāpi lāculi kaṟkaḷi, poruḷātāra vaṇika kāraṇaṅkaḷukkāka peṟuvataṟkākavē uruvākkiya kuṭiyiruppu ākum. cintu veḷiyiṉ lāpi lāculi tēvikkāka uruvākkiya ṣorttukikku appālum cintunākarikap paraval toṭarntatu. kaṭal vaḻiyāka attoṭarpu ōmāṉ, pakriṉ iraṇṭiyum kaṭantu mecapotāmiyā - ekiptu vari nīṇṭatu.
18. ittāliya tolliyalāḷar moriciyō tōci (Maurizio Tosi)  cintuveḷi mālumikaḷum toḻilāḷarkaḷum vāḻnta oru harappā kuṭiyiruppāka rā al - yuṉāy irukkalām eṉpar. -A possible Harappan sea - port in eastern Arabia: Ras - Al Junayz in the sultanate of Oman: “Ancient Pakistan”: Peshavar 1982. Maurizio Tosi. (tōci (1944-) ulakil kaṭaṟ payaṇattoṭakkam, intumāk kaṭalil nīṇṭa tūra vaṇikap payaṇaṅkaḷ, mattiya āciyat tol varalāṟu, cintu nākarikam ākiyavaṟṟil ellām āḻnta pulami uḷḷavar).
19. mecapotāmiya kiṣ (Kish) cūcā (Susa) nakarkaḷukkum  cintu nakaraṅkaḷukkum neruṅkiya vaṇikat toṭarpu iruntatu. cūcā nakaram cumēriyat talinakarāka iruntatuṭaṉ cintu - mecapotāmiyā vaṇikattiṉ mattiya kēntiramākavum  iruntirukkalām. iru pakutikaḷukkum iṭiyilāṉa toṭarpukaḷ arapikkaṭal, pāracīkak kaṭal vaḻiyākavum, āpkāṉitāṉ vaḻiyākac ceṉṟa tarippāti mūlamum pēṇappaṭṭaṉa. cintu nākarikam viṣ, ūr (Ur) mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyatu eṉpar ji. ār. haṉṭar. aṇmik kiḻakkiṉ toṉmi varalāṟu eṉṟa nūliṉ āciriyar  āṉa hāri - rejiṉālṭ hāl cumēriyarum tol tamiḻarum orē iṉattavar tām eṉa neṭunāḷikku muṉṉālēyē terivittuḷḷār. Ancient history of the Near East: Harry Reginald Hall: Methuen London Page 171.
20(i). peruvāṇikamum neruṅkiya uṟavukaḷum mecapotāmiyāvukkum meluhāvukkum iṭiyil pala nūṟṟāṇṭu kālam nilaviṉa; paṇpāṭṭut tākkamum ēṟpaṭṭatu. ulakiṉ mutal cāmrācciyaṅkaḷi uruvākkiya māmaṉṉarkaḷil oruvarāṉa akkāṭ maṉṉaṉ cārkaṉ, (Sargon of Akkad BC 2334 - 2279) taṉatu kaḷimaṇ pativēṭukaḷil clay tablets, akkāṭ tuṟimukaṅkaḷukku vantu tirumpiya meluhā carakkuk kappalkaḷ paṟṟik kuṟittuḷḷār.
(ii). lakāṣ (Lagash) nakariṉ kuṭēyā (Gudea) kōyil kaṭṭuvataṟkut tēviyāṉa marat taḷavāṭaṅkaḷiyum piṟa mūlap poruḷkaḷiyum ēṟṟivanta melukā kappalkaḷ paṟṟiya kaḷimaṇ pativukaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila kaḷimaṇ poṟippukaḷ melukāvil iruntu vanta āṭamparap poruḷkaḷik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. cintunākarika  `pūcāri maṉṉaṉ ciliyip pōṉṟavi cumerilum kiṭittaṉa. iṭatu tōḷukku mēl pōrtti mārpukkuk kuṟukkē valatu iṭuppu vari cellum atē vakip pōrviyi cumēriya maṉṉar cilikaḷilum kāṇalām. cintu nākarikat toḻilāḷarkaḷ  cilar, cumēriyāvil kirāmam oṉṟil vāḻntataṟkāṉa cāṉṟu uḷḷatu. ūr (Ur) nakariṉ (araca parampariyiṉaril - mūṉṟām parampariyiṉar Ur III) āṭci ceytapōtu intak kirāmam tōṉṟiyatu. kuṭiyēṟiya ivarkaḷ cumēriya makkaḷāka māṟiviṭṭaṉar eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. tolitēcattil  neṭuṅkālam vāḻpavarkaḷ tam contap paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷi viṭṭuviṭṭu vāḻum tēcattiṉ moḻi, mata, paṇpāṭu, uṇavuppaḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷi mēṟkoḷvati `akkalcarēṣaṉ (Acculturation) eṉpar māṉiṭaviyalāḷar; cintuveḷiyil kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷum avvāṟē ceyalpaṭṭaṉar.
(iii). cintut toḻilāḷarkaḷum eṭupiṭikaḷum mecapotāmiyāvil kimu 3000 āṇṭaḷaviṉ piṟpakutiyil kuṭiyēṟiyirukkalām eṉpar akō parpōlā. iru nāṭukaḷukkum iṭiyil toṭarpēṟpaṭṭa piṉ 300 āṇṭukaḷil uḷnāṭṭut toḻilāḷarkaḷukkup patilikaḷākak kuṭiyēṟikaḷ ūr camutāyattil iṇintuviṭṭaṉar veḷiyiliruntu vantavarkaḷ aṅkēyē nilikoṇṭu vaṇikam ceyyalāyiṉar; uḷḷūr vaṇikattilum paṅkeṭuttirukkalām. cumēriyarkaḷ tamatu moḻiyil melukā eṉṟu aḻittatu cintuveḷiyiṉ tol - tamiḻp peyarāṉa `mēṭakam eṉpatiṉ tiripākum. [Simo Parpola, Asko Parpola & Robert Brunswig: The Meluhha village: evidence of acculturation of Harappan traders in third millenium in Mesopotamia: Journal of the Economic & Social history of the Orient, 1970]. (vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitarum, tamatu  1940 uriyil pāpilōṉiyāvil kimu 700-l iruntē tamiḻar kuṭiyēṟṟam iruntirukkalām eṉpar: Origin and Spread of the Tamils V.R. Ramachandra Dikshitar).
21. mecapotāmiya vaṇikam caṭṭa pūrvamāṉa oḻuṅku muṟikku uṭpaṭṭiruntatu. `ūr nakari āṇṭa cumēriyar vaṇikam toṭarpāṉa caṭṭaṅkaḷ vitimuṟikaḷ aṭaṅkiya  ūr nāmu caṭṭat tokuppi (Code of Ur-Nammu) veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa kaḷimaṇ kaṭṭip pativukaḷ clay tablets ituvariyil mecapotāmiyāvil kiṭittu avi iṉṟum īrāk, ciriyā paḻamporuḷ miyaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiliruntu celvac ceḻippu, nākarikam ivaṟṟōṭu caṭṭam, oḻuṅkiṟkum mukkiyattuvam koṭutta aracukaḷ annāṭṭi āṇṭaṉar eṉat terikiṟatu. 
22. vaṇikattiṟkup peyarpeṟṟa kimu 3000 kaḷiṉ miyappakutiyil viḷaṅkiya epilā (Ebila) nakaril ulakiṉ mikat toṉmiyāṉa nūlakam (Ebla: the world’s oldest library The Journal of Library History: Summer 1981, pp. 488 - 500) iruntatu. nalla niliyil uḷḷa kaḷimaṇ pativukaḷ clay tablets 1800uṭaṉ, tuṇṭukaḷ 4700, ērāḷamāṉa ciṉṉañciṟu tuṇṭukaḷ ivaṟṟi 1974 - 75 il ittāliyat tolliyalāḷar pōlō mattiyē (Paolo Mathiae) akaḻnteṭuttār. epilā nakar araci kimu 2250 il akkāṭ maṉṉaṉ cārkaṉ tākki aḻittāṉ.
araṇmaṉiyit tīyiṭṭapōtu araṇmaṉi nūlakamum erintatu. kaḷimaṇ nūlakam eṉṟapaṭiyāl tīyil venta kaḷimaṇ kaṭṭikaḷ (tablets) cuṭṭa ceṅkal pōl māṟi uṟutipeṟṟaṉa. innūlkaḷ iraṇṭu moḻikaḷil (cumēriya maṟṟum epilā) uḷḷamiyāl paṭikka iyalkiṟatu. ivi iṉṟu ciriyā nāṭṭil uḷḷaṉa. nāṭṭiṉ varavu celavuk kaṇakku varipaṭaṅkaḷ, koṭukkal vāṅkalkaḷ, nāṭṭu nilavaram, taṇṭaṉic caṭṭam, uḷnāṭṭu - veḷināṭṭu vaṇikamcār caṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷ ivi paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ intak kaṭṭikaḷil uḷḷaṉa. (paḷḷi māṇavarkaḷukku kaḷimaṇ kaṭṭikaḷil kucciyāl eḻutiṭap payiṟci vaḻaṅkum payilakamum inta nūlakattiṟku arukil iruntatu).
23(i). mecapotāmiyāvukkum (cintuppakuti) meluhāvukkum iṭiyilāṉa kaṭal, tari vaṇikam kimu 3000 il toṭaṅki toyviṉṟip paṉṉeṭuṅkālam naṭantati āvaṇaṅkaḷ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa; iru pakuti vaṇikarkaḷum perum ilāpam īṭṭiṉar; mokeñco-tarō perum vaṇika nakaram; aṅku vāḻnta vaṇikarkaḷ piṟkālattil araṇmaṉikaḷ pōṉṟa perum māḷikikaḷik kaṭṭi vāḻntaṉar eṉṟum terikiṟatu.
(ii). vaṇikarkaḷ māttiramaṉṟi (carvatēca vaṇikattiṉ mukkiya aṅkamāka) iṭit tarakarkaḷ, putiya canti piṭittuk koṭuppōr, ivarkaḷum paṇakkārar ākum vāyppu aṉṟum iruntatu. cintu iṭittarakarkaḷ paluccitāṉ malip piratēcattil (mecapotāmiyāvukkum meluhāviṟkum iṭippaṭṭa) kulliyil vāḻntirukkalām eṉpār ṭēl; kulli iṭittarakarkaḷ iru tarappiṉarukkum vēṇṭiyavarkaḷāka iruntaṉar eṉpar ṭēl; iṭittarakarkaḷ eṉṟālē appaṭittāṉē!
24(i). mecapotāmiya, ekiptu, aṇmik kiḻakku Near East nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ ceyta vaṇikattil cintunakaraṅkaḷ ēṟṟumati ceyta poruḷkaḷiṉ uttēcappaṭṭiyal varumāṟu:
āṇ / peṇ aṇiyum taṅkam / veḷḷi / caṅku āparaṇaṅkaḷ; mukappūccu, yāṉit tanta muttirikaḷ / ciṟiya alaṅkārap peṭṭikaḷ, tāyak kaṭṭikaḷ; navaratṉa āparaṇaṅkaḷ (kuṟippāka maṇi mālikaḷ, kivaḷikaḷ, peyṉ, lāpi lāculik kaṟkaḷ) piṟa āparaṇaṅkaḷ, paruttit tuṇi / āṭikaḷ; viliyuyarnta kal patitta āṭamparap poruṭkaḷ, vaḻipāṭṭukkuriya ēṉaṅkaḷ.
(ii). ivitavira erumi, kōḻi pōṉṟaṉavum mecapotāmiyāvukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. lōtal pōṉṟa miyaṅkaḷil nīṇṭa, vāl pōṉṟa aṭippākam uḷḷa uyaramāṉa cāṭikaḷ kiṭittaṉa. avi ēṟṟumatip poruḷkaḷi aṭittu aṉuppiṭa, allatu tirāṭcit tēṟali (wine) aṉuppiṭa payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām. (cintuveḷiyil tēṟalukkāṉa tirāṭci payiriṭṭaṉar). cāṭiyiṉ vālpakutiyāṉatu tiravam mēlēyum, vīḻpaṭivu kīḻēyum niṟkum vakiyil amintatu.
25. paruttinūl koṇṭu nērttiyāka neyta tuṇiyākiya (maliṉ Muslin rakam) cintuveḷiyil iruntu ēṟṟumatiyāṉatu. intat tuṇiyi `cintu eṉṟē mecapotāmiyarkaḷ aḻittaṉar. mokeñco-tarō vīṭukaḷil nūl nūṟkum katirkaḷ, (katirkaḷum vili uyarnta kaṟkaḷ poruttiya katirkaḷ uṭpaṭak) kiṭittaṉa.
26. mecapotāmiyāvil iruntu cintuveḷikku ēṟṟumatiyāṉa poruḷkaḷil veḷḷi, takaram, cempu, neyta  kampaḷi āṭikaḷ, tār (bitumen) aṭaṅkum. cintuveḷiyil kampaḷi āṭu vaḷarkkavilli. tār cintuveḷiyil iyaṟkiyil ciṟitaḷavē kiṭittatāl, atikat tēvikkāka iṟakkumati ceytaṉar; kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷil / paṭaku, kappalkaḷil tār pūci nīrkkacivit taṭuttaṉar. cintuveḷiyil cempum takaramum ōraḷavu iruntapōtum tēvi atikamāyiruntatu.
27(i). kaṭalvaḻiyāka ēṟṟumati ceyyakkūṭiya cintuppakutip peruntuṟi mukaṅkaḷ mūṉṟu: peṭ tuvārakā, lōtal, palakāṭ.  ivaṟṟiviṭac ciṟiya tuṟimukaṅkaḷum cila iruntaṉa. ivi tuṟimukaṅkaḷāka maṭṭumaṉṟi uṟpattikkāṉa kaccāp poruḷ peṟṟuttarum miyaṅkaḷākavum, (ēṟṟumatik kāṉa) poruḷkaḷi uṟpatticeyyum iṭaṅkaḷākavum iruntaṉa. ivvakiyil lōtal muṉṉaṇi iṭam vakikkiṟatu; mokeñco-tarō, harappā nakaraṅkaḷiliruntu mūlap poruḷkaḷi mottamākap peṟṟu lōtalkārarkaḷ tammic cūḻavirunta kirāma, nakara  uṟpattiyāḷarkaḷukku viniyōkittu, poruṭkaḷic ceyvittu  ēṟṟumati ceytaṉar.
(ii). ērāḷamāṉa (veṇkalattālāṉa) veṭṭuk karuvikaḷ, īṭṭi muṉikaḷ; ceppu / veṇkala mīṉpiṭi upakaraṇaṅkaḷ; kaṭaṟ caṅkil ceyta āparaṇaṅkaḷ, viṭūriya āparaṇaṅkaḷ, āṉit tanta āṭamparap poruḷkaḷ; merukūṭṭiya āparaṇakkaṟkaḷ ivaṟṟi lōtal ēṟṟumati ceytatu. ekiptu, tilmuṉ, mākaṉ, cumēriyā, pāracīka vaḷikuṭā ivaṟṟiṉ muttirikaḷ lōtalil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. lōtal akaḻvukaḷil kaṭaṟcaṅku/ yāṉittanta ēṟṟumatip poruḷkaḷum (peṇkaḷiṉ kātaṇikaḷ pala uṭpaṭa) kiṭittaṉa.
(iii). lōtaliṉ alaṅkāra maṇikaḷ (ornamental beads); peyaṉ-il veṭṭiya vicittiramāṉa pala alaṅkāra (designs), maṇikaḷil tuḷi pōṭum vicēṭak karuvikaḷ, nīṇṭa uruḷi vaṭiva maṇikaḷ, pōṉṟavi kaḻuttumāli kaḷākavum, piṟa āparaṇaṅkaḷākavum ēṟṟumatiyāyiṉa. cintu āparaṇa ēṟṟumatikaḷil `tuḷiyiṭṭa ariya kal mālikaḷ mukkiya iṭam vakittaṉa; cumeriya kiṣ, ūr akaḻāyvukaḷil avi kiṭittaṉa. (āpkāṉitāṉiṉ) jalālapāt, cūcā ākiyavaṟṟilum cintu āparaṇaṅkaḷ ērāḷamākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.
28. pāpilōṉi āṇṭa 6ām hamurāpi maṉṉaṉ (Hammurabi) kimu 1760 il veḷiyiṭṭa caṭṭat tokuppil meluhā vaṇikam paṟṟiya kuṟippu uḷḷatu. iṉṟum nalla niliyil pātukāppākavuḷḷa caṭṭat tokuppu atu. civil/ kirimiṉal caṭṭaṅkaḷ, vāṇikam, poruḷātāra uṟpatti, paṉṉāṭṭu vaṇika muyaṟcikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟi avaṉ kālak kaḷimaṇ poṟippukaḷ clay tablets vivarikkiṉṟaṉa.


mecapotāmiya - melukā toṭarpuc cāṉṟukaḷ
29. mecapotāmiya - melukā ivaṟṟukkum cintunākarikap pakutikkum irunta neruṅkiya toṭarpukaḷ piṉvarum pattikaḷil muttirikaḷ, maṇikaḷ, pakaṭik kāykaḷ, cuṭṭamaṇ ēṉaṅkaḷ, ulōkapporuḷkaḷ, eṭikkaruvikaḷ eṉṟa talippukaḷil viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
30. muttirikaḷ: ūr, kiṣ, tel ummā (Tell Umma) teppē kavurā (Tepe Gawra), tel amār (Tell Asmar) tellō (tello) cūcā (Susa), nippūr (Nippur) tel culeymē (Tell Suleimeh) lakāṣ (Lagash) uḷḷiṭṭa 18 iṭaṅkaḷil ceyta akaḻvukaḷil cintu muttirikaḷum avaṟṟip pōṉṟaviyum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. eḻuttup poṟippukaḷi akō parpōlā āyvuceytu kiṣ, nippūr, cūcā ākiyavaṟṟil kiṭittavaṟṟil maṭṭum cintup poṟippukaḷ  ōraḷavu iruntaṉa, perumpālāṉa  iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭittavi palavita eḻuttup poṟippukaḷiṉ kalaviyāka iruntaṉa eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
31(i). maṇikaḷ: cintuveḷiyiṉ ciṟanta aṇikalaṉkaḷil ariya kaṟkaḷ kōrtta kaḻuttu mālikaḷum, kivaḷiyal kaḷum amikiṉṟaṉa. maṇikaḷ mītu kūriya ūci pōṉṟa karuviyāl kīṟic (etching) ceyta vaṭṭam / nērkōṭu / vaḷivu pōṉṟavi nuṭpamākak kīṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itu cintuveḷiyiṉ ciṟanta  toḻilnuṭpamākum. (intat toḻilnuṭpam iṉṟu muṉṉēṟi yiruntālum paṇṭum iṉṟum aṭippaṭi oṉṟutāṉ).  ivvēlippāṭṭu ariya maṇikaḷ cintu miyaṅkaḷilum mecapotāmiya akaḻvukaḷilum kiṭittaṉa. ūr maṉṉar kallaṟikaḷilum immaṇikaḷ iruntaṉa.
(ii). pīppāy vaṭiva maṇikaḷum (barrel shaped beads)  cintunākarikac ciṟappu tayārippu ākum: nīḷam 5 ce.mī; niṟaṅkaḷ pala; ceyporuḷkaḷ, ariya kaṟkaḷ, peyaṉ, lāpi lāculi, cuṭṭamaṇ ākiyavaṟṟil ceytavi; ivaṟṟuḷ cintuveḷiyil tārāḷamākak kiṭitta `kārṉīliyaṉ (Carnelian) kaṟkaḷ payaṉpāṭu atikam. ikkaṟkaḷ maṇ niṟam / civappum mañcaḷum kalanta niṟam / kiṭṭattaṭṭak kaṟuppu niṟam ākiyavaṟṟil kiṭittaṉa. ṭiyaṭṭiṭ (steatite, soapstone) intiyāvilum akaḻntu eṭukkappaṭṭālum teṉkiḻakku īrāṉiṉ teppē yahyā (Tepe Yahya) cantiyil avi ērāḷamāka kiṭittaṉa. cintuveḷikku ammiyam  ikkalli 5000 - 3000 kimu aḷavil ēṟṟumati ceytatu; inta meṉmiyāṉa kallil āparaṇaṅkaḷ / muttirikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. intak kallil ceyta āparaṇaṅkaḷ / muttirikaḷic cūṭēṟṟi valuppaṭuttuvar. intiyāviṉ aṇikalaṉ pārampariyam cintuveḷiyilēyē toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu.
(ii). cittiram poṟitta maṇikaḷ, pīppāy vaṭiva maṇikaḷ ākiyavi cintuveḷiyilum, īrāṉilum, mecapotāmi yāvilum ērāḷamākak kiṭittaṉa. ṭṭiyaṭiṭ māvip paciyuṭaṉ kalantu veḷḷi niṟa maṇikaḷiyum cintuveḷik kaliñarkaḷ uṟpatti ceytaṉar. iṉṟiya karnāṭakāvil iruntu aṉṟu cintuppakutiyiṉar peṟṟa taṅkamum, elekṭiram (Electrum) eṉappaṭum iyaṟki taṅkamum veḷḷiyum kalanta ulōkamum aṇikala uṟpattikku payaṉpaṭṭaṉa.
32. pakaṭik kāykaḷ: ivi paṟṟi ṭēl virivāka āyvu ceytuḷḷār. āṟu pakkaṅkaḷ koṇṭa kaṭṭikaḷum (cubes) nīḷ caturak kaṭṭikaḷum, cintuvil ceyyappaṭṭu mecapotāmiyā uṭpaṭa aṇmik kiḻakku nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟaṉa. ivi viḷiyāṭṭiṟkum, nimittam kūṟuvataṟkum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa.
33. cuṭṭamaṇ ēṉaṅkaḷ: vaḷikuṭā nāṭukaḷ aṉittilum cintuppakuti cārnta cuṭṭamaṇ uṇavut taṭṭukaḷ, nīrkkuṭaṅkaḷ, parukum kiṇṇaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavi akaḻnteṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉavē ivi cintuppakutiyiṉ piratāṉa ēṟṟumatip poruḷāka iruntirukkum. (ivaṟṟiṉ uṭinta pākaṅkaḷ āyirak kaṇakkil kiṭittaṉa). kaṟuppu niṟam tīṭṭiya uyaramāṉa cāṭikaḷ -  harappāvil perumaḷavil uṟpattiyākiṉa; (kaḻuttup pakutic  cintueḻuttup poṟippukaḷ cila cūḷiyil iruntu eṭukkap paṭṭa piṟaku poṟikkap paṭṭavi). ēṟṟumatip poruḷkaḷil ippoṟippukaḷi vicēṭa arttattil ceytirukkalām eṉṟu cilar karutuvar. intac cāṭikaḷ (ēṟkeṉavē kūṟiyatu pōla) tirāṭci matuvi aṉuppap payaṉpaṭṭatāl eḻuttukaḷukkum cāṭi matuvukkum toṭarpu irukkak kūṭum.
34. ulōkapporuḷkaḷ: cintu pakutikaḷil uṟpattiyāṉa ceppu / veṇkala cavarak katti, cātāraṇak katti (oru muṉiyil aḻakāṉa uruṇṭiyuṭaṉ), peṇkaḷ talik koṇṭiyil cerukum ūci, kātu aḻukku eṭukkum mikac ciṟiya karaṇṭi, veṇkala mukampārkkumkaṇṇāṭi pōṉṟaviyellām mecapotāmiyāvilum īrāṉilum akaḻnteṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa -- āṉāl mikak kuṟinta eṇṇikkiyil.
35. eṭikkaruvikaḷ: cintupakuti eṭik karuvikaḷi vaḷikuṭā nāṭṭiṉarum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. harappā eṭi aḷavīṭukaḷ appiṟanāṭṭiṉarum ēṟṟaṉa. tilmuṉ (pakreyiṉ) vaṇikarkaḷum avaṟṟi nampakamāṉaviyāka ēṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar.
36. arapu, mecapotāmiya nāṭukaḷil cintu uṟpattip poruḷkaḷ perumaḷavil kiṭittapōtilum mecapotāmiya uṟpattipporuḷkaḷ perumaḷavil cintuveḷiyil illātiruppatu viyappu. pāracīka vaḷikuṭā muttirikaḷ 6, cintu miyaṅkaḷil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. kilkāmeṣ pōṉṟu, `iru pulikaḷōṭu oruvaṉ pōriṭum kāṭci poṟitta muttiri kiṭittuḷḷatu. mattiya kiḻakku uruḷi vaṭiva muttirikaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.
37. aṇmik kiḻakku nāṭukaḷ cārnta aḻaku cātaṉak karuvikaḷ cintup pakutiyilum kāṇappaṭuvatāl yār mutalil tayārittaṉar eṉak kūṟuvatu kaṭiṉam. mokeñco-tarōvil āṟu aṭi āḻattil kiṭanta ceppuk kōṭari, pōṉṟavi vaṭa āpkāṉitāṉ mutal kirīṭ, kirī vari kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kirīṭ vivacāya viḻākkaḷil payaṉpaṭṭa `ciṟiya vaḷiyattil toṅkaviṭṭa kiṇṇaṅkaḷ (Ring - kernoi) nāṉku mokeñco-tarō, harappāvil kiṭittaṉa; ivi eppaṭi vantaṉa eṉṟu teriyavilli.
38. harappā ucca kaṭṭa nākarika kālam cārnta nimirntu pārttapaṭi nilattil paṭuttirukkum erutiṉ ceppuc cili oṉṟu lōtalil kiṭittatu. ivvaṭivam ūr nakariṉ kali vaṭivaṅkaḷil oṉṟu. kaṭṭiṭap paṇiyil īṭupaṭṭa peṇṇiṉ (mecapotāmiyā kimu 3000 - 2000 kālam cārnta) veṇkalac cili oṉṟu cintuviṉ mēṟkuk kōṭi elliyil kiṭittatu.  avviṭam cintuppakutiyum īrāṉ pīṭapūmiyum cantikkum iṭamākiyāl inta veṇkalac cili paṇpāṭṭut tākkatti uṇarttukiṟatu eṉalām.
39. mecapotāmiyāvi āṇṭa aracukaḷ aṉittum kaḷimaṇ kaṭṭikaḷil varalāṟṟup pativukaḷic clay tablet records ceyvatil pērārvam kāṭṭiṉa. ivaṟṟāl aracar peyar, āṭcikkālam, īṭupaṭṭa pōrkaḷ, mata nampikki, araca āṇikaḷ, caṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷ, vaṇika naṭavaṭikkikaḷ pōṉṟa  vivaraṅkaḷ utaḷḷaṉa. ippaṭippaṭṭavarkaḷ cintuppakutit toṭarpupaṟṟik kuṟippiṭa villiyē eṉa viṉavalām. ērāḷamāṉa attakiya paṇṭik kaḷimaṇ pativukaḷ (paṇṭi mecapotāmiyāvil aṭaṅkiya iṉṟiya nāṭukaḷāṉa) īrāk, ciriyā pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ aruṅkāṭciyakaṅkaḷil muḻumiyāka ārāyappaṭāmal kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. (ekiptiṉ veḷināṭṭut toṭarpu āvaṇaṅkaḷ kūṭa muṟṟāka vācikkappaṭavilli). cintup poṟippukaḷi vācikkat tēviyāṉa tiṟavukōl īrāk / ciriyā / ekiptu nāṭukaḷil putiyuṇṭu irukkalām. kuḻappamum pōrum mikuntuḷḷa annāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟu āyvu ceyya muṭiyāta nili uḷḷatu. aruṅkāṭciyakap paḻam poruḷkaḷiyē aḻittu varukiṉṟaṉar.
40(i). mecapotāmiyāvukkum cintuvukkum iṭiyilāṉa ēṟṟumati atikamā iṟakkumati atikamā? (Balance of trade) eṉṟu pārttāl cintu ēṟṟumatikaḷ paṇamatippu kūṭutalākavum iṟakkumatikaḷ paṇamatippu kuṟivāṉatākavum iruppatu terikiṟatu: atāvatu mecapotāmiyā vaṇikarkaḷ cintu vaṇikarkaḷiṉ kaṭaṉāḷikaḷāka iruntaṉar. itupaṟṟi 1978il vanta āyvuri The balance of trade in South Western Asia in the third Millenium BC: Phillip L. Kohl: (Current Anthropology 19, (1978) pak. 463 - 92 eṉpatākum.
(ii). mecapotāmiyāviliruntu iṟakkumatiyāṉavaṟṟiṉ eccaṅkaḷ cintuvil kāṇappaṭātataṟkāṉa kāraṇam paṟṟi 1979il ṭēl tanta patil: iṟakku matiyāṉavi nukarum poruḷkaḷ / virivāka aḻiyak kūṭiyavi eṉpatākum. āṉāl ṣerīṉ ratnākar `kaṭaṉi īṭu ceyvataṟkāka mecapotāmiyar taṅkaḷ kavarccippeṇkaḷi mokeñco-tarō harappāvukku aṉuppiyirukkalām eṉkiṟār; oṉṟum uṟutiyākat teriyavilli.
41. kimu 3000 ai oṭṭi naṭanta uḷḷūr / ayalnāṭṭu  vaṇikam paṇṭamāṟṟu (barter) vaṇikamāka amintatu. ulōka nāṇayam puḻakkattiṟku vara kālam piṭittatu; nāṇayam vanta piṉṉarum paṇṭamāṟṟu vaṇikam toṭarntatu. āṟām hamurāpi  caṭṭat tokuppu 6 il aṉṟiya camūkattil paṇap payaṉpāṭu paṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. purātaṉa kālattil pacu, erutu, kaṉṟu ivi  paṇattiṟku nikarākap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. paṇamāka ēṟṟavaṟṟil mikat toṉmiyāṉatu taṅkam. kimu 4000 aḷavil ekiptiyar taṅkak kaṭṭikaḷi vaṇikak karuviyākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. (ataṟkum muṉṉar iruntē mecapotāmiyar vaṇikak karuviyāka  veḷḷiyip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar).
42. kimu 3000 aḷavil mecapotāmiyar `ṣēkkal (oru kuṟippiṭṭa eṭi vāl kōtumiyi kuṟippiṭum aḷavīṭu) eṉṟa aḷavīṭṭip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. anta aḷavu, vāṟ kōtumiyip payaṉpaṭutti vāṅkalām, viṟkalām. taṅkam / veḷḷi / veṇkalam ivaṟṟāl āṉa poruḷkaḷiyum immuṟiyil koḷmutal ceyyalām. (atē pōl taṅkam / veḷḷi / veṇkalam / vēṟu etāvatu ivaṟṟik koṭuttum vāṟkōtumiyik koḷmutal ceyyalām; vālkōtumi eṭikku camamāṉa vēṟuporuḷ koṭuttum brŒayh«]. mēlōṭṭamākap pārttāl itu paṇṭamāṟṟākat tōṉṟalām. āṉāl `kuṟippiṭṭa eṭi vāl kōtumi ṣēkkal eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ paṇamākac ceyaṟpaṭṭati aṟiyalām. paṇṭamāṟṟu mikavum kaṭiṉamāṉatu;  māṟṟa virumpupavaruṭiya poruḷ āṉatu piṟarukku tēvippaṭṭāl tāṉ paṇṭamāṟṟu naṭakkum. iṭiyil ṣēkkal pōṉṟa aḷavīṭu varumāyiṉ paṇṭamāṟṟiṉ taṉmi māṟip paṇap puḻakkam ēṟpaṭum. piṟar vēṇṭām eṉṟu nirākarikkāta poruḷ oruvaṉ kiyil irukkum vari evvita vaṇikamāṉālum, (paṇṭamāṟṟu uṭpaṭa) avaṉ kitāṉ ōṅkum.
43. cintu nakaraṅkaḷil taṅkam, veḷḷi ākiyavaṟṟiṉ (kalappaṭam illāta) `tūymiyi (purity) uracip pārkkum kaṟkaḷ touchstones kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu iruntaṉa. ivaṟṟip poṟ kollar payaṉpaṭuttiyirukkalām; allatu `mika mukkiyamāṉa koṭukkal vāṅkalil viliyākat tanta  ulōka matippi uracippārkka utaviyirukkalām. kālap pōkkil `ṣēkkal āṉatu `kuṟippiṭṭa eṭi veḷḷiyik kuṟikkum nāṇayamāka uruppeṟṟatu. aṇmik kiḻakkil paṇṭu vāḻnta yūtar atip perumaḷavil payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. taṟkāla irēl nāṇayamē ṣēkkal eṉṟutāṉ aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu: Shekel is the unit of money in Isrel.
